# Spark & Anvil Company Website

The studio brand (Spark & Anvil) ships a company website at `spark-and-anvil.com` (planned domain) that introduces parents/educators/press/kids to the 131-app portfolio.


> **⚡ THIS IS A CARD (V447-P4b progressive disclosure).** Each rule below keeps its heading (so `§ R-…` cross-references still resolve) + its load-bearing INVARIANT. The FULL detail — rationale, when-it-applies, cross-references, incident histories, reference-impls, examples — lives in **`Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md`** (fetch on demand; same `## ` headings). Regenerate this card: `scripts/split_rule_to_reference.py spark-anvil-website REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE --apply`.

## Scope of hub for the website (UPDATED 2026-05-25)

**Hub owns the website end-to-end.** The app-repo scope rule (hub ≠ implementation) does NOT apply to the website because the site is markup/content (Astro + Tailwind + TypeScript data files), not portfolio Swift app code. Per user 2026-05-25: "web site is not really code so it's okay" + "you own the website."

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-CLOUDFLARE-SCOPED-DEPLOY) · (R-SITE-DOMAINS).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The site is organized into 3 audience/purpose hubs — Play · Story · For Parents & Educators (R-SITE-3HUB-IA; 2026-07-15)

**The site is THREE audience/purpose hubs — `/play` (playable web-clone catalog) · `/story` (illustrated-story + audio-drama library, folds cast/books) · `/for-parents-educators` (adult side-door). The homepage is a LEAN playable-sampler splash, NOT the full catalog: a soft "Who's this for?" AGE-BAND CHOOSER (3–5 · 6–8 · 9–14 · 15–18) LEADS it, then ~3 recency-driven TAP-TO-PLAY teaser sections (play · story · books) + the orientation/trust band — and nothing else. The age chooser is a browse PREFERENCE, NEVER an age-GATE (COPPA — a child-directed site can't block under-13s; `sa:ageband:v1` on-device, no PII, always skippable, every band + content hub one tap away). Nav = `[By age ▾] · Play · Story · Books · For Parents & Educators` (a lean age-dropdown reusing the band/route/pref contract + plain content links; ADR-103) — NEVER the heavy `HubAgeBandSelector` in the header. `/play` is the ONE kid catalog (Playable-now / Coming-soon status tiers; absorbs `/apps`); `/story` is the SOLE story hub (folds `/cast` + `/books` + `/stories`; keeps the rich `/cast/<app>/<char>` chapter leaves); marketing consolidated ~28 → ~8–10. Every deprioritized page keeps ≥1 inbound link (§ R-SITE-DEPRIORITIZE-REACHABILITY); a prominent, independent Privacy link on every page (COPPA). Honest-yield on every freshness/segmentation lever ("calmer pre-filter" / "fresh," never a proven-outcome claim). SEO-N/A (under construction).**

**AMENDMENT (P207 A09, 2026-09-28, ADR-299 Amd 2):** hubs are **`/stories`** + **`/grown-ups`** (read `/story`, `/for-parents-educators` in this file as these). Nav = `[By age ▾] · Play · Stories · Books · Today · Grown-ups`. The old paths are PERMANENT 301s, never removed (printed QRs are immutable, R-PDF-BOOK-QR-LINKS).

→ **Sub-rules / cross-refs:** § R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER (the sampler invariants) · § R-SITE-BOOKS-IA (`/books`) · § R-SITE-DEPRIORITIZE-REACHABILITY (deprioritize ≠ delete) · § R-SITE-SEASONAL-FRESHNESS (the reskin tiebreak) · ADR-039/085/101/103/107.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Site search is ADVANCED (alias/synonym-aware) — it INTERPRETS the query by meaning, indexes FEATURES not just app names, is GLOB-DERIVED so it self-updates as content ships, is client-side/on-device (no third-party search SDK), and is ALWAYS available in the nav (R-SITE-SEARCH; 2026-08-12)

**Site search (`/search` + an ALWAYS-available nav search box on every page) MUST INTERPRET a query by MEANING, not literal name-match — a game's alt-name → the right clone (`othello`→Reversi/GridForge), a concept → its app (`chess`→GambitTales, `mastermind`→DeduceQuest), a subject synonym → the curricular term (`times tables`→multiplication) — and it indexes FEATURES/sub-surfaces (`tangram`, `reversi`, `cribbage`), not just app names. Five hard invariants: (1) the INDEX is GLOB-DERIVED at build time so it AUTO-UPDATES as content ships — NEVER a hand-list; (2) the alias/synonym dictionary is the CURATED, MAINTAINED layer (`src/lib/search/aliases.ts`; no client engine ships synonyms natively) — guarded by `aliases.test.ts` (every concept-alias target resolves to a LIVE `/play` route; un-aliased board/strategy clones WARN); (3) client-side / on-device, NO third-party or hosted search SDK, NO per-child query telemetry (COPPA / § R-SITE-FEEDBACK); (4) zero-results is NEVER a dead end ("did you mean" + browse-by-hub fallback); (5) all standard site UI gates bind (WCAG 2.2 AA · dark-safe `--sa-*` · ≥44px · keyboard/focus · dual-scheme screenshot-DoD). A literal-only search, a non-self-updating index, an alias→404, a hosted-search SDK, or a zero-result dead end is a defect.** Founder-direct 2026-08-12; decision `ADR-114`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-114_SITE_ADVANCED_ALIAS_AWARE_SEARCH.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_SITE_ADVANCED_SEARCH_2026-08-12.md` (engine/canon/UX evidence, the founder examples, phase-2 anon zero-results feed).

> **⚠ SEARCH-0-RESULTS GOTCHA (2026-08-20 P0):** never name a client-`<script>` local `t` (or anything) that shadows an `import { t }` — esbuild elides the import and every `t(...)` throws (green build, 0 results); and test a JS-driven surface by EXERCISING it (`?q=` / input), never a bare load. Detail → REFERENCE § SEARCH-0-RESULTS.

## Site search interprets a query in EVERY supported language — Unicode-aware normalize (never wipe a shipped script), a per-language curated concept→canonical alias layer that REUSES the self-updating English index (translation-before-expansion), on-device/no-SDK; a supported script that normalizes to empty is a DEFECT (R-SITE-SEARCH-MULTILINGUAL; 2026-08-21)

**`/search` MUST interpret a query in the user's home language (`homeLang.ts` `SUPPORTED_LANGS`) by MEANING — extending the advanced alias-aware search (§ R-SITE-SEARCH / ADR-114) to all supported languages. Five hard invariants, each a defect on the failed-dark-mode-axis footing: (1) `normalize()` is UNICODE-AWARE (`\p{L}\p{N}` keep-class + NFD diacritic-fold) — a supported-language script that normalizes to EMPTY (the Latin-only `[^a-z0-9]` wipe that today 0-results Cyrillic `uk` + Devanagari `hi`) is a DEFECT; (2) the per-language alias layer (`ALIASES_BY_LANG` in `aliases.ts`) is the CURATED CLIR layer mapping a foreign concept term → the canonical English token ALREADY IN the glob-derived index (translation-before-expansion; REUSE the self-updating index — NEVER a hand-list, NEVER a per-language full-index translation), Vitest-guarded (every target resolves live); (3) client-side/on-device, NO hosted search SDK, NO per-query telemetry (COPPA — inherits § R-SITE-SEARCH); (4) RESULTS DISPLAY in the SELECTED language (the OUTPUT axis, distinct from query interpretation) — the results island renders in the ACTIVE `homeLang` across EVERY `SUPPORTED_LANG` (its `curLang()` reads the FULL set, NOT the `'en'|'vi'`-only hardcode; re-renders on the `data-home-lang` toggle): result CHROME (count · no-matches · did-you-mean · type badge · the "Ages" band label — no hardcoded English) localizes via `t()`, localizable result CONTENT (subtitle/subject/band) localizes where a string EXISTS with a graceful English fallback, and the brand proper-noun TITLE stays invariant (§ R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS); an `en|vi`-only `curLang()` or a hardcoded-English results string is a DEFECT; (5) zero-results never a dead end + all UI gates + `dir`/RTL for `ar` + dual-scheme screenshot-DoD. GENERALIZE: covers every `SUPPORTED_LANG`; a new roadmap language (ar→zh + tail, `localization.md` § R-I18N-LANGUAGE-ROADMAP) auto-flags in the guard until its layer ships. Honest-yield = recall/UX, never a proven-outcome claim; aliases are concept keywords from verified translations + native-review, never raw MT. Tracked worst-first (NONE<NORMALIZE<ALIASED<FULL) by `scripts/check_search_multilingual_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_SEARCH_MULTILINGUAL_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN). Decision `ADR-170` (a self-hosted library — Pagefind/FlexSearch — is a founder-gated escape hatch only, never the default).** Founder-direct 2026-08-21.

**Result DISPLAY language = the EXPLICIT home language, NEVER a detected query language (ADR-172).** The query is used ONLY to interpret/retrieve, never to switch the display/chrome language (explicit choice wins = least-surprise; short-query language detection is unreliable for shared-script/Latin). INTERPRETATION is widened by the query's SCRIPT only (reliable Unicode-block: Cyrillic→uk · Devanagari→hi · Arabic→ar · Han→zh) so a non-Latin query resolves even when home-lang is unset — a `SCRIPT_TO_LANG` merge, retrieval-only, additive. Flipping the display language off a detected query language, OR adding STATISTICAL detection to guess a Latin query's language, is a DEFECT.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-170_SITE_SEARCH_MULTILINGUAL.md` + `Docs/ADR-172_SEARCH_RESULT_LANGUAGE_FOLLOWS_HOME_NOT_QUERY.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_SITE_SEARCH_MULTILINGUAL_2026-08-21.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_SEARCH_QUERY_LANGUAGE_VS_UI_LANGUAGE_2026-08-21.md` + `Docs/PLAN_SITE_SEARCH_MULTILINGUAL_PROGRAM_2026-08-21.md`.

## The homepage carries a curated, NO-autoplay Playable SAMPLER — play a puzzle/story/pass-and-play/networked-2-player in place, then deep-link to depth ("the spark"); the harmful YouTube mechanics (autoplay · infinite feed · streaks · auto-rotate carousel) are non-starters (R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER; 2026-08-10)

**The homepage's teaser sections (`▶ Play in your browser` · `📖 Stories` · pass-and-play) are TAP-TO-PLAY sampler cards — a user PLAYS a real puzzle/story/pass-and-play/networked-2-player IN PLACE for a few minutes, then follows an end-card DEEP-LINK to the full clone/story ("the spark"). It is a CURATED, FINITE, USER-ADVANCED sampler (the evidence-backed healthy alternative, catch→hold), NOT a literal YouTube feed. A sampler that AUTO-STARTS audio/motion, an INFINITE/algorithmic feed, a STREAK/engagement-farm hook, or an AUTO-ROTATING carousel is a DEFECT on the same footing as a dark route (the child-addictive patterns KOSA/Utah target + our own no-autoplay/no-dark-pattern posture). A companion FINITE browse FEED + RANDOMIZED featured sit alongside. AMENDS § R-SITE-3HUB-IA / ADR-039's lean splash (the ~3 sections stay, now PLAYABLE). Same adopt-the-good-reject-the-harmful discipline as anti-Gimkit + ChessKid-without-the-currency.**

**AMENDMENT (ADR-156, 2026-08-18) — MULTI-GAME + RANDOMIZED sampler · PROMINENT/ENGAGING · UNIFIED play-lobby.** The sampler is NOT chess-only: it samples board-game BREADTH via a data-driven `samplerGames.ts` deck whose POOL grows to ALL home-portable board/puzzle/logic games (ADR-158 reconciles ADR-156: the "~5–6, never all 9" cap is ON-SCREEN — round-robin shows ONE game at a time — so the POOL = breadth while the VIEW stays one-at-a-time), FEATURED default, with the featured game AND "try another game" order ROUND-ROBIN RANDOMIZED client-side (ADR-158: each game once per cycle → no starvation; SSG-safe, never a build-RNG); each game = ONE engine-verified one-move puzzle reusing the shipped renderer/engine (or a self-contained home-safe renderer), home-safe board CSS. The "🎉 Play together" LOBBY is PROMINENT (leads the play-with-others group) + ENGAGING (code-as-hero · tiered entry incl. a self-contained QR [no 3P SDK — COPPA] · roster momentum). The regular networked lobby (game-first play-by-code) + the inverted party lobby (lobby-first pick-together) are ONE surface over a single persistent SparkRoom (pass&play + one online room, both orderings, "pick another game" after any game). All ADR-041 safety invariants stay HARD (no chat/voice · preset emotes · ephemeral names · code-gated PRIVATE only, never public matchmaking · no accounts/PII). An un-added home-portable board game is a TRACKED tail (`check_homepage_sampler_games.py`), never silently dropped.

**AMENDMENT (ADR-164, 2026-08-20) — LAYOUT + BOARD-TAP invariants (R-WEB-CLONE-SAMPLER-BOARD-TAP).** The sampler's 2-col desktop layout puts the board FLUSH to the TOP of the card (no text above it) and moves ALL text into the RIGHT column as prominent CHILD CARDS (lead · gold "YOUR MOVE" HERO task card · controls) — the HOME-SAFE analog of § R-WEB-CLONE-SIDE-PANEL-FIT (ADR-150) + its board-top-flush. TWO board-tap invariants any sampler renderer MUST obey, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (a) a GRAVITY/drop board (Connect-4) is clickable by whole COLUMN (every non-full-column cell drops), never a single cell — else tapping the completing square does nothing ("couldn't drop the disc that makes four in a row"); (b) a piece painted OVER a legal-move dot MUST be `pointer-events:none` (selectable piece re-enabled) so a CAPTURE move (routinely a capture-MATE, e.g. xiangqi/shogi) whose destination is occupied by the captured piece is tappable — mirrors the chess board's `.gbt-piece`; (c) board frame padding is FIXED px, never `%` (a % frame-padding clips the perimeter → top-row targets unclickable).

**AMENDMENT (ADR-189, 2026-08-25) — MULTI-DECK: a SECOND deck for PUZZLE/LOGIC/WORD/CIPHER genres (Program #76).** The homepage sampler is a MULTI-DECK system (board · **puzzle** · story · books). The board deck's contract is *board → ONE move → solved*; non-board genres (cipher · word/anagram · nonogram/sudoku · logic-grid · Hanoi · riddle) get a PARALLEL sibling deck — NEW `samplerPuzzles.ts` + `PuzzleSampler.astro` + per-clone `src/lib/play/<clone>/samplerPuzzle.ts` (REUSE the shipped engine, engine-verified + Vitest), sharing the `SamplerDeckLead` chrome. NEVER merge a puzzle clone into the board round-robin (incompatible render contracts); all R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER invariants bind. Guard `check_homepage_puzzle_sampler.py` + `REGISTRY_HOMEPAGE_PUZZLE_SAMPLER.txt` (SHAPE-B) — generalizes `check_homepage_sampler_games.py` into a sampler-deck coverage family; an un-added portable puzzle clone is a visible tail.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-156_HOMEPAGE_MULTI_GAME_SAMPLER_AND_UNIFIED_LOBBY.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_HOMEPAGE_MULTI_GAME_SAMPLER_LOBBY_2026-08-18.md` + `Docs/ADR-158_SAMPLER_ROUND_ROBIN_AND_DUEL_HOST_AUTOSTART.md` (round-robin + the duel host-auto-start parity) + `Docs/ADR-164_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_BOARD_FLUSH_CHILD_CARDS_AND_BOARD_TAP.md` (layout + board-tap invariants) + `Docs/ADR-189_HOMEPAGE_PUZZLE_SAMPLER_DECK.md` (the second/puzzle deck) + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER.
## The homepage sampler carries a gambittales-style Easy/Standard/Hard difficulty control, and EVERY deck game respects it (R-SITE-SAMPLER-DIFFICULTY; 2026-08-25)

**The homepage playable-sampler carries a learner-facing 3-tier difficulty control — Easy 🌱 / Standard ⚖️ / Hard 🔥 — mirroring the GambitTales clone control: on-device (`localStorage`, no identifier → COPPA-clean, § R-SITE-FEEDBACK), default Standard, dark-safe/≥44px/`aria-pressed`/verdict-not-by-colour-alone. EVERY game in BOTH homepage sampler decks — the BOARD deck (`samplerGames.ts`) AND the PUZZLE/logic/word/cipher deck (`samplerPuzzles.ts`, the 2nd deck, ADR-189/Program #76; generalized 2026-08-25 via one shared `PuzzleSamplerRenderCtx.tier?()` + the reused control) — MUST pick its puzzle via the shared `samplerDifficulty.ts` `pickForTier` (band-preferring + nearest-band backfill; a `difficultyOf` extractor derives difficulty from ALREADY-engine-verified data — structural axes [shift/length/clue/disc count] or discrimination-load [choice/suspect count, correct always shown] — so NO new unverified content is authored) — a deck entry that ignores the tier is a silently-forgotten gap; a NEW entry wires it or carries a `# EXCLUDED:` line. Both decks share ONE on-device pref key (one unified homepage difficulty choice). Honest-yield: a puzzle-selection BIAS, never a proven-outcome claim. AMENDS § R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER (the sampler stands; this adds the tier lever).** Founder-direct 2026-08-25; decision `ADR-190` (+ § Amendment for the puzzle deck); tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_DIFFICULTY.txt` + `scripts/check_homepage_sampler_difficulty.py` (SHAPE-B WARN, 2 decks; baseline 25 wired / 0 unwired = 13 board + 12 puzzle).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-190_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_DIFFICULTY.md`.
## The homepage sampler carries REPLAY-same-type + consistent audio, and every deck bank meets a ≥3-per-tier DEPTH floor (or a curated multi-item POOL) so tiers are differentiated AND replay varies (R-SITE-SAMPLER-DEPTH-REPLAY; 2026-08-26)

**BOTH homepage sampler decks (BOARD `samplerGames.ts` + PUZZLE `samplerPuzzles.ts`) carry, composing ABOVE the ADR-190 tier framework (never replacing it): (1) a REPLAY-same-type control (`🔁 Another <game>`) that re-invokes the CURRENT game/puzzle's render → a fresh tier-picked puzzle of the SAME game at the SAME tier (distinct from `🎲 Switch game →` which round-robins to a DIFFERENT type); (2) CONSISTENT audio for EVERY game (not just chess — chess was the only one reusing the full-clone `board.ts` move SFX) via the render ctx (`playSfx('correct')`/`'wrong'`, reusing `_shared/cloneAudio`, gesture-triggered, respecting the shared on-device `sa:audio:muted` pref, NEVER a constant bed — R-GUARD-THE-RATIO) + a home-safe 🔊/🔇 mute toggle (NEVER edit `board.ts`); (3) a bank DEPTH floor of ≥3 puzzles per tier (≥9 items) for a CONTENT bank (distinct data, intrinsic difficulty — every entry engine-verified + Vitest-pinned, NO new unverified content, ADR-190), OR a curated multi-item POOL (`*_IDS`/`*_SLUGS`) for a PARAMETER bank (tier sets a parameter, so a single fixed item makes replay repeat — refactor to a pool the renderer picks from at random so replay varies the item). A 1-per-tier bank (Easy≈Hard via nearest-band backfill), a replay that repeats one item, or a chess-only-sound inconsistency is a defect on the failed-dark-mode footing. `bank-limited` = a tier capped by the shipped clone bank (documented follow-on, reuse-only). Honest-yield: depth = clearer tiers + replay variety, never a proven-outcome claim. Tracked worst-first by `scripts/check_homepage_sampler_bank_depth.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_BANK_DEPTH.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails ONLY on a deck entry with no depth row = silently-forgotten; **DRAIN COMPLETE 2026-08-27 → 18 ok · 6 pool · 1 bank-limited · 0 thin** from baseline 3 ok + 3 pool + 1 bank-limited + 18 thin). The sole remaining `bank-limited` = **crowntales draughts, whose Easy tier is STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE (a "biggest forced jump" puzzle's winner must be ≥2 captures to beat a rival) → Easy honestly backfills to Standard; Std 6 + Hard 4 are real** — the exemplar of the founder's "honest bank-limited, NOT fabricated" line (an inapplicable tier is documented, never faked with a bogus Easy).** Founder-direct 2026-08-26; decision `ADR-193`; program #80.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-193_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_DEPTH_REPLAY.md`.
## The homepage carries a 5th sampler deck — the MATCH deck: play a FULL board game vs a HUMAN-LIKE bot in 1-2 clicks; graded-engine default + opt-in trained-net upgrade, explicit tier (no DDA/profiling), all board games, calm-rails (R-SITE-MATCH-SAMPLER; 2026-08-28)

**The homepage carries a 5th sampler deck — the MATCH deck ("🤖 Play a full game"; `src/data/home/samplerMatch.ts` + `src/components/home/MatchSampler.astro` + the shared `_shared/samplerMatch.ts` driver + per-clone `matchXxx.ts` render fns) — where a visitor plays a FULL board game vs a HUMAN-LIKE bot in 1-2 clicks, ending in a calm result card → "Play the full →" deep-link. The load-bearing invariant: the DEFAULT bot is the game's GRADED-HEURISTIC engine (instant/home-safe/fail-safe — its realistic beatable mistakes ARE "human-like"; NEVER a handicapped strong solver), and a shipped TRAINED Maia-analog net (chess `maiaEngine.ts` · reversi `reversiHumanlike.ts`, ADR-199/200) is an OPT-IN "🧠 more human" upgrade, gesture-gated + NEVER auto-downloaded (ADR-094 + R-WEB-CLONE-SCHOOL-DEVICE-PHYSICS) + fail-safe→graded; the deterministic engine stays default+floor+CI-truth. All R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER invariants bind (no autoplay · one-at-a-time round-robin · client-RNG · deep-link · calm-rails/anti-Gimkit per R-SITE-FEEDBACK) + explicit Easy/Standard/Hard tier (`samplerDifficulty.ts`, no DDA/profiling) + engine-verified turn loop + dark-safe/WCAG-2.2-AA both schemes + dual-scheme×desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD + a two-mode e2e that EXERCISES a full game + honest-yield (engagement, never a skill-transfer claim). NAME is "Match" NOT "duel" (=networked human-vs-human, R-NAME-NOT-MECHANIC-CONFUSABLE). Supports ALL board games (reference-first Connect-4/Reversi/Chess → the 13-id universe); a home-portable board game with no match entry + no `# EXCLUDED:` line is a VISIBLE tail. Composes ABOVE (never replaces) R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER/ADR-107/156/158 (5th deck; one-move samples breadth, Match samples depth) · R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE · R-WEB-CLONE-HUMANLIKE-ENGINE/ADR-199/200 · ADR-190. Tracked `scripts/check_homepage_match_sampler.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_HOMEPAGE_MATCH_SAMPLER.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, reads origin/staging; `--ci-mode` fails only on an EMPTY deck).** Founder-direct 2026-08-28; decision `ADR-206`; program #92.

→ **Full detail (7 hard invariants, evidence, alternatives):** `Docs/ADR-206_HOMEPAGE_MATCH_SAMPLER.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_HOMEPAGE_MATCH_SAMPLER_2026-08-28.md` + `Docs/PLAN_HOMEPAGE_MATCH_SAMPLER_PROGRAM_2026-08-28.md`.
## Every homepage sampler DECK is dark-mode-safe — darken the CHROME, keep the real BOARDS; the CORE homepage loads no play.css so each self-contained `*Sampler.astro` MUST carry its own `@media(prefers-color-scheme:dark)` override (R-SITE-SAMPLER-DECK-DARK-SAFE; 2026-08-29)

**Every self-contained homepage sampler-deck component (`src/components/home/*Sampler.astro` — PlayableSampler board deck · PuzzleSampler · MatchSampler · any future deck) MUST be dark-mode-safe via its OWN `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` block, because the CORE homepage loads NO `play.css` → each deck self-contains its CSS in an `is:global` `<style>` with HARDCODED light surfaces (cream mat · checkerboard placeholder · letter-tile tokens), making them a dark-mode BLIND SPOT. The fix is a HYBRID (dark-UI research + ADR-155 / R-WEB-CLONE-COLORFUL-MANIPULATIVE-DARK-STAGE + R-WEB-CLONE-FULL-GAME-BOARD): darken ONLY the CHROME (mat · placeholder · hint · the puzzle letter-tile tokens) to a warm dark surface with AA text; the real game BOARDS + pieces keep their intrinsic scheme-independent colours (a white piece stays white; the nonogram keeps its light-empty vs dark-filled B/W grid — R-WEB-CLONE-TASK-VALIDITY). A sampler deck that ships with no dark override (a bright card on the dark homepage) is a defect on the R-WEB-CLONE-DARK-MODE-SUPPORT / R-SITE-DARK-MODE-WHOLE-SITE footing; the verdict is the dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD. Guarded by `scripts/check_homepage_sampler_dark_mode.py` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails a NEW in-scope `*Sampler.astro` with no dark block — auto-discovered, never a hand-list) + `Docs/REGISTRY_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_DARK_MODE.txt`. Composes ABOVE (never re-litigates) R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER · ADR-155 · ADR-189/206/190/193.** Founder-direct 2026-08-29; decision `ADR-214`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-214_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_DECK_DARK_SAFE.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_DARK_MODE_AND_CROSSWORD_2026-08-29.md`.
## A homepage sampler renderer's DEFAULT is LIGHTWEIGHT (pure DOM/SVG, complete-observability, school-device-safe) — a heavy 3-D/WebGL renderer (cubing.js `<twisty-player>`, three.js) is allowed ONLY as an OPT-IN, LAZY, FAIL-SAFE toggle (dynamic `import()`, never the default, never a module-top-level eager import); the cube sampler ships BOTH 2-D flat net (default) + opt-in 3-D (R-SITE-SAMPLER-RENDERER-DUAL; 2026-08-29)

**Every homepage sampler-deck renderer (`src/lib/play/*/sampler*.ts`, `src/data/home/sampler*.ts`) MUST default to a LIGHTWEIGHT pure-DOM/SVG surface — the CORE homepage carries no play.css + sits on the LCP path + a static POB server can't set COOP/COEP. A heavy 3-D/WebGL runtime is allowed ONLY as an OPT-IN, LAZY, FAIL-SAFE enhancement (a dynamic `import('cubing'|'three'|…)` inside an event handler, gesture-gated, fail-safe to the 2-D default) — NEVER the DEFAULT renderer and NEVER a module-top-level (eager) import (which pulls the WebGL bundle onto the homepage for every visitor). The cube sampler ships BOTH: the 2-D flat net is the DEFAULT (all 6 faces visible at once → the one-turn-from-solved task is fully inspectable; a 3-D view hides 3 faces + advantages only high-mental-rotation learners), plus an opt-in 3-D `<twisty-player>` toggle (founder reads 3-D better) whose choice persists on-device (`sa:cube:view`, no PII). An eager/default WebGL sampler renderer is a defect on the R-WEB-CLONE-SCHOOL-DEVICE-PHYSICS / R-SITE-PAGE-WEIGHT-BUDGET footing. Composes ADR-094 (heavy runtime = opt-in enhancement). Tracked `scripts/check_sampler_renderer_weight.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_SAMPLER_RENDERER.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails only on an EAGER-3D renderer). GENERALIZED to the maker-ladder `/play` 3-D renderers (ADR-251 `brick3d.ts` + WorldForge/StudioDojo) by `scripts/check_maker_ladder_3d_lazy.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_MAKER_LADDER_3D_LAZY.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; a heavy-3D renderer reached only via dynamic `import()`/`import type`, never a value static import — detail ADR-251 § Amendment).** Founder-direct 2026-08-29 (*"2-D flat net or 3-D cube? … I do better with 3-D, maybe both"*); decision `ADR-212` (+ ADR-251 for the maker-ladder); program #97.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-212_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_CUBE_DUAL_RENDERER.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_CUBE_RENDERER_2D_VS_3D_2026-08-29.md`.

## Word games (crossword/anagram/spelling/word-build/clue/…) are a first-class WORD-GAME FAMILY of the EXISTING puzzle sampler deck — ENHANCE it (never a new duplicate deck; the deck split is by render-CONTRACT not genre), one-solve micro-puzzles reusing the shipped clone engine, tracked worst-first so no word-game clone is silently un-sampled (R-SITE-WORD-GAME-SAMPLER; 2026-09-06)

**Word games are a genre of the EXISTING homepage PUZZLE sampler deck (ADR-189 `src/data/home/samplerPuzzles.ts` / `PuzzleSampler`), NOT a new parallel deck — the sampler deck split is by render-CONTRACT (board-move vs varied micro-interaction), so a word game is ALWAYS a puzzle-deck entry and a brand-new "word games" deck would DUPLICATE the puzzle deck (a defect). The program ENHANCES the puzzle deck with a first-class WORD-GAME FAMILY: each word-game clone (crossword · anagram · spelling · word-build · clue-deduction · connection · morphology · lexicography) opts in the ADR-189 way — a NET-NEW `src/lib/play/<clone>/samplerPuzzle.ts` (a `PuzzleSamplerRender` mounting ONE tiny self-contained solvable micro-puzzle that REUSES the shipped clone engine + curated bank, engine-verified + Vitest-pinned) + an `app:`-tagged entry in `samplerPuzzles.ts` + a `samplerPuzzles.test.ts` cross-check — inheriting ALL the sampler invariants (each a dark-route-footing defect): `pickForTier` difficulty (ADR-190) over a ≥3-per-tier bank OR a curated multi-item POOL (ADR-193, replay varies); dark-safe DOM (ADR-214); a lightweight pure-DOM renderer, never an eager heavy runtime on the LCP path (ADR-212); client-RNG only (ADR-158); a deep-link end-card; calm-rails / no autoplay / no engagement-farm (R-SITE-FEEDBACK); WCAG 2.2 AA · ≥44px · reduced-motion; honest-yield (a taste, never a proven learning claim). Prefer clones that add a DISTINCT micro-interaction over a near-duplicate anagram. A word-game clone with a shipped engine, un-sampled on the homepage and with no `# WGS-EXCLUDED:` line, is a tracked coverage gap; a NEW parallel word deck, a hand-rolled (not-engine-reusing) entry, or an eager/non-dark-safe/build-RNG entry is a defect. Reference-first (cluepals clue-deduction + crosspals crossword-build), then a standing worst-first fan-out drain. TIER-1 high-blast (homepage LCP) → fresh-context adversarial review + dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD + founder visual gate (R-REVIEW-RISK-TIER). Tracked `scripts/check_homepage_word_game_sampler.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_HOMEPAGE_WORD_GAME_SAMPLER.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, reads origin/staging; `--ci-mode` fails ONLY on a REGRESSION — a word-game clone that was IN going un-sampled). COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) § R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER · ADR-189 (puzzle deck) · § R-SITE-SAMPLER-DIFFICULTY · § R-SITE-SAMPLER-DEPTH-REPLAY · § R-SITE-SAMPLER-DECK-DARK-SAFE · § R-SITE-SAMPLER-RENDERER-DUAL.** Founder-direct 2026-09-06; decision `ADR-240`; program #123.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-240_HOMEPAGE_WORD_GAME_SAMPLER_FAMILY.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_HOMEPAGE_WORD_GAME_SAMPLER_2026-09-06.md`.
## An INTERACTIVE board surface for a game with a promotion CHOICE offers the learner a piece PICKER — never a silent auto-queen; the shared chess renderer pops a Q/R/B/N picker on a pawn-to-last-rank tap (R-WEB-CLONE-PROMOTION-CHOICE; 2026-08-29)

**An INTERACTIVE board surface for a game with a pawn/piece PROMOTION CHOICE (chess Q/R/B/N; shogi promote-or-not) MUST let the learner CHOOSE the piece — NEVER a silent auto-queen — because an underpromotion (=N/=R/=B) can BE a one-move puzzle's solution and choosing is player agency (the lichess/chess.com standard). Realizes the `R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE` "promotion-choice solvability" sub-invariant. Fixed at the SHARED chess renderer `board.ts` (one DRY change → the sampler, match sampler, sparring, pass-and-play, duel, trainer, motifs, patterns, solitaire, learn, vision): a pawn-to-last-rank tap opens a dark-safe Q/R/B/N SVG picker (mover's colour, ≥44px, keyboard, scrim-cancel) that emits the CHOSEN UCI; the deterministic `makeMove(…,promo)` already supports all four. AUTO-BY-RULE promotions (draughts man→king — no choice exists) are EXCLUDED, not a gap; read-only ANALYSIS/game-review boards are N/A (promotion replayed from the stored UCI). A silent auto-queen on an interactive promotion-bearing surface is a defect on the dark-route footing. **DOWNSTREAM (site #3059, 2026-08-30 — the picker emitting the right UCI is necessary-but-not-sufficient):** a caller that tracks its OWN position MUST apply the FULL uci via `applyUci(pos, uci)` (or pass `uci[4]`), NEVER a 3-arg `makeMove(pos, from, to)` after parsing from/to out of the uci — the 3-arg form drops `uci[4]` → `makeMove` defaults `promo='q'` → the board silently re-queens the learner's chosen piece (founder "picked a rook, board shows a queen"); a picker test MUST assert the chosen piece is APPLIED+RENDERED, not merely that the callback fired. Follow-on (tracked, non-blocking): `allLegalMoves()` enumerate underpromotions so the AI/analysis consider them. Tracked `scripts/check_promotion_choice_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_PROMOTION_CHOICE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails only if the shared renderer loses its picker → auto-queen re-opens fleet-wide) + `scripts/check_promotion_uci_applied.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_PROMOTION_UCI_APPLIED.txt` (flags a uci-parsing handler applying via a 3-arg `makeMove`).** Founder-direct 2026-08-29 (*"why can't players select which piece to promote in the chess sampler? audit play + analysis"*); decision `ADR-213`; program #98.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-213_WEB_CLONE_PROMOTION_CHOICE.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_AND_AUDIT_CHESS_PROMOTION_CHOICE_2026-08-29.md`.
## Books are a FIRST-CLASS IA surface — a dedicated `/books` hub + a top-level `Books` nav item + a homepage teaser + per-clone book links; the Companion Book Program (ADR-083) presented as a Storytime-Chess product line (R-SITE-BOOKS-IA; 2026-08-01)

**The Companion Book Program (ADR-083) — per app an illustrated STORYBOOK (dual-tier) · ACTIVITY workbook · COLORING book · engine-verified PUZZLE workbook · multi-VOLUME series (~380 books / 173 apps, V685) — is surfaced as a FIRST-CLASS, browsable product line: a dedicated `/books` hub (faceted by format + searchable), a top-level `Books` nav item (Play · Story · Books · For Parents & Educators = 4, within the 5–6 library-IA cap), a homepage Books featured-teaser, AND per-clone `<BookShelf>` links on each `/play/<app>` landing. This AMENDS R-SITE-3HUB-IA / ADR-039 for the books class ONLY (the 3 hubs stand); the V628 `/books`→`/story` redirect is REMOVED (a redirect + a real page at one path conflict). Books stay FREE downloads (R2 CDN, R-R2-SYSTEM-OF-RECORD) under CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 + the at-cost KDP paperback channel (R-PDF-BOOK-KDP). A book that is shipped but reachable from NO surface is a DARK book (R-WEB-CLONE-NO-DARK-SURFACE); a `/play` clone with books but no `<BookShelf>` link is a missing-integration defect.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-01 (*"a new section on the homepage and nav bar for books exclusively and also integrate the book links to the webclones"*); decision `ADR-085`; research `RESEARCH_BOOKS_PRODUCT_LINE_STORYTIME_CHESS_MODEL_2026-08-01.md`; audit `AUDIT_BOOKS_IA_AND_INTEGRATION_2026-08-01.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Deprioritizing a page off nav/homepage OBLIGATES an orphan check — and the scan MUST match data-array `href:` links, not just HTML-attribute `href="…"` (R-SITE-DEPRIORITIZE-REACHABILITY; 2026-07-16)

**Whenever you deprioritize a page off the nav/homepage (the R-SITE-3HUB-IA "deprioritize ≠ delete" move) OR simplify the nav, you MUST run an orphan check confirming every deprioritized page STILL has ≥1 inbound link from a hub landing (`/play`, `/story`, `/for-parents-educators`) or the global footer — because "deprioritize" silently becomes "delete-from-discovery" the moment a page's last inbound link is the thing you removed. `check-site-internal-links.py` will NOT catch this — it flags BROKEN links, never an ORPHAN (a real page nothing links to). The scan MUST match data-array `href:` links, not just HTML-attribute `href="…"`.**

→ **Sub-rules (defined here; full body → reference):**
- **R-SITE-REDIRECT-NOT-LINKCHECK-VISIBLE** (2026-07-27; corrected 2026-09-28) — a folded/renamed page's old path is an `astro.config` `redirects:` 301 served by the CF adapter (a local `curl` 404 is EXPECTED). The link-check now resolves `dist/_redirects` sources, so a 301 link won't fail the build, but still REWRITE every internal ref (attribute + data-array `href`, all of `src/`, all 7 languages) to the real route: no double hop.
- **R-SITE-DEAD-BUT-IMPORTED-ORPHAN** (2026-07-29) — a reorg can silently orphan a whole FEATURE LAYER: a shared `src/lib/*.ts` still IMPORTED by chrome but whose exported render/getter has ZERO call sites is dead-but-imported (caught by NEITHER the broken-link nor the page-orphan scan); any homepage/`/story`/nav/marketing reorg MUST run a dead-feature-layer pass (confirm each surviving lib's export still has ≥1 call site) — re-wire or delete + its import.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The seasonal + holiday-aware freshness register is a FIRST-CLASS, IA-RESPECTING, date-based (COPPA-clean) layer that RESKINS the existing lean-splash surfaces — never a new clutter section, and never silently dropped by a reorg (R-SITE-SEASONAL-FRESHNESS; 2026-07-29)

**The site's seasonal/holiday-aware freshness register (`src/lib/season.ts` `currentSeason()` → hero/label/curation + play-first ordering; hemisphere-aware) is a FIRST-CLASS layer that MUST stay wired into the visible homepage + `/story` featured surfaces and MUST NOT be silently orphaned by an IA reorg. It is an IA-RESPECTING RESKIN of surfaces that ALREADY exist (the ONE root splash's hero copy + featured LABELS + featured CURATION), NEVER a new banner/section (that would violate the § R-SITE-3HUB-IA lean-splash). DATE-DRIVEN at build time — no cookies/localStorage/IP-geo/identifier → COPPA/GDPR/AU-Code clean; hemisphere detection stays client-timezone-only. A reorg that drops the seasonal wiring (leaving `season.ts` a dead orphan) is a defect on the § R-SITE-DEAD-BUT-IMPORTED-ORPHAN footing.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-080_RESTORE_SEASONAL_FRESHNESS_REGISTER.md` + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The homepage curated selections (featured apps · stories · books) are OPTIMIZED + AUTOMATED through ONE shared pure curation engine — freshness (recency+decay) · variety (MMR cluster-diversity, no mono-cluster flood) · seasonality (a REAL bounded boost, not a dead tiebreak) · daily rotation (build-date epoch seed, SSG-safe) — never a recency-only sort or a hardcoded hand-list; PII-free/COPPA, honest-yield (R-HOME-CURATION-AUTOMATION; 2026-08-27)

**Every homepage CURATED SELECTION — the featured web-clone grid + featured-story grid (`index.astro`) + the story-peek/book-peek pools (`src/data/home/{storySampler,bookSampler}.ts`) — MUST route through the shared, pure, deterministic, PII-FREE curation engine `src/lib/home/curation.ts` (`curate()`/`pickFeatured()`), which optimizes FOUR levers: (1) FRESHNESS — recency ranked into a bounded window with gentle linear decay (fresh leads WITHOUT a steep geometric decay swamping the other levers; the explore/exploit temporal-discount so once-fresh winners don't pin the homepage); (2) VARIETY — an MMR greedy re-rank with a GRADUATED per-cluster diversity penalty (`score = λ·relevance − (1−λ)·clusterCount`) = subject-cluster round-robin, so a single-cluster fan-out wave can NEVER flood the grid with one subject; (3) SEASONALITY — a BOUNDED additive boost for `season.ts SEASONAL_TAGS`-matched items (a REAL lever that lifts a season-matched item a few ranks — REPLACES the previously DEAD "seasonSortComparator tiebreak within EQUAL recency" that never fired since git-mtimes are distinct); (4) ROTATION — a tiny epoch-seeded deterministic jitter (`hash(key + daysSinceEpoch(buildDate))`) that reshuffles COMPARABLE-freshness items day-to-day — seeded off the build DATE, NEVER `Math.random` (SSG-safe; daily rebuild, R-PROD-DAILY-REBUILD). The story/book peek pools are GLOB-DERIVED + SELF-UPDATING (bounded top-N) — NOT hardcoded hand-lists — book candidates cover-CONFIRMED via `books-manifest.json` `hasStandardCover` (a non-null cover path to a missing file freezes prod, R-APP-ICON-SURFACE). A recency-only sort, a hardcoded hand-list, a mono-cluster-flooded grid, a build-RNG, a dead seasonal lever, or a per-user-profiling/PII curation is a defect on the same footing as a dark route. HONEST-YIELD: curation = variety/freshness/appeal, NEVER a proven-engagement/learning claim. COMPOSES ABOVE (never replaces) § R-SITE-SEASONAL-FRESHNESS (extends its dead tiebreak into a real lever) · § R-SITE-3HUB-IA/ADR-039 (SAME lean-splash sections — only the SELECTION is optimized) · § R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER · § R-CONTENT-HUB-CATALOG-COMPLETENESS. The INTERACTIVE board+puzzle sampler DECKS (`samplerGames.ts`/`samplerPuzzles.ts`) are a SEPARATE, already-automated family (client-side round-robin, ADR-156/158/189) — audited HEALTHY (`AUDIT_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_DECKS_2026-08-27.md`); seasonal biasing for a mechanic deck is deliberate N/A. Tracked/guarded: `scripts/check_homepage_curation_automation.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_HOMEPAGE_CURATION.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, reads site origin/staging; `--ci-mode` fails only on a regression — a curated surface losing its engine wiring). GENERALIZES: a NEW curated homepage selection surface added hardcoded (no `curate()` route + no documented `# CURATION-EXCLUDED:` reason) is a tracked gap.** Founder-direct 2026-08-27 (*"featured apps/stories/books not optimized+not automated → optimize+automate by season/freshness/variety; audit the sampler decks too"*); decision `ADR-197`; program #85.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-197_HOMEPAGE_CURATION_AUTOMATION.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_HOMEPAGE_CURATION_AUTOMATION_2026-08-27.md` + `Docs/PLAN_HOMEPAGE_CURATION_AUTOMATION_2026-08-27.md` + `Docs/AUDIT_HOMEPAGE_SAMPLER_DECKS_2026-08-27.md`.
## The canonical LOGO stays CONSISTENT — no Google-Doodle logo program; seasonal freshness rides CONTENT (the homepage reskin), with at most an OPTIONAL restrained homepage "doodle-lite" ACCENT that NEVER touches the lockup/favicon/nav (R-SITE-SEASONAL-LOGO; 2026-07-29)

**The founder asked whether we should change our LOGO seasonally like Google Doodles. Decision (ADR-091): NO for the logo, YES (already shipped) for the homepage. The canonical Spark & Anvil lockup/logomark (`Branding/Logo/`) MUST stay CONSISTENT everywhere it does navigational/trust work — the nav, footer, favicon, app icons, press/brand + social/OG imagery. Seasonal freshness is delivered through CONTENT (the § R-SITE-SEASONAL-FRESHNESS homepage/`/story` reskin, V653), NEVER by swapping the logo. At most, a clone/site MAY carry an OPTIONAL, restrained "doodle-lite" seasonal ACCENT on the homepage HERO surface only (a small seasonal motif near the hero, or a subtle seasonal `ExplainerHero` treatment, for MAJOR tentpoles) — but a seasonal LOGO swap (the full doodle program) is a defect, not a freshness feature.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-29 (*"should we [change our logo/homepage seasonally like Google]? deep web research and think harder. codify too"* + *"go with your recs"*); decision `Docs/ADR-091_SEASONAL_LOGO_DOODLE.md`; evidence `Docs/RESEARCH_SEASONAL_LOGO_DOODLE_2026-07-29.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The canonical BRAND MARK gets an appropriateness review — logo/favicon/OG/app-icon are in scope for R-IMAGE-APPROPRIATENESS, checked in EVERY orientation (upright · rotated 90°/180° · mirrored) + at 16px; an unintended reading is a defect (R-BRAND-MARK-APPROPRIATENESS; 2026-08-20)

**The canonical Spark & Anvil brand mark AND every derived surface (favicon 16→512 · apple-touch · OG/social card · PWA/maskable icons · app icons · press/brand imagery) are IN SCOPE for the § R-IMAGE-APPROPRIATENESS visual review, checked UPRIGHT · ROTATED 90°/180° · MIRRORED · at 16px (the OGC recall lesson) before adoption. A mark with an unintended inappropriate reading is a defect on the shipped-inappropriate-illustration footing; fix SURGICALLY (preserve palette/equity), then re-derive + re-review every derived asset (this is one-time CORRECTNESS of the canon, not a § R-SITE-SEASONAL-LOGO change).** Founder-direct 2026-08-20; `ADR-162` + audit `AUDIT_LOGO_PHALLIC_SUGGESTIVENESS_2026-08-20.md`.

## Seasonal/holiday + cultural content represents a culture by a respectful CULTURAL SYMBOL — NEVER a national flag for a foreign country (R-SITE-NO-FOREIGN-FLAGS; 2026-07-29)

**No kid-facing surface — homepage/`/story` seasonal copy, `/play` clone content, marketing pages, cast/chapter/book-cover/mascot art, or any generated illustration — may represent a country or culture with a NATIONAL FLAG for a foreign country (flag emoji 🇮🇳/🇲🇽/🇨🇳/… or a drawn/rendered national flag). Represent a holiday or culture by a respectful, non-appropriative CULTURAL SYMBOL instead (🪔 diya · 🧧 red envelope · 🌼 marigold · 🌕 moon · 🎨 colour), never a flag. A national-flag emoji/graphic for a foreign country in seasonal/cultural content is a defect on the same footing as an R-IMAGE-APPROPRIATENESS violation.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-29 (*"codify: do not include national flags for foreign countries"*), alongside the V653 CQ inclusive-holiday lift (whose observances are represented by cultural symbols, never flags).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The site supports Vietnamese users via an on-device string-DICTIONARY + `homeLanguage` chrome toggle — NOT a `/vi/` route explosion; NFC + VI-safe font + Southern teacher-voice register; internationalize NOW even English-only (R-SITE-I18N-VIETNAMESE; 2026-08-15)

**Vietnamese web support = an ON-DEVICE string-dictionary + a "Tiếng Việt" `homeLanguage` toggle (localStorage `sa:homeLang`, OFF-by-default, no PII), NOT a `/vi/` route tree across the 189 clones; build i18n READINESS into every new chrome string NOW even while English-only (`localization.md` § R-I18N-READINESS); VI-safe font + double-stacked-diacritic (ề ể ữ) screenshot-DoD; Southern teacher-voice register (glossary decided BEFORE translation); never raw MT to a child (ADR-060); culturalization-screened (§ R-I18N-CULTURALIZATION). A `/vi/` route explosion, a raw-MT VI surface, a broken double-stacked diacritic, or an un-externalized English-only chrome string is a defect.** Founder-direct 2026-08-15; `ADR-139`.

**Next languages after VI (the parity-layer roadmap): Spanish → Arabic → Chinese (Tier A, sequenced), then a demand-gated next-incidence tail — `localization.md` § R-I18N-LANGUAGE-ROADMAP + `ADR-139` Addendum E.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading) + `ADR-139` + `ADR-159` (kid kit-CONTENT VI RENDER, go-live Stage 2: `_shared/dv1.ts` swaps at the `loadKit` choke point, qid-matched, gated `reviewed && shipped` PER KIT so the drain never regresses cleared kits, off-by-default) + `.claude/rules/localization.md` § R-I18N-READINESS + § R-I18N-LANGUAGE-ROADMAP.
## The marketing pages MUST carry the AI-safety-moat positioning — it is the portfolio's most timely differentiator, and a consolidation/rewrite MUST preserve it (R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING; 2026-07-28)

**The company/marketing surfaces (`/for-parents` + `/for-educators` + `/method`, and their consolidated successors `/for-parents-educators` + `/about`) MUST market the portfolio's AI-safety moat — on-device (Apple FoundationModels, nothing leaves the device) · NON-companion (the in-app AI is a Teachable Cast novice the KID teaches, NOT a friend-bot) · NO free-text chat · CAN'T-hand-over-the-answer (Socratic) · compliant-by-design framed against the 2026 regulatory wave (GUARD Act, Character.AI's minor ban, COPPA-2026) — PLUS the "we meet Story Time Chess on its own board" (Logic/GambitTales) proof point. This content is LOAD-BEARING: it is the single most timely, most defensible, most under-marketed differentiator (compliant-by-design exactly as the field bans minors from AI companions). A marketing-page consolidation or rewrite that DROPS the AI-safety section is a regression, not a simplification — the same deprioritize≠delete discipline as § R-SITE-DEPRIORITIZE-REACHABILITY.** Codified per V631 (competitive-analysis next-steps, `Docs/RESEARCH_PORTFOLIO_COMPETITIVE_ANALYSIS_2026-07-28.md` §9 items 1/2; shipped site #1441). Before V631 the moat keywords lived ONLY on `/play` clone pages, never on any marketing surface — a self-inflicted under-marketing gap this rule closes + guards.

- **R-SITE-COMPETITOR-POSITIONING-BY-SHAPE (2026-08-16).** Calibrate the moat marketing to the COMPETITOR'S SHAPE: the free-vs-paid + privacy-villain wedge that works against a PAID/cloud/litigated incumbent (IXL) **COLLAPSES against a fellow FREE 501(c)(3) no-ads doesn't-sell-data peer** (Khan Academy) — deploying it there is a positioning defect (false-equivalence). Against a free-nonprofit peer the honest wedge is AI-safety ARCHITECTURE — **the SAME adaptive-LLM Socratic-tutor capability as a cloud rival (Khanmigo), but IN-BROWSER / ON-DEVICE** (`_shared/teachLLM.ts`: browser Prompt API / WebLLM+WebGPU / transformers.js; nothing transmitted; deterministic floor; `vetEnriched` can't-leak-the-answer; the graded math is a deterministic engine → no LLM arithmetic errors; ADR-065/073/143), NEVER framed as "we don't use AI" — + active-not-passive engagement + on-device zero-collection minimalism ("no login, keeps nothing"), and published efficacy (G2) is an even BIGGER gap (Khan holds ESSA-Tier-1 RCTs — never imply parity). Detail: `Docs/RESEARCH_COMPETITIVE_KHAN_ACADEMY_HEAD_TO_HEAD_2026-08-16.md` (+ IXL sibling `RESEARCH_COMPETITIVE_IXL_HEAD_TO_HEAD_2026-08-08.md`).

- **R-SITE-MOAT-ENGAGEMENT-EVIDENCE (2026-08-28, ADR-208 / Program #93).** The moat marketing MAY deploy three NEW, web-verified, honest-yield proof-points from the 2026 AI-ed-games evidence — each framed as *architecture validation*, NEVER an outcome-parity or "proven-better-learning" claim: (a) **engagement-is-the-wall** — the biggest AI-tutoring RCT (Khanmigo, NBER w35620: 18 TN schools, 2yr, ≈0.06–0.08 SD/yr *= practice WITHOUT AI*, 96% tried but median used it ~1/3 of days + only 17% of mistake-sessions) found the binding constraint is *engagement, not model quality* → our anti-Gimkit, wrapped-in-play, boundary-placed, proactive (POE two-misses-summon-help) design is the third-party-proven answer; (b) **build-time-AI-is-the-measured-win** — Learn Your Way +11-pt recall vs a digital reader → validates our gen-authoring + deterministic-runtime split (the LLM authors OFFLINE, the engine adjudicates at runtime); (c) **named-regulatory-tailwind** — GUARD Act (S.3062, bans AI companions for under-18) · CA SB 867 (4-yr toy-chatbot moratorium) · SB 243 · KIDS Act / COPPA 2.0 (13-yos into COPPA) all target *companion-persona chat* → "while the field bans AI companions for kids, we never shipped one." Never over-claim (no "closes the gap" / no efficacy-parity with Khan's ESSA-Tier-1 RCTs, per § R-SITE-COMPETITOR-POSITIONING-BY-SHAPE).
- **R-SITE-WORLD-MODELS-CONCEPT-ART (2026-08-28, ADR-208 D4).** World models (Genie, Marble; detail ADR-208) are for DESIGN-TIME diorama prototyping ONLY, NEVER shipped runtime gameplay for a kid surface (Hassabis's own "five years ahead, not fifty"; the § R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE no-live-generative-worlds line + the briefs' kill list). Proposing a live-generative-world runtime on a kid surface — or marketing one as a feature — is a defect, not a differentiator.

**R-APP-CLAIM-FROM-FACT:** /apps claims come from a generated fact; 501(c)(3) says pending (`check_method_app_claims.py`).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/RESEARCH_PORTFOLIO_COMPETITIVE_ANALYSIS_2026-07-28.md` §9 + the anchor doc's §3 moats + `Docs/ADR-208_THEME_PARK_SIMULATION_LOOP_LIFT.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_THEME_PARK_AI_EDGAMES_LIFT_2026-08-28.md`.
## Cost / spend disclosure is REWORDED not removed — keep the "Where Your Money Goes" transparency, but present FUNCTIONAL buckets (not hobby-scale micro line-items), ONE reconciled cost figure fleet-wide (a page-vs-page contradiction is a defect), the AI-tooling line framed on OUR terms, and micro-itemization in the audited 990/annual report (R-SITE-COST-TRANSPARENCY-DISCLOSURE; 2026-08-15)

**Every cost / spend / operating-cost / donation surface (`/about` §"How we sustain the work" · `/donate` · `/teach` · `/neurodivergent` · `/donate`+`/impact`) MUST keep its financial transparency (a 2026 nonprofit-funding expectation + a genuine differentiator — removal reads as hiding; 86% donor-give lift; grant committees expect a visible 990/audit) BUT obey four disclosure invariants, each a defect on the same footing as a failed dark-mode axis: (1) ONE RECONCILED cost story fleet-wide — the CURRENT cash figure is CURRENT-tense (~$500/mo), the at-scale ~$50K–$140K/yr is LABELLED PROJECTED / Year-3 and NEVER present-tensed as current; a cost figure that CONTRADICTS another page's number (the `/about` ~$500/mo ↔ `/donate`+`/teach` "~$50K–$140K/yr operating cost" landmine) is a credibility defect; (2) FUNCTIONAL BUCKETS on the public marketing page (Infrastructure & hosting · AI/creative tooling · Compliance & domains · buffer), NOT hobby-scale micro line-items ($2.50 domain / $8 Apple Dev — the natural-classification granularity belongs in the audited FY26 annual report / Form 990, which the page POINTS to; funders assess the functional program-expense ratio, not $2.50 rows); (3) the AI-tooling line framed ON OUR TERMS — human-directed research/planning/production, reviewer-gated, human-in-the-loop; NEVER a naked "AI authors our educational content" cost bullet (it advertises exactly the AI-slop fear whose parent-trust is falling 62%→49% and that our own cited ally Fairplay opposes — a self-inflicted breach of § R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING); distinguish the PRODUCT's on-device non-companion AI (the marketed moat) from DEV-TIME authoring tools; honest, never hidden; (4) KEEP the concrete donor-tier framing ("$5 = one app's domain for a year" — the GOOD line-item form) numerically consistent with the reconciled figures. COMPOSES with § R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING; refines `Docs/PLAN_FOUNDER_SPEND_DISCLOSURE.md`.** Founder-direct 2026-08-15 (*"should we remove/reword the line item costs … deep web research … codify and track"*); decision `ADR-147`; evidence `RESEARCH_WEBSITE_COST_LINE_ITEM_DISCLOSURE_2026-08-15.md`.

**⚠ Sub-gotcha (i18n copy edits):** rewording copy on a `data-i18n`/`data-i18n-html` element — the inline `.astro` edit is NECESSARY-BUT-NOT-SUFFICIENT: the client payload serializes the DICTIONARY `en` (`src/lib/i18n/dict.ts`), not your inline, so you MUST also update `dict.ts` `en` (authoritative) + align `vi` (`distributed-narrative.md` § R-MULTILINGUAL-DN) + refresh the fingerprints (`PARITY_UPDATE=1 npx vitest run src/lib/i18n/parity.test.ts`), or stale dict text ships + `parity.test.ts` fails the build (path is `src/lib/i18n/dict.ts`, not `src/i18n`/`src/data`). Full incident → ADR-147.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-147_WEBSITE_COST_TRANSPARENCY_DISCLOSURE.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_WEBSITE_COST_LINE_ITEM_DISCLOSURE_2026-08-15.md`.
## Every per-clone `NEXT_WEB_CLONE` pickup doc ANCHORS to the current site IA (never hardcodes a stale route) (R-WEB-CLONE-PICKUP-DOC-IA; 2026-07-15)

**Every `CONTEXT_HANDOFF_*_NEXT_WEB_CLONE_<app>.md` pickup handoff MUST carry a "Site IA anchor" that states the CURRENT site IA + defers to `WEB_CLONE_PICKUP_RUNBOOK.md` § "Site-IA context" — and MUST NOT hardcode a stale route (`/stories`, the old marketing homepage, the old flat nav). A pickup doc that references the old IA, or that omits the anchor and silently assumes pre-V257 structure, is a defect.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-15 (*"the existing web-clone pickup docs are now incorrect because of website reorganization … fix and codify"*), after a two-pass V265 audit: the founder's concern was the **per-clone** pickup docs (not the generic runbook, which V265 already reconciled). An exhaustive (zsh-safe) re-verify found the 18 flagged docs carry **no hardcoded stale-IA route** — they *defer* IA to the runbook (V265-corrected) + `<PlayNarrative>` (which auto-links to `/story`) — so nothing was silently stale; BUT there was **no guard** keeping the class IA-correct and **nothing made the IA-correctness explicit** to a builder picking one up. This rule closes both gaps.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every per-clone `NEXT_WEB_CLONE` pickup doc carries an explicit CLONE-ELIGIBILITY verdict — never auto-queue a questionable candidate as a standard clone (R-WEB-CLONE-PICKUP-DOC-ELIGIBILITY; 2026-07-15)

**Every `CONTEXT_HANDOFF_*_NEXT_WEB_CLONE_<app>.md` pickup doc MUST carry an explicit "Clone eligibility (verdict)" section that classifies the app BEFORE proposing surfaces — because "next web clone" pickup docs are auto/hand-generated from a standard tween-16×25-MC template, and that template is WRONG for younger-cluster, device-first, SEL/reflection, out-of-band-age, and no-portable-core apps. A pickup doc that assumes the standard MC-clone shape for a non-standard app, or that queues a questionable candidate for build without a verdict, is a defect.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-15 (*"per-doc eligibility"*), the companion to R-WEB-CLONE-PICKUP-DOC-IA (same V265 lane): the IA rule keeps the docs route-correct; THIS rule keeps them from mis-scoping the app itself.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Provisioning an account-managed Cloudflare backend follows ONE reusable pattern (R-CLOUDFLARE-BACKEND-PROVISIONING; 2026-07-16)

**Every new account-managed Cloudflare backend behind `spark-and-anvil.com` (a Worker + its storage + its route) follows the single reusable playbook in `Docs/GUIDE_CLOUDFLARE_BACKEND_PROVISIONING.md`, and each instance ships a deep-web-researched, cited per-instance RUNBOOK.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-16 ("codify the previous cloudflare provision work too"), generalizing the two shipped instances into one discipline. This is the *how-to-stand-up-a-backend* umbrella; `R-CLOUDFLARE-SCOPED-DEPLOY` is the *who-may-run-the-deploy* authorization rule they both obey.

**Two load-bearing provisioning facts (codified 2026-08-29 from the program #96 account-backend token+route work — verify current, but these held):**
- **The API TOKEN least-privilege set + gotchas** — the scoped token that lets hub automate a full provisioning run (`d1 create` · `kv namespace create` · Turnstile widget · `deploy` [Worker + DO migration] · route) is: **Account** → Workers Scripts:Edit · D1:Edit · Workers KV Storage:Edit · Turnstile:Edit · Account Settings:Read (+ Workers Tail:Read optional); **Zone** → Workers Routes:Edit; **User** → User Details:Read. Two traps: (1) **there is NO "Durable Objects" token-permission group** — DO namespace creation + SQLite migrations are covered by **Workers Scripts:Edit** (applied at `wrangler deploy` via the `migrations` array); and **Durable Objects require a PAID Workers plan**. (2) **`User Details:Read` is REQUIRED** or `wrangler` throws `code: 10000` (the `/user` call is part of the auth flow). There is **no CLI to mint a token** (`wrangler` can't) → dashboard or the `/user/tokens` API, a FOUNDER step; store it in `~/.r2-env.sh` (or a dedicated `~/.cf-provisioning-env.sh`, mode 600) — `wrangler` reads `CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN` from env; **always `wrangler whoami` to confirm the CORRECT account first** (`R-CLOUDFLARE-SCOPED-DEPLOY`). Full step-by-step: `Docs/GUIDE_CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN_CREATION_2026-08-28.md`.
- **A same-origin `/api/*` Worker ROUTE must exist on EVERY served zone** — the site DUAL-SERVES across **`sparkanvil.org` (canonical) + `spark-and-anvil.com` + `spark-and-anvil.org`** (4th zone `foldcommons.org` is unrelated). The **301 canonical redirect applies to PAGE navigations only — it does NOT rewrite a same-origin API `fetch`** — so a `/play` page loaded from any of the three hosts issues its `/api/*` call to THAT host, and the direct Worker Route (+ the token's Zone Resources) must therefore cover **all three** spark-anvil zones (the shipped room Worker already routes `.com` + `.org`; extend to the canonical). Routing on only one zone silently 404s the API for pages served from the others.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## User feedback (likes) → CROSS-USER recommendations via ANONYMOUS AGGREGATES — never per-child profiles, never a dark-pattern (R-SITE-FEEDBACK; 2026-07-15)

**Any user-feedback / like / favorite / recommendation / "kids also liked" / "most-loved" feature on the site MUST deliver its CROSS-USER signal (other users' feedback → other users' recs, per founder-direct) from ANONYMOUS AGGREGATE item statistics that are never tied to any individual child — NEVER from accounts, PII, a persistent per-child identifier, a raw per-user event log, or account-based collaborative filtering; and it MUST be GENTLE + non-manipulative (no engagement dark-patterns; likes never gate content).** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-15 (*"let users provide feedback … such as liking an app/web clone/story … use those signals for recommendations and feature for new users"* + clarification *"i actually meant using users' feedback for recommendations for other users not on the same device"*). Decision: `ADR-040`; research: `RESEARCH_SITE_FEEDBACK_RECOMMENDATIONS_2026-07-15.md`; plan: `PLAN_SITE_FEEDBACK_RECOMMENDATIONS_2026-07-15.md`. **✅ COUNSEL APPROVED 2026-07-16** (the ADR-040 launch gate is CLEARED, on the strict-anonymity basis below); **the store choice is settled = D1 (atomic-UPSERT counters + k-anonymity `WHERE count>=k`), see `Docs/RUNBOOK_CLOUDFLARE_FEEDBACK_AGGREGATE_PROVISIONING_2026-07-16.md`.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A SISTER-SITE partner integration is BUILD-TIME + PII-free by construction — vendored feed (no runtime cross-origin fetch), a frozen additive-only `_commons` contract, a child-safe band age-gate, honest same-tab labels, an allowlist-redirect hand-off, a schema-validated PII-free continuity passport, and NO cross-domain canonical — never a runtime partner dependency on a kids surface (R-SITE-PARTNER-INTEGRATION; 2026-09-22)

**Any cross-site integration between the S&A website and a founder-owned SISTER site (Fold Commons — `foldcommons.org`; Program #199, ADR-285) is BUILD-TIME + PII-FREE BY CONSTRUCTION, obeying eight invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) VENDORED FEED, NO RUNTIME FETCH — the partner's `/apps.json` is vendored to a committed snapshot (`src/data/partners/<site>.apps.json`) + a `--check`-able refresh script (`scripts/refresh-partner-feeds.mjs`); a kids page NEVER fetches a partner at load (partner outage would stall/leak; mirrors the partner's own reciprocal pattern). (2) FROZEN `_commons` v1.1 CONTRACT, ADDITIVE-ONLY (`src/lib/partners/contract.ts`, `$id` `sparkanvil.org/schema/commons-v1.1`; R-APPSFEED-PARTNER-CONSUMABLE) — a `bidirectional` CI test (`contract.test.ts`) validates BOTH the vendored partner feed AND the S&A-emitted feed against the ONE contract; the four legitimate per-site variants (`privacy` string|object · `schemaVersion` number|string · per-site `level` never cross-sorted · optional `license.code`) are TOLERANCES; a required field may never be removed / a type never change without a `$id` bump + both loaders updated. (3) CHILD-SAFE BAND AGE-GATE (`fcAgeGate`, the child-safe-linking-policy / A4 / CARU §3.e / ICO 6-9) — the REGISTRY age-tags ARE the gate (NO modal / parental gate — a link is not COPPA "collection"): **3-5/6-8 = HARD-DENY (no partner links) · 9-14 = only ≤14-appropriate tools (excludes 15+ deep viewers) · 15-18 = any**; deny-by-default for the youngest, never data-reliant alone. (4) HONEST SAME-TAB links (NN/g Back-button; no `target=_blank`) with an explicit "From our friends at <site> · opens <host> · in English" label (7-locale); NO hot-linking (copy partner icons at build, never load from the partner). (5) L2 ALLOWLIST-REDIRECT hand-off (`handoff.ts`) — a `?from&app&id&lang&band&return` landing island builds a "Back to <site>" affordance via `textContent` (never innerHTML) then `history.replaceState` cleans the URL; `return` must be https on an allowlisted partner host (OWASP/RFC-9700 — NEVER an open redirect); non-identifying keys only; fail-safe. (6) PII-FREE CONTINUITY PASSPORT (`passport.ts`) — the only account-free cross-device/cross-site continuity (Safari partitions 3rd-party storage): a versioned, schema-validated, fail-closed export/import of favourites + per-app counts, NO names/ages/email/identifiers; Web-Share ships today, a client QR encoder is a founder-gated dependency (R-DEPENDENCY-SUPPLY-CHAIN / ADR-258). (7) SEO FAMILY HYGIENE — Organization JSON-LD `sameAs` declares the sister site; NO cross-domain `rel=canonical`; cross-domain links stay on an EDITORIAL page (`/partners`), never a templated global cross-domain footer block (Google link-spam). (8) PARTNER-AGNOSTIC + on-device/no-tracking-SDK/COPPA/dark-safe/WCAG-2.2-AA — the loader/contract/handoff/passport are keyed by a `PARTNER_SITES` allowlist so a future sister site reuses the whole substrate; site PRs base=STAGING (ADR-154); TIER-1 shared-chrome moves → founder VISUAL gate. A runtime partner fetch on a kids surface, a divergent/unfrozen contract, a partner link for the youngest bands, an open-redirect hand-off, a PII-bearing passport, a cross-domain canonical, or a templated global cross-domain footer is a defect.** Founder-direct 2026-09-22 (single-owner scope decision — both sites founder-owned); decision `ADR-285`; program #199; guards = the site Vitest (`fcFeed`/`handoff`/`passport`/`contract`.test.ts) + `refresh-partner-feeds.mjs --check` + hub `check_fold_commons_integration_moves.py`. Composes ABOVE (never re-litigates) § R-SITE-FEEDBACK · § R-SITE-SEARCH · § R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE · § R-SITE-3HUB-IA · § R-WEB-CLONE-NO-DARK-SURFACE · § R-SITE-UNIFIED-DASHBOARDS · `portfolio.md` § R-ALPHAFOLD-ERA-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#101, the FC content bridge).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-285_FOLD_COMMONS_INTEGRATION_CONCEPTS_PROGRAM.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_FOLD_COMMONS_INTEGRATION_2026-09-21.md` + `Docs/queue/q-47c987.md`.
## FILE A FOLD COMMONS HANDOFF whenever a change AFFECTS the Fold Commons–facing contract or reciprocal surfaces — the "update BOTH sides" obligation made a standing handoff, because FC is a SEPARATE portfolio the hub never writes and FC's build vendors + reciprocates OUR feed (R-FOLD-COMMONS-CHANGE-HANDOFF; 2026-09-22)

**Any S&A change that AFFECTS Fold Commons — the emitted `/apps.json`/`/apps.jsonld`/`/apps.xml` shape or `_commons` fields (`apps-feed.ts`), the frozen `_commons` v1.1 CONTRACT (`contract.ts` `$id`; R-APPSFEED-PARTNER-CONSUMABLE), the reciprocal `/partners` cross-links / anchors (L0/L1), the cross-site-search partner records, the L2 hand-off key allowlist (`handoff.ts`), the passport schema (`passport.ts`), the SEO `sameAs`, or the canonical/`.org` host used in any cross-link — MUST be accompanied, IN THE SAME WAVE, by a handoff doc filed into the FC hub repo: `foldcommons-hub Docs/HANDOFF_FROM_HUB_<TOPIC>_<date>.md` (hub authors the spec; the FC session implements — the hub NEVER writes FC repos, FC is a separate portfolio governed by `portfolio.md` § R-ALPHAFOLD-ERA-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#101) + `DECISION_FOLDCOMMONS_MIGRATION`). WHY it is load-bearing: FC's build VENDORS S&A's `/apps.json` at build (its `/partners` shows S&A cards) + FC RECIPROCATES anchors, so a silent S&A-side contract/shape/host change BREAKS FC's reciprocal surface with ZERO signal to the FC session (the classic "diverge the shape without updating both sides" defect; the `contract.test.ts` bidirectional gate catches a shape break in OUR CI, but only a HANDOFF tells the FC session to re-vendor / react). The inverse holds symmetrically (FC files `HANDOFF_TO_SPARKANVIL_*` when its feed/reciprocal changes — the precedent this session: FC's ADR-021 feed-v1.1 + `/partners`). A change that touches the FC-facing contract/surfaces WITHOUT the paired FC handoff is a cross-repo DoD defect on the same footing as claiming SHIPPED without verifying the merge. GENERALIZES to any founder-owned SISTER site added to the `PARTNER_SITES` allowlist (§ R-SITE-PARTNER-INTEGRATION invariant 8): a change affecting that partner's consumption files a `HANDOFF_FROM_HUB_*` into that partner's hub repo. Composes ABOVE `portfolio.md` § Cross-Repo Handoff Protocol (the mechanism) + § R-SITE-PARTNER-INTEGRATION (the contract this protects) + `workflow.md` § "Verify Cross-Repo PRs Merged Before Claiming SHIPPED".** Founder-direct 2026-09-22 (*"codify: file handoff for fold commons whenever there are changes that affects fold commons"*).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-285_FOLD_COMMONS_INTEGRATION_CONCEPTS_PROGRAM.md` + `portfolio.md` § Cross-Repo Handoff Protocol.
## Older-band (13-18/15-18) board·puzzle·logic·cube apps MAY offer an OPTIONAL PSEUDONYMOUS account — a scoped carve-out from the no-account brand invariant (R-OLDER-BAND-ACCOUNT-APPS; 2026-08-28)

**An OLDER-BAND (13-18/15-18) board-game · puzzle · logic · Rubik's-cube (+ variants) app — and its `/play` clone — MAY offer an OPTIONAL, PSEUDONYMOUS, on-device-FIRST account that unlocks cross-device sync + a cloud game/solve archive + rated progression + calm leaderboards + a managed teacher/parent surface — a NARROWLY-SCOPED carve-out from the `R-SITE-FEEDBACK` / `R-OLDER-TEEN-BAND-DESIGN` no-account / no-PII brand invariant (ADR-211, program #96). The account is ALWAYS opt-in + on-device-first (every app works fully signed-out — the account only ADDS sync/archive/rating on top of the shipped `${ns}.progress.v1` store; it NEVER walls learning). Nine HARD guardrails bind — a violation is a defect on the ADR-045-hard-guardrail footing:** (1) **13-18 BAND ONLY** — 3-5/6-8/9-14 stay NO accounts (a younger-band account re-opens COPPA); (2) **OPTIONAL + on-device-FIRST** — no learning-critical feature behind the account (the Chess.com paid-retention-metering-of-learning model is REJECTED); (3) **PSEUDONYMOUS, no required PII** — no required name/age/email; generated handle (+passkey-capable, optional recovery); adult-provisionable (teacher/parent, Lichess-Class-shaped) OR teen self-serve at 13+ with consent; (4) **PRIVACY-BY-DEFAULT + SAFETY-BY-DEFAULT** — high-privacy defaults, no open chat/free-text, no dark-pattern consent (AADC/Kids-Code), pre-set-emote social only (inherits `R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER`); (5) **DATA-MINIMIZED + DELETABLE** — store only sync/archive/rating deltas keyed to the pseudonymous handle, no raw per-user event log / behavioral profile, retention/deletion policy, one-tap delete; (6) **NO ads · NO sale · NO profiling · NO paid-learning-metering** (SOPIPA/AADC/`R-SITE-FEEDBACK` §5); (7) **CALM** rating/leaderboards — intrapersonal PB + optional pseudonymous cohort percentile, NEVER scarcity/streak-guilt/leaderboard-to-chase, never gate content; (8) **ACCOUNT-OPTIONAL SHARED BACKEND, founder-provisioned** (Cloudflare, `R-CLOUDFLARE-BACKEND-PROVISIONING` + `R-CLOUDFLARE-SCOPED-DEPLOY`; hub owns code, founder owns account+go/no-go); (9) **COUNSEL-APPROVED (2026-08-28) + disclosed** in the privacy policy (once-cleared like R-SITE-FEEDBACK/R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER; a clone surfacing the account inherits the gate as long as guardrails 1-8 hold; a violation forfeits it). **The carve-out EXTENDS the on-device-first trust posture (a calm/pseudonymous/privacy-first account is itself a differentiator vs local-only csTimer + account-heavy Chess.com) — it does NOT break it; reversibility is HIGH (opt-in + on-device source-of-truth → removing the account reverts to signed-out with zero data loss).** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-28 (*"create a new program to track … board games, puzzles, logic apps for the older age band (13-18 or 15-18) that can create accounts and such … and rubik's cube and variants too"*). Decision `ADR-211`; research `RESEARCH_OLDER_BAND_ACCOUNT_APPS_2026-08-28.md`; plan `PLAN_OLDER_BAND_ACCOUNT_APPS_PROGRAM_2026-08-28.md`; tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_OLDER_BAND_ACCOUNT_APPS.txt` + `scripts/check_older_band_account_apps.py` (HARD-FAIL on a younger-band row or any guardrail-violation flag; SHAPE-B WARN backlog of pending adoptions). Amends `R-OLDER-TEEN-BAND-DESIGN` (distributed-narrative.md). **When it applies:** any 13-18/15-18 board/puzzle/logic/cube app or `/play` clone adding sync/archive/rating/leaderboard/managed-account; site PRs base=STAGING (never main). **Cross-refs:** § R-SITE-FEEDBACK · § R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER · § R-CLOUDFLARE-BACKEND-PROVISIONING · `distributed-narrative.md` § R-OLDER-TEEN-BAND-DESIGN · ADR-211 · programs #61/#91/#92/#95/#10/#8.

**AMENDMENT (2026-08-29, founder-direct — 3 questions; detail → ADR-211 § Amendment + `RESEARCH_OLDER_BAND_ACCOUNT_SCOPE_BAND_SPAWN_2026-08-29.md`):** (a) **NOT all apps** — accounts stay ARCHETYPE-SCOPED; a non-game 15-18 app may adopt **SYNC-ONLY** case-by-case+documented, NEVER blanket. (b) **13-18 ceiling + a 13-15/16-18 TWO-TIER** — younger-teen **13-15** = highest-privacy defaults · rated/leaderboard OFF/gated · adult-provisioning preferred; **16-18** = self-serve + calm comparative; build focus stays 15-18; a 13-18 app is founder+counsel-gated per instance + carries the 13-15 tier note (guard WARNs). (c) **15-18 spawns selective+founder-gated** (R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT) — only distinct-older-curriculum+account-value (W3/W4 older-cube/board-archive/puzzle-ladder); never a blanket tween-twin (else a MODE/existing app; new concepts feed #35). (d) **fan-out** = the eligible-clone adoption drain, not all apps.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading) + `Docs/ADR-211_OLDER_BAND_ACCOUNTS_CARVE_OUT.md` § Amendment.
## Room-code networked multiplayer for web clones — Durable Objects + WebSocket, NO chat, NO accounts (R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER; 2026-07-15)

> **🔴 CRITICAL — the counsel-review launch gate is CLEARED (COUNSEL APPROVED 2026-07-16, founder-direct; ADR-041). Networked room-code multiplayer is UNBLOCKED for shipping to kids.** The V261 transport (`spark-anvil-room` Worker + `Room` Durable Object) is DEPLOYED LIVE on `spark-and-anvil.{com,org}/api/room/*`, AND the "counsel-reviewed before launch" condition that every principle below refers to has been **satisfied once, portfolio-wide** — a clone surfacing the room mode does NOT need a fresh counsel review; it inherits the cleared ADR-041 gate. So Shell B (networked room mode, `_shared/roomMode.ts`) is now a **first-class, shippable** engagement mode alongside pass-and-play + adventure (R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES): wire it onto the ✅-MP archetypes (turn-based/board/deduction/MC-quiz-duel), each with the two-client Playwright smoke below. The safety-BY-DESIGN invariants (NO free-text chat / NO voice / pre-set emotes only / ephemeral generated names / code-gated ephemeral rooms / no accounts / no PII / origin-locked + rate-limited) remain **hard, non-waivable gates** — counsel approval was granted ON THE BASIS of those invariants, so shipping a room mode that violates any of them (e.g. adds free-text chat) FORFEITS the cleared gate and is a critical defect. What is cleared is the *launch gate*; the *design invariants* are permanent. Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-17 ("counsel has approved and it has been codified in the repo. make this critical").

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-CLONE-BIDIRECTIONAL-BACKPORT).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## "Play together" survives WITHOUT the internet via a local-first / online-optional transport ladder — a POB-scoped OFFLINE FALLBACK (LAN relay for web, native no-router nearby for iOS/ForgeVault); never on the online site's default UX, never a NEW client transport, never Web Bluetooth (R-OFFLINE-NEARBY-MULTIPLAYER; 2026-08-19)

**Networked "Play together" MUST keep working when kids have NO/limited internet (the POB case) via a local-first / online-optional transport ladder — T0 pass-and-play · T1 cloud room-code (ADR-041, the online default) · **T2 LAN relay** (hub-owned "SparkRoom-lite" WebSocket in the bundled/ForgeVault server, SAME `/api/room/*`, reached via the shipped `__SA_ROOM_ORIGIN__` `http://`→`ws://` seam → ZERO new client code) · **T3 native no-router nearby** (strategic substrate = founder's `peer-kit` libp2p SDK cross-platform; interim Apple-only = a `NetworkFrameworkTransport` backend behind forgekit `ForgeMultipeerKit`). Four hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) T2/T3 are OFFLINE-FALLBACK tiers for the no-/limited-internet case ONLY — NOT on the online site's default UX (founder scope 2026-08-19); (2) NEVER a new CLIENT transport — T2 is a new SERVER, T3 a new BACKEND, both behind existing seams (§ R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES "never invent a second transport"); (3) Web Bluetooth is a NON-STARTER (Chromium-only, no Safari/iOS, secure-context, GATT-client-only) → true nearby is native-only, reuse-first (`peer-kit`/`ForgeMultipeerKit`), never hand-roll; (4) every ADR-041 safety invariant carries over BY CONSTRUCTION, and offline local-network P2P (collects nothing, nothing leaves the LAN) is the LOWEST privacy surface (outside COPPA's "collected online" core). Hub owns T2 + authors the T3 spec-handoffs; app/SDK sessions write the source (hub NEVER writes Swift/Rust).** Founder-direct 2026-08-19. Composes ABOVE § R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER + § R-SITE-OFFLINE-PORTABLE-BUNDLE + `.claude/rules/multipeer.md`; sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-160_OFFLINE_NEARBY_MULTIPLAYER.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_OFFLINE_NEARBY_MULTIPLAYER_2026-08-19.md`.
## Pass-and-play + multiplayer + adventure engagement modes for appropriate `/play` clones — shared shells, eligibility-by-archetype, adventure BOUNDARY-ONLY (R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES; 2026-07-15)

**Appropriate `/play/<app>` clones carry three iOS-parity engagement modes — (1) pass-and-play same-device hotseat, (2) networked room-code multiplayer, (3) adventure/progression framing — built as SHARED `_shared/` shells and scoped by a per-clone ELIGIBILITY-BY-ARCHETYPE matrix (NOT all clones); pass-and-play is serverless + trivially COPPA-compliant, multiplayer RIDES the V261 transport (never a new one), and adventure mode is BOUNDARY-ONLY + guard-the-ratio (never a decorated critical-path mini-game). A clone that forces multiplayer onto a solo-reflection/solo-tool surface, invents a second multiplayer transport instead of riding V261, or puts adventure narrative/decoration on the active problem-solving loop is a defect — as is an eligible clone that omits a mode without a documented waiver.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-15 (*"i want to add pass-and-play, multi-player play and adventure modes to all web clones that are appropriate for them. do deep web research, plan, create adr and codify."*). Decision: `ADR-042`; research: `RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_SOCIAL_ADVENTURE_MODES_2026-07-15.md`; plan: `PLAN_WEB_CLONE_SOCIAL_ADVENTURE_MODES_2026-07-15.md`. This is the **umbrella** over the three modes; the networked-multiplayer transport lives in § R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER (ADR-041), which this consumes.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Networked BOARD-GAME room play rides the V261 transport via a client `_shared/roomBoard.ts` move-relay — deterministic lockstep, server-authoritative TURN order + client-side LEGALITY, NO server change; the quiz `roomMode.ts` is NOT a board game (R-WEB-CLONE-ROOM-BOARD-RELAY; 2026-08-03)

**A `/play/<app>` clone's networked room-code play of an actual BOARD GAME rides the LIVE V261 SparkRoom transport (ADR-041) as a CLIENT-SIDE move-relay — a shared `_shared/roomBoard.ts` that relays each move as an OPAQUE `{uci,fen}` payload via `SparkRoom.move()`, with NO Durable-Object/server change. The DO enforces TURN ORDER (server-authoritative round-robin + off-turn rejection); the deterministic per-clone engine enforces move LEGALITY on BOTH clients (deterministic lockstep); the relayed FEN is a cheap desync guard. The quiz `roomMode.ts`/`roomVersus.ts` is an MC-QUIZ duel, NOT a board game. Safety-by-design (ADR-041) is HARD + non-waivable: opaque payload + pre-set emotes only, NO free-text chat/voice, ephemeral names, code-gated rooms, no accounts/PII, origin-locked + rate-limited. Build on NEW files + a NEW `/duel` route; DoD = a two-client Playwright smoke + dual-scheme screenshot-DoD + a PARITY row + an iOS `_WEB_BACKPORT` note.**

→ **Sub-rules (defined here; full body → reference):**
- **R-ROOM-BOARD-DICE-RELAY** (2026-08-11) — a board game with RANDOMNESS encodes the RANDOM outcome IN the relayed token (the mover rolls locally, the receiver NEVER re-rolls — it re-derives the legal set FOR the relayed draw + accepts iff plays match); one token = one full turn; randomness is trusted-because-relayed.
- **R-ROOM-RELAY-PAYLOAD-KEY** (2026-08-11) — an `on('move',…)` handler MUST read the payload at `m.move`, NOT `m.payload` (`SparkRoom.move` wires it as `{type:'move', move: payload}`); reading `m.payload` gets `undefined` → the peer's move never applies → control stays disabled forever, INVISIBLE to every local gate (surfaces ONLY in the post-deploy two-client smoke). Mirror `roomBoard.ts`'s `m.move` read; the DoD isn't met until that smoke PASSES.
