- **R-ROOM-RELAY-OFFLINE-VERSION-HANDSHAKE** (2026-08-17) — when a peer is an OFFLINE-BUNDLE (POB) snapshot client, its engine is frozen at package time while a live peer runs the current engine → deterministic-lockstep desync risk. Relay a `protocolVersion` (monotonic int, transport contract) + per-clone `engineVersion`/`bundleSnapshot` in the lobby payload (DO stays engine-agnostic; check is CLIENT-SIDE); on a desync-capable board-game mismatch FAIL-CLOSED to pass-and-play with a preset no-free-text notice + a `latest.json` re-download nudge (SOFT note for the MC-quiz duel); the relayed-FEN desync guard is the backstop. A gentle version-lock (our rooms are ephemeral/unranked → "a kid room can only desync itself"), NOT Minecraft's hard reject. → `DECISION_POB_LAUNCH_DESIGN_2026-08-17`.
- **R-ROOM-APPLY-FROM-ECHO** (2026-10-04) — a room client applies a move only on its echo, locked till the next `turn`; reuse `roomBoard.ts`/Whetstone `echoGate.ts`. → REFERENCE.
- **R-ROOM-BOARD-ONE-TOKEN-PER-TURN** (2026-10-09) — one token = whole turn; unlock is idempotent. → REFERENCE.
- **R-ROOM-SEAT-INTEGRITY** (2026-10-10) — host seated before code shows; rejoin keeps seat. → REFERENCE.
- **R-ROOM-RELAY-LIVE-VERIFY** (2026-08-26) — a networked room mode's `ROOM_E2E_BASE`-gated two-client smoke SKIPS in CI (room API is prod-hosted), so **skip-gated + green CI ≠ verified**: it is UNVERIFIED (its own driving logic unexercised — the JS-driven-surface/search-0-results lesson) until it RUNS GREEN once against a REAL room DO. DoD isn't met until it does. GENERALIZES to any env-gated CI-skipped test (feedback-D1/tutor Worker). Recipe (T1 local same-origin real-DO · the CORS reason localhost→live-prod won't work · T2 literal-prod rides the founder-gated promote) → `Docs/RUNBOOK_NETWORKED_ROOM_MODE_LIVE_VERIFY.md`; tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_NETWORKED_MODE_LIVE_VERIFY.txt` + `scripts/check_networked_mode_live_verify.py` (SHAPE-B WARN, auto-discovers from origin/staging). Reference: deducequest relay-online (site #2829 · ADR-187 § Addendum). Once GREEN, the weekly `networked-room-prod-smoke.yml` re-verifies every networked mode on prod (→ REFERENCE).
- **R-ROOM-BOARD-PARTY-PICK-TURN-OFFSET** (2026-08-30) — the PARTY-HUB game-PICK (and between-game RETURN) rides the SAME opaque `room.move` channel (`partyLobby.ts`, no new wire type), and the DO advances `turnSeat` on EVERY accepted move (server-authoritative turn order) — so by the time a board game mounts, `turnSeat` has advanced past 0 while the clone's engine ALWAYS starts white-to-move at engine seat 0 → white (DO seat 0) reads `isMyTurn=(0===advanced)=false` and is stuck "waiting for the other player". The shared `driveBoard` (`_shared/roomBoard.ts`) MUST reconcile the offset CLIENT-SIDE (no DO change, ADR-135 bound) via pure Vitest-pinned `engineSeatFor(doSeat, firstMoverSeat, n)` + `nextRosterSeat`: the game's FIRST MOVER (white) = the seat the DO turn advanced TO = `nextRosterSeat(room.snapshot.turnSeat)` for the PARTY path (`leaveOnEnd:false`; turnSeat at mount = the pre-advance PICKER seat, since the DO's `{type:'move'}` carries NO turnSeat and the advancing `{type:'turn'}` frame lands AFTER the move-echo that mounts the board), or `room.snapshot.turnSeat` (=0) for a `/duel` (`leaveOnEnd:true`, fresh lobby) → **byte-identical**. The DO `turnSeat` and engine turn then advance in LOCKSTEP, so `room.isMyTurn` stays the correct interactivity gate UNCHANGED. Consequence: the party PICKER plays Black, the non-picker (on-turn) plays White (the only seat the DO will accept a first move from — a symmetric assignment needs a DO/protocol change, founder-gated). Live confirmation = the two-client party-pick smoke against the real DO (§ R-ROOM-RELAY-LIVE-VERIFY). Generalized to every board game by construction; guard `check_room_board_party_offset.py` tripwires the shared fix. → ADR-215.
  - **R-ROOM-BOARD-PARTY-FIRSTMOVER-DETERMINISTIC** (2026-09-08, AMENDS the above) — the `firstMoverSeat` capture MUST be TIMING-INDEPENDENT: recorded DETERMINISTICALLY at the SYNCHRONOUS party-pick echo (`partyLobby.enter()`, where `snapshot.turnSeat` is guaranteed pre-advance = the picker) into a `roomBoard` `WeakMap` (`recordPartyFirstMover`/`partyFirstMoverOf`), NEVER re-derived via `nextRosterSeat(snapshot.turnSeat)` at the ASYNC board mount. The homepage party hub mounts the board inside a dynamic `import().then(…)`, so on an uncached first pick the DO's `turn` frame lands BEFORE `driveBoard` reads the snapshot → the old re-derivation over-advances by one → the on-turn first mover is coloured Black while only White moves (the founder-reported REVERSAL; intermittent — a cached pick was accidentally correct, the class the ADR-215 synchronous test missed). `driveBoard`'s party branch prefers the recorded value (snapshot derivation = defensive fallback); `/duel` never records → byte-identical. Shared-shell only (`_shared/roomBoard.ts` + `_shared/partyLobby.ts`) → fixes all 9 party board games. Guard = `roomBoard.test.ts` production-timing block + regression oracle; verdict = the two-client party-pick live smoke (§ R-ROOM-RELAY-LIVE-VERIFY). Sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-FIRST-MOVER (same "gate was right, seat/colour derivation was the bug" family). → ADR-215 § Amendment.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A game clone REPORTS a finished match to a same-device TourneyForge event via the shared sa:events:v1 emitter — one shared mechanism (`_shared/eventsEmitter` + opt-in `_shared/eventsReport` card), COPPA-safe (nicknames confirmed at report-time, never persisted), feature-detected/no-op-off-bridge; the per-clone POST-wiring is a tracked worst-first drain (R-EVENTS-POST-WIRING; 2026-09-03)

**The EMITTER half of the Program #100 aggregation contract (ADR-216; RECEIVER shipped in TourneyForge `aggregate.ts`). An event-eligible board clone (a same-device 2-player result surface — `hotseat`/`duel`) MUST let the player REPORT a finished match to a TourneyForge event in another same-device tab via the SINGLE shared mechanism — `_shared/eventsEmitter.reportEventResult` on the `sa:events:v1` BroadcastChannel + the OPT-IN `_shared/eventsReport.mountEventsReport` card — NEVER hand-rolled. Five hard invariants, each a dark-route defect: (1) COPPA — only the event's cast NICKNAMES, CONFIRMED at report-time (generated seat labels won't match a host-typed nickname, so a silent auto-emit rarely records; the confirm keeps it PII-free — nothing persisted/no accounts/no identifiers); (2) feature-detected SAFE NO-OP (never throws from game-over; hidden when the bridge is absent); (3) BOUNDARY-ONLY opt-in calm-rails (R-SITE-FEEDBACK/anti-Gimkit); (4) NO new transport (the receiver's same-origin channel); (5) message shape PINNED to the receiver's `isResultMessage` (a Vitest round-trip emit→`applyIncomingResult` = the un-gameable oracle). A hand-rolled channel, a leaked identifier, a throw from game-over, or an always-on (non-opt-in) surface is a defect. Per-clone adoption is a STANDING worst-first drain (a clone with neither a `wired` row nor a `# POST-EXCLUDED:` line is silently-forgotten); reference-first gridforge. Tracked `scripts/check_events_post_wiring_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_EVENTS_POST_WIRING.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, reads SITE origin/staging; `--ci-mode` fails only on a WIRED→unwired regression). COMPOSES § R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES · § R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER-UX · portfolio.md § R-EVENTS-MINDSPORTS-OCCASIONS-PROGRAM.** Founder-direct 2026-09-03; program #100; queue `q-193f95`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-216_EVENTS_MINDSPORTS_OCCASIONS_PROGRAM.md` + `Docs/queue/q-193f95.md`.
## Pass-and-play + room-code play is elevated to a Gimkit-grade FRICTIONLESS join/host/lobby UX — easy·intuitive·prominent·engaging — WITHOUT the engagement-farm economy (R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER-UX; 2026-08-12)

**Every MP-eligible `/play/<app>` clone's multiplayer UX MUST be easy·intuitive·prominent·engaging: a PROMINENT "Play together" entry (landing + CloneNav); a minimal HOST (one tap → big CODE + QR + copy-link + a live LOBBY of arriving ephemeral names + START); a minimal JOIN (code OR QR → nickname/emote → lobby; NO account, cross-device, ≥44px, dark-safe); a frictionless same-device PASS-AND-PLAY handoff (turn banner + 4-stage curtain); reconnect-with-same-code; a 2-player board DUEL auto-starts BOTH on fill (ADR-158 `autoStartHost`; a host stuck on Start while the guest sits on a board = DEFECT; an N-player party keeps its host gate); the party-lobby game-END RETURN is RELAYED — BOTH clients leave to the picker together (a LOCAL-only `returnToPicker` that strands the peer in the finished game = the q-7a3799 cross-navigation desync DEFECT, ADR-158); engaging-but-CLEAN (lobby + shared countdown + calm end-card). It RIDES the V261 transport (never a new one) and never forces MP onto solo surfaces (`R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES` eligibility stands). The ADR-041 safety invariants (no free-text chat/voice · preset emotes · ephemeral names · code-gated · no accounts/PII · origin-locked+rate-limited) are HARD; a currency/streak/scarcity engagement-FARM (Gimkit/ChessKid) is a DEFECT, not a feature (anti-Gimkit; `R-SITE-FEEDBACK`). Deliverable: a SHARED `_shared/roomLobby` + `<RoomLobby>`/`<JoinHost>` shell, reference-first then fanned out; tracked `check_multiplayer_ux_coverage.py` + `REGISTRY_MULTIPLAYER_UX_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; verdict = screenshot-DoD + the two-client smoke).** Founder-direct 2026-08-12; decision `ADR-117`; research `RESEARCH_FRICTIONLESS_MULTIPLAYER_JOIN_UX_2026-08-12`.

**AMENDMENT — the "PROMINENT Play together entry" is SITE-LEVEL (founder-direct 2026-09-10, gimkit.com; ADR-258; ✅ SHIPPED site #3666→staging).** GimKit puts "Join Game" in the top nav + a dead-simple dedicated join surface; every classroom-game leader ships a memorably-URL'd dedicated join surface (kahoot.it/gimkit.com/join/play.blooket.com/joinmyquiz.com). The "prominent entry" invariant above (per-clone landing + CloneNav) is SHARPENED to the SITE level, each a dark-route defect: (1) an always-available **"🎮 Play together"/Join nav affordance** in the nav UTILITY area — NOT a 4th content hub (R-SITE-3HUB-IA lean-splash stands; GimKit keeps Join in the utility area); (2) a dead-simple dedicated **`/join`** surface (autofocus one-input code + Join + inline retype, `?room=` auto-join reusing the #134 `autoJoinCode` seam) → drops into the V261 lobby (ADR-041 safety). HARD: guest / NO account / NO login-or-PII wall (the COPPA MOAT vs GimKit's login funnel — adopt the *join* prominence, reject the *account* funnel; honest-yield); anti-Gimkit; no new transport; TIER-1 (shared nav chrome + a new surface → founder VISUAL gate + two-client LIVE smoke; site PR base=STAGING). ✅ SHIPPED site #3666 (build record → ADR-258 § Build; the `check_multiplayer_ux_coverage.py` guard now carries the site-level Join/`/join` check, `--ci-mode` fails on a post-ship regression). **Host-screen QR is SPLIT-AND-DEFERRED** (R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT): a QR encoder is a R-DEPENDENCY-SUPPLY-CHAIN + R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE founder gate; copy-link/Web-Share ship the phone-hop today.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_FRICTIONLESS_MULTIPLAYER_PROGRAM_2026-08-12.md` + `Docs/ADR-135_LOBBY_FIRST_PARTY_MULTIPLAYER.md` + `Docs/PLAN_LOBBY_FIRST_PARTY_MULTIPLAYER_2026-08-14.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_INVERTED_LOBBY_FIRST_MULTIPLAYER_2026-08-14.md` + `Docs/ADR-258_ALWAYS_AVAILABLE_JOIN_NAV_AND_DEDICATED_SURFACE.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_FRICTIONLESS_JOIN_NAV_AND_DEDICATED_SURFACE_2026-09-10.md`. OVERVIEW #36.
## The Play-Together session is PERSISTENT + RESUMABLE across all three social modes — a player can leave from anywhere and resume; delivered at the SHARED SHELLS (generalizes fleet-wide); on-device/no-PII/calm; resume in `pageshow`+`visibilitychange` NOT `onload` (iOS bfcache); a DO grace-period+snapshot is the FOUNDER-GATED both-leave/mid-board tier (R-PLAY-TOGETHER-SESSION-RESUME; 2026-09-09)

**Every Play-Together session — the homepage PARTY lobby, the regular room-code 2-player DUEL (`/duel` + `HomeDuel`), AND same-device PASS-AND-PLAY — MUST be a PERSISTENT, RESUMABLE session (leave from anywhere → resume), because on iOS Safari a full-page navigation (a stray tap on the long homepage's many links, the back-swipe, an OS tab-reclaim) destroys the live `SparkRoom`/WebSocket and `beforeunload` is IGNORED → the nav CANNOT be blocked, so the fix is session RESILIENCE, never a leave-confirm prompt. Delivered at the SHARED SHELLS so it GENERALIZES fleet-wide by construction (one wiring → every clone's `/duel` + pass-and-play inherits resume — the ADR-191 shell-guard model), in THREE tiers split by deploy gate: TIER-0 a pure `_shared/sessionResume.ts` descriptor (save/load/clear/isFresh; `sessionStorage` primary + short-TTL `localStorage` fallback; no PII — a room code + ephemeral server-nickname + board snapshot are game state, not identity; Vitest-pinned); TIER-1 wire it in — PASS-AND-PLAY persists its LOCAL state → FULL resume incl. exact position (all on-device), and the ROOM-CODE DUEL + PARTY `saveSession` on host/join + phase transitions + a calm ONE-TAP dismissible "🎉 Resume your room (CODE)" pill + rejoin-by-code (room-level resume while a peer holds the room); TIER-2 (FOUNDER-GATED CF DEPLOY, R-CLOUDFLARE-SCOPED-DEPLOY) the `workers/room` DO gains a GRACE-PERIOD alarm (3–5 min, not immediate `deleteAll()` → both can leave) + stores the latest relayed `move` (`{uci,fen}` = full position) + `app/route` + replays a `resume` frame on (re)join + stable-token seat reclaim → both-leave + EXACT-mid-board resume. Hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: resume logic lives in `pageshow`(`event.persisted`)+`visibilitychange` NEVER only `onload` (iOS bfcache-back does not re-fire load; on bfcache entry the WS auto-closes → reconnect on persisted show); ON-DEVICE / no-PII / COPPA (composes R-SITE-FEEDBACK); CALM one-tap dismissible resume, NEVER an auto-redirect / streak / FOMO hook (anti-Gimkit); NO new transport — TIER-2 EXTENDS the V261 DO behind the existing seam (R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER / R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES "never invent a second transport"); a SNAPSHOT (`fen`) not a command-replay for instant resume; dark-safe + WCAG 2.2 AA + ≥44px + the dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD verdict; TIER-1 blast (shared shells + homepage LCP + a DO deploy) → adversarial review + founder VISUAL gate (R-REVIEW-RISK-TIER / R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA) + a two-client live smoke (R-ROOM-RELAY-LIVE-VERIFY: nav-away → return → back in the room); site PRs base = STAGING (ADR-154). Recommendation: ship TIER-0+1 (client-only, no deploy — ~90% of the felt value) then founder-gate TIER-2 (the DO deploy). A social-mode shell with neither a `wired` row nor a `# RESUME-EXCLUDED:` line is silently-forgotten. Tracked `scripts/check_session_resume_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_SESSION_RESUME_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, reads origin/staging; `--ci-mode` fails ONLY on a regression — a `wired` shell gone unwired). COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) ADR-041 (room transport + safety) · ADR-135 (lobby-first party) · ADR-158 (duel host auto-start) · ADR-215 (party first-mover/turn-offset — untouched) · R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER-UX · R-OFFLINE-NEARBY-MULTIPLAYER.** Founder-direct 2026-09-09 (*"a persistent session … return from anywhere and resume their games"* + *"make the regular room code 2-player and pass-and-play session persistent and resumable too"* + *"codify, track, guard, generalize everything"*); decision `ADR-255`; program #134.

**AMENDMENT — the homepage TRIGGERS SHIPPED (site #3655, 2026-09-09) + a load-bearing RESUME-FIRST-MOVER invariant.** The founder-approved triggers shipped on the already-live machinery: (a) a HomeDuel networked-duel **Resume pill** (fleet-inheritable `onRoom`/`autoJoinCode`/`onEnd` seams on `runRoomBoard`→`runRoomLobby`→`runChessDuel`); (b) the party controller **re-enters the in-progress GAME on reconnect** (not just the picker) via pure `partyLobby.resolvePartyResume` reading the DO move-history (`SparkRoom.peekResume()`); (c) the grace-window **"room held — waiting for your friend" affordance** (`driveBoard` on `left`, cleared on roster-refill — the calm Gimkit-Pause analog). 🔴 **NEW HARD INVARIANT — the engine FIRST MOVER on a RESUME is DERIVED from the move-history PARITY (`deriveResumeFirstMover`: `(boardMovesInCurrentGame % 2 === 0) ? turnSeat : nextRosterSeat(turnSeat)`), NEVER the entry-time `snapshot.turnSeat`** — which on a mid-game reconnect is the CURRENT turn, so a snapshot-derived colour FLIPS when rejoining on the PEER's turn (the ADR-215 colour-reversal class, on the resume path; a defect). The resume REPLAY is BOUNDARY-ROBUST (a `party-pick`/`party-return` marker = a GAME-BOUNDARY reset → a party history rebuilds the CURRENT game; a `/duel` history has no markers → byte-identical). Pure + Vitest-pinned (`roomResume.test.ts`). COMPOSES ADR-215 (its PICK-path derivation is UNCHANGED; the RESUME-path first-mover is a NEW case). Full record + the 3 known follow-ons (both-left army swap · party off-turn held · single `RESUME_KEY`) + verification → the ADR § Amendment.

Whetstone rooms too: `check_whetstone_room_resume.py`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-255_PERSISTENT_RESUMABLE_PLAY_TOGETHER_SESSION.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_PERSISTENT_RESUMABLE_PLAY_TOGETHER_SESSION_2026-09-09.md` + `Docs/PLAN_PERSISTENT_RESUMABLE_SESSION_PROGRAM_2026-09-09.md`. OVERVIEW #134.
## Legacy/campaign progression is a cross-cutting learning MODE (shared `_shared/legacyCampaign.ts` shell), NOT a new app — non-destructive · NO fail-state · NO engagement-farm economy · on-device · boundary-only (R-WEB-CLONE-LEGACY-CAMPAIGN-MODE; 2026-08-13)

**A persistent, evolving-world CAMPAIGN across sessions (the *Pandemic Legacy*/*Gloomhaven* mechanic) is delivered as a CROSS-CUTTING MODE — a shared `src/lib/play/_shared/legacyCampaign.ts` shell (pure `LegacyCampaignController` + `CampaignConfig`/`auditCampaign`) an eligible clone opts into with a per-app config, like `roomLobby`/`caseNarrative`/`adventure` — NEVER a new standalone app (`R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT` ceiling stands). It COMPOSES with (never rebuilds) mastery-progression (axis 8) · practice-scheduling (axis 7) · `adventure` (ADR-042) · the DN arc. Hard invariants, each a DEFECT on the footing of a dark route, ENFORCED BY CONSTRUCTION in `auditCampaign` (throws at build): (1) NON-DESTRUCTIVE — the world only ever GROWS; nothing deleted/broken; NO permadeath; (2) NO fail-state / NO punitive loss of earned progress (low-stakes, recursive play, anti-shame); (3) NO currency/stars/gems/streak/lives/scarcity economy (anti-Gimkit, `R-SITE-FEEDBACK` — forbidden-key scan rejects it); (4) ON-DEVICE only — `localStorage`, globally-UNIQUE namespace (`R-WEB-CLONE-PROGRESS-NS-UNIQUE`), no identifier/account/PII/network; (5) BOUNDARY-ONLY (hub/results/reader, never the active solve loop — `R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING` + `R-GUARD-THE-RATIO`); (6) archetype-scoped eligibility (rich multi-surface + real arc only; never a solo-tool/solo-reflection surface); (7) HONEST-YIELD — a research-informed engagement/continuity structure, NEVER a proven learning-gain claim (the legacy-specific outcome evidence is thin). Phase-1 = the engine + Vitest as a 0-consumer shell (the roomLobby precedent, collision-safe, no visual gate); Phase-2 = `<LegacyCampaign>` render + per-clone pilot + screenshot-DoD, worst-first (behind the MAIN-SERIAL §10 gate).** Founder-GO 2026-08-13 (`q-d93b84`, candidate #4); decision `ADR-119`; research `RESEARCH_LEGACY_CAMPAIGN_MODE_2026-08-13.md`. Sibling cross-cutting-MODE program to `R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES` (adventure) + `R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER-UX` (roomLobby).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_LEGACY_CAMPAIGN_MODE_2026-08-13.md` + `Docs/ADR-119_LEGACY_CAMPAIGN_MODE.md`. OVERVIEW #35.
## Web-clone parity is a two-axis Definition-of-Done gate (R-WEB-CLONE-PARITY-DOD; 2026-07-10)

**A `/play/<app>` web clone is NOT "done" — and must NOT be marked shipped — until it satisfies BOTH parity axes against its iOS app, each recorded in the clone's `Docs/web/<app>/PARITY_WEB_VS_IOS.md` ledger with ZERO unexplained 🟡:**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A clone is not shipped until its HUB-SIDE artifacts land + are verified — not just the site PR (R-WEB-CLONE-HUB-SIDE-DOD; 2026-07-15)

**A `/play/<app>` clone is NOT "shipped" — and MUST NOT be claimed shipped — until BOTH halves land: (1) the SITE PR (routes + lib + kits + per-app CSS + `clone.meta.ts`, merged + live-verified) AND (2) the HUB-SIDE artifacts. A clone whose site code is live but whose hub-side artifacts are absent is *site-shipped-but-hub-dark* — a Definition-of-Done violation on the same footing as a missing parity axis.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-15 (*"codify the hub-side DoD rule"*). A clone can merge site-only, leaving the hub no signal it exists. Guard `check_web_clone_hub_side_dod.py` (a TODO stub counts as missing); `gen_parity_ledger.py` writes it.

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-CLONE-BIDIRECTIONAL-BACKPORT).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## A spawned app's `/play` clone is a TRACKED follow-on — a `planned` registry row at clone-readiness, so it can't go silently un-built (R-WEB-CLONE-SPAWN-TRACKED; 2026-07-16)

**Every spawned portfolio app's `/play/<app>` web clone is a tracked follow-on deliverable: the instant the app reaches CLONE-READINESS it MUST get a `planned` row in `Docs/REGISTRY_WEB_CLONES.txt` (which enters it into the clone-candidate pipeline / `AUDIT_WEB_CLONE_NEXT_RANKING`), so it can never sit clone-ready-but-invisible. A clone-ready spawned app with NO registry row (planned / building / shipped) is a tracking defect.** Founder-direct 2026-07-16.

**Reverse direction (P#210 s5):** a web-pioneered clone has no app repo, so it needs its own row; `check_web_clone_coverage.py --ci-mode` fails on any staging clone without one.

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-SPAWN-KIT-ARC-SCAFFOLD).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## A `/play` webclone MAY be built BEFORE (or without) its iOS app — NOT blocked by app-session Swift; web-clone-readiness = a PORTABLE DETERMINISTIC CORE + content + the iOS FEATURE PLAN, never "the iOS app is built" (R-WEB-CLONE-MAY-PRECEDE-IOS; 2026-08-21)

**A `/play/<app>` webclone MAY be implemented BEFORE — or without — its iOS app; it is NEVER blocked by the app's CC session writing Swift. WEB-CLONE-READINESS = (a) a PORTABLE DETERMINISTIC CORE runnable client-side (a SHIPPED engine to reuse — `_shared/`/`chess.ts` — or a buildable TS engine), (b) enough CONTENT (DN cast/chapters/kits-or-younger-activities), and (c) the iOS `Docs/FEATURE_PLAN.md`+`KIT_ARC.md` as the CLONE SPEC — NOT "the iOS Swift app is built." A web-pioneered clone is FIRST-CLASS (`R-WEB-CLONE-PARITY` becomes forward-looking; iOS backports the same plan — `R-CLONE-BIDIRECTIONAL-BACKPORT`). CONFLATING iOS-readiness with web-buildability — deferring a buildable clone "because there's no Swift engine yet" — is a defect that silently forgets a shippable clone (a wrong-rationale `# DEFERRED:` line). Tracked by the SAME `check_web_clone_coverage.py` (a buildable-but-unbuilt clone is `planned`/`building`, never a Swift-blocked `# DEFERRED:`).** Founder-direct 2026-08-21. Reference impl: **pawnpals**. → **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Every active portfolio app has a web-clone row OR a documented exclusion — a standing coverage guard so no clone is silently forgotten (R-WEB-CLONE-COVERAGE-COMPLETENESS; 2026-07-20)

**Every ACTIVE portfolio app (`Docs/REGISTRY_ACTIVE_PORTFOLIO_APPS.txt`) MUST have EITHER a `planned`/`building`/`shipped` row in `Docs/REGISTRY_WEB_CLONES.txt` (R-WEB-CLONE-SPAWN-TRACKED — it's in the clone pipeline) OR a machine-parseable documented coverage entry (`# EXCLUDED: <app> — <reason>` = never a standard clone, web analog documented; `# DEFERRED: <app> — <reason>` = clone-ready-when-content-lands → flips to a `planned` row). An active app with NEITHER is *silently forgotten* — a coverage defect on the same footing as a missing gen-asset guard.** `scripts/check_web_clone_coverage.py --ci-mode` is the standing guard (set-difference of the two registries; fails on any un-covered active app). This is the `/play`-clone sibling of `portfolio.md` § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS + § R-CHAPTER-MULTIBEAT-COMPLETENESS: a **self-checking registry so a clone-eligible app can never fall through** — codified per founder-direct 2026-07-20 (*"audit … we are not missing any portfolio web-clones or not silently forgotten. codify"*), after `AUDIT_WEB_CLONE_COVERAGE_2026-07-20` found 8 active apps with no clone row (+ 1 registry-drift orphan).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every SHIPPED /play clone is reachable on every applicable content hub — a /play clone (`clone.meta.ts`, glob-derived) MUST be registered in the hand-curated `apps.generated.ts` too, or it is DARK on /story + adult /apps; the cross-hub DATA-LAYER completeness guard (R-CONTENT-HUB-CATALOG-COMPLETENESS; 2026-08-26)

**The three content hubs draw membership from DIFFERENT, unequally-self-updating sources — `/play` from the GLOB `PLAY_CLONES` (`src/data/play/*/clone.meta.ts`, auto-updating); `/story` from the HAND-CURATED `apps.generated.ts` `dnCast` (SEMI-MANAGED, NO regenerator) + authored chapters (which drive the story LIST); `/books` from the prebuild-regenerated `books-manifest.json` — so a newer clone globbed onto `/play` can silently fall out of `/story` + the adult `/apps` surface. Every SHIPPED `/play` clone (a `clone.meta.ts`) MUST therefore ALSO be registered in `apps.generated.ts` — else it is DARK-STORY (dark on `/story` + `/apps`, `/books` grouping→"Other"), a DEFECT unless a machine-parseable `# DEFERRED: <app> — <reason>` documents it. A clone REGISTERED but chapter-less is STORY-PEND (tracked-fine — its `/story` visibility rides the DN deep-build drain, § `distributed-narrative.md` R-DNS-DEEP-BUILD-COMPLETENESS; NEVER a duplicate parallel paid-gen build). This GENERALIZES § R-WEB-CLONE-NO-DARK-SURFACE from the route/registry layer to the cross-hub membership DATA layer (composes with, never re-litigates, § R-WEB-CLONE-COVERAGE-COMPLETENESS). The `/play` glob's own build-cache stale-restore risk (a page globbing a growing set whose module-hash never changes) is delegated to § R-SITE-FORCE-PROMOTE-AND-VERIFY + Program #78, not re-solved here. Guard: `scripts/check_content_hub_catalog_completeness.py` (SHAPE-B, reads `origin/staging`; `--ci-mode` fails ONLY on an undocumented DARK-STORY clone) + `Docs/REGISTRY_CONTENT_HUB_CATALOG_COMPLETENESS.txt`.** Founder-direct 2026-08-26 ("newer webclones don't show up in the /play catalog … similar issues in /story and /books … codify, guard, track, generalize"); decision `ADR-192`; Program #79.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-192_CONTENT_HUB_CATALOG_COMPLETENESS.md`.
## The homepage "total apps" count MUST be ≥ the playable-clone count — floor it by construction on the page + a guarded floor constant, NEVER by importing the clone glob into `site.ts` (R-SITE-APPCOUNT-FLOORS-PLAYABLE; 2026-09-10)

**The homepage hero shows `site.appCount` as the "{totalApps} free educational apps" H1 directly above `PLAY_CLONES.length` as "{totalClones} …ready to play right now", so "total" MUST be ≥ "playable" — else it reads "386 of them are ready to play" out of only "335 apps" (the founder-flagged "playable > total" bug: the SELF-UPDATING `/play` glob `PLAY_CLONES` [`src/data/play/*/clone.meta.ts`] outgrew BOTH the hand-curated `apps.generated` [the § R-CONTENT-HUB-CATALOG-COMPLETENESS #79 DARK-STORY lag] AND the stale hardcoded floor `PORTFOLIO_APP_COUNT` in `site.ts`, where `appCount = max(apps.length, PORTFOLIO_APP_COUNT)`). THREE invariants, each a dark-route-footing defect: (1) the HOMEPAGE floors by construction — `const totalApps = Math.max(site.appCount, totalClones)` in `index.astro` (already imports `PLAY_CLONES` → zero new coupling); (2) `site.appCount` stays ≥ the clone count PORTFOLIO-WIDE via the `PORTFOLIO_APP_COUNT` floor kept honest by the guard — NEVER by importing `PLAY_CLONES` into `site.ts` (Nav+Footer pull `~/data/site` onto EVERY page + the glob churns per clone → § R-SITE-BUILD-SKIP-FRIENDLY); (3) GUARDED at the LANE — a new clone pushing `PLAY_CLONES.length` past `site.appCount` REDS a staging PR, forcing a `PORTFOLIO_APP_COUNT` bump OR an `apps.generated` reconcile (never a silent prod contradiction). Semantically "total apps" ⊇ "apps playable now"; `appCount` self-heals to `apps.length` once the catalog catches up to the glob. Guard `scripts/check-appcount-floor.mjs` (prebuild + prebuild:play; emits the DARK-STORY drift as INFO for #79) + Vitest `totalApps ≥ PLAY_CLONES.length` in `portfolio-stats.test.ts` (reds the release-train preflight). REALIZES Report No. 82 / Program #124 (ADR-243) M8 "counter-integrity test"; EXTENDS § R-SITE-STAT-SSOT from "no hardcoded prose literal" to "a derived total never contradicts a co-displayed derived count". Composes ABOVE § R-CONTENT-HUB-CATALOG-COMPLETENESS (#79) · § R-SITE-BUILD-SKIP-FRIENDLY.** Founder-direct 2026-09-10 ("why … apps ready to play right now is greater than the total … fix it permanently"); ✅ SHIPPED site #3702→staging→prod (live-verified 386/386, dual-scheme×desktop+mobile); Program "Counter Integrity (app-count ≥ playable)"; detail → `Docs/CONTEXT_HANDOFF_2026-09-10_HOMEPAGE_APPCOUNT_FLOOR.md`.

## Every shipped clone gets a FORWARD-PORT PLAN synthesizing parity gaps + competitive novel features — a standing guard so the iOS→web forward-port backlog is never silently forgotten (R-WEB-CLONE-FORWARD-PORT-PLAN; 2026-07-31)

**Every shipped `/play/<app>` clone MUST carry a DEEP `Docs/web/<app>/PLAN_FORWARD_PORT_<date>.md` (frontmatter `depth: deep`) — the per-app forward-port backlog built from (a) a FULL AUDIT of the actual iOS APP REPO SOURCE (`Packages/**/Sources/**` — the real views/engines/models/tutorials, NOT just the `Docs/` plans or the ledger inventory), (b) fresh FOREGROUND-SEQUENTIAL deep-web COMPETITIVE research, and (c) HARDER synthesis combining iOS→web parity gaps + net-new competitive features into ONE prioritized backlog. A doc-only synthesis (no iOS-repo audit, no fresh research) is `depth: scaffold` — a legit starting scaffold, NOT the deliverable. A shipped clone with no plan AND no machine-parseable exclusion is silently-forgotten; a scaffold-only clone is a tracked deep-drain item (`check_forward_port_coverage.py` + `REGISTRY_WEB_CLONE_FORWARD_PORT_COVERAGE.txt`).**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The forward-port program tracks COMPLETED (shipped) built-features fleet-wide, not just PLAN coverage — a self-checking completion registry so no shipped forward-port feature is invisible (R-WEB-CLONE-FORWARD-PORT-FEATURE-REGISTRY; 2026-07-31)

**The forward-port program has TWO tracking axes, and both are self-checking registries — a session must never confuse them. (1) PLAN COVERAGE — does each clone have a forward-port plan, at what depth? — is `Docs/REGISTRY_WEB_CLONE_FORWARD_PORT_COVERAGE.txt` + `check_forward_port_coverage.py` (§ R-WEB-CLONE-FORWARD-PORT-PLAN). (2) BUILT-FEATURE COMPLETION — which forward-port FEATURES have actually SHIPPED across the fleet? — is `Docs/REGISTRY_WEB_CLONE_FORWARD_PORT_FEATURES.txt` + `scripts/check_forward_port_features.py` (this rule). The coverage registry's verdict flips ONLY on plan DEPTH (DEEP vs SCAFFOLD), NEVER on build completion — so before this registry existed, a shipped forward-port feature (e.g. a new web-pioneered drill) flipped NOTHING at the fleet level; "which forward-port features have we completed?" was answerable only by grepping ~189 scattered per-clone PARITY ledgers. A shipped forward-port feature that appears in NO fleet completion surface is *silently-forgotten completion* — the same anti-silent-forget discipline as § R-WEB-CLONE-COVERAGE-COMPLETENESS + `portfolio.md` § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS, applied to the BUILD axis.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-31 (*"are we tracking completed features/webclones for the forward port program?"* → *"yes, fleet built-feature completion registry"* + *"audit for past completed features as well"* + *"codify"*).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The canonical UI/UX best-practices reference for every clone (R-WEB-CLONE-UI-UX-BEST-PRACTICES; 2026-07-14)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone — current AND all future — follows the canonical, evidence-based UI/UX best-practices reference at `Docs/GUIDE_WEB_CLONE_UI_UX_BEST_PRACTICES.md`.** It distills what the portfolio has SHIPPED (the prominence program across ~60 themed clones, the shared `.pc-q-*` / `.ff-stage` design system, the screenshot-DoD discipline) into ONE authoring + review guide, grounded in the learning-science + accessibility literature (cognitive-load theory; the 2025 JSIR children's ed-gaming multicriteria study; elaborated-feedback superiority [Shute; Hattie & Timperley]; the seductive-details meta-analysis; WCAG 2.2 target-size/dragging/focus criteria; POE). Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-14 (*"codify the UI/UX best practices for all future web-clones using what we have shipped so far"*).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every clone is MODELED against its domain's best-of-breed competitor + carries a pedagogically-matched SKIN + domain AUDIO — a per-domain THEME LAYER on the shared design system, boundary-placed, guard-the-ratio-safe, NEVER a reward-currency economy; tracked by a registry (R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX; 2026-08-05)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone's UI/UX is ELEVATED per its pedagogy via a standing program: (1) a deep competitor UI/UX BENCHMARK for the domain, (2) a pedagogically-matched per-domain SKIN/theme at the BOUNDARIES (hero · hub · mode-select · illustrated tiles · named opponents · boundary chrome) — a THEME LAYER on the shared design system + DN cast art, NEVER on the active solve loop, (3) domain AUDIO (evidence-safe SFX + OPTIONAL ambient + a mandatory MUTE), (4) a "UI/UX matches pedagogy + age band" verdict. The active manipulative/solve surface stays CLEAN (the theme FRAMES, never clutters); the reward-CURRENCY + streak economy (ChessKid Stars/Gems) is REJECTED. A samey/un-modeled clone is a tracked gap; ambient theme/audio ON the solve loop, a currency/streak economy, or an over-claimed outcome is a DEFECT. ELEVATES (composes with, never replaces) § R-WEB-CLONE-UI-UX-BEST-PRACTICES. The finalized gambittales landing RECIPE (⑪) is the fleet-inheritable reference.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Lifting from a CLASSIC BROWSER-GAME PORTAL (CoolMathGames et al.) is SURGICAL, swap-test-gated, and engine-verified — lift the primitive-mappable puzzle MECHANICS + the ASYMMETRIC-ROLE CO-OP; VALIDATE (don't re-lift) the instant/calm/no-fail ETHOS we already ship; EXPLICITLY SKIP the twitch/skill tail (which IS the empty-entertainment the educators criticize = the anti-Gimkit line) (R-WEB-CLONE-PORTAL-LIFT; 2026-08-10)

**Mining a classic browser-game portal lifts only primitive-mappable puzzle mechanics + asymmetric-role co-op, swap-test-gated and engine-verified for solvability; the instant/calm/no-fail ethos is validated, not re-lifted; pure twitch/skill games are skipped (the anti-Gimkit line).** Decision `ADR-108`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading) + `Docs/RESEARCH_COOLMATHGAMES_GAME_LIFT_2026-08-10.md`.

## The approved gambittales ChessKid reference fans out across the fleet as a CLUSTER-PARALLEL, AGE-BAND- + SUBJECT/DOMAIN/PEDAGOGY-CALIBRATED program — tracked by a self-checking registry; a blanket chess-tween reorg on a non-matching clone is a defect (R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT; 2026-08-05)

**GambitTales is the APPROVED immersion/reorg reference (founder sign-off 2026-08-05). The reference-first gate (§ R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX) is CLEARED → the finalized gambittales landing RECIPE (⑪) fans out across the other 188 shipped `/play` clones as a standing PROGRAM. The fan-out is executed CLUSTER-PARALLEL (multiple hub agents, one per subject cluster) and is CALIBRATED on TWO axes — (1) AGE BAND and (2) SUBJECT/DOMAIN/PEDAGOGY/CLUSTER — so each clone gets a band- AND domain-appropriate variant of the recipe, NEVER a blanket chess-tween copy. A clone reorg'd to the wrong band's layout/register, OR with a domain-mismatched world/cards/CTAs (a chess-world env on a math clone, a busy game world on a calm reflection/SEL clone, "Play now" on a solo-reflection surface), is a DEFECT on the same footing as a failed screenshot-DoD; a clone left un-fanned-out is a tracked coverage gap.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-05 (*"gambittales is approved. codify and create a new program to track the fleet fan-out. make sure multiple hub agents can work on this at the same time … by subject/app cluster"* + *"make sure the fan-out is age band appropriate too"* + *"and subject, domain, pedagogy, cluster appropriate too"*). Program: `Docs/PLAN_WEB_CLONE_IMMERSION_REORG_FANOUT_2026-08-05.md`; the fleet-inheritable recipe is § R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX ⑪ (the ✅ REFERENCE APPROVED block).

**AMENDMENT — the immersion treatment is NEVER "done"; EVERY new webclone MUST carry it, and the newer un-immersed spawns are a tracked BACKFILL drain (founder-direct 2026-08-24).** The 189/189 fan-out was a snapshot; clones spawned SINCE shipped WITHOUT `immersive: true` → a coverage regression it hid. THREE standing invariants, each a defect: (1) **ALL new webclones MUST ship the band+domain-appropriate ChessKid immersion treatment** — a SPAWN-COMPLETENESS dimension (`portfolio.md` § R-SPAWN-COMPLETENESS-DIMENSIONS / § R-WEB-CLONE-SPAWN-TRACKED), not an optional follow-on. (2) the un-immersed spawns are a standing worst-first BACKFILL drain (OVERVIEW #18 sub-drain; queue `q-immersion-backfill`; runbook `RUNBOOK_IMMERSION_FANOUT_PER_CLONE.md`; site PRs → **staging**, single-flight Gemini). The guard `scripts/check_immersion_fanout_coverage.py` AUTO-DISCOVERS every `/play/<app>/nav.ts` on the site ref (never a hand-list) → each un-immersed spawn shows as `reorg:pending`, never silently forgotten. Composes with `portfolio.md` § R-FABLE-NEXTLEVEL-REVIEW (a non-immersed clone is `DEFERRED` from the Fable review until immersed). **(3) 🚫 DON'T-WORK-ON-THIN-CLONES (founder-direct 2026-09-17 "don't work on thin clones. codify this") — a `reorg:pending` clone flagged `thin-surfaces`/`thin-engine` in `REGISTRY_CONTENT_DENAIVE_DEPTH_COVERAGE.txt` (#136 / R-WEB-CLONE-CONTENT-DENAIVE-DEPTH) is `reorg:thin-defer`, NOT an immersion target — immersing a thin clone polishes a shell over shallow content (wrong priority: DE-NAIVE first, IMMERSE later). It is DEFERRED from the drain (NOT counted in reorg-pending) and AUTO-RE-ENTERS as `pending` the moment #136 clears the flag — COMPOSES ABOVE #136, never re-litigates it. Deterministic: the guard cross-references the committed #136 registry (R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH). A session that immerses a thin-flagged clone is working the wrong lane.**

**AMENDMENT to (3) — the thin-defer IMMERSION gate is LIFTED (founder-direct 2026-09-20 "thin clones are approved too").** A `reorg:thin-defer` clone IS a valid immersion target: the Tier-A gen-free immersion is cheap CHROME (nav `immersive:true` + accent-keyed pure-gradient env block + `pc-immersive` on all pages, ZERO paid gen), ORTHOGONAL to the #136 CONTENT de-naive — they proceed in PARALLEL, not the "de-naive-FIRST" sequence (3) imposed. The guard files it `pending-thin` (counted); `--ci-mode` fails on a new or regressed un-immersed clone. Executed 2026-09-20 → the fan-out reached **reorg 465/465** on staging (the final 37 immersed; detail → OVERVIEW #18 + `CONTEXT_HANDOFF_2026-09-20_IMMERSION_DRAIN_COMPLETE.md`; reusable `scripts/gen_immersion_tier_a.py`).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## 🔴 CRITICAL: EVERY `/play` webclone PAGE of an immersed clone carries the ChessKid immersion treatment — not just the landing + some sub-pages; a solve surface stays QUIET (env flattened), boundary/reward surfaces keep the env (R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-ALL-PAGES; 2026-08-31)

**🔴🔴 CRITICAL (founder-direct 2026-08-31 — "why flashcard pages are not immersed? … all webclone pages should have chesskid immersion treatment. make this critical too"): EVERY `/play/<app>/*` PAGE of an IMMERSED clone MUST carry the ChessKid immersion treatment — `pc-immersive` on its `.ff-wrap` — NOT just the landing. An "immersed clone" = its landing `index.astro` is `pc-immersive`; a sub-page of that clone whose `.ff-wrap` lacks `pc-immersive` is a CRITICAL defect on the dark-route footing. The immersion fan-out (§ R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT / #18) immersed the LANDING + SOME mission-kit sub-pages (missions/map/teach/adventure) but MISSED flash/wordplay/certificate (303 pages on 105 of 201 immersed clones, the founder-flagged gap) — because the coverage guard only checked the landing's `nav.ts immersive:true`. The treatment is BOUNDARY-APPROPRIATE (COMPOSES § R-GUARD-THE-RATIO, never contradicts it): the PAGE carries the immersion IDENTITY (skinned left-rail nav + env), but the active SOLVE surface stays QUIET — the env is FLATTENED behind it (ADR-220's `.pc-immersive:has(.mk-flash)::before{flat}` flat-floor, extended to `.mk-word`); boundary/reward surfaces (certificate, the mission MAP/hub) keep the env visible. So "all pages immersed" = every page carries the immersion SKIN with solve loops QUIETED — never a busy env on the solve loop. A NON-immersed clone (landing not `pc-immersive` — e.g. a deliberately flat 15-18 text-forward clone) is NOT forced immersive (the rule binds to consistency WITHIN an immersed clone). WEB-ONLY (§ R-WEB-CLONE-NAV-DIRECTIVE-WEB-ONLY — never propagated to iOS). Guard `scripts/check_immersion_subpage_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_IMMERSION_SUBPAGE_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails on ANY gap = 0-tolerance; drains 303→0, then any future non-immersive sub-page of an immersed clone is caught fleet-wide — closes the #18 landing-only blind spot). Program #105; decision `ADR-222`. Composes ABOVE § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT (#18) · § R-GUARD-THE-RATIO · ADR-220 (#103 flat-floor) · § R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX ⑨-⑪.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-222_WEB_CLONE_IMMERSION_ALL_PAGES.md`.

## Taking the site UI/UX to a PROFESSIONAL level is a standing 4-PILLAR program ABOVE the immersion fan-out — formalize the design SYSTEM (DTCG tokens) · a Figma-AI-assisted SPEC-then-code workflow (Figma = exploration/spec/tokens ONLY, NEVER a code source) · last-mile CRAFT/motion polish · design-REVIEW rigor; tracked by a registry (R-WEB-UI-UX-PROFESSIONAL-ELEVATION; 2026-08-06)

**The immersion reorg fan-out (§ R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT) + best-of-breed modeling (§ R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX / ADR-099) get each `/play` clone to the ChessKid RECIPE (structure + art). Reaching PROFESSIONAL (ChessKid/Duolingo/Prodigy) grade is a DISTINCT, standing program that COMPOSES ABOVE the fan-out (never replaces it), with FOUR pillars — and one HARD, non-waivable line: NO AI-design-to-code tool (Figma Make/Sites · v0 · Lovable · Builder.io · Anima · Locofy) may SHIP CODE to the fleet. Design tooling — including Figma AI — is AUTHORING-TIME ONLY: an EXPLORATION + design-SPEC + design-TOKEN front-end whose output CLAUDE CODE implements into our REAL `_shared/`/`Hub*`/Astro components; generated code forks the design system + misses our WCAG-2.2-AA + dark-mode + reduced-motion + reduced-transparency gates + breaches the on-device/no-third-party-SDK/COPPA trust posture. Adopting an AI-design-to-code tool AS A CODE SOURCE, or shipping any Figma/v0/Lovable-generated markup to a clone, is a defect on the same footing as a dark route.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-06 (*"take our website UI/UX to the next level, the professional level … create a program to codify and track this work. deep web research and think harder. my idea: use tools like figma ai as part of the overall solution"*); decision `ADR-100`; research `RESEARCH_WEB_UI_UX_PROFESSIONAL_LEVEL_2026-08-06.md`; plan `PLAN_PROFESSIONAL_DESIGN_ELEVATION_2026-08-06.md`. This is the DESIGN-side sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TEST-AUTHORING / ADR-082 (adopt a first-party AI *authoring* tool at design/dev time, reject shipping its output blindly, keep the deterministic gates + our own code) — same shape, same guardrails.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## The Professional Design Elevation program scales PORTFOLIO-WIDE as a parallel-able fleet (PDE-Fleet) — the 4 pillars across website + ~189 web clones + ~195 iOS apps, Figma MAXIMIZED (spec/review only, never a code source), multiple hub agents concurrent; tracked by a self-checking fleet registry (R-PDELEV-PORTFOLIO-FANOUT; 2026-08-07)

**The 4-pillar Professional Design Elevation program (§ R-WEB-UI-UX-PROFESSIONAL-ELEVATION / ADR-100) is driven across the WHOLE portfolio — three surface classes: (A) the marketing/CORE website · (B) the ~189 `/play` web clones · (C) the ~195 iOS apps — as a PARALLEL-ABLE fleet program (PDE-Fleet) that reuses the immersion fan-out's cluster-parallel claim machinery so multiple hub agents run it concurrently. Figma is MAXIMIZED at every step (DTCG→Figma-Variables · per-surface Code-to-Canvas capture · `use_figma` · Code Connect) — now proven working DIRECTLY from the hub agent — but the HARD LINE is unchanged: Figma is a spec/exploration/REVIEW surface, NEVER a code source; no design-tool-generated code ships to ANY surface (ADR-100 Pillar 2). A surface elevated by shipping Figma-generated markup, an iOS surface where hub wrote Swift, a parallel lane that mutates a SHARED surface (`play.css`/`global.css`/`Hub*`/`_shared`/the DTCG token JSON/the Figma design-system library) instead of single-flighting it, or a fleet surface left with no coverage AND no documented exclusion, is a defect on the same footing as a dark route.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-07 (*"create a program that maximizes the usage of this design elevation program for the whole portfolio (website, web clones and iOS apps). maximize figma usage too. make the program parallel-able … codify and track."*); program `Docs/PLAN_PORTFOLIO_DESIGN_ELEVATION_FLEET_PROGRAM_2026-08-07.md`; decision `ADR-100` Amendment 5. This is the fan-out sibling of § R-WEB-UI-UX-PROFESSIONAL-ELEVATION (the 4-pillar recipe), exactly as § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT is the fan-out sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX.

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-WEB-CLONE-TEST-PERF).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## A discrete-item clone's content is renderable as a STUDY MISSION KIT — a color-skinned mission map + fixed 6-box mission cards + commit-then-reveal quick-checks + 3-pile spaced flashcards + boundary content-tied delight + a mixed-format boss level + a printable companion, from ONE `MissionKitConfig` (print + interactive parity); calm-gamification NEVER an engagement farm; reference-first on TutorForge, fanned out fleet-wide, guarded (R-STUDY-MISSION-KIT; 2026-08-22)

**Any `/play/<app>` clone with a discrete-item corpus (MC kits · manipulative/POE bank · vocab) MAY render its content as a STUDY MISSION KIT — the design+pedagogy system lifted from the founder's "Matter Masters" kit: a color-cluster MISSION MAP (stars/badges/level-ladder, on-device, unique ns) + fixed **6-box MISSION cards** (`From YOUR notes · Coach says · Memory hook · content-tied Joke · Whoa! · commit-then-reveal Quick-check · optional Trick-question · 2-min Try-it · brain-break`, SAME order every mission) + a **3-pile spaced FLASH deck** (axis-7) + boundary delight (from the shipped humor bank) + word-games + a mixed-format **BOSS LEVEL** (reuse examMode) + a **certificate** + a printable companion + an adult **parent/tutor guide** — delivered as SHARED shells (`_shared/missionKit.ts` + `<MissionMap>`/`<MissionKit>`/`<FlipCheck>`/`<FlashDeck>`), opted into per-app via a `MissionKitConfig` auto-derived from the clone's kits-index/humor-bank/examMode (NEVER hand-rolled), eligibility-by-archetype+age-band. PRINT + INTERACTIVE are two renders of ONE config; the Worker's output is generalized to a topic-agnostic `MissionKitStudyPack` (deploy founder-gated, client degrades gracefully); ~$0 paid gen (Twemoji + open fonts + reuse shipped art/humor). The load-bearing INVARIANT: hold the 10 ADHD-first hard invariants in `REFERENCE §0` — chiefly RETRIEVAL-BEFORE-REVEAL (a quick-check NEVER auto-reveals) · **CALM gamification NEVER an engagement farm** (additive stars/badges tied to LEARNING; NO streaks/scarcity/currency/leaderboards/countdown-timers — anti-Gimkit, `R-SITE-FEEDBACK`) · content-tied delight boundary-only (`R-GUARD-THE-RATIO`) · zero-friction/on-device/no-PII (`R-WEB-CLONE-PROGRESS-NS-UNIQUE`) · NEVER name the condition to the child · honest-yield · all standard gates (dark-safe · WCAG 2.2 AA · dual-scheme×desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD · age-band). A reveal-first quick-check, an engagement-farm form, an ADHD-named kid surface, or a proven-outcome claim is a DEFECT; a samey/un-kitted eligible clone is a tracked gap. COMPOSES ABOVE `R-PDELEV-PORTFOLIO-FANOUT` · `R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT` · `R-ADHD-DELIGHT-GAMIFICATION` · `ADR-177`. Reference-first on TutorForge; fanned out worst-first per cluster; tracked `scripts/check_study_mission_kit_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_STUDY_MISSION_KIT_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN).** Founder-direct 2026-08-22; decision `ADR-181`; program #67.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_STUDY_MISSION_KIT_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (12 patterns × print/interactive/fleet-contract + 10 invariants + tokens + generalized prompt) + `Docs/ADR-181_STUDY_MISSION_KIT_DESIGN_LIFT.md` + `Docs/PLAN_STUDY_MISSION_KIT_PROGRAM_2026-08-22.md`.

## The study kit is RE-SKINNABLE by a curated THEME FAMILY (Mission, Detective, …) whose per-app DEFAULT is CONTENT-DERIVED — the STRUCTURE stays theme-independent, the skin re-labels boundaries only, honest-yield (theme = coherence/appeal, NEVER a learning-gain), never a blanket theme (R-STUDY-KIT-CONTENT-THEME; 2026-08-23)

**The Study Mission Kit's STRUCTURE (`R-STUDY-MISSION-KIT`'s 12 patterns + 6-box mission + retrieval-before-reveal + boss level + flash deck + certificate + parent guide + the 10 ADHD-first invariants) is THEME-INDEPENDENT; the SKIN is a pluggable `KitTheme` from a CURATED catalog — `mission` (theme #1, the #67 default) · `detective` (theme #2, specced verbatim from the "Amelia Math Detective" PDFs) · `expedition`/`quest` (reserve) — re-labelling ONLY boundary chrome (vocabulary labels [Mission Map↔Case Board, Boss Level↔Final Case] · accent palette · mascot ROLE [reuse the clone's shipped DN cast art, `R-DN-PARITY`, ZERO paid gen] · rank-ladder names · callout-tag taxonomy/emoji · the finale motif [boss quiz↔decoder message]). Grounded in Habgood (the theme/fantasy is deliberately SWAPPABLE over fixed mechanics — learning rides the STRUCTURE, the 2022 boost is attentional not motivational) + thematic-coherence (a coherent skin organizes + engages). Each study-kit-eligible app carries a CONTENT-DERIVED DEFAULT theme, TWO-AXIS calibrated (subject/pedagogy × age band, like `R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT`) — deduction/logic/number-sleuthing/proof/cipher → `detective`; science/exploration/general → `mission` — recorded in `REGISTRY_STUDY_KIT_THEME.txt`, PLUS an optional on-device learner theme-toggle (autonomy, no PII). Five hard lines, each a defect: (1) the STRUCTURE never changes with the skin (only the labels/palette/mascot/ladder/finale-motif do); (2) the theme set is CURATED — `auditKitTheme` THROWS on a theme id not in the catalog (never an open-ended per-clone bespoke skin); (3) HONEST-YIELD — a theme is coherence/appeal/attention-fit, NEVER a proven learning-gain (the structure carries learning); (4) NEVER a blanket theme and never a content-mismatched skin (a detective skin on a calm SEL surface, a blanket mission skin on a deduction clone = a defect on the immersion-fanout footing); (5) the skin is BOUNDARY-ONLY (`R-GUARD-THE-RATIO`) + all standard gates (dark-safe · WCAG 2.2 AA · dual-scheme×desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD · age-band) + the 10 ADHD invariants bind. Do NOT rename Mission→Detective (that just swaps one hard-coded skin for another); GENERALIZE to the family. `MissionKitConfig` gains `theme?` (default `mission` → backward-compatible); the skin data lives in a NEW `_shared/kitThemes.ts` (the only `missionKit.ts` edit, single-flight). COMPOSES ABOVE `R-STUDY-MISSION-KIT`/ADR-181. Tracked `scripts/check_study_kit_theme_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_STUDY_KIT_THEME.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails only on an invalid theme id).** Founder-direct 2026-08-23; decision `ADR-183`; program #69.

**AMENDMENT — CATALOG EXPANSION + content-TYPE axis (ADR-185, program #71, founder-direct 2026-08-23).** The curated catalog grows 2→8 with SIX content-derived themes lifted from a Fable-5 PDF batch (a theme-agnostic prompt → one theme per subject): `time-machine` (history/social-studies) · `hero-academy` (grammar/conventions — the villain EMBODIES the misconception) · `championship` (spelling/drill/fluency — self-referenced Personal-Record tracker, **never** a social leaderboard/streak/currency, anti-Gimkit) · `story-kitchen` (writing/composition — the **GENERATIVE variant**: build-a-story generator + revision-checklist rubric, NOT a commit-then-reveal quiz) · `grandmaster` (logic/strategy/board-game — +200 rating ladder) · `escape-room` (cumulative/mixed-review boss — reuse examMode). The selector gains a **content-TYPE axis** (drill→championship · generative→story-kitchen · cumulative-review→escape-room) on top of subject × age-band, because the research shows a *mismatched* theme can HARM learning (a wrong theme is not neutral). All five hard lines carry unchanged; the 6 records SHIPPED W1 (site #2674, SPEC→SHIPPED) + a SIXTH completeness line — a SHIPPED theme record is *shipped-but-dark* until a content-matched reference clone adopts it (`R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS`; dark-theme WARN axis of `check_study_kit_theme_coverage.py`).

**AMENDMENT — EXPANSION WAVE 2 + new interactive mission-TYPEs + a `guild` META layer (ADR-186, program #71, founder-direct 2026-08-24, job1 "Adventurer's Guild" Fable-5 batch).** A SECOND Fable-5 batch (same theme-agnostic prompt + a worksheet) → catalog 8→15 with SEVEN more content-derived themes, each carrying a **new interactive mission-TYPE** beyond a skin (the `story-kitchen`-generative precedent, generalized; a `MissionKitConfig` boundary flag, NEVER a solve-loop change): `newsroom` (journalism — GENERATIVE news-writing) · `courtroom` (argumentation — PROSECUTE-the-false-claim) · `red-pen` (editing — GRADE-THE-NOVICE error-hunt) · `game-show` (trivia — HOST-HOLDS-ANSWERS protégé) · `museum` (**visual arts**/curation — CURATE, ICAP-constructive) · `trail` (self-checking — BRANCHING choose-your-path gamebook, no-fail dead-ends) · `word-pets` (vocab/spelling — SPACED-FLASH collection = the FlashDeck re-skinned). PLUS `guild` — the META cross-kit XP campaign (The Adventurer's Guild; realizes the `quest` reserve) delivered by COMPOSING shipped shells (ADR-176 `/play/me` records/dashboard + ADR-119 non-destructive legacy-campaign), NOT a new engine + NOT a per-kit skin. The 3 error-detection themes are *erroneous-examples* (weak-alone, honest-yield) → MUST show the correct answer + prompt "how did you catch it?" self-explanation + scaffold + age-band-calibrate. All hard lines + guardrails carry unchanged (boundary-only · STRUCTURE fixed · curated catalog · honest-yield · anti-Gimkit · dark-safe/WCAG/dual-scheme screenshot-DoD · content-mismatch = defect · ZERO paid gen). Way-1 = the TutorForge Worker generalizes to the full 15-theme catalog (deploy founder-gated); the fleet default fan-out rides the #67 drain (q-c8a7ce); tracked `q-f3af29`.

**AMENDMENT — every SHIPPED theme MUST have ≥1 reference clone (HARD); a clone MAY be RE-THEMED to a better content fit (founder-direct 2026-08-24; ADR-186 §§ Amendments A/B).** Two composing invariants, each a defect: **(A)** every SHIPPED catalog theme (a `KitTheme` record, mirrored in the guard's `SHIPPED_THEMES`) MUST have ≥1 ACTIVE reference-clone registry row — a shipped-but-dark theme is a `R-WEB-CLONE-NO-DARK-SURFACE` defect; `check_study_kit_theme_coverage.py --ci-mode` now **FAILS** on it (promoted WARN→HARD, ratchet at 15/15). **(B)** a clone's theme MAY be changed to a better content-fit theme (content-CORRECTNESS, not churn) PROVIDED it stays a content-match + passes screenshot-DoD AND does not RE-DARK a theme (moving a clone off its SOLE-reference theme is fail-closed by (A)'s guard). Distinct from the per-clone fan-out WARN drain. → **Full detail** (precedents, discipline, "did we reject themes?"): `Docs/REFERENCE_STUDY_KIT_THEMES.md` § Reference-clone completeness + re-theme + ADR-186 §§ Amendments.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_STUDY_KIT_THEMES.md` (theme abstraction contract + full Detective + the 6+7 expansion-theme specs [§2b/§2c] + the 3-axis content→theme selector) + `Docs/ADR-183_CONTENT_DERIVED_STUDY_KIT_THEMES.md` + `Docs/ADR-185_STUDY_KIT_THEME_FAMILY_EXPANSION.md` + `Docs/ADR-186_STUDY_KIT_THEME_FAMILY_EXPANSION_WAVE2.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_CONTENT_DERIVED_THEME_FAMILY_EXPANSION_{2026-08-23,WAVE2_2026-08-24}.md`.

## The Story-Time-Chess-derived new features fan out fleet-wide as a standing, self-checking program (STC-Fanout) — the M1-M9 piece-move learn-ladder (+M8 misconception-demo) across the board-game cluster, per-cast movement mnemonics portfolio-wide, strategy-through-story book volumes; cluster-parallel, worst-first, honest-yield, no P1 (R-STC-FEATURE-FANOUT; 2026-08-20)

**The lifts mined from the actual Story Time Chess *Storybook* PDF (`RESEARCH_STORYTIME_CHESS_PDF_LIFT_2026-08-20`) fan out across the fleet as a standing, cluster-parallel, self-checking program: (F-LADDER) the graduated M1-M9 piece-move minigame LADDER — incl. the novel M8 impossible-task/misconception-DEMO (teach a hard constraint by productive failure, anti-shame) — ENRICHING the already-shipped `/learn` surface across the 17 board-game clones, archetype-scoped, delivered as a shared `_shared/pieceMoveMinigames`-style ladder over each clone's deterministic engine (Vitest-pinned, cast-piece-skinned per ADR-166, screenshot-DoD); (F-MNEMONIC) a per-cast-member movement/rule "say-out-loud" MNEMONIC portfolio-wide (free in-session-Opus, verified against the member's `dnCast` `role` primitive — R-DN-PARITY, never guessed); (F-BOOK) a strategy-through-story book volume + strategy-puzzle-workbook for board-game apps' book lines (ADR-083/085). It is DEPTH-ENRICHMENT of shipped surfaces, NOT a from-scratch build, and carries NO P1 (R-WIP-DISCIPLINE); every registry "gap" is a candidate §5b-verified against fresh origin before any build (the `/learn` feature already ships fleet-wide — never rebuild it). Reuses the § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT / § R-PDELEV-PORTFOLIO-FANOUT cluster-parallel claim machinery; worst-first by pedagogical priority (Logic leads, § R-WEB-CLONE-FORWARD-PORT-PEDAGOGICAL-PRIORITY); honest-yield (Story Time Chess has NO rigorous independent efficacy study — capability/engagement claims only, never proven-outcome). A board-game clone with neither a `ladder:` row nor a `# LADDER-EXCLUDED:` line is silently-forgotten. The pure 3-6 younger-band narrative-chess app stays a founder-gated § R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT decision — this program does NOT spawn it. 🔴 AGE-BAND CALIBRATION is a HARD, LOAD-BEARING axis (like § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT's two-axis calibration): Story Time Chess's NATIVE register is ages 3–8 (pre-reader, adult-read-aloud, silly-story-HEAVY, act-it-out, ~one-piece-per-WEEK pacing), but the fan-out TARGETS are dominantly 9–14 tween clones — so a VERBATIM lift onto a tween clone is an age-band MISMATCH DEFECT (too-young — the INVERSE of § R-YOUNGER-BAND-CONTENT-REGISTER; NN/g: kids reject content pitched even one band off in BOTH directions). Calibrate per the clone's `clone.meta.ts` `ageBand` (read at build-start, §5b): **9–14** = lift the STRUCTURE (isolation-first M1-M9 ladder + mnemonic + mastery-gating) with a TWEEN register — concise/respectful copy, LIGHTER narrative wrapper (cast frames, never dominates), FASTER self-paced (not one-piece-per-week, not adult-read-aloud, not "act it out"), text+optional-audio; **6–8** = the FULLER Story-Time register fits (audio-first/low-text per § R-YOUNGER-CLUSTER-NO-MC-KITS, bigger targets per § R-YOUNGER-BAND-LAYOUT-NAV, gentler pacing, rhyming mnemonics); **15–18** = MATURE register (no cutesy cast-piece silliness, no childish "say-out-loud" mnemonic — reframe as a concise rule-of-thumb or omit; favour the M9 calculation/target-select puzzles) per § R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED. A ladder/mnemonic pitched at the wrong band is a defect on the same footing as the immersion fan-out's wrong-band layout.** Founder-direct 2026-08-20 (*"create a program to fan out all the new features to the rest of the portfolio/fleet. codify and track everything"*). Tracked: `Docs/REGISTRY_STORYTIME_FANOUT_COVERAGE.txt` + `scripts/check_storytime_fanout_coverage.py` (SHAPE-B WARN). Instance of `portfolio.md` § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_STORYTIME_FEATURE_FANOUT_PROGRAM_2026-08-20.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_STORYTIME_CHESS_PDF_LIFT_2026-08-20.md`.

## Web clones have an automated test suite — Vitest units + Playwright a11y/SEL smoke (R-WEB-CLONE-TEST; 2026-07-12)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone is covered by an automated test suite in `spark-anvil-site`: `npm test` (Vitest — the SPM-unit analog) asserts mechanic LOGIC + hand-authored bank invariants + the shared `_shared/` round-shell contract; `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright — the XCUITest analog) drives every `/play` route headless to assert it renders + throws zero console/runtime errors + (SEL routes) exposes the crisis footer. This layer catches the "builds green, ships wrong" class the build-time gates cannot see (a bank with a wrong answer, a mechanic that throws on load).** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-12 (*"prioritize testing"*) after implementing `Docs/PLAN_WEB_CLONE_TESTING_STRATEGY_2026-07-12.md` (site PR #483). On its first run the Playwright smoke gate found + fixed a real production crash (`/play/sleuthlab/casefiles` `RangeError` on difficulty-5 cases).

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (**R-TEST-INTEGRITY**; 2026-08-23, ADR-184 — an agent may NOT weaken/delete a test or its assertions to get green (watch assertion-free tests, deleted assertions, over-mocking); a green suite achieved by loosening a test not fixing the code is a DoD defect. Our deterministic engine-verify IS the un-gameable oracle for gradeable surfaces (`R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE` / `-PORTED-PUZZLE-SOLVABILITY`) — keep it the enforcer. MUTATION-TEST a load-bearing SHARED `_shared/` engine before it fans out fleet-wide — a coverage number that survives injected mutants is false comfort) · (R-WEB-CLONE-SEL-CRISIS-FOOTER-SCOPE) · (R-WEB-CLONE-TEST-PERF) · (**R-WEB-CLONE-KIT-TEST-OVERLAY-EXCLUDE**; 2026-08-21 — a kit-bank test that GLOBS `kits/*.json` (`readdirSync`/`import.meta.glob`, shared OR per-clone) MUST exclude the `*.dv1.<lang>.json` i18n OVERLAYS — per-qid translation maps validated by `dv1.test.ts`, NOT MC banks; globbing them reds the shared gate (167 false fails, 2026-08-21, #2517). Guard `check_kits_test_overlay_exclusion.py`; a SPECIFIC-file read is exempt. Detail + the two exclusion forms → ADR-168 § Addendum) · (**R-DV1-OVERLAY-QID-MATCH**; 2026-08-21 — a DV1 overlay renders by QID-MATCH at the `_shared/dv1.ts` loadKit choke point, so every SHIPPED overlay kit's qids MUST match a SITE-served kit's qids; an overlay authored against a divergent LEGACY hub `Resources/Questions` kit (synthetic qids the site never serves) is HOLLOW — `shipped:true`, builds green, renders ENGLISH (fractionforge kit_01+kit_03, fixed 2026-08-21). Guard `check_dv1_overlay_qid_match.py --ci-mode` + `Docs/REGISTRY_DV1_OVERLAY_QID_MATCH.txt`; a PARTIAL/HOLLOW shipped kit is a defect. Detail → ADR-168 § Addendum).

**R-DISTRACTOR-ORACLE (2026-10-04):** a mode's test runs every authored wrong answer through the mode's checker and asserts it is false.

**R-REAL-BROWSER-PROBE (2026-10-13):** a feature CI can't run ships a real-browser probe; its fakes model the browser's refusals (`check_on_device_dictation.py`, ADR-323 Amd 3).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading) + `Docs/ADR-168_DV1_MULTILANGUAGE_CONTENT_DELIVERY.md` § Addendum.
## Playwright + Vitest is the test backbone; AI-assisted AUTHORING rides Playwright's native Test Agents + FIRST-PARTY MCP (never TestDriver / a third-party AI-vision vendor); AI-vision is reserved for genuinely uncontrolled surfaces only (R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TEST-AUTHORING; 2026-07-29)

**The `/play` fleet's test suite is Playwright (e2e a11y/SEL smoke) + Vitest (units) and STAYS that way — a deterministic, free, accessibility-tree/DOM-based engine on runners we own. AI-assisted test AUTHORING is delivered by Playwright's OWN native Test Agents (Planner / Generator / Healer, ≥ v1.56) + the FIRST-PARTY Playwright MCP server (`playwright run-test-mcp-server`), driven from Claude Code at AUTHORING time only (zero CI cost — the agents never run in CI). Adopting a commercial AI-VISION testing agent (TestDriver.ai or equivalent) for the fleet — OR pointing `.mcp.json` at a third-party/hosted testing vendor — is a defect: it imports LLM non-determinism into gates we deliberately keep flake-free + cheap, adds per-vision-minute cost to an actively-cost-trimmed CI, and breaches the no-third-party-SDK / on-device / COPPA trust posture. AI-vision is reserved ONLY for a genuinely UNCONTROLLED surface (a hosted third-party embed we don't own, or a native app) where the a11y tree/selectors cannot reach — we have none today; a new one is a fresh, scoped decision, not a reversal of this rule.** Founder-direct 2026-07-29 (quotes in ADR-082). Decision: `Docs/ADR-082_WEB_TESTING_PLAYWRIGHT_VS_TESTDRIVER.md`; evidence: `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_TESTING_TESTDRIVER_VS_PLAYWRIGHT_2026-07-29.md`; workflow: `Docs/GUIDE_WEB_CLONE_PLAYWRIGHT_AI_AUTHORING.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Screenshot analysis is a MANDATORY gate for UI/UX + Definition of Done (R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD; 2026-07-13, made mandatory-per-clone 2026-07-14)

**Screenshot analysis is a MANDATORY ship gate — not optional. Any UI/UX-affecting change to a `/play` clone (or any site surface), AND every NEW clone at ship time, is NOT DoD-complete until the surface(s) have been RENDERED, CAPTURED, and VISUALLY ANALYZED by the agent at BOTH a desktop and a mobile viewport, in BOTH color schemes, with the analysis recorded. The gate asks TWO questions per surface per condition, and BOTH must pass: **(1) does it RENDER correctly?** (hierarchy · prominence · contrast · spacing · alignment · clipping · reading order · dark-safety) and **(2) is the LEARNING TASK still PERFORMABLE?** (§ R-WEB-CLONE-TASK-VALIDITY). The automated suite asserts a surface renders/doesn't-throw/is-keyboard-operable/has-accessible-names — it is BLIND to whether the surface LOOKS right (hierarchy, prominence, contrast, spacing, alignment, clipping, reading order). Only looking at the pixels catches "builds green + passes every test + looks wrong."**

→ **Sub-rules (defined here; full body → reference):**
- **R-PR-SCREENSHOT-REQUIRED** (2026-09-17, founder-direct) — SCREENSHOTS ARE REQUIRED FOR EVERY VISUAL CHANGE, PER PR: any PR that affects a rendered surface (a `/play` clone, a site page, shared chrome/CSS) MUST produce the dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD captures for the touched surface(s), and the PR body MUST record that they were captured + visually analyzed (the `R-EVIDENCE-PACK` before/after-screenshots item). AND at SESSION HANDOFF the session MUST **OPEN the screenshots folder in Finder** (`open <dir>`, e.g. `open /tmp/uiux-<prog>/`) so the founder can eyeball them for the visual gate. A visual-change PR with no captured/analyzed screenshots, or a handoff that doesn't open the folder, is an incomplete DoD. A NON-visual PR (docs/rule/guard/logic-only, no rendered-surface change) is exempt — state "no visual surface" in the PR body. This is the per-PR + handoff operationalization of the mandatory gate below; composes `workflow.md` § R-SESSION-CLOSE-OUT (the open-folder step) + § R-EVIDENCE-PACK + § R-REVIEW-RISK-TIER (a TIER-1 shared-chrome visual change also gets the founder VISUAL gate). Founder-direct 2026-09-17 ("screenshots are required for visual changes for each PR and open folder of screenshots during session handoff").
- **R-SCREENSHOT-DOD-FORCED-COLOURS** (2026-10-12, #229 IVE E4) — a shared-chrome change is also checked in forced colours (Windows High Contrast; Playwright `forcedColors: 'active'`): filled buttons keep a border, selected/current states use system colours, data swatches opt out (`forced-color-adjust: none`). → ADR-324.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-TASK-VALIDITY** (2026-08-25) — gate question 2, GENERALIZED beyond dark mode: if a task asks the learner to JUDGE a perceptual property (colour · size · position · motion · sound · order), that property MUST SURVIVE every shipped condition (dark · mobile · reduced-motion · reduced-transparency · translated(DV1) · read-aloud · offline). A surface that RENDERS perfectly while its task becomes UNANSWERABLE is a CORRECTNESS defect. **Trigger: if the prompt asks about a property, verify that property survives the condition.** Tracked `check_task_validity_conditions.py` + `REGISTRY_TASK_VALIDITY_CONDITIONS.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; candidates, never asserts a defect).
- **R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-COMPOSITE** (2026-07-24) — the per-surface gate is BLIND to CUMULATIVE composition: a fleet-wide additive-island rollout must ALSO capture + judge the FULL composed page (whole `teach.astro`/landing, desktop+mobile × light+dark) for reading-load/scroll-length/hierarchy, not just the new island.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-REDUCED-TRANSPARENCY** (2026-08-05) — headless Chromium DEFAULTS `prefers-reduced-transparency: reduce`, so a glass/immersive/translucent surface captures its SOLID fallback; FORCE `no-preference` via CDP (`Emulation.setEmulatedMedia`), capture both states, pixel-sample the margin when in doubt.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-STALE-SERVE** (2026-08-06) — a lingering local static server on the capture PORT silently serves the WRONG clone's dist; `pkill -f serve-abs` (or a fresh unique port) before each capture, and ground-truth a wrong-looking shot against the BUILT HTML (`grep -c pc-feat-art dist/client/play/<clone>/index.html`), not another screenshot.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-E2E-SERVE-CLOBBER** (2026-08-08) — do NOT serve `/play` DoD via `e2e-serve.mjs` (it can trigger a CORE `npm run build` that overwrites `build:play`'s `dist/client`); serve with `python3 -m http.server --directory dist/client` on a fresh port + `cp public/play/<clone>/*.webp` in (`build:play` stashes `public/`).
- **R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-SHUFFLED-ROUND** (2026-08-09) — to DoD a specific puzzle in a `shuffle(items).slice()` round, RELOAD-until-the-target-is-FIRST (don't precompute a fixed index plan), activate SVG board squares by focus+Enter (not `.click()`), and compute solve lines OFFLINE via `npx tsx` importing the engine.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-FULLPAGE-DOWNSCALE** (2026-08-27) — a FULLPAGE shot of a TALL page (>14000px) is reader-displayed at ~2000px (~7× downscale) → dense DARK-MODE prose averages to PALE/WHITE blocks = a FALSE "dark section unthemed" defect; CONFIRM against a `-top.png` above-the-fold crop (`uiux-prod-sweep.mjs`, site #2849) or a `getComputedStyle` `color`+walked-up-`background` probe BEFORE firing a dark/contrast fix. Computed-style sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-STALE-SERVE; any long-page screenshot-DoD.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-CENTERED-OVERFLOW** (2026-09-27) — never CENTRE the content of a scroll container: the overflow is clipped off the START edge, unreachable by scrolling, and only a behaviour drive catches it. Use `justify-content: safe center` or auto inline margins. Guard `check_centered_overflow_css.py` (fails on a NEW candidate). → reference § this heading.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-FULLPAGE-FIXED-BACKDROP** (2026-08-27) — a FULLPAGE dark capture of an IMMERSIVE surface (`.pc-immersive`/`.pc-band-env`, whose env backdrop is `.pc-immersive::before{position:fixed;inset:0}` over a `body:has(.pc-immersive){background:transparent}`) shows a FALSE "light-in-dark TAIL" (Meet-the-cast · Kids-also-liked · grown-ups footer render on white below the fold): a `position:fixed` backdrop paints only ONE viewport in a stitched fullPage PNG and does NOT repeat below viewport-1 → the transparent body falls through to the white browser canvas in the IMAGE ONLY. In a real browser the fixed backdrop is always in the viewport → the tail is always dark. CONFIRM with a VIEWPORT-scrolled capture (not fullPage) OR a `getComputedStyle` walked-up-`background` probe on STAGING before flagging/fanning a dark-tail fix (`R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA` empirical gate). Sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-FULLPAGE-DOWNSCALE; detail → `Docs/DECISION_P74_MATH_DARK_TAIL_PROBE_2026-08-27.md`.

- **R-PLAY-HEADING-SIZE** (2026-10-04) — preflight unsizes `h1`–`h6`; a new surface's stylesheet sizes its headings.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## UI testing + screenshot-DoD MUST cover DARK MODE, not just light (R-WEB-CLONE-DARK-MODE-TEST; 2026-07-14)

**⚠ Dark verification asks BOTH gate questions — renders-correctly AND task-still-performable (§ R-WEB-CLONE-TASK-VALIDITY); a legible dark surface whose TASK is unanswerable in dark is still a defect.** **Every `/play` clone (and every site surface) MUST be verified in BOTH color schemes — `prefers-color-scheme: light` AND `prefers-color-scheme: dark` — by the automated Playwright gate AND the screenshot-DoD pass. A surface that renders + reads correctly in light but is broken in dark (unreadable text on a dark panel, AA-contrast failure, an accent/semantic colour that vanishes, an un-themed white flash) is a defect, exactly as if it failed in light.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-14 (*"make sure the UI testing cover dark mode as well because there are a lot of dark mode issues with the web site."*). The site themes via **media queries** (`@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` in `global.css` + `play.css` + per-app `.pc-theme-<app>` tokens), so dark mode is the OS/browser default for a large fraction of real users and was previously **untested + un-screenshotted**.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Dark-mode SUPPORT best practices — theme through the shared vars; never render light-in-dark (R-WEB-CLONE-DARK-MODE-SUPPORT; 2026-07-15)

**Every `/play` clone MUST render correctly in `prefers-color-scheme: dark` by theming through the SHARED design-system variables — never by hardcoding light hex on its own surfaces. A clone (or a shared-surface change) that renders bright-white-in-dark, or dark-on-dark / light-on-light unreadable, is a defect on the same footing as a failed parity axis.** This is the *design/authoring* companion to `R-WEB-CLONE-DARK-MODE-TEST` (which is the *gate*): -TEST tells you dark is verified; -SUPPORT tells you how to build so it passes. Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-15 (*"codify dark mode support best practices in repo"*) after V237 found the entire `/play` layer rendered light-in-dark and fixed it with one shared block.

**⚠ DARK-ACCENT-AS-TEXT sub-axis (ADR-274; 2026-09-15).** A per-app accent `--pc-{select,correct,incorrect}` (or its `-strong` ink, P#125) used as small-label TEXT (`color:var(--pc-…)`) but defined in `themes/<app>.css` with NO `@media(prefers-color-scheme:dark)` counterpart stays deep-on-dark → SUB-AA (dark-on-dark-unreadable — the downscaled screenshot-DoD misses it, a computed-contrast probe catches it). FIX = the aidforge/**ledgerquest** dark-only `@media` accent block (light byte-identical; icon+WORD not colour-alone), or — for a token ALSO used as a solid BACKGROUND — the **clean-split** (lift the token + re-pin every solid-fill-with-text block [app-own + a fixed fleet-shared #fff-on-select set] to the ORIGINAL hex → byte-identical bg, only TEXT lifts; recipe ADR-274 §batch-11, made AIRTIGHT for the astro-scoped/source-order/inherited-text/nested-var re-pin blind spots §batch-12). The **CORRECT/INCORRECT** analog is `--ci-clean-split` (§batch-14): lift the accent + re-pin the fleet-shared `.pc-q-choice--<tok> .pc-q-key` + `.pc-daily-choice--correct` + `.pc-q-feedback--<tok>` icon + app-own solid fills; the `app_has_light_bg`=False subset is statically airtight (no hardcoded light island). §batch-15 (s35) drains the **`app_has_light_bg`=True** subset via `--probe-proven` (the proven select-flow analog): a per-block static proof (accent-text lands ONLY on dark paper/border — 0 on any hardcoded-light bg) closes R3 statically, gated empirically by the two-sided driven probe (`probe_dark_accent_driven.mjs --tok correct|incorrect`, the s35 token-agnostic INJECT). 🔴 The **6th re-pin blind spot** = the JS-INLINE-FILL class: a clone that sets `el.style.background='var(--pc-<tok>)'` in TS (a state button/LED, WHITE text, no modifier class) is NOT statically re-pinnable → the generator's new `js_inline_accent_bg` gate SKIPs it (the SOURCE grep is the airtight gate — the rendered probe can CONFIRM but not DISPROVE a state-gated fill; deferred to a token-split/JS follow-on = batch-16). own-light-`-bg`-island apps + the JS-inline apps defer to the follow-on. Both GENERATED (`gen_dark_accent_safe_batch.py --clean-split|--ci-clean-split [--probe-proven]`, which MERGES into an existing dark block, never skips) + AIRTIGHT **TWO-SIDED** probe-gated (TEXT-side `--tok --lifted` ≥AA + BG-side `--check-repin` = no light text on a lifted-accent bg). Standing fleet worst-first drain (the guard's TEXT detection excludes `*-color:` compounds + decorative `::before`/`::after` glyphs — ADR-274 §s30/s31) guarded by `check_theme_dark_accent_text_contrast.py` + registry (hub, SHAPE-B WARN regression-only). 🔴 The **7th/8th re-pin blind spot** (§batch-17) = the FLEET-SHARED `#fff`-on-accent FILLS in `play.css` (`.pc-cta-play` primary CTA + the quiz badge/feedback-icon/daily fills) that a NON-clean-split lift never re-pinned → white-on-lifted-accent sub-AA (mapforge CTA 2.51); the token-agnostic `--repin-shared` RETRO mode back-fills them to the ORIGINAL hex (byte-identical, ink-override-aware) + the go-forward emit folds the shared re-pin into EVERY lift path so it can't re-open (175 select + 21 ci + 6 JS-inline-token-split shipped s36). 🔴 The **own-`-bg`-island `-bg`-FLIP** (`--ci-bg-flip`, batch-16(b)) is the last MECHANIZED class + the ONLY VISIBLE surface change (RISK-TIER-1, not byte-identical): a LIGHT `--pc-<tok>-bg` island carrying accent-TEXT gets a COUPLED THREE-var dark override — LIFT `--pc-<tok>` + DARK-FLIP `--pc-<tok>-bg` (`hue_preserving_darken`) + neutral on-island text light-in-dark BY CONSTRUCTION (`--ff-ink` UNDEFINED fleet-wide → inherits `.ff-card` `--ff-anvil` #ECE8E3);
