STATIC-AIRTIGHT (a hardcoded-dark / stays-dark-var on-island → SKIP hand tail) + two-sided static AA + reference-first screenshot-DoD + **founder train VISUAL gate** (51 shipped s37, **68→24**; 24 hand-tail = color-mix-`-bg`/select-not-on-island/hardcoded-dark). 🔴 The **DRIVEN-TWO-SIDED-PROBE-PROVEN lift** (`--probe-lift` + `--proven-pairs app:tok`, batch-18/s38) is the airtight emit gate the STATIC `clean_split_plan` could never be — for a `(app,tok)` whose TEXT-side driven probe is SAFE it LIFTS + re-pins all solid fills WITHOUT the static accept/reject, clearing the whole `app_has_light_bg`-gated + clean-split-false-reject hand tail (**0 NOT-SAFE fleet-wide** — the `app_has_light_bg` gate was over-conservative; the lifted accent lands only on dark surfaces), gated by the BG-side `--check-repin` for the non-solid residual. A driven-probe-**INCONCLUSIVE** correct/incorrect token (unreachable answered-state) is STATIC-proven via the fact that `_generated/play.tokens.css` GLOBALLY dark-flips `--pc-{correct,incorrect}-bg`→#22371f/#3a2222 (but NOT `--pc-select-bg`) → the island is dark in dark mode → a plain lift is safe; accordingly `ci_bg_flip_plan` is now FALLBACK-TOLERANT + **GLOBAL-FLIP-AWARE** (correct/incorrect = probe-lift, NEVER a per-theme `-bg`-flip; only `select`'s light `-bg` island genuinely flips). **24→1** (s38) → 🔴 **1→0 DRAIN COMPLETE** (s39, #4032): the last flag gambittales/select drained via the §batch-16a board-piece TOKEN-SPLIT. **411 themes scored, 0 flagged (from a 260 baseline); the guard is now a REGRESSION-ONLY tripwire (`--ci-mode` fails only on a NEW flag).** → ADR-274 §batch-9→19.

**⚠ WHITE ON ACCENT FILL:** add `oklch(from <fill> min(l, 0.53) c h)`; paper ink → `#fff` (ADR-274 s20–21).

**⚠ HEX FALLBACK:** `var(--pc-x, #hex)` with `--pc-x` undefined for the clone stays light in dark → `check-text-token-fallback.mjs`.

**⚠ BLACK GLYPHS:** a near-black emoji (👣 ⚫ ♠️) as a nav icon or landing H1 vanishes in dark mode → site `cloneNav.icon.test.ts`, no exceptions.

**⚠ NON-TEXT:** dark `--pc-select` <3:1 (focus ring) fails 1.4.11; lifts re-pin #fff fills (`check_theme_dark_select_nontext.py`, `check-theme-dark-select.mjs`). Hero CTA: label ≥1.17rem bold, fill/ink ≥3:1 (`check-hero-cta-contrast.mjs`).

**⚠ UNDEFINED-TOKEN sub-axis (P#87 s16→s19):** a never-defined `var(--x)` is invalid (border vanishes) or always LIGHT (hex fallback). On /play use the real token (`--ff-anvil/paper/warm`); `check-css-token-defined.mjs`: new undefined pair (0) or bridge use (25 frozen) fails. **One setter per accent:** a clone stylesheet loads after its theme, so re-declaring `.pc-theme-<app> { --pc-select }` cancels the theme's dark lift → `check-theme-accent-cascade.mjs`. → ADR-274 § Undefined tokens + § s19.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Dark-mode coverage is WHOLE-SITE, not just /play — marketing/company chrome + light-only imagery (R-SITE-DARK-MODE-WHOLE-SITE; 2026-07-15)

**Dark-mode support + verification apply to EVERY site surface — the marketing/company pages (home, `/for-parents`, `/for-educators`, `/mission`, `/about`, `/press`, `/apps`, `/cast`, `/stories`, `/books`, the `/play` index) and the shared chrome (Nav, Footer, hybrid-glass) — NOT only the `/play` clones.** `R-WEB-CLONE-DARK-MODE-TEST` / `-SUPPORT` were written `/play`-scoped; this rule extends them site-wide. Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-15 (*"the rest of the web site also have dark-mode issues too, not just the /play unit"* + *"fix and codify"*) after the V251 audit (`Docs/AUDIT_SITE_DARK_MODE_2026-07-15.md`).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Toggle visibility via the `[hidden]` attribute, backed by a global `[hidden]{display:none!important}` — a component's own `display:` silently defeats `[hidden]` → "stuck" UI (R-SITE-HIDDEN-ATTR-DISPLAY; 2026-07-20)

**Any element shown/hidden at runtime by toggling the `hidden` attribute / `.hidden` property (the site's near-universal pattern — `AffectCheckIn`, `MasteryProgress`, `AgencyLoop`, the feedback rails, `ReadingAccess`, etc.) RELIES on the global base rule `[hidden] { display: none !important; }` (in BOTH `src/styles/global.css` site-wide AND `src/styles/play.css` /play base). Never rely on a component's own `display` cooperating with the UA `[hidden]{display:none}`, and never "hide" by only setting the attribute if the element also has an explicit CSS `display`.** Codified after the founder-reported fractionforge **"Before you start" (the `<AffectCheckIn>` card) stuck-after-tap** bug (site PR #1108).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## A FLIP / front-back reveal shows exactly ONE face — revealing the answer HIDES the prompt face, never stacks both; verified by EXERCISING the flip, not a bare load (R-WEB-CLONE-FLASHCARD-FLIP-SINGLE-FACE; 2026-08-25)

**A `/play` FLIP / front-back reveal affordance (a card with a question face + an answer face + a "Flip"/reveal control — the shared `FlashDeck.astro` study-kit flashcards + any future flip card) MUST show exactly ONE face at a time: revealing the answer HIDES the prompt face (and its prompt-only chrome, e.g. a "say it before you flip" nudge), NEVER stacks both. A reveal that appends the answer BELOW a still-visible prompt is a defect on the failed-dark-mode footing. Fix + guard live at the SHARED shell (one fix → all clones). Verified by EXERCISING the flip in a Playwright e2e (back-visible + prompt-HIDDEN + 0 console err), NOT a bare load (R-WEB-CLONE-TEST / the search-0-results lesson). DISTINCT from a joke "tap to reveal the punchline" (HumorBreak), where the setup legitimately stays; the FLIP framing triggers the one-face contract. Composes R-SITE-HIDDEN-ATTR-DISPLAY + R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD. Guard `scripts/check_flashcard_flip_single_face.py` + registry (SHAPE-B; `--ci-mode` fails on regression). Program #77; decision `ADR-191`.**

→ **Full detail (root cause, the 121-clone incident, Option A/B/C, gates):** `Docs/ADR-191_FLASHCARD_FLIP_SINGLE_FACE.md`.

## A `/play` flashcard is CALM & MINIMAL — the CARD is the whole screen (one idea / one face / whitespace / a tiny corner tag / one subtle accent); keep the reveal-then-grade mechanic, STRIP the study-console chrome (R-WEB-CLONE-FLASHCARD-CALM-MINIMAL; 2026-08-30)

**Every `/play/<app>` flashcard surface (the shared `FlashDeck.astro`, 121 clones; any future flip/flashcard) is CALM & MINIMAL — the CARD is the entire surface, NOT a study-management console wrapped around a card. Load-bearing invariant, each violation a defect on the failed-dark-mode footing: (1) ONE IDEA / ONE FACE / whitespace — front = one short prompt only, back = one short answer only, hero-sized centered legible type, generous whitespace, a tiny CORNER TAG (`TERM #N`/`ANSWER #N`), ONE subtle accent; NO eyebrow/title band, NO how-it-works panel, NO footer disclosures, NO 3-chip pile-status band + proportion strip ON the card surface (the minimum-information principle + "one thing per screen"/extraneous-load, worse for kids' smaller WM); (2) KEEP the mechanic minimally — single-face flip (composes ADR-191, reveal HIDES the prompt) + reveal-then-self-grade Leitner 3-pile, the 3 grade buttons on the BACK only, a single quiet "tap to flip" folding the say-it-aloud retrieval nudge (reveal-then-grade IS active recall — strip the console, never the mechanic); (3) DEMOTE the console via PROGRESSIVE DISCLOSURE (composes R-WEB-PROGRESSIVE-DISCLOSURE/ADR-203) — pile dashboard → ONE quiet counter (`3 / 20`); full pile breakdown + how-it-works + more-ways + reset behind ONE unobtrusive `⋯` OFF the card face (NEVER hides the card/crisis-footer/privacy-link); (4) all gates bind — dark-safe `--mk-*`/`--ff-*` + WCAG 2.2 AA both schemes + ≥44px + keyboard/focus-to-revealed-answer + reduced-motion + read-aloud curb-cut retained (R-COGNITIVE-ACCESSIBILITY) + the 13-theme KitTheme layer survives (theme overrides ONLY the accent), verdict = dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD; (5) CALM — no streaks/scarcity/timer/score/leaderboard (R-SITE-FEEDBACK/anti-Gimkit); (6) HONEST-YIELD — lower extraneous load/calmer/clearer, NEVER a proven learning-gain. Delivered at the SHARED shell (one fix → all 121 clones) → TIER-1/high-blast (single-flight + adversarial review + founder VISUAL gate, R-REVIEW-RISK-TIER/R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA). SKIP the literal print "cut-here" dashed line on the interactive web card (reserve for the PDF flashcard-sheet); adopt only the corner tag + subtle accent. Composes ABOVE (never re-litigates) ADR-191/R-WEB-CLONE-FLASHCARD-FLIP-SINGLE-FACE · ADR-181/R-STUDY-MISSION-KIT · R-WEB-PROGRESSIVE-DISCLOSURE · R-WEB-CLONE-QA-PROMINENCE · the KitTheme family. Guard `scripts/check_flashcard_calm.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_FLASHCARD_CALM.txt` (SHAPE-B tripwire; `--ci-mode` fails if the shared shell regresses a calm invariant — the ADR-191 shell-guard model, since the surface is ONE shell not a per-clone drain). Program #103; decision `ADR-220`.**

→ **Full detail (the evidence base, the 8-zone audit, options A/B/C, waves):** `Docs/ADR-220_WEB_CLONE_FLASHCARD_CALM_MINIMAL.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_FLASHCARD_CALM_MINIMAL_2026-08-30.md`.

## A flashcard FRONT is a QUESTION / retrieval PROMPT — never a bare topic label; the back is the ANSWER — and Q&A CONTENT quality (answerable · single defensible answer · unambiguous · not-bundled) is a standing fleet axis (R-WEB-CLONE-FLASHCARD-FRONT-IS-A-QUESTION; 2026-08-31)

**A `/play` flashcard's FRONT MUST be a specific QUESTION / retrieval PROMPT (the shared `FlashDeck` — `cardsFromMissions` front = `quickPair(m).q`, the mission's real retrieval prompt for every kind; a `What is {title}?` fallback where absent) — NEVER a bare topic/study-LABEL (`m.title`, e.g. "Cross-Topic Connections"). The BACK is the ANSWER (`quickPair(m).a`), with the concept term + definition (`sourceNote`) + memory hook as QUIET supporting context; the redundant back "Quick check" disclosure is removed (it WAS the question → now the front). A bare-label front prompts only RECOGNITION; a specific question forces RETRIEVAL — the whole point of a flashcard (recall-not-recognition). A flashcard front that shows a bare label instead of an answerable question is a content defect on the failed-dark-mode footing. Delivered at the SHARED shell → all 121 clones, reusing the mission's own authored prompt (ZERO new authoring / ZERO paid gen). COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) ADR-220/R-WEB-CLONE-FLASHCARD-CALM-MINIMAL (#103 — the card is still the screen: corner tag / whitespace / one idea) + ADR-191/R-WEB-CLONE-FLASHCARD-FLIP-SINGLE-FACE (#77 — reveal HIDES the question). BROADER: Q&A CONTENT quality across the mission/flashcard surfaces (front is an answerable QUESTION · answer PRESENT + single/defensible · not-bundled sub-questions · central concept) is a standing self-checking axis — COMPOSING (never duplicating) `portfolio.md` § R-KIT-QUALITY-AXIS (the 16×25 MC-kit Haladyna item quality) + § R-WEB-CLONE-QA-PROMINENCE; a deterministic Q&A-STRUCTURE scorer `scripts/check_qa_quality.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_QA_QUALITY.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, reads origin/staging; flags label-only fronts / empty answers / bundled sub-questions), with the SEMANTIC lift a worst-first free-in-session-Opus drain. Honest-yield: retrieval-quality signal, NEVER a proven learning-gain. Program #104; decision `ADR-221`.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-221_FLASHCARD_FRONT_IS_A_QUESTION_AND_QA_QUALITY.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_QA_CONTENT_QUALITY_2026-08-31.md`.

## A component's scoped `<style>` styles ONLY its OWN template elements — an element rendered by a CHILD component needs its layout CSS in THAT child, never the parent page (R-ASTRO-COMPONENT-STYLE-SCOPING; 2026-07-22)

**Astro scopes each component's `<style>` to the elements in THAT component's template (via a per-component `data-astro-cid-*` attribute). So layout CSS for an element that is rendered by a CHILD/extracted component MUST live in that child's own `<style>` (or be `is:global`) — a rule in the PARENT page's scoped `<style>` compiles to `.foo[data-astro-cid-PARENT]` and NEVER matches the child's element, which then silently falls back to whatever global/base class it also carries. The failure is invisible at build + link-check + a normal render (the element still appears, just with the wrong/base layout); only measuring computed style or the screenshot-DoD catches it.** Incident (the /play/teach TeachCard layout, site #1206) → reference.

**Same trap, script-built markup (P#209 S4):** an element a `<script>` creates (`innerHTML`, `createElement` + `className`) never carries `data-astro-cid-*` either, so a scoped rule for it never matches — wrap it in `:global(…)` (component-prefixed) or use `is:global`. Guard `scripts/check-scoped-style-js-markup.mjs` (prebuild, both units).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A bespoke inline-markdown renderer of AUTHORED PROSE must parse `**strong**` BEFORE `*em*` (+ `` `code` `` + HTML-escape) — a single-`*` rule leaks/CORRUPTS `**bold**`; the real-markdown `<Content/>` + Typora paths are fine (R-SITE-BESPOKE-MD-INLINE-ORDER; 2026-07-30)

**Any surface that renders AUTHORED PROSE through a HAND-ROLLED regex markdown→HTML helper (a `mdToHtml`/`renderPromptInline`-style `set:html` of chapter beat prose, reflection prompts, or any generated narrative) MUST parse the inline tokens in the correct ORDER — HTML-escape `&`/`<` first, then `` `code` ``, then `**strong**` BEFORE `*em*` — because a naive single-asterisk-only rule (`s.replace(/\*([^*]+)\*/g,'<em>$1</em>')`) does NOT match `**bold**` cleanly: it leaves the OUTER asterisks (`**X**`→`*<em>X</em>*`) and, on a beat mixing `**bold**`+`*em*`, actively SCRAMBLES the emphasis. A single-`*`-only bespoke renderer is a defect the moment its content contains `**bold**` (or `` `code` ``).** Codified per founder-flag 2026-07-30 (`raw-markdown.png`: numbersense/nudge-and-gap beat 0 showed `**72 − 38**` as literal `*72 – 38*`) — root cause + fix `InterleavedChapterAudioPlayer.astro` `mdToHtml` (site #1497, V682); impact was 1,049 chapters / 1,888 `**bold**` occurrences + 64 with `` `code` ``, all in that ONE shared reader.


A chapter `primitive:` subtitle renders through `src/lib/inlineEmphasis.ts`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A TypeScript GENERIC cast (`as Record<K, V>` / `as Foo<Bar>`) INSIDE an Astro template expression breaks the build — the compiler parses `<K, V>` as a JSX tag; HOIST the cast to the frontmatter script (R-ASTRO-TEMPLATE-GENERIC-CAST; 2026-07-31)

**An inline TypeScript cast whose type carries angle brackets — `(x as Record<string, number>)[k]`, `(y as Map<string, T>)`, any `as Foo<Bar>` — placed in an Astro `.astro` TEMPLATE expression (`{ … }` in the markup body) FAILS the build: Astro's template compiler parses the `<string, number>` / `<Bar>` as a JSX/HTML OPENING TAG, yielding a cryptic `Expected ")" but found "\`<string, number data-astro-cid-…>)…"` rollup/vite error at the file. The fix is to HOIST the generic cast into the frontmatter script block (the `---` fence at the top), where TS generics parse fine, then reference the pre-cast const in the template (no `<>` in the markup).** Codified per the V685 volume-surface build (`story/index.astro` `(volumeBooks as Record<string, number>)[book.slug]` → build failed; fixed by `const volumeCounts: Record<string, number> = volumeBooks;` in the frontmatter + `volumeCounts[book.slug]` in the template). This is the Astro-template sibling of § R-ASTRO-COMPONENT-STYLE-SCOPING / § R-SITE-BESPOKE-MD-INLINE-ORDER / § R-SITE-HIDDEN-ATTR-DISPLAY — a `.astro` idiom that produces a wrong result (here a hard build error) dressed up as ordinary code.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A `width:100%` (or lazily-injected) child COLLAPSES to 0px inside a `display:flex`/centered parent that has NO explicit width — give the CONTAINER `width:100%`; the automated gates are blind to it (R-WEB-CLONE-FLEX-PERCENT-WIDTH-COLLAPSE; 2026-07-31)

**A child sized `width:100%` (or a lazily-appended custom element / canvas that has no intrinsic width until it renders) collapses to 0px WIDTH when its parent is a `display:flex`/`inline-flex` (or otherwise shrink-wrapping) container that has NO explicit width and sits inside a CENTERED column (`align-items:center` / `text-align:center` / a shared `.ff-stage`). The parent shrink-wraps to its content; the content is `width:100%` of the shrink-wrapped (≈0) parent → a circular collapse → the child renders INVISIBLE (height often still correct, so the element IS in the DOM — it's just 0-wide). The fix is one line: give the flex CONTAINER `width:100%` so it fills the centered stage, then the child's `width:100%` (capped by any `max-width`) resolves to a real width. build + link-check + a normal render are BLIND to it (the element exists, no error); only a computed-style measure (`getBoundingClientRect().width`) or the screenshot-DoD catches the 0-wide element.** Codified per the founder-reported cubesensei "3-D cube is missing" bug (site #1516): `.cs-net-3d` was `display:flex; justify-content:center` with NO width, so the lazily-loaded `<twisty-player>` (cubing.js, `width:100%`) rendered **0×300** (invisible blank area) → `.cs-net-3d { width: 100% }` → the cube renders **340×300**. This is the layout sibling of § R-ASTRO-COMPONENT-STYLE-SCOPING / § R-SITE-HIDDEN-ATTR-DISPLAY — a CSS idiom that produces a wrong result (an invisible element) dressed up as success.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## One shared layout system — 8pt spacing scale + container + reading-measure + section rhythm (R-SITE-LAYOUT-SPACING; 2026-07-15)

**Every site page lays out through ONE shared system — an 8pt spacing scale, a single content container, a 66ch reading-measure for long-form copy, and a consistent section-rhythm token — defined in `global.css`; pages MUST NOT hand-roll ad-hoc widths + spacing per page.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-15 (*"almost all web pages on the website have layout and spacing issues. do deep web research if needed"* + *"fix and codify"*). Evidence base: `Docs/RESEARCH_SITE_LAYOUT_SPACING_BEST_PRACTICES_2026-07-15.md`; audit: `Docs/AUDIT_SITE_LAYOUT_SPACING_2026-07-15.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## The `--space-*` spacing scale is ENFORCED by a shift-left raw-px WARN gate — spacing uses the scale, drift is tracked, never silent (R-SITE-SPACING-TOKEN-ENFORCEMENT; 2026-08-07)

**SPACING (margin / padding / gap / inset / positional / sizing) on every site + `/play` surface SHOULD use the generated `--space-*` scale (`src/design-tokens/` → `theme.css`: 4/8/12/16/24/32/48/64/96 px), NOT ad-hoc raw px — and this is now ENFORCED by a shift-left gate, `scripts/check-css-raw-px.mjs`, wired into `prebuild` + `prebuild:play`. This is the "code enforces reality" half of R-SITE-LAYOUT-SPACING: the 8pt scale was BUILT (ADR-100 Phase 1b, the DTCG `--space-*` + Figma `space/*` Variables) but had ~0 adoption + 0 enforcement across `play.css`/`global.css`/all 189 clone stylesheets → inconsistent spacing, uneven empty space, overwhelm (the q-db0941 root cause). A NEW raw spacing-px added to `src/styles/**` where a `--space-*` step fits is drift; the gate makes it visible.** Codified per the q-db0941 Layout/Spacing/Density Hygiene program (founder GO 2026-08-07; `Docs/PLAN_LAYOUT_SPACING_DENSITY_HYGIENE_2026-08-07.md`, ADR-100).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_LAYOUT_SPACING_DENSITY_HYGIENE_2026-08-07.md`.
## Fonts are SELF-HOSTED (never a CDN — a COPPA/GDPR + perf invariant), a display+body+dyslexia PAIRING (Geist·Lora·OpenDyslexic), and font TYPE + DEFAULT SIZE are AGE-BAND-ADAPTABLE by construction AND user-tunable via ReadingAccess; fluid `clamp()` for display is rem+vw NEVER vw-only (WCAG 200%-zoom); guarded against a CDN-font regression (R-SITE-TYPOGRAPHY; 2026-09-17)

**Every site + `/play` typeface is SELF-HOSTED (woff2 in `public/fonts/`, `@font-face` `font-display:swap`, latin-subset) — NEVER a font CDN (`fonts.googleapis.com`/`gstatic`/a remote `@import`/`@font-face src:url(https://…)`), because a font CDN leaks every visitor's IP to a third party = a COPPA third-party-data-recipient hole on a kids site (+ a GDPR IP-transfer, + slower LCP/CLS under per-site cache partitioning). Six hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) SELF-HOST/NO-CDN (the COPPA moat; composes `R-DEPENDENCY-SUPPLY-CHAIN` + `R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE`). (2) The PAIRING is `Geist` (display/brand) + system/`Lora` (long-form reading serif) + `OpenDyslexic` (opt-in) — NEVER a new typeface for its own sake, NEVER OpenDyslexic-as-default (a "dyslexia font" is an option not a requirement; spacing beats a special font). (3) 🔴 AGE-BAND-ADAPTABLE font TYPE + DEFAULT SIZE — the DEFAULT content size is band-appropriate FLEET-WIDE BY CONSTRUCTION: the shared `<CloneNav>` stamps `html[data-ageband]` at parse on every `/play` page (band read `config.ageBand ?? PLAY_BAND_BY_SLUG[slug]` — a slim generated slug→band map from `clone.meta.ts`, the authoritative SSOT; NOT the sparse optional nav-config ageBand, and NOT the `apps.generated` barrel per R-SITE-BUILD-SKIP-FRIENDLY), and `global.css` scales `#main-content` by `--pc-band-font-scale` (**3-5 = 1.15× · 6-8 = 1.08× · 9-14 = 1.0× · 15-18 = 1.0×**; never below the 16px floor) — applied on the SAME `#main-content` zoom lever as ReadingAccess so the band default COMPOSES MULTIPLICATIVELY with the learner/parent user text-size (band × user-scale, one property). 9-14/15-18 keep the base (mature = base, not smaller). Composes `R-YOUNGER-BAND-LAYOUT-NAV` (CloneNav band tab-sizing) + `R-COGNITIVE-ACCESSIBILITY` (ReadingAccess). Changing the band scale is a TIER-1 fleet-wide VISIBLE change → founder visual gate (R-REVIEW-RISK-TIER + R-PR-SCREENSHOT-REQUIRED). (4) FLUID `clamp()` for display = rem+vw in the middle term (`clamp(1.6rem,1.2vw+1.4rem,2.4rem)`) — a vw-ONLY middle (`clamp(1.05rem,3vw,1.3rem)`) fails WCAG 1.4.4 resize-to-200% (no zoom scaling); min<max, fluid line-height. (5) READING floors + `text-size-adjust:100%` (never `none`) + 66ch measure + WCAG 1.4.12 survives + LEFT-ALIGN never justify + no all-caps/italics prose blocks; dyslexia = ≥1.5 line-height + ≥~12% letter-spacing (opt-in). (6) LOADING — `font-display:swap` (or `optional` non-critical), preload ≤2 critical WITH `crossorigin`, immutable cache on `/fonts/*.woff2`. Guarded by `scripts/check-fonts-self-hosted.mjs` (prebuild + prebuild:play; HARD-fail on any CDN/remote font ref + missing body-floor/`text-size-adjust`; SHAPE-B WARN on a NEW vw-only `font-size:clamp()`). Composes ABOVE `R-SITE-LAYOUT-SPACING` · `R-COGNITIVE-ACCESSIBILITY` · `R-YOUNGER-BAND-LAYOUT-NAV` · `R-WEB-UI-UX-PROFESSIONAL-ELEVATION`; PRINT sibling = `distributed-narrative.md` § R-PDF-BOOK-FONT-SIZE (web + PDF share ONE age-band-calibration discipline).** Founder-direct 2026-09-17; Program #193; evidence `Docs/AUDIT_FOLDCOMMONS_TYPOGRAPHY_LIFT_2026-09-17.md`.

**Zoom-safe `vw` (P#210 s2):** inside the zoomed `#main-content` a `vw` is zoomed too, so a full-bleed `100vw` or a sized `min(94vw, …)` overflows; divide each ≥85vw term by `--pc-main-zoom`. Guard `check-zoom-safe-vw.mjs`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading) + `Docs/AUDIT_FOLDCOMMONS_TYPOGRAPHY_LIFT_2026-09-17.md`.
## A device-specific feature is SKIPPED, never a reason to skip the whole app (R-WEB-CLONE-DEVICE-FEATURE-SKIP; 2026-07-12)

**Requiring device-specific functionality is NOT a clone-eligibility blocker. If a feature needs a capability the browser cannot deliver on-device — AR/RealityKit/ARKit, Vision, CoreMotion/gyroscope, camera/mic capture, real-time haptics, Game Center, MultipeerConnectivity, SpriteKit *physics*, on-device FoundationModels — that ONE feature is skipped for the web clone (⛔ waived · platform-only, OR 💡 iOS-ENHANCE if it delivers novel LEARNING); the REST of the app MUST still be ported to the web.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-12 (*"loosen the blocker check: if a feature requires device-specific functionality, then that feature can be skipped for the web clone. the rest of the app should be ported to web"*).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Web-app clones must keep feature parity with their iOS app (R-WEB-CLONE-PARITY; 2026-07-08)

**A browser learning-app clone of a portfolio iOS app (the `/play/<app>/*` route tree — FractionForge is the first, `/play/fractionforge`) MUST maintain feature parity with that app's LEARNING-RELEVANT features, UNLESS a specific delta is EXPLICITLY WAIVED with a documented rationale in the app's parity ledger.** Parity is the default; every gap is either closed or explicitly justified — never silently dropped. Codified per user-direct 2026-07-08 (*"codify the requirement that fractionforge iOS app and web page need to have feature parity unless explicitly allowed not to"*).

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-CLONE-BIDIRECTIONAL-BACKPORT).

**R-WEB-CANONICAL-APP (2026-10-10, ADR-320):** a founder can declare an app's browser build canonical (`REGISTRY_WEB_CANONICAL_APPS.txt`; first: the chess ladder). Its iOS app is a feature-frozen companion with a published scope (`Docs/web/<app>/IOS_COMPANION_SCOPE.md`). Web features are then not parity gaps and get no `*_WEB_BACKPORT` handoff; correctness, safety and OS fixes still go to iOS. Guard `check_web_canonical_apps.py`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Web-clone UI/UX parity — the clone must carry its iOS app's visual + interaction character (R-WEB-CLONE-UX-PARITY; 2026-07-10)

**A `/play/<app>` web clone MUST reproduce its iOS app's UI/UX *character* — visual identity + interaction design — to a reasonable degree, UNLESS a specific delta is EXPLICITLY WAIVED with a documented rationale in the clone's UI/UX parity ledger.** This is the visual/interaction sibling of `R-WEB-CLONE-PARITY` (which governs *learning-feature* parity). Codified per user-direct 2026-07-10 (*"do full audit of fractionforge and grammarforge ios app ui/ux and create ui/ux parity for their web clones with reasonable exceptions. codify the ui/ux parity requirements with reasonable exceptions"*).

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-CLONE-BIDIRECTIONAL-BACKPORT).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A clone's COLOR SCHEME must be FUNCTIONAL · INTUITIVE · ENGAGING · PROMINENT — accessible-by-contrast, a 60-30-10 hierarchy with a role-consistent reserved accent, age-band-calibrated vibrancy, and distinct-hue-not-color-alone semantics (R-WEB-CLONE-COLOR-SCHEME; 2026-07-28)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone (and every site surface) MUST use a color scheme satisfying all FOUR founder axes, each a defect on the same footing as a failed dark-mode axis: FUNCTIONAL — accent/semantic/text clear WCAG 2.2 AA in BOTH schemes (4.5:1 text · 3:1 large · 3:1 NON-TEXT SC 1.4.11 · dark accents APCA-sane · meaning NEVER by colour alone SC 1.4.1); INTUITIVE — a 60-30-10 hierarchy (calm dominant ground · structured secondary · a RESERVED accent = per-app `--pc-select` meaning ONE role everywhere; accent role-drift is a defect); ENGAGING — accent chroma AGE-BAND-CALIBRATED (3-5/6-8 warm-saturated-objects · 9-14 confident-vivid-not-neon · 15-18 sleek/mature-NOT-cutesy; washed-out/muddy is both an engagement + a contrast defect); PROMINENT — placement + contrast + reservation, not area (the accent does the 3-card Q&A work; the ground/stage stays QUIET — flat fill, no background-image, no ambient motion on the solve loop, § R-GUARD-THE-RATIO).**

- **R-WEB-CLONE-COLORFUL-MANIPULATIVE-DARK-STAGE (2026-08-18, ADR-155):** a clone MAY force a scheme-INDEPENDENT dark backdrop on the manipulative-MOUNT container (NOT `.ff-stage` — ADR-118 strips its bg in the immersive column) ONLY for a **saturated/emissive object whose colours must POP** (cube · light-ray · spectrum); forcing dark on a colour-JUDGMENT task, a calm/sensory surface, or a non-saturated manipulative is a defect. Detail + per-clone audit → ADR-155.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-BUTTON-BOX-CONTRAST (2026-08-18, q-51aa36):** every choice/action/Continue/CTA button MUST carry a VISIBLE BOX + AA-contrast text in BOTH schemes — `.pc-q-choice` is LAYOUT-ONLY, so a button carrying it WITHOUT `.ff-btn` (or a per-clone box) renders transparent + near-invisible (a defect on the failed-dark-mode footing). Carry `.ff-btn`/`--primary` or a per-clone dark-safe `--ff-*` box (`:not([class*="--correct/--incorrect/--wrong"])`-guarded); the shipped ROOT FIX gives `.pc-q-choice:not(.ff-btn)` a default box in `play.css`. Guard: `scripts/check_boxless_choice_buttons.py` + `REGISTRY_BUTTON_CONTRAST.txt` (SHAPE-B; verdict = screenshot-DoD). Detail → `Docs/queue/q-51aa36.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading) + `Docs/ADR-155_WEB_CLONE_DARK_MANIPULATIVE_STAGE.md`.
## The question→answer→feedback flow is the shared PROMINENT surface — feedback is the climax, not a muted footnote (R-WEB-CLONE-QA-PROMINENCE; 2026-07-13)

**The question→answer→feedback flow is, after the manipulative itself, the MOST prominent element of every clone — and it is rendered by TWO shared shells (`src/lib/play/_shared/mcRound.ts` for the 16×25 MC kits + `customRound.ts` for bespoke mechanics), so its design is portfolio-canonical, not per-clone.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-13 (*"the question then answer flow for all web clones … should be the most prominent besides the actual manipulatives themselves … more functional, intuitive and engaging and especially prominent."*), implemented in site PR #527 on the evidence in `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_QA_FLOW_PROMINENCE_2026-07-13.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The manipulative is a prominent CARD/STAGE — same studio treatment as the Q&A cards, quiet background (R-WEB-CLONE-MANIPULATIVE-PROMINENCE; 2026-07-13)

**The interactive MANIPULATIVE (the hands-on surface — fraction bar, ray-trace bench, cipher wheel, spinner, grid…) is, together with the Q&A flow, the most prominent element of a clone, and it wears the SAME studio card treatment as the `.pc-q-*` question/answer cards** — the shared `.ff-stage` class (bold outline + hard drop-shadow + whitespace isolation + centering). Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-13 (*"make all the manipulatives prominent too"* + *"should the manipulatives have the card treatment like the question and answer cards too?"* → **yes**), on the evidence in `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_MANIPULATIVE_PROMINENCE_2026-07-13.md`; foundation shipped site PR #531 (fractionforge pilot).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Playback / media controls are KID-SIZED + age-band-aware — a native `<audio controls>`/`<video controls>` bar is NOT acceptable as a primary kid control surface (UA-locked-small); use a custom transport (R-WEB-CLONE-MEDIA-CONTROL-SIZE; 2026-07-30)

**A kid-facing playback / media control surface (the `/cast` audio player, any `/play` transport) MUST be KID-SIZED + AGE-BAND-AWARE — a native `<audio controls>`/`<video controls>` bar is NOT acceptable as a primary kid control (UA-locked-small); use the shared `BaseAudioPlayer` custom transport with its `ageBand` prop (younger 3-5/6-8 = bigger play/skip/scrub hit-halos; tween 9-14 default; scrub hit-halo ≥44px per § R-WEB-CLONE-DRAG-TARGET, dark-safe). Reuse `BaseAudioPlayer` + `ageBand` for any future kid media control; never re-introduce a native bar. (✅ SHIPPED V672: multi-beat `/cast` player ported off the native bar; 163 multi-beat apps = 136 tween + 22 younger, 0 are 15-18.)**

**AMENDMENT — GENERALIZES to the native FILE INPUT: a raw `<input type="file">` ("Choose Files / No file chosen") is NOT acceptable as a kid-facing PRIMARY control either (R-WEB-CLONE-UA-CONTROL-NOT-KID-PRIMARY; founder-flagged 2026-08-23).** Same principle as the native media bar: a UA-default control is small, unstyled, and un-engaging. A kid-facing UPLOAD surface (TutorForge's `/study` PDF/photo upload — ADR-177 — and any future doc-ingest surface) MUST present a PROMINENT, ENGAGING, styled **drop-zone** (icon + bold "Tap to add …" lead + a clear ≥44px target; dark-safe `--ff-*`/`--pc-*`; focus-within ring; drag-and-drop with a drag-over state on pointer, the "drag" hint hidden on touch; reduced-motion-safe) — keeping the native `<input type="file">` VISUALLY-HIDDEN-BUT-KEYBOARD-FOCUSABLE inside the label so click/Enter opens the picker and all existing file-handling is unchanged. A raw native file input as the primary kid upload control is a UI/UX defect on the same footing as a native `<audio controls>` bar. (✅ SHIPPED 2026-08-23: `/play/tutorforge/study` `.tf-dropzone`, site #2663.) GENERALIZES: any UA-default control (`<audio>`/`<video>`/`<input type=file>`/native date-picker etc.) surfaced as a PRIMARY kid control → replace with a custom styled ≥44px affordance.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## No animated / real-time sound-wave visualizer on the kid audio-drama / chapter player — the karaoke line-highlight + beat progression ARE the pedagogical "playing/position" cue; a Web-Audio `AnalyserNode` over the R2 media can IRREVERSIBLY MUTE the audio (R-AUDIO-NO-DECORATIVE-VIZ; 2026-07-30)

**A kid-facing audio-drama / chapter player (the `/cast` `BaseAudioPlayer`/`InterleavedChapterAudioPlayer` surface) MUST NOT add a sound-wave / audio visualizer — neither a real-time reactive `AnalyserNode` waveform NOR a static pre-computed-peaks waveform. The player's EXISTING synchronized karaoke line-highlight (VTT cues) + beat-image progression + kid-sized scrub bar (V672) already deliver the "audio is playing / where you are" feedback, and they do it PEDAGOGICALLY (they point at the narrated text). A waveform is a competing, non-pedagogical visual channel → a seductive detail on a comprehension surface. Proposing a waveform is a founder-escalation over this rule + the seductive-detail canon; the default is to add nothing.** Codified per founder-ask 2026-07-30 (*"should we animate the audio dramas with sound waves … deep web research and think harder"*); decision `Docs/ADR-084`; evidence `Docs/RESEARCH_AUDIO_DRAMA_WAVEFORM_VISUALIZATION_2026-07-30.md` (5 converging arguments + sources).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every DRAG / positional / slider manipulative MUST have a single-pointer + keyboard alternative AND a ≥44px handle with a drag affordance — drag-only is a defect (R-WEB-CLONE-DRAG-TARGET; 2026-07-20)

**Any manipulative a learner moves by DRAGGING — a slider knob, a draggable marker/token/handle, a drag-to-place tile, a pinch/drag surface — MUST ship ALL of: (1) a discoverable SINGLE-POINTER alternative to the drag (visible ◀▶/±/tap-to-place buttons or tap-the-track — WCAG 2.5.7 Dragging Movements); (2) KEYBOARD operability (arrow keys / Home-End on a focusable `role="slider"`, WCAG 2.1.1); AND (3) a ≥44px pointer/touch target for the handle, verified ON MOBILE where a fixed-viewBox SVG shrinks the handle below the CSS size (WCAG 2.5.8 + the portfolio kid-target standard), WITH a clear drag AFFORDANCE (raised drop-shadow "knob" + `grab`/`grabbing` cursor) so it reads as grabbable. A drag-ONLY manipulative, or one whose handle renders < ~44px on a phone, is a defect on the same footing as a missing parity axis.** Founder-report 2026-07-20 (FractionForge number line; incident → reference).

**R-NO-REDRAW-ON-FOCUS:** a `focus` handler never redraws — a press focuses before the click, so a redraw swallows the tap (`check_no_redraw_on_focus.py`).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A `/play` surface that DEPICTS a physical unit (ruler/scale/true-size object) MUST render at TRUE physical size via the shared calibration module OR be framed as a RELATIVE instrument — never imply a device-dependent pixel size IS the real-world size; and stay legible on mobile (R-WEB-CLONE-PHYSICAL-UNIT-FIDELITY; 2026-07-24)

**Any on-screen VISUAL that depicts a physical UNIT — a ruler, a true-size object being measured, a scale/gauge, a protractor — MUST either (a) render at TRUE physical size via the shared `_shared/screenCalibration.ts` module (one-time on-device credit-card calibration → 1 cm on screen = 1 real cm), OR (b) be explicitly FRAMED as a RELATIVE reading instrument (read/align/subtract off the ticks) with an honest note that the pixel size is approximate — NEVER implying that a device-dependent pixel size IS the real-world size. AND it MUST stay LEGIBLE + USABLE on mobile (big tick labels; a too-long true-size ruler SCROLLS + auto-frames the measured object — never silently shrinks below physical truth into tiny illegible marks). A unit-visual that renders at an arbitrary/tiny device-dependent size while presenting as "a centimeter ruler" is a defect on the same footing as a dark route.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-24 (5-message refinement: *"how do we make sure the on screen units are accurate to the real world units … there are actual visuals of the units on screen … on mobile the visuals look tiny … the visuals sizes don't match physical sizes … deep web research and think harder"*); research `RESEARCH_MEASUREQUEST_UNIT_ACCURACY_2026-07-24.md`; audit `AUDIT_MEASUREQUEST_UNIT_ACCURACY_2026-07-24.md`; reference impl MeasureQuest `/measure` (site #1350).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/RESEARCH_MEASUREQUEST_UNIT_ACCURACY_2026-07-24.md` (research) + the reference impl.
## Manipulative/representation expansion follows the CRA ladder + an Applications rung — and it generalizes to NON-math clones via Bruner's modes (R-WEB-CLONE-CRA-LADDER; 2026-07-17)

**The manipulative BREADTH+DEPTH expansion axis (ADR-048 / `PLAYBOOK_WEB_CLONE_EXPANSION.md`) is ORGANIZED by the CRA ladder — Concrete → Representational → Abstract — plus a fourth APPLICATIONS / transfer rung; and it applies to BOTH math AND non-math clones (the ladder generalizes via Bruner's enactive→iconic→symbolic modes, which are domain-general).** When expanding a clone's manipulatives/representations, audit its surfaces by rung and expand to fill missing rungs — a primitive taught only ABSTRACTLY (an MC/Concepts surface) earns a Concrete + Representational surface; a primitive with only a manipulative earns an Applications/transfer surface. Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-17 (*"apply the cra model of singapore math to our expansion playbook … more concrete/manipulatives, more representational modes and more abstract modes"* + *"can we apply the cra model to non-math web clones?"* + *"add 'applications' as another axis"* + *"codify … especially the one about applying cra model to both math and non-math web-clones"*).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## No clone ships THIN — every clone carries a RICH, pillar-and-band-appropriate interactive surface; MC-only + a single thin manipulative/puzzle/creative surface is an enrichment gap (R-WEB-CLONE-NO-THIN-SURFACE; 2026-07-25)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone MUST carry a RICH interactive surface appropriate to its pillar AND age band — a real manipulative + puzzle (math/science/logic), a rich creative surface (creative), a deep reflection/decision surface (SEL/15–18), or a rich band-appropriate activity (younger) — NOT a bare multiple-choice quiz and NOT a single thin one-off surface. A clone that is MULTIPLE-CHOICE-ONLY, or whose sole non-MC surface is thin/token, is an ENRICHMENT GAP on the same footing as a missing CRA rung.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-25 (*"audit all webclones to find multiple-choice only webclones and enrich them with manipulatives, puzzles, etc."* + *"how about webclones that are thin in manipulatives and puzzles, etc."* + *"we don't want webclones with thin manipulatives and puzzles and creative surfaces, etc."* + *"codify these"*). This is the fleet-wide **floor** under the ADR-048 depth program — R-WEB-CLONE-CRA-LADDER says *how* to expand (fill rungs), R-WEB-CLONE-MANIPULATIVE-PROMINENCE says *how prominent* the surface is; THIS says every clone must clear the not-thin bar in the first place.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/AUDIT_MC_ONLY_CLONES_2026-07-25.md` + `PLAYBOOK_WEB_CLONE_EXPANSION.md`.
## A clone is VISUAL-FIRST, not TEXT-HEAVY — for 9–14 AND younger, the manipulative/visual-aid/diagram/illustration is the PRIMARY comprehension + attention channel; supporting text is CONCISE + CHUNKED, never the dominant surface; 15–18 is EXEMPT (R-WEB-CLONE-VISUAL-FIRST; 2026-08-08)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone for the 9–14 band AND younger (3–5 / 6–8) MUST be VISUAL-FIRST: a RELEVANT manipulative / visual aid / diagram / model / illustration carries the concept as the PRIMARY comprehension + attention channel, and supporting text (instructions · stems · feedback · teach · narrative) is CONCISE + CHUNKED — never a wall of prose that buries the visual. A TEXT-HEAVY clone — one where reading dominates the surface, OR where a concept is DESCRIBED in a block of words that a visual could CARRY better — is a defect on the same footing as a thin surface. 15–18 is EXEMPT (deliberately text-forward case-narratives per `distributed-narrative.md` § R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED — a text-forward 15–18 clone is NOT a text-heaviness defect).** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-08 (*"a lot of webclones are text-heavy for the 9-14 age band and younger … manipulatives, visual aids, and illustrations are very important for understanding and sustaining attention"*); decision `Docs/ADR-104_WEB_CLONE_VISUAL_FIRST.md`; evidence `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_VISUAL_FIRST_TEXT_DENSITY_2026-08-08.md`. This is the **text-density / visual-primacy** axis that sits OVER the existing surface rules — it composes with, and does not duplicate, § R-WEB-CLONE-NO-THIN-SURFACE (does a rich surface EXIST?) · § R-WEB-CLONE-MANIPULATIVE-PROMINENCE (is it a prominent card?) · § R-WEB-CLONE-CRA-LADDER (representation breadth) · § R-WEB-CLONE-QA-PROMINENCE — a clone can pass ALL of those and STILL be text-heavy.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Every clone gets a per-clone VISUAL-AID-NEEDS deep-research analysis — think harder about the SPECIFIC visual aids each clone's concepts need; picturable-concept-first, foreground-sequential, tracked (R-WEB-CLONE-VISUAL-AID-NEEDS; 2026-08-08)

**Every in-scope (9-14/younger) `/play` clone gets a per-clone VISUAL-AID-NEEDS analysis — a DEEP-RESEARCHED + THINK-HARDER pass that names the CONCRETE visual aids the clone's core concepts NEED (the specific diagram / model / manipulative / illustration per concept, grounded in the domain's visual-teaching canon + best-of-breed), what the clone currently SHIPS, and the GAP — recorded at `Docs/web/<clone>/VISUAL_AID_NEEDS_<date>.md` and tracked by the `needs:` column of `Docs/REGISTRY_WEB_CLONE_VISUAL_FIRST.txt`. This is the UPSTREAM, research-driven "what does this clone need?" half of the R-WEB-CLONE-VISUAL-FIRST drain — it identifies the PICTURABLE-concept-in-prose defects (the drain's real target) and drives the remediation (which visuals to Gemini-gen / build); the screenshot-DoD stays the downstream VERDICT. A clone remediated without first knowing what visual aids its concepts need is guessing.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-08 (*"for each webclone, do deep research and think harder about what visual aids the webclone need … codify and track everything … add to work queue and prioritize"*). Companion to § R-WEB-CLONE-VISUAL-FIRST (the review/verdict axis) — this is the needs-analysis axis; the two share ONE registry.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every `/play` clone's CONTENT is DE-NAIVED (synthetic/toy/shallow → canonical-corpus-grounded / pedagogically-deep, engine-verified, NEVER padded) and its content + features DEEPENED + BROADENED, NEWER clones first — a standing Fable-review axis over the shipped depth/de-naive rules, worst-first + recency-weighted (R-WEB-CLONE-CONTENT-DENAIVE-DEPTH; 2026-09-10)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone's INTERACTIVE CONTENT (kit banks · puzzle/manipulative/level banks · POE items · prompts/stems/feedback · teach content) is DE-NAIVED — synthetic/toy/hand-thin/shallow content REPLACED with canonical-corpus-grounded / pedagogically-deep content (the § R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-CANONICAL-SOURCE Lichess-de-naive generalized fleet-wide, sourcing the standing `Resources/PuzzleCorpus/` library, ADR-196) — and its content + FEATURE set is DEEPENED + BROADENED, with NEWER clones prioritized (they shipped after the depth-expansion drains → systematically thinner/naiver). A new AXIS of the Fable-Reviewed Next-Level Elevation program (§ R-FABLE-NEXTLEVEL-REVIEW / ADR-188 Amendment 16; Program #136), COMPOSING ABOVE (never re-litigating) § R-WEB-CLONE-NO-THIN-SURFACE · § R-WEB-CLONE-CRA-LADDER · § R-WEB-CLONE-VISUAL-FIRST / -VISUAL-AID-NEEDS · § R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-CANONICAL-SOURCE · § R-PUZZLE-CORPUS-LIBRARY · ADR-048 expansion axes 7–12 · § R-KIT-QUALITY-AXIS / -DISTRACTOR-LENGTH-CUE · the forward-port family (which own the levers) · § R-CHAPTER-AFFECT-ANCHORING-REVIEW (#110 — chapters are ITS axis; this is the clone's INTERACTIVE content + features). TWO-LAYER (the #110 model): a DETERMINISTIC STRUCTURE guard scores naive/thin SIGNALS (mc-only · thin-surfaces · synthetic-corpus · shallow-bank · missing-CRA-rung · text-heavy · no-expansion-axes) → worst-first + recency-weighted ordering (tiers NAIVE<THIN<OK<DEEPENED<REVIEWED); the SEMANTIC "is this genuinely shallow / under-taught?" judgment is a SINGLE serial read-only `model: fable` review over a per-clone content bundle → a de-naive + depth/breadth PLAN → `# DENAIVE-REVIEWED:`. EIGHT hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) DE-NAIVE = REPLACE, NEVER PAD (never pad a bank/distractor/surface to hit a metric — § R-KIT-DISTRACTOR-LENGTH-CUE never-pad + filler tripwire — never trim a correct definition); (2) ENGINE-VERIFIED stays binding (a real CC0 corpus is NOT exempt — § R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-ENGINE-VERIFIED / -BOARD-GAME-ENGINE / -PORTED-PUZZLE-SOLVABILITY); (3) NEWER-CLONES-FIRST (recency-weighted worst-first; a NEW spawn ships de-naived/deep content or opens a tracked debt — § R-SPAWN-COMPLETENESS-DIMENSIONS); (4) content authoring is FREE in-session-Opus ($0, R-INSESSION-OPUS-FREE) — the paid tail (corpus import + visual aids the concept genuinely needs, § R-WEB-CLONE-VISUAL-AID-NEEDS) is SINGLE-FLIGHT Gemini + R2-verified + FOREGROUND (R-GEMINI-KEY-SERIAL / R-GEN-DRAIN-VERIFY-R2-GROUND-TRUTH / R-GEN-NO-BACKGROUND); (5) REDISTRIBUTE — a hub-source de-naive is SOURCE-ONLY until it reaches hub-kit + app-repo + web (§ R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE, verify on the CONSUMERS); (6) HONEST-YIELD — richer content / clearer representation / truer transfer signal, NEVER a proven learning-gain; (7) standard gates bind — on-device/COPPA/dark-safe/WCAG-2.2-AA-both-schemes/age-band/boundary-only + engine-verify + dual-scheme×desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD; a TIER-1 shared-shell de-naive → adversarial pass + founder visual gate (R-REVIEW-RISK-TIER); (8) POSTURE — site PRs base=STAGING (ADR-154), Explore/export reads origin/staging, hub codification=main, ZERO paid gen in the REVIEW phase. A naive/thin clone with neither a tier row nor a documented exclusion is silently-forgotten; a padded/un-engine-verified/hub-only-not-redistributed "de-naive" is a defect. Tracked worst-first + newer-first by `scripts/check_content_denaive_depth_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_CONTENT_DENAIVE_DEPTH_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, reads origin/staging, auto-discovers all clones; `--ci-mode` fails only on a DEEPENED→NAIVE regression). Instance of `portfolio.md` § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.** Founder-direct 2026-09-10; decision `ADR-259`; program #136. Engine-first triage + candidate guards (engine-primitive, answer-position) → ADR-259 § Amendments s46–s47.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-259_WEB_CLONE_CONTENT_DENAIVE_DEPTH_BREADTH.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_CONTENT_DENAIVE_DEPTH_2026-09-10.md` + `Docs/PLAN_CONTENT_DENAIVE_DEPTH_PROGRAM_2026-09-10.md` + `Docs/ADR-188_FABLE_REVIEWED_NEXTLEVEL_ELEVATION.md` § Amendment 16.
## A CREATION-mode clone's free-build/design surface ships STARTER TEMPLATES — named, engine-verified example creations the learner LOADS → inspects → REMIXES; a blank creation canvas is itself a thin surface, and starter templates are its de-naive DEEPEN lever (R-WEB-CLONE-STARTER-TEMPLATES; 2026-09-12)

**A CREATION/DESIGN-mode `/play/<app>` clone (a build/design/create/curate surface — bricktots' build-a-place, worldforge's editor, the `*Lab` designers, museum-curation clones) SHOULD ship STARTER TEMPLATES: a curated set of NAMED example creations the learner LOADS → inspects → REMIXES on the editable surface — because a blank free-build canvas with NO starter example is ITSELF a thin surface (a blank canvas → paralysis, esp. 3-5), which more challenge levels never fix. Worked-example scaffolding (§ R-WEB-CLONE-SCAFFOLDED-HINTS) + the ForgeCartridge "a world is a file" model (ADR-248/252). SEVEN invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) CREATION-MODE ONLY (solve-only clones have nothing to template → the ordinary #136 bank-depth axis); (2) ENGINE-VERIFIED, never a stub — each template passes the clone's own validity/audit gate AND (where a goal exists) is reachable, so a loaded template always gives a satisfying result; pinned by the clone's Vitest oracle; NEVER padded; (3) LOAD → REMIX, not a gated level (a starting point the child owns after loading); (4) ON-DEVICE / calm / NO unlock currency-streak-scarcity (anti-Gimkit, § R-SITE-FEEDBACK); (5) BAND-APPROPRIATE + recognizable names; (6) all standard gates bind (dark-safe · WCAG 2.2 AA · ≥44px · keyboard/focus · reduced-motion; verdict = dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD); (7) HONEST-YIELD (lower blank-canvas friction + worked-example scaffolding, never a proven learning-gain). It is a #136 DEEPEN mechanism for creation clones (a clone deepened by shipping engine-verified starter templates counts DEEPENED, ADR-259). Reference-first bricktots (`TEMPLATES` over `FREE_WORLD` + `templatesAreValid` + a "✨ Try a build" load-and-remix affordance, site #3786); fleet fan-out is a standing worst-first drain, FOUNDER-GATED per clone (each is a UI change → the visual gate; a shared affordance fanning fleet-wide is TIER-1, § R-REVIEW-RISK-TIER). Tracked `scripts/check_starter_template_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_STARTER_TEMPLATE_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; auto-discovers creation-mode clones from 3 signals (creation-engine import · starter-template export · creations-store write) so a bespoke editor is caught too (#187); `--ci-mode` fails ONLY on a regression — an `ok` clone losing its templates). COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) § R-CREATION-ENGINE (ADR-248) · § R-WEB-CLONE-SCAFFOLDED-HINTS · § R-WEB-CLONE-CONTENT-DENAIVE-DEPTH (#136) · § R-WEB-CLONE-NO-THIN-SURFACE. Instance of `portfolio.md` § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.** Founder-direct 2026-09-12; decision `ADR-266`; program #143.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-266_WEB_CLONE_STARTER_TEMPLATES.md`.
## Every `/play` clone has a composite PARITY INDEX — a value-weighted, RECENCY-weighted (newer-first) scorecard AGGREGATING the per-axis parity registries into ONE worst-first worklist with gap→owning-program routing; a pure meta-aggregator that COMPOSES (never recomputes) the axis-guards (R-WEB-CLONE-PARITY-INDEX; 2026-09-12)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone carries a composite Newer-Clone Parity Index (NCPI) — a DETERMINISTIC, value-weighted, RECENCY-weighted per-clone parity scorecard that AGGREGATES the committed per-axis parity registries — **14 axes (5→14, 2026-09-12 q-6df976; weights → ADR-265 § Axis expansion): LEARNING** content(#136,+`age_d` spine)·dn(R-DN-DEEP)·tutor(#46)·kit-quality(#66)·visual-first·visual-aid(#34)·records(#61); **REACH** i18n(#157); **AESTHETIC** immersion(#18)·scene(#137)·subpage(#105); **ENGAGEMENT** mp(R-MP-UX)·humor(#72)·study-kit(#67); **+10 wave-2 (2026-09-29, → 24)** reflection·teach-depth(#45)·chapter-affect(#110)·cast-ensemble·landing-cast·catalog(#79)·feature-links(#186)·density(#89)·run-mode·starter-templates(#143); **+5 wave-3 (2026-09-30, → 29)** board-feature·dn-deep-build·humanlike(#87) + DEFECT-ONLY answer-position·turnlink (present only on a live defect); every per-clone registry is an axis OR a skip row in `REGISTRY_NCPI_AXIS_DISPOSITION.txt` (guard `check_ncpi_axis_disposition.py`); UNIVERSE = #136 spine ∪ (#18∩#46), never invisible; refresh axis registries off FRESH staging before `--write-registry` (ADR-265 § wave 2) — into ONE worst-first + NEWER-FIRST ranked worklist, routing each sub-par axis to its OWNING program's drain. It answers the question the ~15 per-axis programs individually could not: "which webclone is furthest from our BEST clones — newest-first — and which program closes each gap?" (founder-direct 2026-09-12: "all webclones but prioritize newer webclones first"). SEVEN hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) COMPOSES, never recomputes — reads the committed axis registries as SSOT; each axis-guard stays the authority; NCPI adds ZERO new per-axis truth; (2) VALUE-weighted, not raw-count — content depth (learning) weighted highest, aesthetic axes lower (the 6.4%-trap avoidance); (3) an ABSENT axis is EXCLUDED from a clone's denominator, NEVER scored 0 (IRT-style — never penalize a clone for an N-A/noisy criterion); (4) RECENCY-weighted newer-first — `priority = (100-index)*recency_boost(age_d)`, the recency spine = #136's `age_d`, so NCPI needs NO git/site access + is fully reproducible from the committed registries; (5) HONEST-YIELD — a prioritization SIGNAL, never a proven-quality/learning claim; parity is measured to OUR OWN best clones on OUR codified VALUE axes (never blind competitor-matching); (6) NEVER forces remediation — SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails ONLY on a REGRESSION (a clone whose committed index drops ≥ threshold or newly FAR); per-clone gen rides the owning program's Gemini-gated/founder-gated drain; (7) hub-codify base=main; the drains it feeds are the site programs (PRs base=STAGING, ADR-154; Explore reads origin/staging). GENERALIZES (invariant #7, DEMONSTRATED at scale): a NEW parity axis = extend `WEIGHTS` + an absent-axis-safe loader — the index absorbs it (5→14 axes 2026-09-12, TIER-1 adversarial PASS-WITH-NITS [subpage per-clone-0.0-cliff fixed]; content-dominant + `pending`-is-a-mild-gap = no false-tanking of un-reviewed tracking states; detail → ADR-265 § Axis expansion). Live counts → the registry header. Guard `scripts/check_web_clone_parity_index.py` (`--write-registry` / `--worklist [--newer N]` / `--ci-mode`) + `Docs/REGISTRY_WEB_CLONE_PARITY_INDEX.txt`. COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) § R-WEB-CLONE-CONTENT-DENAIVE-DEPTH (#136) · § R-WEB-CLONE-SCENE-ART-RICHNESS (#137) · § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-FANOUT (#18) · § R-WEB-CLONE-IMMERSION-ALL-PAGES (#105) · `localization.md` § R-WEBCLONE-I18N-PARITY-MATRIX (#157) · `portfolio.md` § R-FABLE-NEXTLEVEL-REVIEW (#74) · § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS · **the 9 expansion-axis programs** (named in the axis list above; owning-program routing via the `gaps` field). Instance of the self-checking-registry pattern applied to cross-axis prioritization.** Founder-direct 2026-09-12; decision `ADR-265`; program #142.

**AMENDMENT — NCPI-DRIVEN REMEDIATION + WEBSITE COVERAGE (R-NCPI-DRIVEN-REMEDIATION; founder-direct 2026-09-13; Program #144 / ADR-268).** NCPI covers every web clone plus the 3 site hubs (`band=site`, site-only axes; clone indices unchanged). **Program #144 is the remediation drain**: worst-and-newest first, each gap routed to its OWNING program's gated drain (`--remediation`), free axes first. NCPI composes, never remediates; never pad; honest-yield; site PRs base=STAGING. `--ci-mode` fails on a regression, on >20 unscored staging clones (R-NCPI-UNIVERSE-FRESH), or when a fleet-wide axis registry (content/immersion/tutor/i18n) misses >20, which scores them as if the axis were N/A (R-NCPI-AXIS-FRESH).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-265_WEB_CLONE_PARITY_INDEX.md` + `Docs/ADR-268_NCPI_DRIVEN_REMEDIATION_AND_SITE_COVERAGE.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_PARITY_INDEX_2026-09-12.md`.
## Prefer Predict-Observe-Explain (predict-before-reveal) when a clone re-renders a deterministic sim/mechanic (R-WEB-CLONE-POE; 2026-07-14)

**When a `/play/<app>` clone re-renders a DETERMINISTIC, model-predictable simulation or mechanic (a phase cycle, an energy-flow graph, a truth table, a physics/parameter model, a rule the learner can reason toward), the DEFAULT framing is a scored Predict-Observe-Explain (POE) loop — the learner commits a prediction BEFORE the outcome is revealed, then reconciles the reveal — NOT a free-play "tap-and-watch" arena.** POE is one of the best-evidenced moves in science-education research (meta-analysis of 35 studies: Hedges' **g ≈ 0.98** on science achievement), and the cognitive engine is **errorful generation + prediction error** (committing a prediction — even a wrong one — then getting feedback encodes the answer better than watching), not "the sim is fun." Free-play iOS SpriteKit sims (and apps like Tinybop) leave this on the table, which is why a POE re-render is the portfolio's reference **web-pioneered → iOS-backport** feature. Full evidence base + design spec + worked examples: **`Docs/RESEARCH_PREDICT_OBSERVE_EXPLAIN_MECHANIC_2026-07-14.md`**. First consumer: `/play/curiosityquest` (Water Cycle predict-the-phase · Food Web predict-the-cascade · Logic Lab predict-then-run).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Seven Theme-Park simulation-loop MECHANICS compose ABOVE shipped shells — calibrated-confidence · causal-replay · two-meters · run-100-times · truth-panel · jagged-assistant · transparency-screen; each honest-yield · on-device · boundary-placed · screenshot-DoD-gated; reference-first then fan out (R-THEME-PARK-SIM-LOOP-MECHANICS; 2026-08-28)

**Seven genuinely-new cross-cutting `/play` MECHANICS lifted from the Hassabis/Theme-Park AI-ed-games research (ADR-208, Program #93), each COMPOSING ABOVE a shipped shell (never replacing it), each a defect on the dark-route footing if it violates the SHARED invariants — HONEST-YIELD (a mechanic is engagement/metacognition/coherence, NEVER a proven learning-gain) · ON-DEVICE / no-PII · BOUNDARY-placed (`R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING` / `R-GUARD-THE-RATIO`, never on the active solve loop) · dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD · NEVER a reward-currency/streak economy (anti-Gimkit, `R-SITE-FEEDBACK`) · the DETERMINISTIC engine stays the TRUTH (the mechanic is additive + fail-safe, no live-generative-worlds, no free-text-to-a-model-for-kids): (1) CALIBRATED-CONFIDENCE — extends `R-WEB-CLONE-POE` with a confidence bet + a running Brier/calibration curve ("how good are your guesses?") + proactive help on two consecutive misses in an item family (a metacognitive-awareness SIGNAL, NEVER a proven quick-outcome — Brier jointly measures calibration+resolution + calibration training needs many trials); (2) CAUSAL-REPLAY — a post-session timeline the learner scrubs, dragging arrows between cause→effect events checked against the deterministic trace (the annotated replay is the learning artifact + parent-visible evidence); (3) TWO-METERS — a dual objective (Profit+Fairness / Speed+Safety / Growth+Habitat) computed from outcomes, making ethics a MECHANIC not a lecture; (4) RUN-100-TIMES — a stochastic Monte-Carlo ensemble/variability UI over a seeded engine (predict the spread → run once → run 100 → compare; variability literacy from age 8); (5) TRUTH-PANEL — a visible seam where a sim/generated/historical surface departs from the real record (the model≠world / AI-hallucination lesson; composes DN + honest-yield + the AI-safety moat); (6) JAGGED-ASSISTANT — an AI-literacy mode where an in-game helper is brilliant-on-hard/wrong-on-easy → teaching calibrated TRUST in AI (WHEN to trust it; PRE-AUTHORED + human-reviewed, nothing generates live; composes `R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TUTOR` + `R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING`; since ADR-320 D2 any CHECK scores as wise, calibration is an unscored summary line via `trustSummaryHtml`, and no copy says checking wastes effort — guard `check_honest_numbers.py`); (7) TRANSPARENCY-SCREEN — a parent-facing "what this app knows about your child + every AI line logged, nothing persisted beyond progress" surface (composes ADR-176 dashboards + the COPPA on-device posture). Each is REFERENCE-FIRST-then-fan-out; a fleet-fanning shared `_shared/` shell gets an adversarial review (`R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA`). Tracked worst-first by `scripts/check_theme_park_lift_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_THEME_PARK_LIFT.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN).** Founder-direct 2026-08-28; decision `ADR-208`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-208_THEME_PARK_SIMULATION_LOOP_LIFT.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_THEME_PARK_AI_EDGAMES_LIFT_2026-08-28.md`.
## Practice-scheduling is the 7th expansion axis — spaced retrieval + interleaving + edge-of-competence, ON-DEVICE + calm-rails (R-WEB-CLONE-PRACTICE-SCHEDULING; 2026-07-18)

**A `/play/<app>` clone's EXPANSION includes a distinct 7th axis — PRACTICE-SCHEDULING / MASTERY SEQUENCING: the cross-item, cross-session lever governing WHICH item the learner sees next + WHEN a learned item RESURFACES (spaced retrieval + interleaved "Mixed practice" + edge-of-competence next-item selection). It is ORTHOGONAL to the manipulative "assessment depth"/"bank depth" (those are within-surface + within-session); it is realized ON-DEVICE (`localStorage`, no identifier → COPPA-trivial); and it MUST be calm-rails (no due-count dread / streak-guilt / gating). This is the **Return Rule** (ADR-277). A discrete-item clone (MC-kit / manipulative-POE bank) with no resurfacing/interleaving/adaptive-next is a parity gap; a solo-creative-tool / solo-reflection clone waives it with a one-line rationale.** Codified per founder-greenlight 2026-07-18 (add a full 7th expansion axis) on the evidence in `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_EXPANSION_ADDITIONAL_AXES_2026-07-17.md` (ADR-048 amendment). This is the sequencing sibling of `R-WEB-CLONE-POE` (a mechanic) + `R-WEB-CLONE-CRA-LADDER` (representation breadth) — the same expansion program, a different lever.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Mastery-progression is the 8th expansion axis — intrapersonal progress + achievements + goals, ON-DEVICE + calm-rails, NO leaderboards/currency/streak-guilt (R-WEB-CLONE-MASTERY-PROGRESSION; 2026-07-18)

**A `/play/<app>` clone's 8th expansion axis — MASTERY-PROGRESSION / GOAL & ACHIEVEMENT: a clone-wide, cross-session, intrapersonal sense of accomplishment + reason to return — a calm progress bar (XP-from-first-try-mastery), an achievements shelf keyed to LEARNING milestones, optional learner-set goals / personal-best ("beat YOUR best"). Realized ON-DEVICE (`localStorage`, no identifier → COPPA-trivial); DEFINED by its EXCLUSION of the maladaptive forms — NO leaderboards / currency / streak-guilt / scarcity. A discrete/gradeable clone with no progress/achievement/goal layer is a parity gap; a solo-reflection/SEL clone ⛔-waives it; a BESPOKE-STORE / bespoke-adaptive clone ⛔-waives it (wiring `<MasteryProgress namespace>` to a non-`${ns}.progress.v1` store renders an empty card = a defect).** (Verify-before-waiving recipe → REFERENCE.)

**R-NO-RETURN-BADGE:** no badge for returning; retired ones stay readable (`check-return-badges.mjs`).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Scaffolded hint-ladder is the 9th expansion axis — articulate-before-hint · PROGRESSIVE multi-level hints · FADED worked example · misconception-targeted remediation, ON-DEVICE (R-WEB-CLONE-SCAFFOLDED-HINTS; 2026-07-18)

**A `/play/<app>` clone's EXPANSION includes a distinct 9th axis — SCAFFOLDED HINT-LADDER: the in-round pedagogy lever that turns a stuck attempt into a PROGRESSIVE, FADED scaffold instead of a one-shot hint. The web round shells (`_shared/mcRound`/`customRound`) shipped one-shot elaborated feedback + a single hint; this axis adds a shared `_shared/scaffold.ts` engine that drives (i) an ARTICULATE-BEFORE-HINT gate (no hint before the first wrong attempt), (ii) PROGRESSIVE multi-level hints (one rung per wrong attempt, least→most revealing), (iii) an optional FADED WORKED EXAMPLE as the terminal rung before the answer is revealed, and (iv) optional per-wrong-option MISCONCEPTION-targeted remediation. It is realized ON-DEVICE (pure TS, no PII, no AI evaluator → COPPA-trivial) and the portfolio ALREADY owns the engine on iOS (`ForgePedagogy`: `PolyaScaffold` · `WorkedExampleProvider` · `MisconceptionDetector`).** A discrete-item archetype (MC-kit · manipulative/POE bank) whose wrong-answer path is a single hint → generic reveal is a DEPTH gap; a solo-creative-tool / solo-reflection clone with no gradeable item ⛔-waives it (one line). Codified per founder-greenlight 2026-07-18 (ForgeKit-mining wave, ADR-048 Decision 9) on `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_EXPANSION_FORGEKIT_MINING_2026-07-18.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The Teachable Cast is a REUSABLE shared shell — the learner TEACHES a novice cast member; adopt it, never hand-roll (R-WEB-CLONE-TEACHABLE-CAST; 2026-07-21)

**The web impl of `R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY` (`distributed-narrative.md`) is a SHARED shell — the deterministic `src/lib/play/_shared/teachableAgent.ts` engine + the reusable `src/components/play/TeachableCast.astro` component — that a clone opts into with a per-app `TeachConfig`, exactly like `passAndPlay`/`affect`/`agency`/`scaffold`. A clone that wants an AI-mentor surface MUST reuse this shell (never hand-roll a per-clone teachable-agent), and the shell's integrity invariants are enforced BY CONSTRUCTION.** The frame: the learner is the TEACHER, a novice cast member is "still learning" the primitive it embodies (R-DN-PARITY), and the learner's teaching IS the practice (protégé effect; the antidote to metacognitive laziness). Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-21 (R1 pilot, `RECOMMENDATION_PORTFOLIO_INNOVATION_NEXT_BETS_2026-07-20.md` + `PLAN_TEACHABLE_CAST_PILOT_R1_2026-07-20.md`).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The 15–18 adapted-DN-S is a SHARED text-forward CASE-NARRATIVE shell — realistic personas + decision→consequence→reflect, ZERO illustrations; adopt it, never hand-roll (R-WEB-CLONE-CASE-NARRATIVE; 2026-07-22)

**The web impl of the 15–18 band's ADAPTED DN-S (`distributed-narrative.md` § R-OLDER-TEEN-DN-ADAPTED; the mature analog of the tween `/cast` illustrated chapter-books + `/story`) is a SHARED shell — the deterministic `src/lib/play/_shared/caseNarrative.ts` engine + the reusable `src/components/play/CaseNarrative.astro` component — that a 15–18 clone opts into with a per-app `CaseConfig`, exactly like `TeachableCast`/`passAndPlay`. A 15–18 clone wanting a DN-S/story surface MUST reuse this shell (never hand-roll a per-clone case surface), and the shell's invariants are enforced BY CONSTRUCTION (`auditCase` throws at build on a violation).** Decision: `ADR-066`; research: `RESEARCH_OLDER_TEEN_ADAPTED_DN_S_2026-07-22.md`; plan: `PLAN_OLDER_TEEN_ADAPTED_DN_S_2026-07-22.md`. Pilot SHIPPED: LedgerQuest/Priya (compound interest), site PR #1225.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
**R-CASE-NO-ANSWER-IN-SETUP (2026-10-02, P#205 §S12):** a case's beats give data and each persona's rule, never THIS case's result before its decision (reveal-before-retrieval = defect). Every case is judged in `REGISTRY_CASE_ANSWER_LEAK.txt`; guard `check_case_answer_leak.py`. → REFERENCE § R-CASE-NO-ANSWER-IN-SETUP.

## Ensemble Teach-the-Cast is a SHARED shell — teach the cast's CONNECTIONS (node→edge→map): a PAIR (the edge) → a whole-cast CAPSTONE (jigsaw); adopt it, never hand-roll (R-WEB-CLONE-ENSEMBLE-TEACH; 2026-07-22)

**The ENSEMBLE layer over the shipped single Teach-the-Cast (§ R-WEB-CLONE-TEACHABLE-CAST) is a SHARED shell — the deterministic `src/lib/play/_shared/ensembleTeach.ts` engine + the reusable `src/components/play/EnsembleTeach.astro` component — that a clone opts into with a per-app `EnsembleConfig`. Where the single teach is a NODE (teach ONE novice ONE primitive), the ensemble teaches the cast's CONNECTIONS: a PAIR = the EDGE (how two primitives RELATE — the higher-transfer act) → the whole-cast CAPSTONE (an authentic integrative problem where each cast member contributes ONE primitive — jigsaw). A clone wanting ensemble/connection/capstone teaching MUST reuse this shell (never hand-roll), and the invariants are enforced BY CONSTRUCTION (`auditPair`/`auditCapstone`/`auditEnsemble` throw at build on a violation).** Decision: `ADR-067`; research: `RESEARCH_ENSEMBLE_TEACH_THE_CAST_2026-07-22.md` (protégé effect · teaching-relationships→transfer · Betty's-Brain concept-map [multi-agent = NOVEL] · jigsaw d≈0.59 · capstone synthesis). Pilot SHIPPED: FractionForge (2 pairs + a bake-sale capstone), site PR #1228.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Teach-the-Cast is measured + deepened on the ICAP ladder — the learner must CONSTRUCT (build/order/demonstrate), not just pick from a menu; a menu-only teach surface is ICAP-Active (crude), tracked worst-first (R-WEB-CLONE-TEACH-DEPTH; 2026-08-15)

**A `/play/<app>` Teach-the-Cast surface's DEPTH is graded on Chi & Wylie's ICAP ladder (Passive<Active<Constructive<Interactive; biggest jump Active→Constructive). A 1-of-2 menu-pick teach surface is ICAP-**Active** (crude); a deep one is **Constructive** — the learner GENERATES a teaching artifact via a deterministic NO-FREE-TEXT build act (`TeachConfig.constructiveAct`: buildable causal MAP the novice reasons over · order-steps · demonstrate-on-manipulative), the novice's error DERIVED from that construction (Betty's-Brain) — and, founder-gated, **Interactive** (adaptive on-device-LLM novice, ADR-065). Kill the single-`plannedMisconception` default (author `spec.misconceptions[]` ≥2, band-calibrated). ELEVATES (never replaces) § R-WEB-CLONE-TEACHABLE-CAST/-ENSEMBLE-TEACH/-VISUAL-FIRST; every R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY invariant holds BY CONSTRUCTION (no answer-handover, construct-before-hint, boundary-placed, on-device/COPPA, `auditConstructiveAct` throws). A menu-only surface, single-slip quiz, or LLM-without-a-Constructive-floor (INT-NO-FLOOR) is a tracked DEPTH gap; a padded fake act is a defect. Tracked worst-first (A0<A1<C<I) by `check_teachable_cast_depth.py` + `REGISTRY_TEACHABLE_CAST_DEPTH.txt` (SHAPE-B).** Founder-direct 2026-08-15; decision `ADR-142`; program #45. **✅ DRAIN COMPLETE 2026-08-16 — the whole 146-clone fleet is ICAP-Interactive (0 A0/A1/C · 146 I); Phase-4 shipped the CONSTRUCTION-AWARE novice under a SUCCESS-ONLY answer-leak boundary (a wrong-build verdict is kept verbatim + never sent to the LLM — a blacklist is an unwinnable paraphrase arms race; enrich only the success path). Detail → `ADR-142` § Phase-4 record.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-142_TEACHABLE_CAST_DEPTH_PROGRAM.md` + `Docs/PLAN_TEACHABLE_CAST_DEPTH_PROGRAM_2026-08-15.md` + `RESEARCH_TEACHABLE_CAST_DEPTH_MAXIMIZATION_2026-08-15.md`.
## The AI tutor is MAXIMIZED as a Socratic, answer-WITHHOLDING, adaptive-scaffolding tutor fleet-wide — NEVER an on-demand answer chatbot (a proven learning-penalty HARM), on-device, integrity-first (R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TUTOR; 2026-08-15)

**"Maximize AI tutor" across teach-the-cast + the whole `/play` fleet means maximizing the PEDAGOGICALLY-CONSTRAINED levers — L1 the deterministic scaffold FLOOR fleet-wide (articulate-before-hint → progressive faded hints → faded worked example → misconception remediation, R-WEB-CLONE-SCAFFOLDED-HINTS axis 9, adopted by ~7 clones today) · L2 Socratic "tutor asks, you answer" (choice-framed, NO free text, with response-scaffolds so answers aren't shallow) · L3 elaborated per-distractor misconception feedback (R-WEB-CLONE-QA-PROMINENCE) · L4 the adaptive on-device-LLM tutor tier (opt-in/default-OFF, FOUNDER-GATED to widen, `vetEnriched` extended so it CANNOT leak the answer) — NEVER an on-demand-answer chatbot. The 2026 evidence is decisive + design-dependent: on-demand-answer tutors cause a measurable, durable "learning penalty" (Wharton: HALF the gains, harm persists even when learners know it; children amplified-risk), while Socratic answer-WITHHOLDING scaffolding teaches — so an answer-on-demand surface is a defect on the dark-route footing that FORFEITS the AI-safety moat (§ R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING), and this VALIDATES + STRENGTHENS § R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY (can't-hand-over-the-answer, non-companion, on-device, no-free-text-to-a-model-for-kids). The deterministic floor stays load-bearing (LLM OFF ⇒ fully functional); the tutor is boundary-placed (§ R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING), defends productive struggle (no "just ask the AI" shortcut). Tracked worst-first (T0 none < T1 deterministic floor < T2 adaptive LLM) by `check_ai_tutor_coverage.py` + `REGISTRY_AI_TUTOR_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B; baseline 2026-08-15 = 193 clones, 28 T0 / 162 T1 / 3 T2).** Founder-direct 2026-08-15; decision `ADR-143`; program #46.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-143_AI_TUTOR_MAXIMIZATION_PROGRAM.md` + `RESEARCH_AI_TUTOR_MAXIMIZATION_2026-08-15.md`.
## User-uploaded study material → AI-generated learning + Socratic-tutoring + PRINTABLE-PDF resources runs on a SERVER-MEDIATED Claude-API backend, uploads EPHEMERAL/zero-retention, tutor answer-WITHHOLDING over a deterministic grader-FLOOR, items VETTED+engine-verified, ADHD-first, honest-yield (R-DOC-TO-LEARNING-RESOURCES; 2026-08-21)

**Any surface that ingests a USER-UPLOADED document/photo/pasted text (PDF · textbook page · notes · worksheet) and generates LEARNING RESOURCES from it — interactive quiz · flashcards · study guide · Socratic tutoring · printable PDF — MUST obey nine hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) SERVER-MEDIATED Claude API via ONE hub-owned Cloudflare Worker (`spark-anvil-tutor`; R-CLOUDFLARE-BACKEND-PROVISIONING) serving BOTH web + iOS — `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is a CF SECRET, NEVER a client key, NEVER committed; model = `claude-fable-5` (founder "best Claude model"; `claude-opus-4-8` = documented FOUNDER-GATED cost fallback AND the **REFUSAL BACKSTOP** — on a Fable-5 `stop_reason:refusal` false-positive on a benign topic, retry the SAME request on `claude-opus-4-8` [`fallbacks`/fallback-credit], and only if BOTH decline return a safe "try rephrasing" message, never a raw refusal to a kid; never a silent downgrade — ADR-179 D5); deploy = FOUNDER CF-account step (R-CLOUDFLARE-SCOPED-DEPLOY). [Fable-5 API specifics — omit `thinking`, no sampling params, `output_config.format` json_schema, vision+PDF blocks, SSE, `refusal` handling, org ≥30-day-retention/no-ZDR — → ADR-177. TYPED-TOPIC input (CQ parity) is allowed under a 7-layer guardrail stack + Socratic + ephemeral (source-grounded upload stays the safer default); LIFT+BEAT CQ's prompting (structured schema · per-grade reading level · standards-map) with Fable-5 + Haladyna item-rubric + deterministic engine-verify; Teach-the-Cast adopted at ICAP-Interactive; study-topic→fleet-clone deep-link via § R-STUDY-TOPIC-CLONE-DEEPLINK — all → ADR-179.] (2) UPLOADS ARE EPHEMERAL — processed in-request, NEVER stored/retained/logged on our side; NO PII off-device beyond the transient call; NO per-child telemetry (COPPA; R-SITE-FEEDBACK / R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE; Plausible stays the only cookieless analytics). (3) SOCRATIC ANSWER-WITHHOLDING tutor ONLY — never an on-demand answer bot (the measured, durable learning-penalty HARM; forfeits the AI-safety moat); a DETERMINISTIC engine is the GRADER + fail-safe FLOOR (the LLM scaffolds, NEVER adjudicates correctness — it is "a poor judge of understanding"); a `vetEnriched`-style guard ensures tutor text CANNOT leak the answer (R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TUTOR / R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY). (4) GENERATED ITEMS VETTED BEFORE DISPLAY — safety-screened + (gradeable) engine-verified (AI cards can be wrong; R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-ENGINE-VERIFIED / QA-prominence). (5) PDF OUTPUT is a FIRST-CLASS deliverable — downloadable, print-clean worksheet / flashcard-sheet / study-guide PDFs, client-rendered (no server dependency for the PDF itself), boundary-placed (founder-direct 2026-08-21 "make sure PDF output is supported"). (6) ADHD-FIRST delivery — the EF-scaffold backbone (chunking · one-task-per-screen · pause/save/resume · gentle visual timers never stress-timers · calm rails · NO dark patterns; R-EXECUTIVE-FUNCTION-SCAFFOLD) + the Reading-Access backbone (R-COGNITIVE-ACCESSIBILITY) are INHERITED, not re-invented. (7) HONEST-YIELD marketing — retrieval-practice / reduced-setup-friction framing; NEVER a proven-outcome or "closes the ADHD gap" claim; market the Socratic / answer-withholding / no-data-retention / no-tracking ARCHITECTURE, never "runs on your device" (the calls are server-mediated); composes with R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING. (8) The `/play/<app>` clone stays per-app-namespaced (ADR-033), tests + dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD gated, site PRs base=STAGING (ADR-154); iOS is a Swift HANDOFF (hub NEVER writes Swift). (9) ON-DEVICE ACTIVITY/INPUT/OUTPUT RECORDING — every learner ACTIVITY + the INPUTS (uploaded materials / topics, kept as the learner's own content) + the generated OUTPUTS (quizzes · flashcards · study guides · PDFs) are RECORDED ON-DEVICE (localStorage/IndexedDB, globally-UNIQUE namespace per R-WEB-CLONE-PROGRESS-NS-UNIQUE, no identifier/account/PII/network) and ACCESSIBLE LATER via a "My study library" surface (re-open · resume · re-study · re-download the PDF). This is DEVICE-LOCAL persistence and is NOT in tension with invariant (2): the SERVER retains NOTHING; the DEVICE keeps the learner's own library, nothing transmitted. Composes with axis-8 mastery-progression + axis-7 practice-scheduling + the records/dashboards program (q-f977d7 / R-SITE-UNIFIED-DASHBOARDS): TutorForge's library store is READ by the device-wide records aggregator, never re-implemented. (10) CROSS-MATERIAL SOURCE-GROUNDING (ADR-177 addendum, W-FP #4) — when a pack is built from MULTIPLE user sources, each generated item CITES which source it came from (a per-item "from:" vet chip + a "built from your sources" synthesis banner), via DETERMINISTIC PII-FREE positional labels (`your notes`/`your PDF`/`photo N`; NEVER a transmitted filename — inv2) + OPTIONAL additive schema fields (per-item `source` + pack `sources`/`sourceSummary`, so a topic-only pack / old client degrades gracefully); source-grounding is a TRUST / hallucination-reduction / VET aid (an instance of inv4), DISPLAY-ONLY — the deterministic engine stays the grader of record (inv3), never a correctness/score signal; guard `scripts/check_source_grounding_guard.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_SOURCE_GROUNDING_GUARD.txt` (SHAPE-B). A client-side key, a retained/logged SERVER-side upload, an answer-GIVING tutor, an unvetted generated item, a missing PDF-output path, a lost/un-re-openable on-device activity/output, an over-claim, OR a transmitted-filename / correctness-signalling source citation is a defect.
