GENERALIZES to ANY future document-ingesting learning surface, not just TutorForge.** Founder-direct 2026-08-21 (spawn TutorForge — level up CuriosityQuest's PDF/image→quiz into rich ADHD-first learning + Socratic-tutoring + printout resources on a Claude-API backend + "use the best Claude model" + "make sure PDF output is supported"). Decision `ADR-177`; program #63.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-177_TUTORFORGE_DOC_TO_LEARNING_RESOURCES.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_TUTORFORGE_DOC_TO_LEARNING_2026-08-21.md` + `Docs/CONCEPT_SPAWN_TUTORFORGE_2026-08-21.md` + `Docs/PLAN_TUTORFORGE_PROGRAM_2026-08-21.md`. Tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_TUTORFORGE_PROGRAM.txt` + `scripts/check_tutorforge_program_coverage.py` (SHAPE-B WARN).
## A study/topic surface DEEP-LINKS to relevant fleet `/play` clones by TOPIC via the glob-derived search alias index + the curated transfer map — knowledge-graph-by-topic, on-device, no per-child profile, LIVE routes only, gentle (R-STUDY-TOPIC-CLONE-DEEPLINK; 2026-08-21)

**Any surface that knows what a learner is STUDYING (a detected topic from their materials OR a typed topic — e.g. TutorForge) MAY deep-link them to a relevant fleet `/play` clone to PRACTICE it ("studied fractions → `/play/fractionforge`") — intrinsic integration + the cross-app transfer thesis — under six hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) REUSE, never reinvent — resolve topic→clone via the GLOB-DERIVED `R-SITE-SEARCH` alias/synonym index (self-updating, never a hand-list) + the curated `R-WEB-CLONE-TRANSFER-MAP` edges; NO new matcher; (2) knowledge-graph-by-TOPIC, NOT user-PROFILING — no per-child topic profile, no persistent identifier, no telemetry (COPPA; R-SITE-FEEDBACK); topic detection is ON-DEVICE; (3) LIVE routes only — deep-link a `planned`/absent clone is a dark link (fail-closed, resolve against shipped `/play/<app>` routes; R-WEB-CLONE-NO-DARK-SURFACE); (4) BAND-APPROPRIATE — link to a clone in the learner's age band (R-WEB-CLONE-AGE-BAND-ZONES), never cross-band; (5) GENTLE / honest-yield — "practice this here" suggestion, boundary-placed, dismissible, NEVER forced/interrupting/auto-redirecting, no dark pattern (R-SITE-FEEDBACK); (6) accessible (dark-safe · ≥44px · dual-scheme screenshot-DoD). The SAME topic→clone resolution ALSO yields the topic's SUBJECT-CAST character (that clone's `dnCast.members[]`) for a **Teach-the-Cast novice** — teach the FLEET character who embodies the subject (protégé effect; reuse the shipped `/cast/<app>/<char>.webp` portrait = ZERO paid gen), R-DN-PARITY-preserved · band-appropriate · boundary-only (ADR-179 D3), with TutorForge's own learning-science study-coach cast as the no-match fallback + meta-learning novice. GENERALIZES fleet-wide (any study/topic/results surface can adopt it). A hand-list matcher, a per-child topic profile, a dark-route deep-link, a cross-band link, or a forced redirect is a defect.** Founder-direct 2026-08-21 ("should we deep-link to fleet webclones based on the topics users study? codify/track/guard/generalize"). Decision `ADR-179` D2; tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_STUDY_TOPIC_DEEPLINK.txt` + `scripts/check_study_topic_deeplink_coverage.py` (SHAPE-B WARN). Composes `R-SITE-SEARCH` · `R-WEB-CLONE-TRANSFER-MAP` · `R-DOC-TO-LEARNING-RESOURCES`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-179_TUTORFORGE_TOPIC_DEEPLINK_TEACHCAST_PROMPTING_GUARDRAILS.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_TUTORFORGE_TOPIC_INPUT_DEEPLINK_TEACHCAST_2026-08-21.md`.
## The `/teach` page presents its stacked teach islands as PROGRESSIVE-DISCLOSURE TABS — one mode at a time via the shared `<TeachModes>`; NEVER stack ≥2 heavy teach islands all-visible-at-once (R-WEB-CLONE-TEACH-MODES-DISCLOSURE; 2026-07-24)

**A `/play/<app>/teach` page that mounts ≥2 of the heavy teach islands — `<TeachableCast>` (`pc-cast`) · `<EnsembleTeach>` (`pc-ens`) · `<ConceptMap>` (`pc-cmap`) · `<TeachReflection>` (`pc-reflect`) — MUST present them via the SHARED `src/components/play/TeachModes.astro` progressive-disclosure wrapper (an accessible tablist, ONE mode visible at a time), NOT stacked all-visible-at-once. Adopt it with ONE inserted line (`<TeachModes />` after the page intro, before the island mounts); NEVER hand-roll a per-clone tab/accordion, and NEVER leave ≥2 heavy islands stacked (that is the founder-flagged overwhelming-teach defect — the R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD-COMPOSITE trap: each island passes its own screenshot-DoD while the composed page is cumulatively too dense).** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-24 (*"the teach the cast, teach the connection, teach the ensemble content is overwhelming, confusing … layout/spacing/navigation issues"*); decision `ADR-077`; research `RESEARCH_TEACH_SURFACE_UX_2026-07-24.md` (progressive/staged disclosure reduces EXTRANEOUS cognitive load — Sweller CLT; ≤4 parallel equal-weight heavy sections done one-at-a-time → tabs are the correct pattern, NN/G — NOT a vertical stack, NOT a deep accordion). Pilot SHIPPED: fractionforge (site #1331).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The website + every `/play` clone stages information by PROGRESSIVE DISCLOSURE to avoid overwhelm — level-1 = the primary task + summary (self-sufficient), genuinely-secondary/low-frequency content deferred behind a clear affordance (tabs=`<TeachModes>` · native `<details>`=`<DisclosureSection>`), band-appropriate density; NEVER hide the primary task/manipulative, crisis footer, privacy link, or CTA (that is the "sneaking" dark pattern); a first-class MANDATORY UI/UX review axis in Program #74 (R-WEB-PROGRESSIVE-DISCLOSURE; 2026-08-27)

**The website + every `/play/<app>` clone MUST stage information by progressive disclosure so no surface OVERWHELMS: level-1 shows the PRIMARY task + summary and is SELF-SUFFICIENT (a learner completes ~80% of the task without opening anything, NN/g); genuinely-secondary, LOW-FREQUENCY content (the stacked "more ways to practice" tiles · the ≥2nd teach mode · per-question breakdowns · dense marketing/method detail · the long cast/story rails below the primary rail · deep settings) is DEFERRED behind a clear disclosure affordance — TABS (`<TeachModes>`, ADR-077, the multi-mode case) or native `<details>`/`<summary>` (the shared `<DisclosureSection>`, a dense section) — with density held to the working-memory BAND CEILING (3–5/6–8 = ~1–2 parallel options, visual-first · 9–14 = ≤~4–5 · 15–18 = ≤~6). SEVEN hard guardrails, each a defect on the failed-dark-mode footing: (1) REVEAL on demand, NEVER HIDE the decision/safety-critical — the primary task/manipulative, the SEL/crisis-support footer, the COPPA/privacy link, required trust/legal content, and the primary CTA stay on LEVEL 1 ALWAYS (concealing relevant content = Gray-et-al. 2018 "sneaking," a dark pattern judged by EFFECT not intent — UX-Tigers "decision-critical belongs on level 1"); (2) level-1 self-sufficient (~80%), split deferrals by FREQUENCY-of-use, never by "it looks cluttered"; (3) accessible BY CONSTRUCTION — native `<details>`/`<summary>` FIRST (no JS/ARIA), else a real `<button>` + synced `aria-expanded` + heading-wrapped accordion headers + keyboard + visible focus; NEVER the `<div onClick>` anti-pattern; collapsed content NOT reachable by the virtual cursor; reduced-motion-safe; dark-safe `--sa-*`/`--ff-*`/`--pc-*`; ≥44px trigger; correct disclosure-vs-accordion pattern (WAI-ARIA APG); (4) never OVER-layer ("click fatigue") — clear signifiers + information scent + CONCRETE labels ("Show all 8 practice modes", never a bare "More"); (5) the manipulative + the Q→A→feedback flow stay PROMINENT — PD defers boundary/secondary chrome, never the active solve loop (R-GUARD-THE-RATIO / R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING); (6) HONEST-YIELD — "calmer / lower extraneous load / clearer," NEVER a proven learning-gain (the 2026 Anik-&-Bunt study measured PERCEIVED learning + satisfaction/error-rate); (7) FIRST-CLASS MANDATORY UI/UX review AXIS in the Program-#74 Fable prompt — every future review of the site + every clone judges density + staging (the self-perpetuating mechanism). GENERALIZES ADR-077 `<TeachModes>` (teach page) to the WHOLE fleet + iOS-by-handoff (a `ForgeUI` progressive-disclosure spec; hub never writes Swift). Tracked `scripts/check_progressive_disclosure_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_PROGRESSIVE_DISCLOSURE_COVERAGE.txt` (auto-discovers site+clone landings from origin/staging; **live tallies in the registry** — the enumerated dense-marketing/hub universe is itself a coverage surface, kept complete, detail → ADR-203). TWO guard axes: density-tier (SHAPE-B WARN, regression-only) + the **ADR-203 §1 "no-sneaking" HARD axis** — a nesting-aware scan makes guardrail (1) DETERMINISTIC: any crisis-footer/privacy-link hidden INSIDE a `<details>`/`<DisclosureSection>` = a defect (baseline 0, `--ci-mode` FAILS on ANY; detail → ADR-203). The shared `<DisclosureSection>` v2 (`{count}` label-interpolation + `heading` APG accordion-set prop) + `<TeachModes>` `data-teach-mode` open-discovery + the bare/orphan-label lint `scripts/check_disclosure_labels.py` (`REGISTRY_DISCLOSURE_LABELS.txt`, SHAPE-B) convert §3+§4 (concrete-label + accordion-set shape) from author-prose into the type surface + a machine check — pick the primitive by SHAPE (2–4 parallel heavy modes→tabs · 1 dense block→a DisclosureSection · a set→`heading`; ADR-203 § Authoring-convention). Composes ABOVE (never re-litigates) R-WEB-CLONE-TEACH-MODES-DISCLOSURE · R-COGNITIVE-ACCESSIBILITY · R-SITE-3HUB-IA · R-WEB-CLONE-NAV-LINK-HYGIENE · R-WEB-CLONE-SIDE-PANEL-FIT · R-SITE-PAGE-WEIGHT-BUDGET. Verdict = the dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD (R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD).** Founder-direct 2026-08-27; decision `ADR-203` (Program #89, added to Program #74 / ADR-188 Amendment 8); deep research `RESEARCH_PROGRESSIVE_DISCLOSURE_SITE_WEBCLONE_2026-08-27.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-203_PROGRESSIVE_DISCLOSURE_NEXTLEVEL_DIMENSION.md` + `Docs/PLAN_PROGRESSIVE_DISCLOSURE_PROGRAM_2026-08-27.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_PROGRESSIVE_DISCLOSURE_SITE_WEBCLONE_2026-08-27.md`.
## Standards-mapped parent/educator report is the 10th expansion axis — the FIRST adult-facing axis, ON-DEVICE + export-on-demand, NO accounts/server/PII (R-WEB-CLONE-PROGRESS-REPORT; 2026-07-18)

**A `/play/<app>` clone's EXPANSION includes a distinct 10th axis — the FIRST ADULT-facing one — a STANDARDS-MAPPED PROGRESS REPORT: a read-only "how it's going" view for a parent/educator + an EXPORT-ON-DEMAND (CSV / print), DERIVED ON-DEVICE from the SAME `${ns}.progress.v1` store the learner-facing axes (7/8) write. It is DEFINED by COPPA-avoidance-by-construction: NO accounts, NO server, NO hosted dashboard, NO PII off-device — a private, on-device view + a user-triggered export.** Every other axis is learner-facing; a clone has no adult-facing surface, though the site has a `/for-parents-educators` hub + per-app standards mapping + grade/level chips, and iOS owns the engine (`ForgeReporting`: `parentConferenceReport` · `standardsCSV` · `strengths`/`growthAreas`/`recommendations` · `StandardProficiency`). A discrete/gradeable clone (MC-kit · manipulative/POE bank) writing the shared `makeProgress('ns')` store with no adult report is a parity gap; a solo-creative/reflection clone or a bespoke-store clone ⛔-waives it (the SAME eligibility criterion as axes 7/8 — it reads `${ns}.progress.v1`; a bespoke IndexedDB store → an empty report = a defect, so verify `makeProgress('<ns>')` + a `kits-index.ts` before wiring). Codified per founder-greenlight 2026-07-18 (ForgeKit-mining wave, ADR-048 Decision 10) on `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_EXPANSION_FORGEKIT_MINING_2026-07-18.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The fleet ships TWO unified on-device dashboards — a LEARNER dashboard (`/play/me` "My Journey") + a PARENT/EDUCATOR dashboard (`/for-parents-educators/dashboard` "Family Dashboard") — aggregating every clone's on-device store; no account/PII/server, export + DELETE-ALL, honest-yield (R-SITE-UNIFIED-DASHBOARDS; 2026-08-21)

**The `/play` fleet MUST surface a learner's cross-clone activities/accomplishments in TWO unified, on-device dashboards, each a defect on the dark-route footing if missing/violated: (1) a LEARNER dashboard `/play/me` ("My Journey") + (2) a PARENT/EDUCATOR dashboard `/for-parents-educators/dashboard` ("Family Dashboard"). BOTH are built ENTIRELY from the per-clone `*.progress.v1`/`*.records.v1` stores already on the device via the shared PURE, READ-ONLY `src/lib/play/_shared/records.ts` `deviceRecords()` aggregator — so per-clone `progress.ts` is NEVER mutated. Six hard invariants: (a) ON-DEVICE ONLY — no account/server/identifier/PII off-device (strongest-COPPA; no cross-device sync — export-on-demand is the portability path); (b) the family dashboard carries CSV/print EXPORT + a prominent "Delete all on-device data" (the COPPA parental-deletion right, local); (c) HONEST-YIELD — visibility-of-growth / agency / reflection / actionable next-step FEEDBACK, NEVER a proven learning-gain claim (2024 LAK: engagement is the signal, only WITH feedback); (d) NON-COMPARATIVE / INFORMATIONAL — surfaces existing learning-keyed achievements + calm "days active"; NO leaderboard/currency/ranking/streak-guilt (composes § R-WEB-CLONE-MASTERY-PROGRESSION + § R-SITE-FEEDBACK, invents no economy); (e) dark-safe + WCAG 2.2 AA + reduced-motion + age-band-aware, verdict = dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD; (f) reachable (Play nav / `/for-parents-educators` + every eligible clone's CloneNav links `/play/me`). CROSS-CLONE sibling of axis-8 (learner) + axis-10 (parent/educator) — those are PER-CLONE, this is the device-wide view. iOS parity = a `HANDOFF_FROM_HUB_*` ForgePortal/ForgeReporting spec (hub never writes Swift). Tracked `scripts/check_records_dashboards_coverage.py` + `REGISTRY_RECORDS_DASHBOARDS_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN).** Founder-direct 2026-08-21; `ADR-176`; program OVERVIEW #61.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-176_ON_DEVICE_RECORDS_AND_UNIFIED_DASHBOARDS.md` + `Docs/PLAN_LEARNING_RECORDS_FAMILY_DASHBOARDS_PROGRAM_2026-08-21.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_ON_DEVICE_RECORDS_AND_DASHBOARDS_2026-08-21.md` + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A clone RECORDS every learning-relevant accomplishment on-device, not just MC-practice — reflections · teach-the-cast · stories/chapters read · books · POE · creation-mode builds, via the additive `recordActivity()` companion store; a shipped-but-unrecorded accomplishment is a silent gap (R-WEB-CLONE-ACTIVITY-RECORD-COMPLETENESS; 2026-08-21)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone MUST record its learner's learning-relevant ACCOMPLISHMENTS on-device — not just the MC-practice `<ns>.progress.v1` already captures. Accomplishments beyond MC (reflection completed · teach-the-cast/ensemble completion · story/chapter read [incl. a 15-18 `<CaseNarrative>` case, `progressNs` opt-in] · book opened · POE prediction committed · **creation-mode build** on an ADR-248/266 maker clone → the `'create'` kind, #144) are recorded via the shared ADDITIVE `recordActivity(ns, kind)` helper writing a companion `<ns>.records.v1` key — NEVER mutating `.progress.v1` (byte-safe against any concurrent per-clone `progress.astro` work). A shipped accomplishment surface whose completion is not recorded is a silent gap: it can't appear in the unified dashboards (§ R-SITE-UNIFIED-DASHBOARDS) and the on-device record is incomplete — a defect on the dark-surface footing. On-device only, no identifier/PII (inherits axis-8/10 + § R-SITE-FEEDBACK). Eligibility mirrors axes 7/8/10; a solo-tool / bespoke-store / younger-no-MC clone may carry a `# RECORDS-EXCLUDED:` line. Tracked worst-first by the `records:` axis of `REGISTRY_RECORDS_DASHBOARDS_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN).** Founder-direct 2026-08-21; `ADR-176`. The "shipped ≠ recorded" sibling of § Asset Consumer Audit ("registered ≠ wired") + § R-WEB-CLONE-NARRATIVE-INTEGRATION ("authored ≠ integrated").

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-176_ON_DEVICE_RECORDS_AND_UNIFIED_DASHBOARDS.md` + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Affect / self-regulation check-in is the 11th expansion axis — GENTLE session-start check-in + co-regulation off-ramp, ON-DEVICE, NO mood-surveillance (R-WEB-CLONE-AFFECT-CHECKIN; 2026-07-18)

**A `/play/<app>` clone MAY carry a distinct 11th axis — an AFFECT / SELF-REGULATION CHECK-IN: a GENTLE, OPTIONAL, dismissible session-START check-in ("how are you arriving?") + an on-demand CALM-DOWN / co-regulation off-ramp (a short tap-through grounding + breathing sequence), placed at a session boundary. It is DEFINED by its guardrails — because the evidence is context-dependent, not universally positive.** Distinct from the results `reflect.ts` (a one-shot post-round reflection): this is the session-start affect-labeling moment + a between-practice calm-down a stuck learner can reach. iOS parity: `ForgeEmotionAware` (`AffectCalibrator`/`CoRegulationEngine`/`SensoryRamp`) + DIR/FEDC. Codified per founder-greenlight 2026-07-18 (ForgeKit-mining wave, ADR-048 Decision 11) on `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_EXPANSION_FORGEKIT_MINING_2026-07-18.md`.

**Fleet (P#144 s45):** on every landing or a `# REFLECT-EXCLUDED` waiver (never on a #192 AffectKit app, a non-English app, or a grown-up-led host); below the game on console landings; `younger` for 3–8. Tools: `mount_affect_checkin.py` · `check_dn_reflection_coverage.py --reflect-ci-mode`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A clone surfaces its ALREADY-AUTHORED humor seed bank as a shared BOUNDARY "Brain break" island — relevance-forward, tap-to-reveal, NO dark patterns, NO illustrations (R-WEB-CLONE-HUMOR-BREAK; 2026-07-28)

**A `/play/<app>` clone MAY surface its app's ALREADY-AUTHORED humor seed bank (70/app: 20 puns + 20 riddles + 15 absurd + 15 fun facts — iOS-bundled but historically DARK on the web) via the SHARED `src/lib/play/_shared/humor.ts` engine + `src/components/play/HumorBreak.astro` boundary island, opted-into with a per-app data module ported by `spark-anvil-hub/scripts/port_humor_seeds_to_web.py`. A clone that wants a humor surface MUST reuse this shell (never hand-roll), and the invariants below are hard gates — a violation is a defect on the same footing as a dark surface.** This is DISTRIBUTION of authored content (§ R-WEB-CLONE-NARRATIVE-INTEGRATION "authored ≠ integrated"), NOT new authoring. Decision: `ADR-079`; fit assessment: `RESEARCH_PORT_ONBOARDING_HUMOR_EASTEREGG_TO_WEBCLONES_2026-07-28.md`. Pilot SHIPPED: FractionForge (site PR #1446).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every eligible `/play` clone's humor is a SELF-CHECKING coverage axis — a joke-less clone (no `humor.ts`) is a tracked backfill gap, never silently forgotten; PORTABLE (seed bank exists → port now) worst-first, then NEEDS-BANK (author first) (R-WEB-CLONE-HUMOR-COVERAGE; 2026-08-24)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone that ships WITHOUT jokes/humor — no `src/data/play/<app>/humor.ts` (so its Mission-Kit jokeIds AND its `<HumorBreak>` island BOTH degrade to joke-less) — is a TRACKED backfill gap, NEVER silently forgotten. This is the WEB-DISTRIBUTION sibling of `portfolio.md` § R-HUMOR-SEED-COVERAGE (the hub-side "is a 70-seed bank AUTHORED?" axis): THIS axis is "is that bank PORTED to the clone as `humor.ts`?" — the "authored ≠ ported/distributed" gap (§ R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE / § R-WEB-CLONE-NARRATIVE-INTEGRATION). Self-checking (the R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS upgrade of the previously HAND-maintained `REGISTRY_HUMOR_BREAK_COVERAGE.txt` mounted-ledger, frozen at V664): `scripts/check_web_clone_humor_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_WEB_CLONE_HUMOR_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, reads origin/staging, `--ci-mode` exits 0 — both consumers degrade gracefully). Backfill = a STANDING worst-first drain: PORTABLE (seed bank exists → `port_humor_seeds_to_web.py --app <app>`, cheap) → NEEDS-BANK (author the band-appropriate bank first, composes R-HUMOR-SEED-COVERAGE); DEFERRED = younger band (register-inappropriate), EXCLUDED = humor-DOMAIN clones (jokes ARE the curriculum). A joke-less eligible clone with no registry class, OR a ported bank left un-mounted, is the silent-forget this forbids.** Founder-direct 2026-08-24 (*"create a new program to audit/backfill clones without jokes/humor. codify, track, guard, generalize everything"*). Composes ABOVE § R-WEB-CLONE-HUMOR-BREAK; program #72. → full class defs: registry header + `q-web-clone-humor`.

**"ported ≠ wired" sub-axis (2026-08-25):** porting `humor.ts` isn't sufficient — a clone fanned out joke-less keeps `mission/[m].astro` `joke:null` + `useHumorBank:false` so the Mission-Kit Joke box stays OMITTED (5-box); re-wire it (per-mission joke resolved in `getStaticPaths` + flip the flag) when the bank lands. Guard `check_mission_kit_humor_wiring.py` + `REGISTRY_MISSION_KIT_HUMOR_WIRING.txt` (UNWIRED = `useHumorBank:false` while `humor.ts` exists).

## Brain breaks are a THREE-shell boundary system lifted from CuriosityQuest — reveal card (+ at quiz-END) · trick-questions · focus timer — reward-economies STRIPPED, boundary-only, no dark-patterns (R-WEB-CLONE-BRAIN-BREAKS; 2026-07-29)

**CuriosityQuest's three kid-loved brain-break systems are lifted to every `/play` clone as THREE SHARED `_shared/` shells, each ADAPTED by the SAME rule — the reward economy is STRIPPED, the surface is boundary-only (never mid-question), on-device/no-PII, dismissible + optional, dark-safe (`--ff-*`/`--pc-*`), reduced-motion-safe, age-band-aware, and screenshot-DoD-gated. Never hand-roll a per-clone brain break; never copy a reward economy onto one. The reward economies are the ONLY thing dropped — pedagogy + placement + content + calm framing transfer.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-29 (*"kids really like curiosityquest's implementation of brain breaks and brain teasers, especially the ones at the END of quizzes and questions. audit and lift … for all webclones"* + *"lift all 3 … if they make sense"*); decision `ADR-081`; audit `AUDIT_CURIOSITYQUEST_BRAIN_BREAKS_2026-07-29.md`; evidence `RESEARCH_BRAIN_BREAKS_QUIZ_BOUNDARY_2026-07-29.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every clone's progress-store namespace MUST be globally UNIQUE — a shared ns commingles two clones' on-device stores (R-WEB-CLONE-PROGRESS-NS-UNIQUE; 2026-07-19)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone's PRIMARY progress namespace — the `makeProgress('<ns>')` value, which keys the on-device store at `localStorage['<ns>.progress.v1']` and is read by `<MasteryProgress>` (axis 8), the practice-scheduling review (axis 7), and `<ProgressReport>` (axis 10) — MUST be GLOBALLY UNIQUE across the whole fleet. Two clones sharing one namespace write the SAME localStorage key, so their per-kit progress / mastery / XP / streak / report COMMINGLE — a real on-device data-corruption bug (clone A's kit results pollute clone B's mastery + report, and vice-versa). A duplicate namespace is a defect on the same footing as a dark route.** Codified after the axis-10 rollout surfaced **11 pre-existing collision groups** (e.g. `gf` shared by grammarforge/geometryforge/gridforge; `dq` by dancequest/deducequest/discretequest; `cf`/`df`/`eq`/`lf`/`pq`/`rr`/`sl`/`spl`/`tt` each by a pair) — each had been silently commingling since those clones shipped.

**AMENDMENT — the uniqueness invariant covers the WHOLE on-device ns SURFACE, not just `makeProgress` (P#186 2026-09-27):** a clone ALSO writes on-device under `recordActivity('<ns>',…)` (the #61 companion records key `${ns}.records.v1`) and via `<X>_NS='<ns>'` learn-path/store literals — so a collision living ONLY in that layer is the SAME commingling defect, even when both clones' `makeProgress` ns differ. Reference: **pawnpals** (`PP_LP_NS='pp'` + `recordActivity('pp')`) ↔ **pathpals** (`makeProgress('pp')` + `recordActivity('pp')`) both write `pp.records.v1` — invisible to a `makeProgress`-only scan. The guard `check-play-namespace-unique.mjs` + `namespace-unique.test.ts` now run an EXTENDED scan harvesting `makeProgress` + `recordActivity` + `*_NS` + `progressNs` + `(CONST)` args + `${NS}.x.v1` template keys (s43) across each clone's `src/{lib,pages,data}/play/<app>/**`, hard-failing on any NEW cross-clone collision; a pre-existing collision with a tracked migration is a documented `KNOWN_NS_COLLISIONS` WARN baseline (train-safe) — removed the moment its migration lands (then it hard-fails if it reappears). **s44:** the scan also reads FULL-KEY literals (`'swt.record.v1'`, comment-stripped) and FAILS an INTRA-clone split (a shell `progressNs` ≠ the clone's own `makeProgress` ns → two dashboard rows). ⚠ Historical commingled `${ns}.records.v1` is ALREADY mixed across the colliding clones and cannot be cleanly un-attributed → a migration is **fresh-start-forward** (a one-time note leaves the corrupt historical key), NEVER a copy-old-`${ns}.*`-into-the-new-key shim (which drags the other clone's records in). Tracked: `q-pawnpals-ns-migration` (part 1 guard SHIPPED site #4160; part 2 pawnpals migration deferred to its attended founder-visual-gated session).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Learner AGENCY is the 12th expansion axis — meaningful CHOICE (autonomy-support) + an explicit AAR loop, ON-DEVICE + calm-rails, that COMPOSES the existing axes (does not rebuild them) (R-WEB-CLONE-AGENCY; 2026-07-19)

**A `/play/<app>` clone's EXPANSION includes a distinct 12th axis — LEARNER AGENCY: developing the learner's agentic capacity (Bandura's four properties via the OECD Anticipation-Action-Reflection loop) by giving them REAL, learning-RELEVANT CHOICE over what/how to learn next + threading goal→choose→predict/act→self-assess/reflect into ONE learner-directed, on-device, calm-rails loop. Its scope is DELIBERATELY NARROW + honest-yield: agency is MOSTLY ALREADY OWNED by the LIVE axes — forethought/goal-setting by axis-8 (learner-set goals), anticipation by POE, self-reactiveness by PolyaScaffold, self-reflectiveness by DIR/FEDC reflection + the self-explanation fold — so this axis adds ONLY the un-owned MEANINGFUL-CHOICE lever + the AAR orchestration, and it COMPOSES (never rebuilds) the others. A clone that ships a decorative/irrelevant "choice," a system that auto-drives the path under an agency label, or an agency surface on the active solve-path is a defect.** Codified per founder-greenlight 2026-07-19 ("everything is approved") on the evidence in `Docs/RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_EXPANSION_AGENCY_AXIS_2026-07-19.md` (ADR-048 Decision 12). Sibling to axis-7 (§ R-WEB-CLONE-PRACTICE-SCHEDULING) + axis-8 (§ R-WEB-CLONE-MASTERY-PROGRESSION) — same on-device / calm-rails / compose-shared-shell shape.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The GambitTales chess FEATURE FAMILY is REPLICATED across every board-game & puzzle app — archetype-scoped feature PARITY, tracked by a self-checking cross-app registry, delivered via LIFTED shared shells over per-game deterministic engines; new games (xiangqi/go/shogi) are founder-gated concept-spawns (R-BOARD-GAME-FEATURE-REPLICATION; 2026-08-04)

**The portfolio runs a standing program to REPLICATE the GambitTales chess feature family (learn-to-play · board-vision · tactics/motifs · pattern-recognition · endgame/mate · timed rush · review-SRS · guess-the-move POE · schematic essence-diagrams · teach-the-cast · adventure · trick/brain-break · networked duel · bot opponent · engine analysis · hotseat · versus · focus · progress) across EVERY board-game & puzzle app (chess/xiangqi/shogi · go · connect-4/reversi/gomoku/hex/dots-boxes · checkers/draughts · mastermind · Rubik's cube · backgammon · bridge). Feature transfer is ARCHETYPE-SCOPED (board-vision ≠ Mastermind; a networked duel ≠ the solo cube; go's tactics ARE tsumego). An APPLICABLE-but-unbuilt feature is a tracked PARITY GAP; a self-checking cross-app registry keeps no game/feature silently forgotten; new games (xiangqi/go/shogi) are founder-gated concept-spawns. Delivered via LIFTED shared shells over per-game deterministic engines.**

→ **Sub-rules (defined here; full body → ADR-113):**
- **R-BOARD-GAME-MSO-DISCIPLINE-GATE** (2026-08-12, ADR-113) — the recurring *"MSO also runs game X — add it?"* founder-gate has a standing test: add a game only if it (a) is DN-swap-able, (b) is NOT gambling-coded, AND (c) adds an un-covered curricular primitive. **Othello = Reversi → COVERED** (gridforge: learn/reversi/hotseat/duel + cast Flank; keep the trademark-safe "Reversi" name — "Othello" is trademarked, never rebrand). **Gambling-coded games (poker · blackjack · slots · roulette · baccarat · betting variants) = DEFAULT-REJECT** — a branded gambling surface for kids is a defect on the CRITICAL gambling-adjacency footing (ChanceForge/TableForge Bones/Hand precedent); the no-stakes math core (probability · EV · hidden information) is extracted into the EXISTING probability/hidden-info surfaces (ChanceForge · TableForge Hand · dealtales bidding · pipquest), never a money/chips/casino-register surface. **A single-mechanic non-gambling game that passes the swap-test thinly = a MODE, not a standalone app** (cribbage → the no-stakes make-15/combinatorics `/play/dealtales/cribbage` "Cribbage Count" surface, stakes stripped). **DOMINOES + MULTIPLAYER CARD GAMES + UNO-TYPE (ADR-165, 2026-08-19; 9-14):** (d) **Dominoes = build-worthy DEDICATED APP** (🟢, founder-gated) — a MULTI-VARIANT strategic family (Block/Draw/**All-Fives-Muggins**/Mexican-Train) whose open-end mental-arithmetic-to-multiples-of-5 primitive is un-covered → clears the standalone bar (pipquest/seedsong/millsong precedent), UNLIKE single-mechanic cribbage; (e) **non-gambling multiplayer card games (Rummy sets/runs · Hearts/Spades trick-taking) + Uno-type shedding (Uno/Crazy-Eights) = MODES in `dealtales`** (🟡) — the cards archetype + `cards.ts` is dealtales-OWNED, so a new card app is a 🔴 duplicate; each single game is single-mechanic→mode (reuse `cards.ts` + V261 room-lobby, one DN cast char each); (f) poker/blackjack/betting = DEFAULT-REJECT (unchanged). All modes ship POINTS, never currency.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_BOARD_GAME_FEATURE_REPLICATION_PROGRAM_2026-08-04.md` + ADR-097 + `Docs/ADR-113_MSO_OTHELLO_POKER_CRIBBAGE.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_MSO_OTHELLO_POKER_CRIBBAGE_FIT_2026-08-12.md` + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A board-game clone supports its game's VARIANTS (Chess960 · King-of-the-Hill · Turkish draughts · 9×9 go · Kalah · …) as a first-class rule-set dimension ORTHOGONAL to the feature set — ENGINE-tiered, engine-verified, DN-cast-consistent, age-band-calibrated, boundary-only, no-gambling, honest-yield; fit-assessed per app + fanned out; a smaller-board variant doubles as a younger-mode; Bughouse/Fog-of-War/4-player are founder-gated STRUCTURAL (R-BOARD-GAME-VARIANT-SUPPORT; 2026-08-27)

**A `/play/<app>` board-game clone MAY support its game's VARIANTS — a change to the game's RULE-SET (full taxonomy + per-app verdicts in the registry) — as a first-class dimension ORTHOGONAL to the 20-feature parity set (§ R-BOARD-GAME-FEATURE-REPLICATION): a variant changes the rules, then inherits the whole feature set. Hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) ENGINE-TIERED build (tiers A–E in ADR-198 §2, grounded in the real engine): A win-condition OVERLAY (KOTH/3-check, cheapest — only `gameOutcome` changes) < B start/legality filter < C start-generalization (Chess960 = castling file-aware + X-FEN) < D new move-TYPE (Crazyhouse REUSES droptale/shogi's drop engine) < **E STRUCTURAL/networked/non-turn-based (Bughouse · 4-player · Fog-of-War) = FOUNDER-GATED — the ADR-041/095 2-player transport can't host them; NEVER force-fit or promise near-term**; (2) ENGINE-VERIFIED — each variant's legality + win-condition are pure-engine Vitest invariants (§ R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE / -PUZZLE-ENGINE-VERIFIED); (3) DN-CAST-CONSISTENT — same pieces → same characters (§ R-DN-PARITY / R-CAST-PIECE-SINGLE-CHARACTER); (4) BOUNDARY-ONLY mode-selector on a QUIET stage (§ R-GUARD-THE-RATIO), full recognizable board (§ R-WEB-CLONE-FULL-GAME-BOARD); (5) AGE-BAND-CALIBRATED — a SMALLER-BOARD variant (Dōbutsu 3×4 · mini-shogi · 9×9 go · smaller morris · Hypergammon) doubles as a YOUNGER-MODE → coordinate with § `portfolio.md` R-YOUNGER-BOARD-GAME-FIT/-MODE (#58; build once, surface both), never a blanket every-variant fan-out; (6) NO-GAMBLING/no-dark-pattern (ADR-113 CRITICAL gate) + on-device/COPPA + dark-safe/WCAG-2.2-AA/dual-scheme screenshot-DoD; (7) networked variant play (2-player turn-based) rides the LIVE V261 `roomBoard` relay, NEVER a new transport (§ R-WEB-CLONE-ROOM-BOARD-RELAY); (8) HONEST-YIELD — an engagement + principle-ISOLATION lever (KOTH→centre control · 3-check→king safety · Chess960→principles-over-memorization), NEVER a proven learning-gain; (9) NEVER a new standalone app — variants are MODES (§ `portfolio.md` R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT). REFERENCE-FIRST = KOTH on gambittales. Fit-assessed per app (four gates: DN-swap · no-gambling · engine-tier · band → 🟢 build / 🟡 mode-or-younger-mode-or-engine-extension / 🔴 founder-gated-or-reject) so nothing is force-built AND nothing is silently forgotten. Tracked `scripts/check_board_game_variant_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_BOARD_GAME_VARIANT_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; a board-game app with neither a variant row nor a `# VARIANT-EXCLUDED:` line is silently-forgotten). A force-fit ill-fitting variant, an un-engine-verified variant, a cast-incoherent variant, an on-solve-loop selector, a near-term Bughouse promise, a gambling-coded rule-set, or an over-claimed outcome is a defect.** Founder-direct 2026-08-27 (*"support all the chess variants … create a program to track this … also fan out … to the rest of the board games … deep web research and think harder"*); decision `ADR-198`; program #86.

**AMENDMENT — Tier-E DECOMPOSES; it is NOT one "whole new transport" monolith (ADR-207, 2026-08-28, W7 founder-GO).** The deployed room DO (`workers/room`) is ALREADY N-seat (`MAX_SEATS=8`) with round-robin turn order — the "2-player" assumption lives only in the CLIENT `roomBoard.ts`. So the three Tier-E variants split by TRUE requirement: **Fog-of-War vs-BOT = a CLIENT-SIDE visibility filter over `chess.ts`** (no-checks/king-capture; no human adversary → nothing to cheat; NO transport/deploy) — shippable now, reclassified E→D; **Bughouse = a BOUNDED `BughouseRoom` DO extension** (2-board Match-Coordinator + per-board turn + cross-board pass-routing, still relay/opaque-moves) — deploy founder-gated; **networked Fog-of-War = a SERVER-AUTHORITATIVE `FogRoom` DO** (client-holds-full-board is the chess.com hidden-info exploit) — deploy founder-gated; **4-player = a from-scratch 4-army cross-board engine** (networked RIDES the existing N-seat DO). ADR-041 safety invariants stay HARD (Bughouse team comms = preset emotes only); a networked variant is not DoD-met until a live multi-client smoke passes (§ R-ROOM-RELAY-LIVE-VERIFY).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-198_BOARD_GAME_VARIANT_SUPPORT.md` + `Docs/ADR-207_BOARD_GAME_TIER_E_TRANSPORT.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_BOARD_GAME_VARIANTS_2026-08-27.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_BOARD_GAME_TIER_E_TRANSPORT_2026-08-28.md` + `Docs/PLAN_BOARD_GAME_VARIANT_PROGRAM_2026-08-27.md`.
## The chess apps LIFT the beginner-onboarding craft of Duolingo Chess + ChessKid in CALM form only — a bite-sized guided learn-PATH + warm anti-shame coaching VOICE + a "your first game" confidence arc — reference-first on GambitTales then band-calibrated to PawnPals; the engagement economy is a permanent REJECT and our correctness/no-farm/free/on-device wins are surfaced as MOATS (R-CHESS-LEARNING-PARITY; 2026-08-28)

**Our chess `/play` apps (GambitTales tween 9-14 · PawnPals younger 6-8) reach parity with the beginner-acquisition genius of Duolingo Chess + ChessKid by LIFTING their onboarding craft in CALM form ONLY — NOT feature catch-up (GambitTales is already 20/20 board-game-feature-complete + at/above ChessKid parity per the 2026-08-15 teardown; Duolingo caps ~1500 Elo + ships ~24% broken puzzles). The three lifts, built as SHARED shells (never hand-rolled), reference-first on GambitTales then band-calibrated to PawnPals (R-YOUNGER-BAND-CONTENT-REGISTER): (1) a bite-sized GUIDED learn-PATH + calm named level-ladder over the shipped /learn+M1-M9+mastery axis-8 (Duolingo star-path ∩ ChessKid Pawn→King) with CALM stars only; (2) a personality-forward COACHING VOICE via the DN cast/mentor — WARM, anti-shame, boundary-only (R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING / R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY — never sarcastic-at-the-kid, never hands the move); (3) a guided "ready to play your first game" confidence arc. Three permanent lines, each a defect if crossed: the ENGAGEMENT ECONOMY is a by-design REJECT (streaks/hearts/leagues/quests-as-currency · Stars/Gems/pets/leaderboards/tournaments · public random matchmaking · accounts/PII/cloud reporting · talking-head videos [DN chapters are the analog] — anti-Gimkit / R-SITE-FEEDBACK / ADR-099); our wins are MOATS surfaced as honest-yield marketing (engine-verified puzzle CORRECTNESS vs Duolingo's ~24%-broken · no-farm/free/on-device/no-account · real engine + on-device Socratic tutor + DN cast-as-curriculum); and every build is engine-verified + dual-scheme×desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD + on-device/no-PII + honest-yield (retention design, NEVER a proven learning-gain). DEDUPE against the shipped gambittales forward-port plan (vision · guess-move · error-pattern coach · openings · defense · puzzle-theme breadth q-f6654d · code-gated Puzzle Duel — already planned, tracked PARTIAL, never re-proposed). GENERALIZES to the board-game feature-replication family (R-BOARD-GAME-FEATURE-REPLICATION) where the archetype fits, never a padded fan-out. Site PRs base on `staging` (ADR-154); iOS = a `_WEB_BACKPORT` handoff (hub never writes Swift). Tracked `scripts/check_chess_learning_parity_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_CHESS_LEARNING_PARITY.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN).** Founder-direct 2026-08-28; decision `ADR-204`; program #90.

**AMENDMENT — W5–W10 ADVANCED lifts + FLEET fan-out (reopened 2026-08-28; ADR-204 § Amendment).** Two COMPREHENSIVE competitor reports (Duolingo Chess + ChessKid full-feature) added a SECOND lift tier beyond the W2–W4 on-ramp, each calm-form + shared-shell + reference-first gambittales+pawnpals then **fanned out across the board-game fleet where the archetype fits** (founder-direct *"lift for gambittales, pawnpals, and fan out for the rest of the fleet"*): **W5 conversion-helper** (stuck-converting-a-won-position scaffold in `/endgame`, articulate-before-hint, never auto-plays — NET-NEW) · **W6 in-game coach-mode** (opt-in learner-triggered "hint?" on `/versus`, answer-withholding — NET-NEW) · **W7 endgame workouts** (repeat-to-mastery pattern drill, no stress timer) · **W8 start-level chooser** (`_shared/learnPath` "where should I start?" — SHELL enhancement, fans out free) · **W9 pick-your-coach** (`_shared/coachVoice` choose your DN cast coach, no currency — SHELL enhancement) · **W10 game-review interactive-retry** (missed-move → retry over the engine; ENRICHES ADR-205/#91 `gameAnalysis`, coordinated not duplicated). §5b-deduped on staging (coordinate Vision Trainer `vision.ts` + capture-practice `learn.ts` ALREADY shipped → never re-proposed; Puzzle-Map cosmetic worlds = re-affirmed REJECT). All base disciplines bind; tracked worst-first by the registry `advlift`/`advfanout` axis + `q-chess-learning-parity`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-204_CHESS_LEARNING_COMPETITIVE_PARITY.md` (+ § Amendment W5–W10) + `Docs/PLAN_CHESS_LEARNING_COMPETITIVE_PARITY_PROGRAM_2026-08-28.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_COMPETITIVE_DUOLINGO_CHESS_HEAD_TO_HEAD_2026-08-28.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_COMPETITIVE_CHESSKID_HEAD_TO_HEAD_2026-08-28.md` (+ the settled `Docs/RESEARCH_CHESSKID_VS_GAMBITTALES_COMPETITIVE_2026-08-15.md`).
## chess.com/Lichess features are lifted CALM-FORM via a census — the fleet already ships ~90% (COVERED, never rebuilt); lift the 4 net-new (skills-habit-chain · daily-puzzle-hearts-NO-streak · best-moves-shelf · NVUI-blind-mode), REJECT the paid retention farm (metering-learning/crowns-currency/leagues/prize-events/celebrity-voices/FOMO-drops/streaks/ads/broadcasts/APIs/DDA-bots) and MARKET the rejection; reference-first gambittales+pawnpals → board-game fleet (R-CHESS-PLATFORM-FEATURE-LIFT; 2026-08-28)

**Lifting chess.com/Lichess features (Report No. 72 "The Same Fish") follows the SAME adopt-good / reject-farm / market-the-rejection census as § R-CHESS-LEARNING-PARITY + § R-DUOLINGO-FEATURE-LIFT — the THIRD chess-competitor census (after ADR-204 #90 + ADR-209 #94). Both sites run the SAME Stockfish 18 + mine the SAME corpora; everything that differs is a PRODUCT decision, and the fleet already ships ~90% → a CENSUS, not a re-lift. Three buckets (full matrix = ADR-210 + the registry): (a) COVERED (18) — re-affirm, NEVER rebuild (engine ADR-094 · corpus ADR-196 · game-review ADR-205/204-W10 · human-like bots ADR-199/200 · cast-pieces ADR-166 · coach-voice ADR-204-W9 · variants ADR-198 · openings-as-PRINCIPLES ADR-096 · vision/masters/learn shipped · dashboards ADR-176 · give-learning-free moat; incl. annotated master-game study=/masters+trail, rote-repertoire REJECTED); (b) LIFT (4 net-new calm-form, reference-first gambittales+pawnpals → board-game fleet) — **L1** SKILLS habit-mastery chain (5-track Fundamentals·Openings·Tactics·Strategy·Endgame, points earned DURING game-review for good HABITS, payout-shown-first, off-switchable, DERIVED from shipped gameAnalysis, NO currency, composes-ABOVE axis-8) · **L2** DAILY-PUZZLE calm ritual (hearts+coach-tip, daily refresh, print/save card, **NO STREAK AT ALL**, no ad/monetization wall, closable-anytime) · **L3** BRILLIANT/best-moves intrapersonal shelf (reuse gameAnalysis+games.v1, non-comparative) · **L4** NVUI BLIND-MODE (keyboard board-nav+move/threat announce+touchscreen-for-blind, composes ReadingAccess, WCAG 2.1.1/2.4.7); (c) REJECT (12 non-waivable, MARKET the rejection — the report §11 shows chess.com rations exactly what makes you better) — metering-of-LEARNING · crowns/hint CURRENCY · Leagues · prize events · celebrity voice clones · FOMO themed-bot drops · STREAKS · ads/tracking · broadcasts · OAuth/Board/Bot APIs/openness · tournaments/Swiss · DDA adaptive bots (adopting any FORFEITS the give-learning-free moat = critical defect). EIGHT hard guardrails (each a dark-route-footing defect): calm-form-only (anti-Gimkit R-SITE-FEEDBACK) · shared-shell + R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA(high-blast) · on-device/COPPA · boundary-placed (R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING/R-GUARD-THE-RATIO) · engine-verified truth (L1/L3 derive from shipped gameAnalysis; no new unverified content) · HONEST-YIELD (engagement/accessibility/reflection, NEVER a proven learning/rating gain) · DN-cast-not-celebrity · all standard gates + site PRs base=STAGING (ADR-154) + iOS `_WEB_BACKPORT` handoff (hub never writes Swift). Tracked `scripts/check_chess_platform_lift_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_CHESS_PLATFORM_LIFT_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` FAILS only on a reject-row drift-to-build or malformed row). Fan-out is a standing worst-first drain (clone×lift rows; a board-game clone with neither a lift row nor a `# LIFT-EXCLUDED:` line is silently-forgotten, R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS). Composes ABOVE § R-CHESS-LEARNING-PARITY · § R-BOARD-GAME-FEATURE-REPLICATION · § R-DUOLINGO-FEATURE-LIFT · § R-WEB-CLONE-PORTAL-LIFT · cognitive-accessibility § R-COGNITIVE-ACCESSIBILITY (L4).** Founder-direct 2026-08-28 ("lift all features/app concepts if they make sense … create a new program to track and fan out … codify/guard/track/generalize"); decision `ADR-210`; program #95.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-210_CHESS_PLATFORM_FEATURE_LIFT.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_CHESS_PLATFORM_FEATURE_LIFT_2026-08-28.md` + `Docs/PLAN_CHESS_PLATFORM_FEATURE_LIFT_PROGRAM_2026-08-28.md`.
## Duolingo/Duolingo-Music features are lifted CALM-FORM only — adopt the pedagogy (play-along, MIDI, on-device speech, prep-isolation, guidebook, honest proficiency), REJECT the engagement-farm (streaks/energy/leagues/gems/nudges/ads), and MARKET the rejection; Duolingo-Music is a music-cluster MODE, never a new app (R-DUOLINGO-FEATURE-LIFT; 2026-08-28)

**Lifting Duolingo & Duolingo-Music features into the fleet follows the adopt-good / reject-farm / market-the-rejection discipline (the § R-WEB-CLONE-PORTAL-LIFT + § R-CHESS-LEARNING-PARITY shape) — because Duolingo's own 2026 pivot is walking BACK the engagement-farm (Hearts/Energy/streaks/leagues) that suppressed its growth, exactly the layer § R-SITE-FEEDBACK / anti-Gimkit forbids. Three buckets (ADR-209 matrix, 27 items): (a) COVERED (12) — chess (ADR-204/205), math cluster, MUSIC LITERACY (clefquest/melodymice/harmonyforge/beatforge = Duolingo-Music's pedagogy), stories/DuoRadio (DN-S), flashcards, Explain-My-Answer (our Socratic answer-WITHHOLDING tutor is better), mastery-checks, character-voice, one-habit-layer (ADR-176), AI-cost-collapse, free-everything, scenario-roleplay — re-affirm, NEVER rebuild; (b) LIFT (6, calm-form, worst-first drain) — L1 play-along scored-song MODE · L2 Web-MIDI · L3 speaking-input · L4 prep-before-hard isolation · L5 Guidebook card · L6 honest proficiency indicator; (c) REJECT (8, non-waivable) — streaks/energy/leagues/gems/friend-nudge/seasonal-FOMO/ads/match-timer (adopting any FORFEITS the moat = critical defect). THREE HARD guardrails, each a dark-route-footing defect: G1 speaking-INPUT is on-device-ONLY (`processLocally:true`, default-OFF, fail-safe to tap, NEVER cloud — cloud Web-Speech ships a child's VOICEPRINT = explicit COPPA-2026 biometric PII, $51,744/day; absent-if-unavailable, founder-gated to widen); G2 Web-MIDI is opt-in/feature-detected/gesture-gated/secure-context/no-SysEx/fail-safe-to-tap (Chromebook-yes, iOS-WebKit-no); G3 play-along maps STANDARD notation (not abstract piano-roll) + requires read-on-SIGHT (not mimicry) + accuracy stars are INTRAPERSONAL (no leaderboard/streak/energy) + the scrolling staff IS the manipulative (R-GUARD-THE-RATIO) + honest-yield (fluency practice, never a proven gain). SCOPING (R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT): Duolingo-Music is NOT a new app — the music cluster covers the pedagogy; the play-along+MIDI+speech mechanic is a MODE (single-mechanic→mode), reference-first clefquest, iOS = ForgeKit handoff (hub never writes Swift). Build waves ride single-flight-Gemini + screenshot-DoD + adversarial-review (high-blast shared shells) + site PRs base=staging. The reject bucket feeds § R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING (the calm alternative to the farm the field is walking back). Tracked `scripts/check_duolingo_lift_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_DUOLINGO_LIFT_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` FAILS only on a reject-row drifting to a build state = a silent engagement-farm adoption). **APP-CONCEPTS axis (2026-08-28):** the companion brief's 11 concept cards (C1–C11) are `R-NEW-APP-CONCEPT-FIT`-censused (verdicts SSOT = `duolingo-c*` rows in `REGISTRY_INNOVATIVE_EDGAME_CANDIDATES.txt`/ADR-116) — 5 🟢 whitespace (a VI-first heritage-language line + a DIR parent dyad-play coach) each a per-title founder GO (`R-REC-VS-FOUNDER-DIRECT` — "everything approved" ≠ ceiling bypass), the rest COVERED/MODE.** Founder-direct 2026-08-28; decision `ADR-209`; program #94.

## A board-game clone renders the game's FULL, RECOGNIZABLE board/apparatus as the interactive manipulative — clean + functional (all real components + canonical layout), NEVER decorative skeuomorphism — where the game has a recognizable physical board (R-WEB-CLONE-FULL-GAME-BOARD; 2026-08-10)

**A `/play/<app>` board-game clone MUST render the game's FULL, RECOGNIZABLE board/apparatus as the interactive manipulative — every real component in the canonical physical layout (the peg decoding board, the 24-point backgammon board, the goban, the N×N grid), rendered CLEAN + FUNCTIONAL — WHENEVER the game has a recognizable physical board. This is the manipulative-prominence treatment (chess renders 8×8 because the board IS the game), NOT a tension with the quiet-stage canon: the full board is the manipulative; what stays forbidden is DECORATION *on* the board (wood-grain/leather textures, heavy 3-D bevels, ambient background scenes, on-loop animation — the seductive-detail per § R-WEB-CLONE-MANIPULATIVE-PROMINENCE + § R-GUARD-THE-RATIO). A board game shown as an ABSTRACT proxy (a chip-list + a text tally) instead of its recognizable board is a fidelity defect on the same footing as a thin manipulative; a board rendered with decorative skeuomorphism is the opposite defect.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-10 (quote → REFERENCE); decision + audit + cited research `Docs/AUDIT_BOARD_GAME_FULL_BOARD_FIDELITY_2026-08-10.md`.

- **R-WEB-CLONE-CONNECTED-SEQUENCE-ORIENTATION (2026-08-21, ADR-169):** a clone rendering a CONNECTED SEQUENCE (domino chain · card run/meld · track — adjacent pieces must share a value at the touching face) MUST orient each piece at render time so touching faces MATCH; a fixed-order render draws high-value joins REVERSED (the tiletales bug). Compute orientation in a PURE, Vitest-pinned engine helper, NOT ad-hoc in the view; only the screenshot-DoD sees a reversed-but-present piece → a fidelity defect on the failed-dark-mode footing. Detail + reference impl → ADR-169.
## A board-game clone MAY carry a 3-mode CAST-PIECE SKIN (Story-Time-Chess-style character↔piece overlay) — default PLAIN pieces, opt-in on-device toggle, NO currency economy, INTEGRATED map only (the char embodies the piece), static + board-tap-safe; archetype-scoped + tracked (R-WEB-CLONE-CAST-PIECE-SKIN; 2026-08-20)

**A `/play/<app>` board-game clone with DISTINCT piece TYPES MAY carry a CAST-PIECE SKIN — the digital equivalent of Story Time Chess's attach-a-character-to-the-piece — delivered as the shared `_shared/castPieces.ts` shell (never hand-rolled) with THREE modes: `pieces` (standard piece only — the fleet DEFAULT) · `hybrid` (piece + small cast portrait badge in a corner) · `cast` (big cast portrait + small piece glyph in a corner). Seven hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) DEFAULT `pieces` on every surface except the Learn / how-pieces-move surface (where `cast` may default — the task IS the piece), always learner-overridable; (2) OPT-IN on-device toggle (`localStorage`, no PII), NO currency/unlock/streak to change the skin (reject ChessKid Stars/Gems; anti-Gimkit; § R-SITE-FEEDBACK); (3) INTEGRATED map only — per-app `castPieceMap` maps each piece TYPE to a character that BEHAVIORALLY EMBODIES it (§ R-DN-PARITY), `auditCastPieces` throws on an unmapped type/unresolved portrait; a decoupled map is a defect; (4) STATIC anchor, quiet stage — no motion/chatter on the overlay during the solve loop (§ R-GUARD-THE-RATIO); (5) BOARD-TAP unbroken — overlay `pointer-events:none` (§ R-WEB-CLONE-SAMPLER-BOARD-TAP family); (6) accessible + dark-safe — overlay `aria-hidden`, both schemes, reduced-motion-safe, ≥44px halo unchanged, WCAG 2.2 AA, verdict = dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD; (7) ZERO paid gen — reuse shipped `/cast` portraits; (8) COLOUR-AWARE map (`type → {w,b}`, the StoryTime-Chess model) — ROYALS (K/Q) per-colour DISTINCT characters, TROOPS (R/B/N/P) ONE shared character per type; white vs black ALWAYS distinguished (piece glyph + black-army shadow-tint), and where a per-colour portrait isn't authored yet both sides share one `provisional:true` + tint pending a tracked black-royal cast-expansion (paid gen, gated). Archetype-scoped: STRONG chess-family · APPLICABLE checkers · ANALOG backgammon/card-face · N/A single-uniform-piece (go/connect-4/mill/mancala). Default-plain reconciles it with § R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING (the char helps the opt-in novice, is absent for the calculator — expertise reversal); honest-yield "learn what each piece does," never "makes you a better player." Tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_CAST_PIECE_SKIN_COVERAGE.txt` + `scripts/check_cast_piece_skin_coverage.py` (SHAPE-B WARN).** Founder-direct 2026-08-20; `ADR-166`; a `castpieces`-token feature in the § R-BOARD-GAME-FEATURE-REPLICATION family. **(9) OPTIONAL per-side CREST (ADR-166 Amendment 2, founder-approved 2026-08-21):** an OPTIONAL `sideCrest:{w,b}` overlay — one heraldic emblem per ARMY worn by every piece on that side — as a colour-INDEPENDENT army-ID (WCAG 1.4.1) + a cheaper substitute for the black-royal portrait expansion; ADOPT opt-in only on the TWO-ARMY piece games (STRONG chess-family · APPLICABLE checkers · ANALOG backgammon), N/A (inapplicable, not withheld) on single-uniform-token + single-player; all invariants (1)-(8) carry, `auditCastPieces` requires both crest assets resolve; ONE gen'd crest/side (paid, single-flight, founder-gated fan-out, reference-first gambittales), tracked by a `crest:` axis on the coverage registry. A per-CHARACTER primitive-emblem (distinct feature) composes with § R-STC-FEATURE-FANOUT F-MNEMONIC.

Carve-out: PawnPals (6–8) defaults to `cast` everywhere (ADR-321 Amd 2). Portraits light-bodied (dark army = filter); twins split by square (ADR-321 Amd 3).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-166_WEB_CLONE_CAST_PIECE_SKIN.md` (Amendment 2) + `Docs/RESEARCH_STORYTIME_CAST_PIECE_SKIN_2026-08-20.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_STORYTIME_CHESS_COLORING_BOOK_LIFT_2026-08-21.md`.
## A board-game clone MAY carry CAST PEEK — a transient hover(desktop)/focus(keyboard)/tap(touch) reveal of the DN cast member who embodies each piece TYPE over the STANDARD board (portrait + movement mnemonic); reuses the ADR-166 map (zero gen), WCAG 1.4.13, board-tap-safe, on-device toggle NO currency; AND every piece-identity overlay/reveal resolves to a SINGLE character per type — an ensemble "A-and-B" portrait is a defect (R-WEB-CLONE-CAST-PEEK + R-CAST-PIECE-SINGLE-CHARACTER; 2026-08-22)

**A `/play/<app>` board-game clone MAY carry CAST PEEK — the shared `_shared/castPeek.ts` shell (`attachCastPeek`, one call; `resolveCastPeek`/`auditCastPeek`/`classifyPortraitIdentity`) that on a deliberate HOVER (fine pointer) · keyboard FOCUS · LONG-PRESS (touch, ~450ms) of a piece reveals a transient card of the DN cast member who embodies that piece TYPE (portrait + one-line movement MNEMONIC, R-DN-PARITY / STC F-MNEMONIC), vanishing on leave/blur/Esc/tap-away. It KEEPS the STANDARD pieces (the truer digital analog of Story Time Chess's REMOVABLE cutout than ADR-166's persistent swap), REUSES the ADR-166 `CastPieceMap` (ZERO paid gen), and COMPOSES with ADR-166 (active in `pieces`(default)+`hybrid`, SUPPRESSED in full `cast`). Non-waivable invariants (each a dark-route-footing defect): WCAG 2.2 §1.4.13 (dismissible/persistent + keyboard-focus + touch-long-press; hoverable deliberately waived — the card is a TOOLTIP, see amendment) · **BOARD-TAP UNBROKEN — a plain TAP is NEVER a peek trigger; the board owns it (select/move). Peek fires ONLY on a fine-pointer HOVER, keyboard FOCUS, or a touch LONG-PRESS (which swallows just its own trailing click, never also-moving); a touch `pointerenter`/`click` must NOT reveal (touch has no hover). This is the touch tap-vs-move DISAMBIGUATION — the founder-flagged mobile "a tap activates cast peek OR a move?" (✅ RESOLVED site #2783, q-70b7d5); pinned by `castPeek.runtime.test.ts` (6 interaction assertions).** · QUIET STAGE (static, R-GUARD-THE-RATIO, reduced-motion-safe) · on-device toggle NO currency (anti-Gimkit / R-SITE-FEEDBACK) · reuse shipped `/cast` portraits · dark-safe `--ff-*`/≥44px, verdict = dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD · archetype-scoped (reuse ADR-166 scoping). NEVER hand-roll a per-clone reveal. `R-CAST-PIECE-SINGLE-CHARACTER` (applies to BOTH Cast Peek AND the ADR-166 skin): every piece TYPE resolves to a SINGLE character — a two-distinct-named ENSEMBLE portrait (`*-and-*`/`twin-*`/"A & B") is a DEFECT (founder-flagged 2026-08-22: gambittales R/B/N duo→solo captain-castle/mason-the-interposer/linnet-the-netweaver), a uniform COHORT collective (`pawn-patrol`) is acceptable; `auditCastPeek` throws deterministically at map load. Fan-out tracked by `check_cast_peek_coverage.py` + `REGISTRY_CAST_PEEK_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B); a `castpeek` feature in the R-BOARD-GAME-FEATURE-REPLICATION family. Founder-direct 2026-08-22.**

**AMENDMENT — the peek is a TOOLTIP, not a hover-CARD: NO interactive content in the transient reveal, and NEVER overload the move gesture (R-WEB-CLONE-CAST-PEEK-NO-INTERACTIVE-REVEAL; founder-flagged 2026-08-22).** "Vanishing on leave/blur/Esc" is only safe for a NON-interactive tooltip (`role=tooltip` = portrait + mnemonic ONLY). Two founder-flagged defects prove the reveal must NEVER carry interactive content NOR share the primary gesture, each a dark-route-footing defect: (a) **DESKTOP** — a `/cast` LINK inside a vanish-on-leave popup is unclickable (pointer-travel fires `mouseleave` → dismiss BEFORE the link) = a direct **WCAG 2.2 §1.4.13 HOVERABLE** violation (the SC this rule *claims*); (b) **TOUCH** — a single TAP is the board's MOVE gesture, so "TAP (touch)" as the peek trigger collides with "BOARD-TAP UNBROKEN". The FIX (one design resolves both): the transient hover/focus reveal stays NON-interactive + auto-dismiss-OK; any `/cast` LINK lives in a **PERSISTENT card opened by a DELIBERATE action** — desktop click/Enter, touch long-press or an explicit "ⓘ"/peek-mode affordance (never the move-tap) — dismissed by Esc/outside-click. If a reveal MUST stay hover-interactive, it is a hover-CARD and MUST implement the full 1.4.13 contract (`closeDelay` grace + the card is a hover target + a safe-polygon bridge [Floating UI `safePolygon`] + Esc + keyboard-reachable link). Fix in the shared `_shared/castPeek.ts` (high-blast → R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA + two-mode touch/pointer Playwright coverage). Tracked: [[q-aaed5e]] (desktop) · [[q-70b7d5]] (touch). **✅ SHIPPED (P#87 s15, site #4300):** card `pointer-events:none` ALWAYS, no control inside; ONE persistent per-app toggle in `.ff-sxs-side`, never the board row. Guards: `check_cast_peek_coverage.py` shell axis + live `probe-board-click-through.mjs`.

**AMENDMENT — Cast Peek is DISABLED on the networked competitive loop (R-CAST-PEEK-NOT-ON-COMPETITIVE-LOOP; founder-flagged 2026-08-30).** Cast Peek is a LEARNING aid ("meet the pieces"); on the NETWORKED duel / party-hub board it collides with the competitive tap-to-move loop (and its toggle mis-mounts on the `.sa-board-host` homepage) — the founder-reported "cast-peek recurring in play-together." A live 2-player competitive game IS the active loop, where § `distributed-narrative.md` R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING says learning aids do NOT belong. So a board renderer's DUEL/party adapter MUST pass `enableCastPeek=false` (the same mechanism ADR-207 uses for Fog-of-War); Cast Peek stays on the solo `/play`+`/learn` surfaces. Fixed on chess `duelView.ts`; the sibling duel adapters that `attachCastPeek` (generalstale/xiangqi · crowntales/draughts · droptale/shogi) are a tracked drain (`check_room_board_party_offset.py`). → ADR-215.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-180_WEB_CLONE_CAST_PEEK.md` + `Docs/ADR-215_PLAY_TOGETHER_BOARD_ROOM_FIXES.md` + `Docs/PLAN_CAST_PEEK_FANOUT_PROGRAM_2026-08-22.md`.
## A real chess engine (Stockfish) may be embedded ONLY as an on-device, OPT-IN, FAIL-SAFE WASM Web-Worker ENHANCEMENT layer — for ANALYSIS + TEACHING + an optional "Boss" tier; NEVER the default kid opponent, NEVER on the active solve loop, NEVER hands over the answer, NEVER replaces the deterministic engine (R-WEB-CLONE-CHESS-ENGINE-WASM; 2026-08-03)

**A `/play` chess/strategy clone MAY embed a real engine (Stockfish 18) ONLY as an ADDITIVE, on-device, OPT-IN, FAIL-SAFE WASM Web-Worker layer via the shared `_shared/chessEngine.ts` (vendored-from-own-origin + Web Worker + gesture-gated opt-in download + graceful fallback). It powers ANALYSIS (boundary-only eval/best-line/blunder review) + TEACHING (articulate-before-hint nudges) + an OPTIONAL skill-limited "Boss" tier. It is a defect on the footing of a dark route to: (a) make full-strength SF the DEFAULT kid opponent (the graded heuristic `chess.ts` stays default — SF blunders statistically not humanly → a poor LEARNING opponent), (b) run an eval bar / engine chatter on the ACTIVE solve loop (§ R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING / § R-GUARD-THE-RATIO — analysis is boundary-only), (c) hand over the engine's move on a LIVE puzzle/hint (§ R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY — a faded step/nudge only), (d) REPLACE the deterministic `chess.ts` (it stays the CI truth + fail-safe fallback), or (e) REQUIRE threads (the threaded lite build runs ONLY on a per-route isolated page; the committed single-thread lite = the floor + any-failure fallback, ADR-297).**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-094` + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A HUMAN-LIKE game engine (a Maia-analog — an on-device NN that predicts human moves rating/rank-matched, making LEARNABLE mistakes) is the pedagogically-correct OPPONENT enhancement for a board-game clone — opt-in · fail-safe · never the default · never replaces the deterministic engine · BAND-CALIBRATED · founder-gated per-engine; a superhuman or above-band opponent is the defect (R-WEB-CLONE-HUMANLIKE-ENGINE; 2026-08-27)

**A `/play` board-game clone's OPPONENT quality is a tracked axis (H): H1 GRADED DETERMINISTIC (the mandatory FLOOR = CI truth + fail-safe, `R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE`) < H2 HUMAN-LIKE NN (a Maia-analog — an on-device ONNX/WASM net predicting human moves at a chosen rating/rank → learnable, ZPD-matched mistakes) < H3 HUMAN-LIKE + SEARCH (piKL/ALLIE; research/founder-gated ceiling). An H2 engine MAY be adopted ONLY as an ADDITIVE layer carrying EVERY ADR-094 invariant, generalised — each a defect on the dark-route footing: (a) ON-DEVICE ONNX/WASM, single-threaded, no COOP/COEP, own-origin/R2-vendored weights (reuse `_shared/maiaEngine.ts` — never hand-roll; `R-WEB-CLONE-SCHOOL-DEVICE-PHYSICS` / `R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE`); (b) OPT-IN + lazy + FAIL-SAFE (default-OFF; any load/timeout/error → the H1 engine); (c) NEVER the default opponent + NEVER replaces H1; (d) NEVER hands over a live-puzzle move + analysis boundary-only (`R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY` / `R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING` / `R-GUARD-THE-RATIO`); (e) 🔴 BAND-CALIBRATED — verified to play AT the tween band's level (rating/rank-condition + move-temperature + imperfect-memory); naive behavioral-cloning "averages away" weakness → a mis-calibrated H2 that plays ABOVE-band is a defect on the superhuman-opponent footing (the human-like mistakes ARE the pedagogy; NEVER fake-blunder — handicap honestly, win ~40–60%, `R-DIR-CAPSTONE-BOARD-GAME` F2); (f) HONEST-YIELD (engagement/matched-difficulty/confidence, NEVER a proven math/IQ/skill-transfer claim; `R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING`). Eligibility-by-archetype (never blanket): H2-TARGET = deep two-player adversarial strategy; H1-SUFFICIENT = simple/solved adversarial with no published human-move NN (NOT a gap); H2-N/A = non-adversarial solo. Adopting an engine beyond the shipped chess/Maia is FOUNDER-GATED + its own ADR-094-shaped decision (`R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE`); web-first, iOS parity = a ForgeKit handoff (hub never writes Swift). Live H2 candidates: Go/KataGo-HumanSL → stonesong (🟢), Xiangqi/XQSV → generalstale (🟡). Tracked by `scripts/check_humanlike_engine_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_HUMANLIKE_ENGINE_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails only on a board-family clone with no row). GENERALISES ADR-094's chess-scoped WASM clause into the fleet-wide human-like-OPPONENT layer; COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) `R-WEB-CLONE-CHESS-ENGINE-WASM` (the orthogonal strong-engine ANALYSIS axis) + `R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE` (the H1 floor) + `R-BOARD-GAME-FEATURE-REPLICATION`.** Founder-direct 2026-08-27; decision `ADR-199`; program #87.

**AMENDMENT — the H2 net's SKILL INPUT + its ONNX-in-a-worker LOADING are three silent-defect axes proven on a real device (R-WEB-CLONE-HUMANLIKE-ENGINE-IO; q-5d4d68 Whetstone → q-d4be7b kids-fleet, 2026-09-25); each builds GREEN + passes every non-inference gate:** (1) 🔴 SKILL INPUT = the model's ACTUAL trained encoding, VERIFIED BY A HEADLESS PROBE, NEVER ASSUMED — maia3 takes the RAW Elo (`clampElo`→`[400,2600]`, floor lowered by s4), NOT the 0-10 `eloToCategory` bucket (which made it a ~7-Elo queen-hanger); a different net differs (reversi has NO skill input) — confirm each via a legal-move-masked `onnxruntime` probe (the `workflow.md` § R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA empirical gate). 🔴 **CONVERSION corollary (generalized 2026-09-25, stonesong Go/KataGo-HumanSL q-87d4ba):** when SELF-CONVERTING a rank/skill-conditioned net to ONNX, the exporter MUST EXPOSE the skill input as a traced tensor — a stock converter often traces only the standard inputs + DROPS it, and a TWO-SKILL functional probe (two ranks → the policy MUST DIFFER, e.g. L1>0.01) MUST confirm the skill signal survived export BEFORE adoption — a structurally-present-but-functionally-dead skill input is the conversion-time twin of the assumed-encoding defect (→ ADR-201 §CONVERSION RESOLVED). (2) WORKER VAR — `importScripts(ort…)` declares a global `ort`; a top-level `let ort` collides ("Can't create duplicate variable") → use `ortRT`, read `self.ort`. (3) SAME-ORIGIN ORT — ORT (importScripts'd from the CDN) resolves its `.mjs` glue via a dynamic `import()` whose `import.meta.url` base is the CDN, so WebKit blocks the cross-origin MODULE path (`import()` / `new Worker({type:'module'})`) **EVEN WITH VALID CDN CORS**. Serve `/vendor/…` same-origin WITHOUT committing the ~11.8 MB runtime (`R-SITE-HISTORY-PURGE`): build-time-copy from R2 into a GITIGNORED `public/vendor/onnxruntime-web/`; the ~45 MB MODEL stays a CDN CORS `fetch`. 🔴 VERSION-LOCK — loader+glue+binary MUST be ONE matched ORT build (1.23.0); `wasmPaths` CANNOT bridge versions. Opt-in bots fail-safe to graded (→ a real iOS/Safari device is the verify gate, `R-ROOM-RELAY-LIVE-VERIFY` sibling). (4) THREADS — a worker gates multi-thread on `self.crossOriginIsolated` (`_shared/ortThreads.ts`), never a hard `numThreads = 1`; (5) 🔴 WORKER COEP — on an isolated page a dedicated worker whose OWN script response lacks COEP is REFUSED (`ERR_BLOCKED_BY_RESPONSE`, fail-safe hides it: #4197 silently disabled every homepage bot) → `/vendor/<dir>/*` serves COEP; verify a route by STARTING its worker, not just `crossOriginIsolated`; (6) Safari has no COEP `credentialless` → isolating Safari needs `require-corp`, which REFUSES any cross-origin no-cors host without CORP → the flip is gated on every such host sending `CORP: cross-origin` (CDN zone rule, shipped 2026-09-29; `check_cdn_corp_for_isolation.py`; Playwright `route.fulfill` BYPASSES COEP, so only a native-header test proves it). GENERALIZES to every ONNX/H2 consumer + its worker. Guarded `scripts/check_humanlike_engine_io.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_HUMANLIKE_ENGINE_IO.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, 6 axes incl. `worker-skill-feed` [ADR-199 Amd 3] + `worker-threads`/`worker-coep` [p87 s10], reads sibling origin; `--ci-mode` fails on a regression). Shipped fleet-wide (site #4153+#4154; Whetstone q-5d4d68). Detail → `Docs/RESEARCH_MAIA_SAFARI_CORS_SAME_ORIGIN_2026-09-25.md` + `Docs/queue/q-5d4d68.md` + `Docs/queue/q-d4be7b.md` + ADR-094/199/200.

**AMENDMENT (2026-09-27, #87):** §4 calibration is BIDIRECTIONAL (net can sit above OR below the graded floor — temperature vs piKL-λ) and must verify the net TERMINATES a game; record `band-calibrated:` (guarded). Detail → REFERENCE § this heading + ADR-201/202 §4.

**AMENDMENT (2026-09-28, #87 s4) — strength ≠ human-like:** a tier knob is ALSO human-likeness-checked — prefer the skill input or a graded-MIX over a hot T (which can flatten the policy into non-human noise); calibrate vs the REAL graded bot, never a proxy; every shipped knob has an `artifacts/<game>/calibration.json` record, enforced by `scripts/check_humanlike_calibration_drift.py` (`--ci-mode` fails DRIFT/UNRECORDED/UNSHIPPED, and MODEL-DRIFT when the served model file ≠ the recorded `served_model`). A smaller build = quantize the incumbent first + a paired-fidelity gate (int8 Maia-3 GO, Amd 5). Detail → ADR-199 Amendment 2/5.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-199_HUMANLIKE_GAME_ENGINE_PROGRAM.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_HUMANLIKE_GAME_ENGINES_2026-08-27.md` + `Docs/PLAN_HUMANLIKE_GAME_ENGINE_PROGRAM_2026-08-27.md`.
## A board-game clone AUTO-SAVES every played game on-device + offers a BOUNDARY, ARCHETYPE-TIERED, PREDICT-BEFORE-REVEAL engine review — never a server archive, never a fabricated verdict, never a label-dump (R-WEB-CLONE-GAME-SAVE-ANALYSIS; 2026-08-27)

**Every eligible board/strategy `/play/<app>` clone AUTO-PERSISTS each finished game (versus-bot · hotseat · duel) to an on-device `<ns>.games.v1` store (globally-UNIQUE ns, SIBLING of `.progress.v1`/`.records.v1`, never mutates them; no account/network/PII/identifier — a stronger COPPA privacy moat than lichess/chess.com/ChessKid) via the SHARED `_shared/gameSave.ts` `makeGameStore(ns)` (never hand-rolled), surfaced as a "My games" list (replay · analyze · delete-one · delete-all; read by the ADR-176 device-wide records aggregator; `deleteAllDeviceData` sweeps `.games.v1`). AUTO-SAVE is UNIFORM across all games; the ENGINE ANALYSIS is ARCHETYPE-TIERED by each shipped engine's eval primitive (the de-naive-feasibility-test's sibling) via the SHARED game-agnostic `_shared/gameAnalysis.ts` (the OPEN lichess Win%/accuracy formulas + gentle celebrate-good-AND-bad classification): **A** full eval-graph+Win% (chess/gambittales +Stockfish WASM, xiangqi, draughts) · **B** EXACT (connect-4 solved, reversi endgame — needs a true solver) · **C** stochastic-rough, NO accuracy headline (backgammon) · **D** NONE on-device → descriptive-only. Hard invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: ON-DEVICE only; BOUNDARY-placed (never an eval bar on the active solve loop — R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING / R-GUARD-THE-RATIO / ADR-094(b)); the deterministic engine is the truth (WASM is additive/opt-in/fail-safe/single-threaded, R-WEB-CLONE-SCHOOL-DEVICE-PHYSICS); **PREDICT-BEFORE-REVEAL not a label-dump** ("labels are conclusions, not lessons" — ask "what was better here?" choice-framed BEFORE the reveal, compose R-WEB-CLONE-POE + R-AI-MENTOR-INTEGRITY articulate-before-hint + R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TUTOR Socratic); GENTLE celebrate-good-AND-bad labels (never "Blunder??", verdict never by colour alone); INTRAPERSONAL only (never a leaderboard/Elo-shame — anti-Gimkit); **a Tier-D FABRICATED verdict, an over-claimed "GM analysis" for a heuristic engine, a Tier-B "EXACT" label on a bounded search, a server/account archive, or an on-solve-loop eval bar is a DEFECT** (honest-yield — the 3.6× self-analysis efficiency is chess-specific SUPPORTING data, never a proven per-app outcome). Archetype-scoped; iOS = a `HANDOFF_FROM_HUB_*` spec (hub never writes Swift). EXTENDS R-BOARD-GAME-FEATURE-REPLICATION #16 "engine analysis" + RIDES ADR-094. Reference-first gridforge/Connect-4 (Tier-A strong-heuristic; true Tier-B EXACT solver = a documented future enhancement). Tracked `scripts/check_game_save_analysis_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_GAME_SAVE_ANALYSIS_COVERAGE.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN).** Founder-direct 2026-08-27; decision `ADR-205`; program #91 (origin #83).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-205_GAME_SAVE_AND_ENGINE_ANALYSIS.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_GAME_SAVE_ANALYSIS_2026-08-27.md` + `Docs/queue/q-aff07d.md`.
## A board/strategy-game clone = PURE engine + Vitest-pinned + a GRADED HEURISTIC AI (not a strong solver) + engine-verified puzzle legality (R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE; 2026-07-16)

**A `/play/<app>` clone that ships a real playable board/strategy game (chess/draughts/Connect-4/Reversi/Gomoku/Hex/Dots-&-Boxes/backgammon/shogi/…) is built as a PURE, exported engine (`legalMoves`/`applyMove`/`winner`/`chooseMove` — no DOM) with a Vitest invariant spec; a thin `run<Game>()` UI mounts it; the AI opponent is a GRADED HEURISTIC sized as a credible KID opponent (NOT a strong/perfect solver); and any position-PUZZLE surface's stated solution is cross-checked LEGAL + BEST against that same engine. Dark-mode-safe board by construction; quiet stage (colour rides pieces, no ambient motion). This ties together the per-axis sub-rules below — it does NOT replace them.**

→ **Sub-rules (defined here; full body → reference):**
- **R-BOARD-GAME-COMPLETE-DRAW** (2026-08-09, ADR-105) — the engine MUST detect the FULL draw set (chess/draughts/xiangqi: checkmate · stalemate · threefold/fivefold repetition · fifty-move · insufficient material), tracking a key history + halfmove clock in `makeMove`, wired into EVERY mode's game-over check; end-detection is the ENGINE's job, never the move-chooser's; a canonical library is a test oracle only; Vitest-pin each rule. A draw constant no board reads fails `check-draw-rule-wired.mjs`.
  - **⚠ MISSING DRAW-DETECTION = a NON-TERMINATING game = a CI-flaky self-play test (2026-08-30, ADR-215).** An engine whose `outcome()` returns only ongoing/checkmate/stalemate (NO repetition/no-progress draw) lets a game LOOP FOREVER — a REAL "bot match never ends" user bug AND the root cause of an intermittent CI failure: a graded-bot SELF-PLAY test that breaks RNG ties with `Math.random` depends on the GLOBAL RNG state, which varies in the full parallel suite, so ~20% of trajectories hit a perpetual/repetition → the test's bounded loop exits with `undefined` → `expected undefined to be defined` (passes in isolation, flakes in the full sweep — the RNG-ordering sibling of R-ZSH-NO-WORD-SPLIT: a green-in-isolation test that's wrong under real conditions). TWO standing disciplines: (a) the ENGINE detects the full draw set so EVERY game terminates (xiangqi was the reference gap — added a 120-ply no-capture `noCap` counter → `{kind:'draw'}`, generalstale, site #3058); (b) a graded-bot self-play TEST SEEDS its RNG (`vi.spyOn(Math,'random').mockImplementation(seededPrng)`) so it is DETERMINISTIC + suite-order-independent, with a generous ply bound (≈ pieces × no-progress-cap), NEVER a tight bound that hides non-termination. Audit: chess/shogi/connect4/oware detect draws; **crowntales/draughts has 0 draw-signals = a suspected same-gap** (tracked drain, `q-playtogether-boardroom`).
  - **⚠ GENERALIZES beyond adversarial engines to ANY game/SIM loop — incl. a Monte-Carlo race SIM with an UNSATISFIABLE win condition (2026-08-31, ADR-257).** tableforge `design.ts simulate()` had a naked `while (winner<0)` with NO cap + an `exactFinish` win UNSATISFIABLE for a randomizer whose min single move is >1 (2d6 min 2 can't land one-short) → the loop spins FOREVER (a real multi-hour 100%-CPU runaway, worsened by running it in backgrounded sweeps). INVARIANT: EVERY game/sim/search loop (tooling OR product) MUST be BOUNDED by a hard turn/ply/iteration cap (bail to leader/draw) — a naked `while(winner<0)`/`while(true)`/`while(!done)` is the bug whether the end condition is a missing draw OR an unsatisfiable win — AND carry a TERMINATION test (a bounded self-play that RETURNS on the pathological config; removing the cap hangs the suite, not a browser). Fixed: `design.ts MAX_TURNS` + `design.test.ts` termination test + `challenges.ts isHangRisk()` UI pre-guard. ORCHESTRATION half (never background an uncapped loop) → `workflow.md` § R-BOUNDED-COMPUTE-NO-BACKGROUND-RUNAWAY. Guard: `scripts/check_bounded_game_loops.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_BOUNDED_GAME_LOOPS.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails on a NEW unbounded `/play` engine loop beyond baseline).
- **R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-ENGINE-VERIFIED** (2026-07-17) — a position-puzzle bank's solution is legality-checked AND correctness-checked (quiescence-aware search): the intended move is a clean BEST non-trivial material win / forced mate (not suboptimal, doesn't hang, actually wins, not replaceable by a simpler free capture); author from a deterministic seeded GENERATOR, never hand-constructed sparse positions.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-SIDE-TO-MOVE** (2026-07-25) — side-to-move + board ORIENTATION derive from the FEN active colour (never assume "white to move"); a mixed-corpus bank (Lichess CC0 ≈40% black-to-move) makes an all-white assumption a latent defect; Vitest-pin the solution's first move belongs to `sideToMove(fen)`.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-TILING-EXACT-COVER** (2026-08-09) — a tiling/dissection manipulative's exact-cover is a pure-engine Vitest invariant (sample on an INCOMMENSURATE grid — irrational x/y step+offset — so no sample lands on a shared rational edge → no phantom gaps/overlaps); drawing the engine-verified polygons makes the render correct-by-construction.
- **R-SVG-FILL-STYLE-NOT-ATTR** (2026-08-09) — a JS-created SVG element's fill that must beat a base CSS `fill:` rule MUST be set via inline `.style.fill`, NOT `setAttribute('fill',…)` (a presentation attribute loses to any CSS rule → transparent render); build/Vitest are blind, only the screenshot-DoD catches it.
- **R-WEB-CLONE-SVG-TILE-HIGHLIGHT-ZORDER** (2026-08-30) — a tiled-`<rect>` board whose SELECTION/highlight is an SVG `stroke` on the square rect (chess `.gbt-sq--sel`/`--from`, and every board renderer using the same pattern) MUST RAISE the highlighted rect above its neighbours (`svg.insertBefore(rect, pieceLayer)`) when the highlight class is added — because the squares are painted in idx order, so a highlighted square's centered stroke is COVERED on the 2 sides facing its later-drawn higher-idx neighbours → "only 2 sides of the highlight show" (founder-reported). Raise on select/reveal/flash (still BELOW the piece layer so a piece renders on top); re-lowering on clear is unnecessary (an un-highlighted rect is inert). build/Vitest DOM-order-pinnable + screenshot-DoD (all 4 sides, both schemes). Fixed on chess `board.ts`; the sibling tiled-rect renderers (draughts/xiangqi/go/backgammon/strategy) are a tracked worst-first drain (`check_room_board_party_offset.py` DRAIN). → ADR-215.
- Plus 6 implementation-recipe invariants → reference: **graded-heuristic-AI-not-a-solver** (match search to branching; beam-limit huge-branching) · **POE/guess-the-move target determinism** (unique principled rank, never a random-tie `chooseMove`) · **pointer-events fallthrough** (every SVG overlay `pointer-events:none`) · **promotion-choice solvability** (every legal transform keeps the puzzle solvable) · **native two-human HOTSEAT** (additive `{mode}` over an EXISTING vs-AI loop) · **spawn-app board clones** (maximal web-pioneer → `_WEB_BACKPORT` handoff + ForgeKit-lift candidate).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A board that renders pieces as chess/symbol UNICODE GLYPHS coloured by CSS `fill` MUST be mobile-emoji-font-SAFE — split the codepoints (outline vs solid) + force text presentation (VS15 + `font-variant-emoji:text`), or the colour-emoji font paints every piece solid black and the fill is ignored (R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GLYPH-MOBILE; 2026-08-01)

> **⏭ PLANNED UPGRADE → SVG PIECE SETS (V836)** — ChessKid/Lichess-style SVG piece PATHS + on-device set picker (the "most bulletproof alternative" this rule names). Licensing gate: Lichess sets OPEN (attribution); ChessKid art PROPRIETARY (ship our own style, never their SVGs). Vitest-pin the `{color}{role}`→asset map; screenshot-DoD. Full spec → reference / work-queue V836.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A manipulative whose TEXT claims a piece spans N faces/parts/colours MUST HIGHLIGHT across those N faces/parts — a single-face highlight that contradicts the text is a render-vs-copy defect (R-WEB-CLONE-MULTI-PART-HIGHLIGHT; 2026-08-01)
