**When a manipulative's copy asserts a piece/object spans MULTIPLE faces/parts/colours (a Rubik's corner "3 faces meet → 3 colours"; an edge "2 faces"; a molecule's bonds), the interactive HIGHLIGHT illustrating it MUST span those same real parts — never a single-face/single-part proxy; gray-out MASKING the rest makes the one multi-part piece pop. A highlight that rings N stickers on ONE face while the text says "3 faces meet" is a render-contradicts-copy defect. Map the highlight to the piece's REAL geometry (derive from the engine's validated model, e.g. cube.ts's Kociemba facelet scheme, not by eyeballing the net); where a flat 2-D depiction can't show "N faces meeting" add the 3-D/rotatable view (§ R-WEB-CLONE-FLEX-PERCENT-WIDTH-COLLAPSE guards its container); pin the highlight geometry with a Vitest invariant. Only the pixels (screenshot-DoD) catch a highlight-vs-copy mismatch.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A puzzle bank prefers a CANONICAL, FREE, human-vetted REAL-GAME corpus over a synthetic generator — still engine-verified (R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-CANONICAL-SOURCE; 2026-07-24)

**When a `/play` clone needs a bank of position-puzzles (chess tactics, and by analogy any domain with a public real-example corpus), PREFER importing a curated, difficulty-filtered subset from a CANONICAL, FREE (CC0/public-domain), human/engine-VETTED REAL-EXAMPLE corpus over hand-authoring or synthesizing positions — because engine-verified-correct (legal + non-hanging + best) is necessary but NOT sufficient for INSTRUCTIVE: a synthetic 4-piece toy position is material-correct yet pedagogically naive (no real-game transfer). The imported bank MUST still pass the clone's existing material-correctness gate (R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-ENGINE-VERIFIED) — real puzzles are not exempt from engine verification. A clone shipping a synthetic/generated position-puzzle bank when a canonical CC0 real-game corpus exists (and would pass the same gate) is a quality defect on the founder-flagged "naive" footing.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-24 (*"the puzzles in gambittales are still very naive. find canonical and free puzzles on the web if possible"* + *"deep web research … think harder … better tooling"*), after replacing GambitTales' 119 synthetic puzzles (avg **4.5 pieces** on board — toy positions) with 150 real-game tactics (avg **18.8 pieces**) from the **Lichess open puzzle DB (CC0)**.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/RESEARCH_AND_AUDIT_GAMBITTALES_PUZZLE_SOURCE_2026-07-24.md`.
## The portfolio keeps a standing REAL PUZZLE & GAME CORPUS LIBRARY in the hub — collect free/CC0/PD real DBs per genre (positions AND master games) into `Resources/PuzzleCorpus/`, commit the CURATED pool + MANIFEST (raw gitignored), so future sessions MINE them the way chess mined Lichess (R-PUZZLE-CORPUS-LIBRARY; 2026-08-27)

**The hub maintains a standing CORPUS LIBRARY — free/CC0/public-domain real game+puzzle databases collected per genre into `Resources/PuzzleCorpus/<genre>/` — so a sampler/clone bank de-naives from a REAL corpus instead of a synthetic generator (generalizes § R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-CANONICAL-SOURCE from chess-only to the fleet). Two corpus CLASSES: `puzzle` (position/puzzle banks) + `master` (full master/tournament GAME collections — for future features: opening trainers, game replay/review, "study a master game"). Load-bearing invariants, each a defect: (1) HYGIENE — commit ONLY the bounded curated pool (per-source ≤8MB, total ≤80MB, guard-enforced) + a per-source `MANIFEST.<id>.json` (url·license·sha256·count·schema·retrieval-cmd·date); the raw multi-GB dump goes to `Resources/PuzzleCorpus/_raw/` (GITIGNORED, reproducibly re-downloadable via `scripts/collect_puzzle_corpus.py`) — NEVER commit a raw dump (the ADR-047 re-bloat lesson). (2) LICENSE — game records/positions/solved-outcomes are FACTS (not copyrightable) → collectable EVEN from an UNLICENSED / `license:None` / NOASSERTION DB (**founder-direct 2026-08-27 "databases with no licenses. go"**), recording honest provenance (source URL + stated terms) in the MANIFEST; prefer CC0/PD/permissive where available; a genre blocked ONLY by a missing in-env TOOL gets the tool INSTALLED, not deferred (**founder-direct 2026-08-27 "install the tools"** — e.g. `py7zr` for the shogi floodgate `.7z`; a collector RUNTIME tool, raw gitignored/reproducible, a documented exception to the hub's zero-committed-dep posture); only an EXPLICITLY-RESTRICTED source (RenjuNet NC) or a genuinely-unavailable clean bulk SOURCE stays documented `# CORPUS-PENDING:` — never faked. (3) MINING ≠ COLLECTING — this LIBRARY collects; wiring a corpus into a bank is a separate future drain, and every mined item STILL passes the shipped engine gate (§ R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-ENGINE-VERIFIED — a real position is not exempt). (4) COMPLETENESS — every sampler-mining genre has a COLLECTED corpus OR a documented `PENDING`/`N/A` disposition (R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS); a genre with neither is silently forgotten. Guard `scripts/check_puzzle_corpus_coverage.py` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` fails only on a regression — a COLLECTED pool gone missing / over-budget) + `Docs/REGISTRY_PUZZLE_CORPUS_COVERAGE.txt`. Baseline 2026-08-27: 11 COLLECTED (chess·connect-4·reversi·sudoku·word·go·riddle·xiangqi·draughts·shogi·gomoku) · 1 PENDING (backgammon — no bulk published archive; sampler needs engine-labeled decisions) · 10 N/A (nonogram reclassified N/A = engine-derivable, hex/dots-boxes precedent).** Founder-direct 2026-08-27 (*"collect real games/puzzles databases online and save in the repo for further mining in the future … include game databases for master games too … deep web research and think harder"*); decision `ADR-196`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-196_REAL_PUZZLE_GAME_CORPUS_LIBRARY.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_REAL_PUZZLE_GAME_CORPUS_2026-08-27.md` + `Docs/PLAN_REAL_PUZZLE_GAME_CORPUS_PROGRAM_2026-08-27.md` + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A historical-game BANK (ClassicsForge / any clone rendering real recorded games) ships a game only when it clears the 4-part CONTENT BAR — ≥2-source-byte-identical (≥1 genuinely independent) · engine-cross-verified · divergences canonical+tagged (never a 2nd move list) · public-domain — with a below-bar game HELD-and-tracked and a corrupt lineage text blacklist-guarded (R-HISTORICAL-GAME-BANK-PROVENANCE; 2026-09-16)

**A `/play` clone that renders REAL recorded historical games as a curated bank (ClassicsForge = the reference — Program #187/ADR-270 AnvilReader; and any ScanForge annotated-game shelf · PageForge-chess · #128 masters-game study) ships a game ONLY when it clears the four-part CONTENT BAR, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) the full move list is BYTE-IDENTICAL across ≥2 sources, ≥1 GENUINELY INDEPENDENT (a non-database book/article/Wikipedia OR a founder-supplied engine-validated python-chess/Stockfish derivation — two shared-root ChessBase lineages are "no known divergence," NOT two independent sources); (2) ENGINE-CROSS-VERIFIED — the SAN replays cleanly through the shipped reader/chess engine (`games.test.ts`, the correctness oracle: illegal/ambiguous throws; disambiguation/castling/promotion honored), reaches the claimed result, and for a founder-brief game reaches board positions BYTE-IDENTICAL to the brief's independent FENs (two engines agreeing = the DECISIVE gate, stronger than a 2nd fetchable DB that doesn't exist for a fetchability-gapped game); (3) DIVERGENCES → CANONICAL + TAGGED (the termination-variant rule) — a termination-point split or one-square hand-copy transposition ships the canonical line and NOTES the alternate in first-party commentary, NEVER a 2nd move list, never guessed; (4) PUBLIC-DOMAIN (`year < 1930` gate). ⚠ A copy repeating the archive's headers is not a second lineage (ADR-291 §Amd16). A game below the bar is HELD-and-TRACKED (never silently forgotten — R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS); a corrupt lineage text that is engine-LEGAL but a mis-transcription is BLACKLIST-guarded by a `games.test` assertion of the canonical move (R-TEST-INTEGRITY — assert the CORRECT fact); commentary is FIRST-PARTY calm-register (ADR-270 inv-5, never a book's annotations); verdict = the dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD (correct winner-side orientation, dark-safe board, task-valid, 0 console err). COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) § R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE / -PUZZLE-ENGINE-VERIFIED (the engine oracle) · § R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-CANONICAL-SOURCE (canonical-over-synthetic) · § R-PUZZLE-CORPUS-LIBRARY. Tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_CLASSICSFORGE_HELD_GAMES.txt` + `scripts/check_classicsforge_held_games.py` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` cross-checks the held list against the shipped bank on origin/staging — a resolved game flipped, a regression caught).** Founder-direct 2026-09-16 (the v2 held-games brief); decision `Docs/ADR-270_SCAN_READ_RETAIN_CONCEPTS_PROGRAM.md` § Content-provenance methodology; Program #187.

## A schematic TEACHING DIAGRAM of a spatial pattern is an engine/model-verified CANONICAL example rendered as a signalling-CUED, cropped-to-the-chunk schematic — NEVER a bare un-annotated depiction, and never a generic (merely-legal) position (R-WEB-CLONE-SCHEMATIC-TEACHING-DIAGRAM; 2026-08-03)

**Any `/play` surface showing a small SCHEMATIC DIAGRAM to teach the SHAPE of a spatial pattern/relationship (a chess tactic's "essence"; a geometry angle/vertex relationship; a molecule's bonds; a circuit loop) MUST render it as (a) a CANONICAL recognisable example (the textbook archetype, not a generic merely-legal construction), (b) CROPPED to the tight chunk, (c) with SIGNALLING CUES drawn OVER the geometry (arrows/highlighted line/target marks/change cue), (d) a shape+label LEGEND, and (e) ENGINE/MODEL-VERIFIED + Vitest-pinned (legal/valid AND the annotation is TRUE). A bare un-annotated depiction (a signalling failure for novices) or a generic merely-legal example (the "legal ≠ canonical" defect) is a defect on the footing of a failed dark-mode axis. The teaching-diagram sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-CANONICAL-SOURCE + § R-WEB-CLONE-PUZZLE-ENGINE-VERIFIED.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## A board legality RELAY is promotion-piece-TOLERANT + applies via the UCI (source of truth); never re-queen an underpromotion (R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-UNDERPROMOTION; 2026-09-06)

**Any chess/board legality RELAY or move-consumer (the pass-&-play `onMove`, the networked room adapter's `isLegal`/`apply`, a vs-bot match loop) MUST be promotion-piece-TOLERANT and MUST apply the move by its UCI (the source of truth), because the shared board picker (ADR-213 / R-WEB-CLONE-PROMOTION-CHOICE) emits the learner's CHOSEN promotion — `e7e8r`/`e7e8b`/`e7e8n` — but the move generator (`allLegalMoves` via `uciFor`) enumerates ONLY the `=Q` form (`e7e8q`). Two silent-defect patterns are BANNED: (1) a raw `allLegalMoves(pos).some(m => m.uci === uci)` legality check — it rejects EVERY underpromotion, so the picked piece is dropped and the move just doesn't happen (queen "works", knight/rook/bishop "does nothing"); (2) applying a move via `makeMove(pos, from, to)` after parsing `from`/`to` out of a UCI — it defaults `promo='q'` and silently RE-QUEENS the learner's choice. Use the shared `chess.ts isLegalUciMove(pos, uci)` (exact-or-4-char-base match) for legality and `applyUci(pos, uci)` for application; a match loop must carry the human's FULL uci into the picked `Move`, not the found `=Q` move.** Codified per founder-report 2026-09-06 ("pawn promotion to a piece different from a queen still not working in the homepage sampler deck") after the ADR-213 picker fix shipped the CHOICE but the DOWNSTREAM consumers still rejected/re-queened it on all three homepage chess surfaces (pass-&-play + networked duel via `duelChess.isLegalUci`; vs-bot MatchSampler via `matchChess`). **Do NOT "fix" this by expanding `allLegalMoves` to enumerate all four promotions** — that perturbs the AI eval / mobility heuristic / the `puzzles.test.ts` puzzle-correctness gate / the `essence.test:228` "exactly one reply" assertion (wide blast radius); the tolerant relay + uci-apply is the DRY, zero-blast-radius fix. Guard: `src/lib/play/gambittales/underpromotion.test.ts` (pins `isLegalUciMove` + `CHESS_CORE` + `matchChess.chessCore` across q/r/b/n). Sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE (engine-verified legality) + § R-WEB-CLONE-PROMOTION-CHOICE (the picker that emits the choice this rule must honor).

## The networked board FIRST MOVER is unlocked BEFORE the server's first turn frame — a null `turnSeat` defaults to the first mover, never a locked White (R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-FIRST-MOVER; 2026-09-06)

**On the networked room board (`_shared/room.ts` + `roomBoard.ts`), the whose-turn / interactivity gate MUST treat a not-yet-assigned `turnSeat` (still `null` before the server broadcasts its first `turn` frame) as the game's FIRST MOVER (seat 0 / White) — via the pure `isSeatTurn(seat, turnSeat) = seat !== null && seat === (turnSeat ?? 0)` — so the first mover can make the OPENING move. A STRICT `seat === turnSeat` reads `0 === null` → false → White is stuck "waiting for your friend" at game start AND `room.move()` silently no-ops off-turn → the opening move is impossible.** Codified per founder-report 2026-09-06 ("in a chess game, the player with white pieces can't make the first move" in the homepage play-by-code lobby) — the prior party-seat fix (`0e22f998d`) reconciled seat ASSIGNMENT (which already defaulted `turnSeat ?? 0`) but never the interactivity/move GATE, which strict-compared against the raw null. This is party-SAFE: party/hub flows set `turnSeat` (the picker's seat) before the board mounts, so `?? 0` only ever triggers on a fresh direct duel's opening; the server remains the turn AUTHORITY (it rejects a genuinely off-turn move), so the default only unlocks the CORRECT first mover's UI, never the wrong side. Guard: `src/lib/play/_shared/room.test.ts` (pins `isSeatTurn` first-mover-at-null + strict semantics after a real turn frame). Sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER / § R-WEB-CLONE-SOCIAL-MODES (the room-board relay this hardens).

## A ported LEVEL/PROGRAM bank is FILTERED to provably-solvable + Vitest-pinned; a block-coding clone is a HAND-WRITTEN deterministic interpreter, never a JS eval (R-WEB-CLONE-PORTED-PUZZLE-SOLVABILITY; 2026-07-16)

**When a `/play` clone ports a LEVEL / MAZE / program-execution bank from its iOS app (grid puzzles, robot-programming levels, any bank with a "is this solvable?" property), the porter MUST filter the bank to PROVABLY-SOLVABLE entries with a solver (BFS over the state space) + pin solvability with a Vitest invariant, and any excluded malformed source entry MUST be documented in the parity ledger — never shipped. AND: a block-coding / robot-command clone's execution engine is a PURE, HAND-WRITTEN deterministic interpreter (exported for Vitest), NEVER a JS `eval` / `Function()`.** This is the LEVEL/execution sibling of R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE's "engine-verified puzzle legality" (that rule pins a position-puzzle's `correctMove ∈ legalMoves`; this pins a whole level as *reachable* + the interpreter as *faithful + safe*). Codified from the CodeRealm build (2026-07-16), where **6 of 30 shipped iOS levels were malformed** — Bit starts walled-in / the goal is unreachable via the builder's forward+turn palette — and would have shipped as **unplayable-but-green** clones (they build, smoke-pass, and *look* fine; only a solver catches them). This is the DATA-level form of the R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD "looks-right-but-unplayable" trap: the eye can miss an unsolvable grid, a BFS cannot.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every clone declares a GRADE BAND + LEVEL, shown on /play and sorted within its cluster (R-WEB-CLONE-GRADE-LEVEL; 2026-07-13)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone MUST declare a grade band + a level in its per-app registry entry (`src/data/play/<app>/clone.meta.ts`), the `/play` index MUST render a grade-band + level chip on each clone card, and clones MUST be sorted within each subject/cluster section by grade band then level (youngest/easiest first).** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-13 (*"add grade band and level for each web clone on the /play page and sort the web clones by level grade bands for each subject/cluster"* + *"codify the grade band and level rule for all future web clone builds"*). Standing requirement for **every** clone — current (backfill) AND all future builds.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Within a subject cluster, clones sort in PEDAGOGICAL (curricular-progression) order — a curated per-clone rank, not alphabetical and not merely grade-band+level (R-WEB-CLONE-PEDAGOGICAL-ORDER; 2026-07-23)

**Within each `/play` subject cluster, clones MUST be ordered by their CURRICULAR / PEDAGOGICAL PROGRESSION — the sequence in which the concepts build on one another (prerequisite/learning-path order) — NOT alphabetically and NOT merely by grade-band+difficulty-level. This is a curricular JUDGMENT the hub agent makes IN-SESSION (exactly like the `R-WEB-CLONE-CLUSTER-MAP` cluster classification) and records explicitly as a per-clone `pedagogicalOrder` rank in the clone's registry entry (`src/data/play/<app>/clone.meta.ts`); the `/play` subject-cluster sort keys on it FIRST, then grade-band+level (R-WEB-CLONE-GRADE-LEVEL) as the tiebreak, then alphabetical last. It is MOST load-bearing for the 9–14 age zone (the band with the 9 rich subject clusters — R-WEB-CLONE-AGE-BAND-ZONES); the 3–5 / 6–8 zones are subject-minimal/flat, so the progression order matters less there (grade-band+level is sufficient).** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-23 (*"sort the webclones in pedagogical order in their subject cluster … especially for the 9-14 age band … codify these"*). This is the clone-index analog of `R-DN-CAST-ORDER` (which sorts a cast/story list by authored ARC order, never alphabetical): the browse ORDER of a subject IS a pedagogical signal, so a learner opening "Math" should see a coherent learning path (e.g. number sense → place value → operations → fractions → ratio/proportion → pre-algebra → geometry → probability/statistics), not a random or alphabetical jumble.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The hub agent CLASSIFIES a clone's /play cluster by curricular judgment — free-text keyword matching is a fallback, not the source of truth (R-WEB-CLONE-CLUSTER-MAP; 2026-07-14)

**A `/play` clone's subject-cluster grouping (the section it renders under on the `/play` index) is a CURRICULAR CLASSIFICATION the hub agent makes IN-SESSION — recorded explicitly, not left to fragile free-text keyword matching.** The `/play` index groups clones into a fixed pedagogical cluster set — **9 clusters as of V288 (2026-07-17): Math · English & Language Arts · Science · Computer Science · Logic & Puzzles · Social Studies · SEL · Visual Arts & Design · Music & Performing Arts** (the V288 reorg added Computer Science [CSTA] and split the overloaded "Create" → Visual Arts & Design + Music & Performing Arts [National Core Arts]; see `RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_SUBJECT_TAXONOMY_2026-07-16.md` + `AUDIT_WEB_CLONE_SUBJECT_REORG_2026-07-16.md`). The 2026-07-14 narrative below references the pre-V288 7-cluster set (…SEL · **Create**); the order-is-load-bearing principle it establishes is unchanged — it now applies to the new branches (see the ordering note at the end of this rule). Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-14 (*"a couple of english language arts web clones are grouped in the Create cluster … do full audit and fix all the groupings and codify"* + *"should we codify a rule saying that the hub agent must use in-session Opus to classify the web clone for grouping?"* → yes).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## `/play` splits into THREE age-band zones — Ages 3–5 · 6–8 · 9–14 — before subject clusters (R-WEB-CLONE-AGE-BAND-ZONES, formerly R-WEB-CLONE-YOUNGER-CLUSTER-SPLIT; 3-band reband 2026-07-18, ADR-052)

**The `/play` catalog splits by AGE BAND at the top level into THREE zones, rendered top-to-bottom — `Ages 3–5` (Pre-K/K) → `Ages 6–8` (grades 1–3, early elementary) → `Ages 9–14` (grades 4–8, the tween core; no clone exceeds grade 8) — each a labeled band with a divider. The 3–5 and 6–8 zones are subject-MINIMAL (flat, subject as a card badge); the 9–14 zone keeps its 9 subject clusters + youngest-first sort. A clone rendered in the wrong age zone — especially a grade-1–3 clone (incl. grade 3: ReadRise/TimesQuest) intermixed into the 9–14 subject clusters — is a defect.** Rebanded per founder-direct 2026-07-18 (*"rethink the age bands … 3-5 and 6-8 and 9-14 … fix it for the whole portfolio hub and website too and codify"*, ADR-052), superseding the 2-band (5–8 younger / 9–14 tween) model + the ad-hoc grade-3 bridge placement. This sits ABOVE `R-WEB-CLONE-CLUSTER-MAP` (subject grouping) — age is the FIRST split, subject the second (within 9–14).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The deep-research step MUST yield an explicit backport-candidate list — mined CROSS-PLATFORM, "strict port" is earned, never assumed (R-WEB-CLONE-BACKPORT-MINING; 2026-07-11)

**The Phase-2 deep research (`WEB_CLONE_PICKUP_RUNBOOK` § 3.2b) is the designated "well for novel features," and its source landscape is CROSS-PLATFORM — web + iOS + Android + physical/board-game/research — NOT web-only. Every clone's `RESEARCH.md` MUST carry a `## Domain landscape` survey (of the best-in-class in the domain across ALL those surfaces) ending in an explicit, evidence-based `## Backport candidates` list — the best learning-relevant ideas OUR apps (iOS AND web) LACK — and classify EACH one. A conclusion of "strict port → no backport" is VALID ONLY after that list exists and every candidate is classified; it must be EARNED by the mining, NEVER asserted by default.** Codified per user-direct 2026-07-11 (*"the deep web research step should have provided a lot of novel ideas for iOS backport, correct?"* → *"codify this requirement"*; then *"why are we doing deep web research for web-based novel features only? why not including novel features and ideas from ios and android apps too?"* → *"make sure to backfill and also codify it as a rule too"*), after the V95/V97/V98 clones (claimcraft/jestforge/witquest) each defaulted to "strict port" and skipped producing the candidate list — using the research only for positioning, not for its load-bearing backport-discovery purpose — AND after the mining was found to be artificially scoped to the *browser* landscape only.

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-WEB-CLONE-TRACK-B-BUILD-DEFAULT).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Missing question kits are AUTHORED by in-session Opus to match the portfolio — never skipped, never Gemini-gen (R-WEB-CLONE-KITS-OPUS-AUTHOR; 2026-07-14)

**When a clone's source app has NO question-kit banks (a composition-only app, a docs-only app with unwritten kits, or any app lacking the portfolio-standard 16×25 MC set), the kits are AUTHORED FRESH by the in-session Opus model (the running Claude Code session) to match the other portfolio apps — a full 16 kits × 25 = 400 MC items — NOT skipped, NOT waived as "bespoke-only," and NOT generated by Gemini.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-14 (*"you build the question kits and ship them too"* + *"ship the question kits to the ios app repo too"* + *"codify the rule that if question kits are missing, use the in-session Opus to author them to match other portfolio apps"*). This **supersedes** the earlier "a composition-only app is an all-bespoke clone with no MC Concepts surface (⛔-waived, HaikuQuest precedent)" posture — the portfolio standard is that **every** clone has a Concepts MC surface, and a missing bank is an *authoring task*, not a waiver.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Younger-cluster (ages 5-8) apps are EXEMPT from the 16×25 MC-kit obligation — activity-based formats, not multiple-choice banks (R-YOUNGER-CLUSTER-NO-MC-KITS; 2026-07-14)

**The portfolio-standard 16 kits × 25 = 400 CCSS-tagged, Bloom-leveled, TEXT multiple-choice bank MUST NOT be authored for younger-cluster (ages 5-8 / K-2) apps. It is the documented, research-backed ⛔ EXCEPTION to `R-WEB-CLONE-KITS-OPUS-AUTHOR`** — a younger-cluster app with no `kit_*.json` is NOT a coverage gap, and authoring one would ship a developmentally-inappropriate, low-validity surface. Younger-cluster apps use **audio-first, low-text, manipulative/tap/drag** activity formats instead. Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-14 (*"should the younger cluster app repos have the question kits? … codify the research-backed evidence"*), full evidence in `Docs/RESEARCH_YOUNGER_CLUSTER_QUESTION_KITS_2026-07-14.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Younger-band (3–5 & 6–8) clone TEXT + FEEDBACK is calibrated to TWO DISTINCT registers — never tween/adult register, read-required-for-a-pre-reader, person-praise, or shame-adjacent feedback (R-YOUNGER-BAND-CONTENT-REGISTER; 2026-07-24)

**Every `/play` clone with `ageBand:'3-5'` or `'6-8'` MUST calibrate its instructional/label/stem TEXT and its correct/incorrect/hint/reveal FEEDBACK to the band's register — and the two bands are DISTINCT (never one register for both). A younger-band clone carrying tween/adult register, a read-REQUIRED multi-clause stem a pre-reader can't decode, person-praise ("you're so smart"), or shame-adjacent / non-gentle feedback is a content-quality defect on the same footing as a dark route.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-24 (*"make the content in the 3-5 and 6-8 age bands age appropriate … i noticed some text and feedback … not age appropriate"*); research `RESEARCH_YOUNGER_BAND_CONTENT_APPROPRIATENESS_2026-07-24.md`; audit `AUDIT_YOUNGER_BAND_CONTENT_2026-07-24.md`. This is the content-register sibling of § R-YOUNGER-CLUSTER-NO-MC-KITS (format) + § R-BRIDGE-BAND-DESIGN (grade-3) — same "classify by the band, never treat kids as one audience" discipline (NN/g: kids reject content pitched even one grade off).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Younger-band (3–5 & 6–8) clone LAYOUT · SPACING · NAVIGATION is calibrated to the band — bigger targets, low density, SHALLOW/few-option nav; never the tween tap-size, deep hierarchy, or option-overload (R-YOUNGER-BAND-LAYOUT-NAV; 2026-07-24)

**Every `/play` clone with `ageBand:'3-5'` or `'6-8'` MUST calibrate its LAYOUT, SPACING, and NAVIGATION to the band — not just its text/feedback (§ R-YOUNGER-BAND-CONTENT-REGISTER). A younger-band clone carrying tween-sized tap targets (the ≥44px floor rather than the young-children ≥~2cm benchmark), a crammed/high-density screen, or a deep / option-overloaded navigation (e.g. a pre-reader clone with 11–12 nav destinations) is an age-appropriateness defect on the same footing as above-band text.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-24 (*"make sure layout, spacing and navigation are age appropriate too … audit, deep web research and think harder"*); research `RESEARCH_YOUNGER_BAND_LAYOUT_NAV_2026-07-24.md`; audit `AUDIT_YOUNGER_BAND_CONTENT_2026-07-24.md` § layout/nav. Sibling to § R-YOUNGER-BAND-CONTENT-REGISTER (content) — same "classify by the band, never treat kids as one audience" discipline (NN/g).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## No feature may ship dark — every clone route + novel feature must be wired + visible (R-WEB-CLONE-NO-DARK-SURFACE; 2026-07-11)

**A `/play/<app>` clone is NOT done until EVERY route AND every novel/web-pioneered feature it ships is REACHABLE + VISIBLE to a user — linked from the landing (or from another linked, reachable surface) — and the clone has its `src/data/play/clones.ts` registry row. A surface that exists in the code but no user can navigate to is *shipped-but-dark* and is a defect.** This is the web-clone analogue of the portfolio **Asset Consumer Audit** (`.claude/rules/portfolio.md` § "registered ≠ wired") and the DN **authored ≠ integrated** discipline (`R-CAST-EXPANSION-INTEGRATION`): building the feature is the START of the obligation, wiring it into the user's navigable path is the completion. Codified per user-direct 2026-07-11 (quote in the reference).

**Dramas (P#210):** a drama plays on its chapter (extras: "More stories") or in a `season-*.json`, else `check-drama-reachable.mjs` fails; surfacing dark content is gated by the chapter-prose guards — a defective script is held back (`drama-match.mjs`), never shown (baseline 112, `q-0093eb`).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Every program-shipped REIMAGINING/FEATURE route is LINKED from its app's LANDING page — not merely added to `nav.ts more` (the CloneNav overflow drawer); being in `more` is reachable-but-under-surfaced, a program feature belongs on the app's front door (R-WEB-CLONE-REIMAGINING-LINKED-FROM-APP; 2026-09-15)

**Every program-shipped REIMAGINING/FEATURE route of a `/play/<app>` clone (a new activity/mode/flagship route added by a program — Program #186 flagships, #93 mechanics, #186-E5 `/run`, #130 `/loot`, #111 `/project`, etc.) MUST be LINKED from its app's LANDING page (`src/pages/play/<app>/index.astro` — the magazine / featured / activity grid), NOT merely added to `nav.ts more` (the CloneNav overflow drawer). A `more`-only feature is reachable via the hamburger drawer but NOT surfaced from the app's front door — a discoverability gap the founder flagged 2026-09-15 ("why kitchen and kingdom are not linked from their apps?") after the two D1 flagships (`/gambittales/kingdom` + `/fractionforge/kitchen`) shipped `more`-only. A program-shipped reimagining/feature is "linked from the app" iff EITHER it is a PRIMARY CloneNav tab (in `nav.ts links`, always-visible) OR its href appears on the landing `index.astro`; a `more`-only feature absent from the landing is a gap unless a `# LINK-EXCLUDED: <app> <route> — <reason>` line documents it (genuine reference/utility page or a JS-console landing). Signal is SELF-UPDATING (no hand-list): a `nav.ts` entry whose trailing `//` comment names a `Program #NNN`/`ADR-NNN`. This is a PROMINENCE/discoverability axis ABOVE the anti-dark floor — COMPOSES (never re-litigates) § R-WEB-CLONE-NO-DARK-SURFACE (a `more` entry clears the anti-dark floor; THIS requires the LANDING) · § R-WEB-CLONE-NAV-LINK-HYGIENE (§4 one-primary + tile grid) · § R-WEB-CLONE-CLONE-NAV (ADR-076) · the immersion magazine norm (ADR-099/222 — "activities live on the browse hub; More is reference/utility only"). Future reimaginings/features add their landing link in the SAME PR by construction. Honest-yield: front-door discoverability, never a proven engagement claim. Tracked worst-first by `scripts/check_reimagining_linked_from_app.py` (SHAPE-B WARN, reads SITE `origin/staging`; `--ci-mode` fails ONLY on a regression — a route recorded `primary`/`linked` gone `unlinked`) + `Docs/REGISTRY_REIMAGINING_LINKED_FROM_APP.txt` (baseline 2026-09-15: ~400 `more`-only features dark on their landings = the standing worst-first fleet drain, rides the #74/#75 fan-out with per-landing founder visual gates). Instance of `portfolio.md` § R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS.** Founder-direct 2026-09-15; decision `ADR-272`; program #186.

**DRAIN MECHANISM (ADR-273, Session-22): drain the gap with the shared `<ProgramFeatureGallery>` component, NEVER 186 bespoke landing cards** — a `R-FABLE-REC-FANOUT` shared-shell fan-out. The component reads each clone's `nav.ts` RAW SOURCE (Vite `?raw` glob) via the pure Vitest-pinned `_shared/programFeatures.ts`, parsing the IDENTICAL `// Program #NNN` signal the guard uses (DRY → render can't drift from the guard's tracked set; untagged utility `more` entries excluded by construction), and renders the program features as a dark-safe self-contained landing "More ways to play" section (reuses `.ff-card`; optional `exclude`; empty→renders nothing; SELF-UPDATING as new features land). The guard marks a `more` feature `linked` if its href is on the landing OR the landing mounts `<ProgramFeatureGallery app="<app>"` (minus `exclude`). Fan the 2-line mount worst-first (pedagogical-cluster priority) toward 0, each batch build-green + a stratified screenshot spot-check; TIER-1 shell reviewed once, fan-out mechanical, founder VISUAL gate at the nightly train.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-272_REIMAGINING_LINKED_FROM_APP.md` + `Docs/ADR-273_PROGRAM_FEATURE_GALLERY_LANDING_DRAIN.md`.

## Every multi-surface clone carries its OWN in-app nav bar — the shared `<CloneNav>`, never a lone back-link (R-WEB-CLONE-CLONE-NAV; 2026-07-23)

**A `/play/<app>` clone is a multi-surface mini-app (landing + sub-routes), so it MUST carry its OWN in-app nav — the shared config-driven `<CloneNav>`/`<CloneTabs>` (`src/components/play/CloneNav.astro` + the `CloneNavConfig` type + `auditCloneNav` in `src/lib/play/_shared/cloneNav.ts`) — on EVERY clone page, linking the clone's key surfaces with `aria-current` on the active one. A sub-route that offers only a lone `← Menu` back-link (no way to jump to sibling surfaces) is a navigation defect; the SITE Nav (Play·Story·For Parents&Educators) is NOT the clone's nav — it takes the user OUT of the clone.** Codified per founder-report 2026-07-23 (*"individual web app … missing its own nav bar, not site nav bar"*); pilot decision `Docs/ADR-075`. A clone opts in with a per-app `CloneNavConfig` (`{ name, home:'/play/<app>', links:[{label,href}] }` — curated KEY surfaces, real routes only per R-WEB-CLONE-NO-DARK-SURFACE); the bar is dark-safe (`--ff-*/--pc-*` only), ≥44px targets, a `<nav aria-label>` landmark. Do NOT hand-roll a per-clone bar. `cloneNav.test` pins `auditCloneNav` (home is a real `/play/<app>` route; every link a real sub-route under it).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-076_CLONE_TABBAR_IA_CONTENT_REORG.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_CLONE_IA_TABBAR_REORG_2026-07-23.md` + `Docs/ADR-075_PER_CLONE_IN_APP_NAV.md`.
## `<CloneNav>` mobile floating-glass tab-bar — RETIRED (R-WEB-CLONE-TABBAR-FLOATING-GLASS; 2026-07-23)

> **🔴 RETIRED FLEET-WIDE (V838) — SUPERSEDED by § R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX ⑩** (left slide-out drawer, no bottom bar). Glass/≥44px/dark-safe/`<nav aria-label>` invariants carry to the drawer PANEL; a clone still rendering the floating bottom bar is a defect. → **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## 🔴 CRITICAL: the bottom-nav → left-drawer directive (and every `/play` webclone nav/layout/immersion directive) is WEB-ONLY — NEVER propagated to iOS via handoff (R-WEB-CLONE-NAV-DIRECTIVE-WEB-ONLY; 2026-08-14)

> **🔴🔴 CRITICAL (founder-direct 2026-08-14 — "codify and track and escalate to critical"): the founder's "move all bottom navigation to a drop-down/slide-out drawer on the left" directive (V838 / § R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX ⑩, which RETIRED the floating bottom bar R-WEB-CLONE-TABBAR-FLOATING-GLASS) applies to `/play` WEBCLONES ONLY — a shared-WEB `CloneNav`/`play.css` change shipped to `spark-anvil-site`, NOT an iOS app change. More generally: a `/play` webclone UI/UX directive (nav model · IA/tab reorg · layout · immersion · skin) is WEB-ONLY BY DEFAULT and MUST NOT be propagated to the iOS surface via any `HANDOFF_FROM_HUB_*` doc, and MUST NOT alter any iOS app's native navigation, UNLESS the founder issues a SEPARATE, EXPLICIT directive naming the iOS surface. iOS apps navigate with native SwiftUI (`TabView`/`NavigationStack`/`NavigationSplitView`) — a native bottom TabView is HIG-idiomatic, NOT a defect to be "fixed" by a web drawer; and any iOS nav change is Swift SOURCE, which the hub NEVER writes (CLAUDE.md § CRITICAL). Filing an iOS handoff to move the bottom bar to a left drawer is a defect on the same footing as a dark route. VERIFIED CLEAN 2026-08-14: 0 nav-directive handoffs across all 210 app repos (fresh `origin/main` sweep). Guard: `scripts/check_webclone_nav_directive_ios_leak.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_WEBCLONE_NAV_DIRECTIVE_IOS_LEAK.txt`.** Decision `Docs/ADR-132_WEBCLONE_NAV_DIRECTIVE_WEB_ONLY.md`; evidence `Docs/AUDIT_WEBCLONE_NAV_DIRECTIVE_IOS_SCOPE_2026-08-14.md`. → **Full detail:** ADR-132 + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX ⑩ (the web left-drawer the directive shipped).
## Immersive FULLSCREEN "focus mode" — RETIRED (R-WEB-CLONE-FULLSCREEN; 2026-07-31)

> **🛑 RETIRED FLEET-WIDE (V841 / site #1720) — full IMMERSION (§ R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX ⑨) IS the focus mode.** KEPT: `--ff-focus` focus-RING styling + board-maximized layout (owned by immersion + § R-WEB-CLONE-SPATIAL-SIDE-BY-SIDE). Re-adding a separate focus toggle is a defect. → **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A SPATIAL-manipulative clone (board/cube/grid/geometry) uses SIDE-BY-SIDE `[manipulative | controls]` as its DEFAULT desktop layout — biggest possible manipulative + one-screen fit; stacked on portrait/mobile; NEVER universal (R-WEB-CLONE-SPATIAL-SIDE-BY-SIDE; 2026-08-02, ADR-086)

> **⏭ PLANNED EXTENSION → 3-COLUMN (V835)** — nav LEFT · board CENTER (maximized) · controls RIGHT for board-game/logic-puzzle apps (extends the 2-col below by moving clone nav to a left rail); mobile stays single-column board-first. Reuse/extend the `.ff-sxs` machinery. Full spec + amendment → reference / work-queue V835.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## In the 3-column immersive layout the board sits in a SINGLE frame — the board COLUMN (`.ff-sxs-main`) IS the frame; a board must NOT carry a second full card (`.ff-stage`) inside it (box-in-box is a defect); the `.ff-stage` prominence card is KEPT for stacked/mobile only (R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-SINGLE-FRAME; 2026-08-12, ADR-118)

**In the immersive 3-column board layout (`.ff-wrap.pc-immersive:has(.ff-sxs)`, desktop ≥1000px) the board COLUMN `.ff-sxs-main` is ALREADY its own opaque panel frame, so the board sits in that SINGLE frame — a board that ALSO wraps itself in a second `.ff-stage` card renders a redundant box-in-box (double border/shadow/padding that shrinks the board), a defect on the footing of a failed dark-mode axis. Fix = one DRY shared-`play.css` rule that NEUTRALIZES the inner `.ff-stage` FRAME (border/shadow/bg/radius/padding — never its flex centering) inside the immersive board column; `.ff-stage`'s prominence card is RETAINED for stacked/mobile (<1000px) + 2-col (900–999px). Tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_WEB_CLONE_BOARD_FRAME.txt` + `scripts/check_board_frame_nesting.py` (SHAPE-B); a NEW 3-col board clone hand-rolling a non-`.ff-stage` framed wrapper inside `.ff-sxs-main` = a tracked gap.** Composes § R-WEB-CLONE-SPATIAL-SIDE-BY-SIDE · § R-WEB-CLONE-MANIPULATIVE-PROMINENCE · § R-WEB-CLONE-FULL-GAME-BOARD · § R-WEB-CLONE-BEST-OF-BREED-UI-UX ⑨.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Panels FIT their content — a square board's panel HUGS it (no tall-rectangle-around-a-square) · aids are REASONABLE-size vs their window · Q&A + teach-the-cast panels are UNCRAMPED (R-WEB-CLONE-PANEL-FIT; 2026-08-15, ADR-140)

**Every `/play` clone's panel↔content GEOMETRY + density is a layout gate on FOUR axes, each a screenshot-DoD-footing defect: (A) FIT — an aid/board is a REASONABLE size vs its panel/window (never dwarfed OR overflowing): size a square/aspect-bound aid by the SMALLER dimension (`clamp(min, min(90vh, cqi|100vw−side), max)` + `aspect-ratio` + centered), NEVER an absolute-rem or width-only-viewport cap. (B) HUG — a SQUARE/compact board must NOT sit in a TALL RECTANGULAR full-height panel (a big empty rectangle framing a square); the panel HUGS it — defeat flex/grid `align-items:stretch`→`flex-start`/center + `min-height:0`, board `aspect-ratio:1/1` bounded by the smaller dim, panel `height:fit-content`, centered — the INVERSE of `AUDIT_BOARD_PANEL_RESPONSIVENESS` (boards-not-growing); N/A for deliberately full-width sequencers/waveforms/tracks. (C) UNCRAMP — the Q&A (`.pc-q-*`) + teach-the-cast islands carry breathing room on the `--space-*` scale (≥16–24px internal · 24–48px section · spacing-to-group; CLT) — EXTENDS `R-SITE-SPACING-TOKEN-ENFORCEMENT`. (D) GENERAL layout hygiene umbrella. Verdict = the dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD, PRE-TRIAGED worst-first by `measure_board_panel_fill.cjs`; fixed PER-APP CSS, NEVER a blanket shared-`play.css` change. A mis-fit/mis-hugged/cramped panel is a defect on the footing of a failed dark-mode axis; an un-reviewed clone is a tracked gap. COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) `R-VISUAL-AID-PROMINENCE-ENGAGEMENT`(#34) · `R-WEB-CLONE-SPATIAL-SIDE-BY-SIDE`(ADR-086) · `R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-SINGLE-FRAME`(ADR-118) · `R-SITE-LAYOUT-SPACING` · `R-SITE-SPACING-TOKEN-ENFORCEMENT`.** Founder-direct 2026-08-15 ("go. but next session"); program #44; decision `ADR-140`; tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_LAYOUT_HYGIENE.txt` + `scripts/check_layout_hygiene.py` (SHAPE-B WARN).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-140_WEB_CLONE_LAYOUT_HYGIENE.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_LAYOUT_HYGIENE_2026-08-15.md` + `Docs/PLAN_LAYOUT_HYGIENE_PROGRAM_2026-08-15.md`.
## The 3-column immersive RIGHT (controls) panel = MULTIPLE grouped CHILD CARDS in ONE right-sized SCROLLING column — never oversized, never a flat overwhelming block, never split into 2 (top/bottom) panels (R-WEB-CLONE-SIDE-PANEL-FIT; 2026-08-17, ADR-150)

**In the 3-column immersive layout (nav-LEFT · board-CENTER `.ff-sxs-main` · controls-RIGHT `.ff-sxs-side`) the RIGHT controls panel MUST be ONE right-sized SCROLLING column holding MULTIPLE grouped CHILD CARDS — a screenshot-DoD-footing defect if not. Five invariants (full detail → ADR-150): (1) NOT OVERSIZED (width ≈ `clamp(20rem,24vw,~30rem)`, NEVER up to 50rem/800px — SUPERSEDES q-3d3095's "maximize the right panel" over-widen); (2) CHILD CARDS NESTED IN ONE PARENT PANEL (prompt·choices·hint·difficulty as distinct Common-Region cards, Miller-chunked ≤~5, nested INSIDE the panel's ONE framed parent frame; child cards SUBTLER than parent [1px border + faint scheme-safe `--ff-ink` tint + small radius, no heavy shadow] = cards-within-a-panel [Xcode-Claude model] — never a flat wall, never floating frameless cards, never double heavy frames); (3) ONE SCROLLING COLUMN + NO 2-BOX HERO SPLIT (`flex-wrap:nowrap; overflow-y:auto`; never wraps into a 2nd column / ad-hoc top+bottom panel pair — the founder-flagged "splits into 2"; s3: the flat-grid shape [q-3d3095, gridforge board family + crowntales/draughts] framed `.ff-hero` as a SEPARATE row-1 card above the panel = 2 boxes → close the col-2 `row-gap` + strip seam borders/radii to FUSE hero+side into ONE panel; scoped to `:has(> .ff-hero):has(> .ff-sxs)`, the other 5 clones untouched); (4) CONSISTENT across all 7 three-col clones (cityforge·crowntales·cubesensei·deducequest·gambittales·geometryforge·gridforge — flat-grid hero folds into the SAME stack); (5) PRESERVES the composed three-panels/no-wrapper-frame/dark-safe/`--space-*` invariants; (6) BOARD-TOP-FLUSH is HARD (amendment q-4ba827, 2026-08-18): a title/prompt/hero card must NEVER sit ABOVE the board — relocate it into the RIGHT panel; applies to the WHOLE board/puzzle/logic 3-col set (BROADER than the 7), tracked by the registry's `flush:` axis. THREE sources fixed DRY+scoped (customRound `.pc-q-stem` relocated into `.ff-sxs-side` · page-level `.ff-hero` hidden on desktop board route · grandchild-wrapper head grid-move) → **ADR-150 § Amendment**. Delivered SHARED (DRY) via `play.css`+`_shared/customRound.ts` — SINGLE-FLIGHT + FOUNDER-GATED, HIGH-BLAST → `R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA` (dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD). COMPOSES ABOVE (never re-litigates) `R-WEB-CLONE-PANEL-FIT`(ADR-140; axis-C is Q&A *cramping* [too-tight], this is *oversizing/overwhelm/split*) · ADR-118 · ADR-086 · #34 · `R-SITE-SPACING-TOKEN-ENFORCEMENT`.** Founder-direct 2026-08-17 ("multiple child cards with scrolling"; ref "that's how the claude agent in xcode works"); decision `ADR-150`; research `RESEARCH_SIDE_PANEL_LAYOUT_3COL_2026-08-17`; tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_SIDE_PANEL_FIT.txt` (+ `flush:` axis) + `scripts/check_side_panel_fit.py` (SHAPE-B WARN); queue `q-ba2f70` (right-panel) · `q-4ba827` (board-top-flush).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-150_WEB_CLONE_SIDE_PANEL_FIT.md` (§ Amendment = board-top-flush) + `Docs/RESEARCH_SIDE_PANEL_LAYOUT_3COL_2026-08-17.md`.
## A 3-col spatial clone's outer MOUNT WRAPPER must NOT carry a narrow `max-width` cap that strangles the `.ff-sxs` row on desktop — the board overflows + OVERLAPS the controls panel (R-WEB-CLONE-3COL-WRAPPER-NO-NARROW-CAP; 2026-09-07)

**A per-clone board-game mount wrapper (`.<x>-play` — `.sg-play`/`.ow-play`/`.ms-play`) that wraps the `.ff-sxs` 3-col row MUST NOT keep a narrow absolute `max-width` cap on DESKTOP (≥1000px): it strangles the row below `board min-width + .ff-sxs-side min 16rem` → the board overflows and OVERLAPS the controls panel (viewport-independent; founder-flagged 2026-09-07 shogi/oware/morris — the q-6ac5d3 retrofit added the inner overrides but left the outer wrapper cap unlifted). FIX: lift the cap (`.pc-theme-<clone> .<x>-play { max-width: none }`) inside the clone's `@media (min-width: 1000px)` block (mobile keeps the narrow column). GUARDED by the site prebuild `scripts/check-3col-wrapper-cap.mjs` (reds a FAST staging PR on a `.<x>-play` cap + `.ff-sxs-main` + no lift) + the hub runtime audit `scripts/audit_3col_overlap.mjs`. Composes R-WEB-CLONE-SPATIAL-SIDE-BY-SIDE · R-WEB-CLONE-SIDE-PANEL-FIT · ADR-086/118/246. Detail + fleet-audit (3 fixed / 14 clean) → `Docs/queue/q-p127-3col-overlap.md` + site PR #3533.**

## A clone's CONTENT + sections + nav links stay DEDUPED, CONSOLIDATED, and STREAMLINED — no duplicate content, redundant/orphan links, or over-crammed landing; one primary task per page (R-WEB-CLONE-NAV-LINK-HYGIENE; 2026-07-23, content-dedup scope added 2026-07-23)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone's CONTENT + sections + navigation MUST stay deduped, consolidated, and streamlined: (CONTENT) NO duplicate/near-duplicate content block rendered in >1 place — each piece lives in ONE canonical location; (1) NO redundant legacy `← Menu` back-link once `<CloneNav>` is present; (2) NO duplicate link from both a tab AND an inline strip; (3) NO orphan/dead in-clone links (every link resolves + is reachable); (4) ONE-PRIMARY-TASK-per-page — the landing keeps its ONE core surface prominent + a compact "More ways to practice" tile grid for secondary CloneNav destinations (DEMOTE, never delete a link), audit card ORDER not just count (a buried primary is a defect), grid children rely on the grid `gap` (never a per-child `margin-top`); (5) consistent section ORDER across clones. A duplicate content block, a buried primary, or an orphan link is a defect.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every clone must tightly integrate the DN narrative assets — mascot, cast portraits, storybooks, audio dramas, DIR/FEDC reflection (R-WEB-CLONE-NARRATIVE-INTEGRATION; 2026-07-11)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone landing MUST surface the portfolio's Distributed-Narrative assets — the app MASCOT, the CAST PORTRAITS, a link from each cast member to their illustrated T1/T2 multi-beat STORYBOOK + AUDIO DRAMA, and a between-practice DIR/FEDC affect-recognition REFLECTION — not a bare cast name-list.** A clone that ports the learning kits but leaves the mascot, portraits, storybooks, and audio dramas dark is only half-built: the DN thesis is *"the cast IS the curriculum"* (Naul+Liu empathetic-characters + distributed-narrative axes), so the characters + their stories are load-bearing motivation, not decoration. Codified per user-direct 2026-07-11 (*"why the mascot illustrations, cast characters portraits, t1/t2 multi-beat storybooks with illustrations, audio dramas are not tightly integrated with the web clones? … and dir/fedc reflections too … fix for all shipped web clones and codify as a rule"*).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## DN + reflection SURFACING is a BAND-AWARE portfolio-wide coverage guarantee — a standing guard, because RAW per-surface counts MISLEAD (R-WEB-CLONE-DN-REFLECTION-COVERAGE; 2026-07-24)

**Every eligible `/play` clone MUST carry BOTH (a) its BAND-APPROPRIATE DN baseline surface AND (b) a reflection surface, OR a machine-parseable waiver — and this is a STANDING, self-checking guard (`scripts/check_dn_reflection_coverage.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_DN_REFLECTION_COVERAGE.txt`, the R-ASSET-GEN-COMPLETENESS pattern), NOT a one-session sweep — because a naïve per-surface count MISLEADS: the portfolio uses band-specific DN surfaces, so most raw "gaps" are band-appropriate WAIVERS, not defects.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-24 (rapid burst: "reflection rollout portfolio-wide" + "add support for distributed narrative portfolio wide … audit, deep web research, think harder"), after the audit (`AUDIT`/`RESEARCH_AND_AUDIT_PORTFOLIO_DN_REFLECTION_SURFACING_2026-07-24.md`) found the band-appropriate DN baseline was already **188/189 = 99.5% complete** (the naïve "168/189 PlayNarrative" undercount was all correctly-waived 15-18 clones) with **northquest the ONE genuine gap** (fixed V592), while reflection had ~38 real gaps.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/RESEARCH_AND_AUDIT_PORTFOLIO_DN_REFLECTION_SURFACING_2026-07-24.md`.
## A clone landing surfaces its FULL cast + BOTH story kinds — the solo `dnCast.members` rail AND the ENSEMBLE stories; a member/story that exists but renders NOWHERE is a silent display gap, guarded band-aware (R-WEB-CLONE-CAST-STORY-DISPLAY; 2026-07-30)

**Every `/play/<app>` clone landing MUST surface its cast + stories COMPLETELY — (a) the SOLO cast rail (`<PlayNarrative>` iterating `dnCast.members[]`, each linked to its `/cast/<app>/<char>` story) AND (b) an ENSEMBLE-stories rail linking every multi-character/combined chapter (`equi-stretch`, `the-pawn-cohort`, `the-combination`). An ensemble chapter is a real `/cast/<app>/<slug>` story, but `<PlayNarrative>` historically iterated ONLY solo members, so 95 ensemble stories across 56 apps rendered NOWHERE on the landing (`/story` DID surface them; the per-clone landing did not) — a silent display gap on the same footing as a dark surface. A member or story that EXISTS but is displayed on NO clone surface is a defect.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-30 (*"audit the cast characters/stories displayed for each webclone … a lot of them are missing, including the ensemble cast characters and stories … verify, track, codify. nothing silently forgotten"* + *"audit … each app/webclone has at least 2 ensemble multi-beat chapters"*). SHIPPED: the `<PlayNarrative>` ensemble rail (site PR #1495) + `scripts/build-ensemble-stories-manifest.mjs` → `src/data/ensemble-stories.json`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/AUDIT_WEB_CLONE_CAST_STORY_DISPLAY_2026-07-30.md`.
## Web-clone user + developer guides must track the code (R-WEB-CLONE-GUIDE-SYNC; 2026-07-08)

**Every `/play/<app>` web clone has TWO living guides that MUST be updated in the SAME change-set as any code change that affects what they document — a visitor-facing USER guide and a maintainer-facing DEVELOPER guide.** A code change that lands without the matching guide update is incomplete, exactly like a cross-repo PR that ships without verifying the merge. Codified per user-direct 2026-07-08 (*"codify the requirement that user guide and developer guide for the web clone of fractionforge be kept up-to-date with the web clone code"*). Follows the precedent set by the `/cast` page guides (`GUIDE_CAST_PAGE_USER.md` + `GUIDE_CAST_PAGE_DEVELOPER.md`).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## The R5 cross-app transfer map grows ONLY by verify-before-assert + single-flight; read `transfer.ts` on origin for the live count, never the handoffs (R-WEB-CLONE-TRANSFER-MAP; 2026-07-22)

**The R5 cross-app transfer meta-layer — the curated `src/lib/play/_shared/transfer.ts` map (`TRANSFER_MAP`/`linksFrom`/`validateLink`) + the shared `<TransferBridge fromApp="<slug>" />` boundary card (root `section.pc-transfer`, rendered by `src/components/play/TransferBridge.astro`) — makes NEAR-transfer EXPLICIT between `/play` clones that share a deep structure. It GROWS only by curatorial judgment under four hard disciplines; a link added without them is a defect on the same footing as a dark route.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-22 (a status→grow→"codify these" cycle that took the map 15→19 pairs). This is the operational "how to grow it" companion to the guardrails in the `transfer.ts` header + `Docs/PLAN_CROSS_APP_TRANSFER_R5_2026-07-21.md`; the transfer metric is the star measurement for the R4/ADR-061 efficacy backend, so link QUALITY (real shared structure) matters more than link count.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_CROSS_APP_TRANSFER_R5_2026-07-21.md` + the `transfer.ts` header.
## 🔴 CRITICAL: App icons are the ADULT App-evaluation identity, NOT the /play kid-browse card identity — AND a NON-NULL asset path to a MISSING file FREEZES the prod build (R-APP-ICON-SURFACE; 2026-07-28, escalated CRITICAL 2026-08-22)

**App icons (`public/apps/<slug>/icon.webp`) are the identity for the ADULT App-evaluation surface (`AppCard` → `/for-parents-educators/portfolio` + `/apps/[slug]` leaves — where the iOS App-Store icon is the correct parity identity, already shipped, null-guarded). They MUST NOT be put on the kid-facing `/play` browse cards, which already carry a 100%-covered DN MASCOT tile (`.pl-card-mascot`). Swapping `/play` mascot→icon (or adding an icon alongside) is a defect: it REGRESSES image coverage (icons cover ~147 apps < ~189 clones; mascots are 0-missing) and DILUTES the on-thesis "cast IS the curriculum" DN identity — and app icons' baked opaque backgrounds would need the rounded-tile/plate dark-mode treatment (R-SITE-DARK-MODE-WHOLE-SITE) for a net-negative change.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-28 (*"should we use app icons for the webclones?"* → verify-before-action NO for `/play`); decision `Docs/ADR-078`.

**🔴 CRITICAL BUILD-BREAKER invariant (founder-direct 2026-08-22 "missing icon will cause failed build in prod … make that rule critical").** `AppCard.astro` degrades gracefully ONLY when a path is NULL (`{iconPath && …}{!iconPath && monogram}`). An app SURFACED in `apps.generated.ts` with a **NON-NULL asset path** — `iconPath` (icon-sm.webp) · `iconHeroPath` (icon.webp) · `mascotPath` (mascot.webp) — pointing at a **MISSING file** is a HARD broken link → the postbuild `check-site-internal-links.py` FAILS → **the prod CORE build FREEZES** (the "missing icon degrades gracefully → NEVER a build-fail" claim is TRUE only for a NULL path; a non-null path to a missing file is a build-breaker). **PRECEDENT: `pawnpals` hotfix `fe73c921e` — "ship app icon+icon-sm+mascot webps — unblock frozen prod CORE."** So the SPAWN gate (composes § R-SPAWN-COMPLETENESS-DIMENSIONS, R-CHAPTER-ART-COMPLETION): before an app is added to `apps.generated` with any non-null asset path, its file(s) MUST be shipped — OR that path stays `null` (→ graceful monogram/mascot). This GENERALIZES beyond icons to EVERY non-null `apps.generated` asset path. Shift-left guard: `check_icon_coverage.py` now has a **BUILD-BREAKER axis that FAILS `--ci-mode`** on any non-null asset path → missing file, checked against `origin/staging` GIT-TREE ground truth (blobless-safe; NOT the stale local working tree — R-GIT-REMOTE-TRACKING-STALE). The site's postbuild link-check remains the authoritative backstop. **⚠ `tutorforge` is tracked `# ICON-DEFERRED:` — it MUST ship an icon (or keep every asset path null) before it lands in `apps.generated` (W4).**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-078_APP_ICONS_ADULT_SURFACE_NOT_PLAY_CARDS.md`; guard `scripts/check_icon_coverage.py` (build-breaker axis + coverage axis) + `Docs/REGISTRY_ICON_COVERAGE.txt`.

## Tech stack (locked in)

- **Static site generator**: Astro 4.x (per `DECISION_FIGMA_FOR_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` + `PLAN_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md`)
- **Styling**: Tailwind CSS with token-mapped brand palette (`tailwind.config.js` defines `forge`, `anvil`, `spark`, `warm`, `slate`)
- **Hosting**: Cloudflare Workers (static assets, via Workers Builds Git integration; migrated from Cloudflare Pages)
- **Analytics**: Plausible (privacy-first, no cookies, COPPA-safe)
- **Forms**: Formspree or Netlify Forms (press contact, parent feedback)
- **No TRACKING SDKs — but learning-experience SDKs are ENCOURAGED (R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE; founder-direct 2026-08-19).** The public kid-facing surfaces (site + `/play` webclones) PROHIBIT any tracking / analytics / telemetry / advertising / session-replay / fingerprinting / data-exfiltration SDK — the real COPPA "no tracking, no kid data leaves the device" invariant (Plausible + Cloudflare Web Analytics are the ONLY analytics — both cookieless, no identifier, no fingerprinting, disclosed on /privacy (founder-direct 2026-09-29, ADR-302 § Amd 2: the edge-injected CF beacon is kept; it is this program's RUM source); CF Web Analytics runs on EVERY family-site zone, EU included (founder-direct 2026-10-02 "for EU too": zone setting "Enable", never "excluding the EU"; guard `check_family_site_guard_enrollment.py --online`); omitted from the POB). They ENCOURAGE third-party SDKs/libraries that materially improve the LEARNING experience **provided each stays on-device · no-tracking · COPPA-clean · WCAG-2.2-AA + dark-safe · reuse-first** — precedent already shipped: Stockfish WASM (§ R-WEB-CLONE-CHESS-ENGINE-WASM / ADR-094), cubing.js, the on-device Prompt-API/WebLLM tutor tier (§ R-WEB-CLONE-AI-TUTOR / ADR-143). This NARROWS the former blanket "no third-party SDKs" to its load-bearing core (tracking), and is DISTINCT from INTERNAL tools (e.g. the ADR-161 work-queue dashboard MAY use a CDN/third-party charting lib — no kids, no data collection). A tracking SDK on a public surface is a defect on the dark-route footing; adopting a learning SDK that phones home / needs an account / breaks the on-device posture is the same defect. Adoption of a new learning SDK is founder-gated + ADR'd (composes with ADR-082 / ADR-100 / ADR-114 / ADR-143).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

The website adopts a **HYBRID** Liquid-Glass-inspired accent layer: chunky-cartoon brand register remains the primary visual identity; 3-5 narrowly-scoped accent surfaces use mature Glassmorphism 2.0 (semi-opaque overlay + `backdrop-filter: blur(8-16px)` + thin border). **No SVG-displacement refraction** — Chromium-only, broken on mobile Safari, GPU-expensive on school iPads. See `Docs/ADR-014_HYBRID_LIQUID_GLASS_WEBSITE.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_LIQUID_GLASS_WEBSITE_2026-05-29.md` for the full decision + 29-source research.

## The site is a multi-UNIT build split — one Astro repo, many deploy units behind a path-routing dispatcher (R-SITE-BUILD-SPLIT; 2026-07-10)

**The `spark-anvil-site` repo is ONE Astro project that builds into MULTIPLE independent Cloudflare deploy units, fronted by a single host-agnostic dispatcher Worker that routes purely by URL path.** This is the load-bearing architecture behind the `/play` clones, the `.org`/`.com` dual-serve, and the (planned) per-cluster + cast/chapters carve-outs. It is codified here — not only in `ADR-032` — because every session loads the rules file, and a decision doc "decays in visibility" (workflow.md § Audit-to-canonical-propagation). ADR-032 is the full decision + rationale; THIS is the standing convention. **SYMMETRIC (ADR-153): PLAY builds ONLY `/play` (`build-play.mjs`) AND CORE builds everything EXCEPT `/play` (`build-core.mjs` stashes `src/pages/play/` OUT; `postbuild … --unit core`) — `/play` built ONCE; `/play` HTML in CORE `dist` MUST be 0.**

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-SITE-DOMAINS).
- **R-SITE-BUILD-STASH-GLOB-COUPLING (ADR-153):** before a build unit EXCLUDES a route subtree, GREP other units' pages for a build-time `import.meta.glob`/`Astro.glob` over it — the glob silently resolves to **0** when the subtree is stashed (green build, silent content loss). Re-source from a PREBUILD on-disk manifest, never a build-time glob. Sibling of § R-SITE-CORE-PARSE-GATE. Detail: ADR-153.
- **R-SITE-E2E-SCOPED-BUILD-REFERENCE-CLONES (2026-08-21):** the SCOPED e2e build (`build-e2e.mjs`, `keep = scope ∪ SEL`) stashes non-kept `/play/<app>` OUT → an always-run spec that HARDCODES a clone route (`passandplay.spec.ts`→grammarforge; board-duels→gridforge/…) 404s → **false-red e2e** on any scoped PR not touching it (silently chronic; NOT a prod defect). The scoped build MUST union in the clones the specs navigate to, DERIVED from `/play/<clone>/` literals in `tests/e2e/*.spec.ts` (drift-safe, like `SEL_APPS`). Same exclusion-breaks-a-consumer class as above. Fix: site PR #2534.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Automate the build-time fix via native caching — NOT hand-rolled units or dashboard work (R-SITE-BUILD-AUTOMATION; 2026-07-20)

**The core-build bottleneck (prerendering ~1,981 routes, ~90% of them the 1,776 cast/chapter pages) is attacked by AUTOMATION in a fixed priority order, and the site stays SSG-first. Do NOT hand-roll another per-unit relocate script (like `build-play.mjs`) and do NOT create Cloudflare build units by dashboard clicking — both are superseded by the levers below.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-20 (deep-web-researched: `RESEARCH_BUILD_AUTOMATION_ASTRO5_UPGRADE_2026-07-20.md` + `RESEARCH_SSG_VS_SSR_ARCHITECTURE_2026-07-20.md`). Keep SSG (no wholesale SSR — our pages have no per-user data, so SSR only relocates the render build→first-request at added cost; `RESEARCH_SSG_VS_SSR`).

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-SITE-REPOS-PRIVATE) · (R-SITE-BUILD-OUT-OF-ORDER; 2026-08-12) · (R-SITE-BUILD-SELECTIVITY; ADR-120) · (R-SITE-WATCH-PATH-SYNC; ADR-120) · (R-SITE-INCREMENTAL-CACHE-HYGIENE; ADR-131).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Keep Astro + related npm packages CURRENT (latest stable) for bug fixes · security · major features — a STANDING discipline, each dep/config bump its OWN gated quiet-window PR (R-SITE-DEPENDENCY-CURRENCY; 2026-08-14, ADR-133)

**The site's `astro` · `@astrojs/*` · `vite` · `tailwindcss` · `vite-plugin-pwa` · `vitest` · `@playwright/test` · `zod` + the rest of `package.json` are kept on the LATEST STABLE release for bug fixes, security, AND major new features — a standing (periodic `npm outdated` → floor-bump + `npm update`; adopt the next MAJOR when it stabilizes), NOT a one-time upgrade. This AMENDS ADR-087's conservative "don't upgrade to chase a feature" posture (founder-direct 2026-08-14 — we now DO adopt major features, deliberately + gated). Non-waivable: a dep/config/lockfile bump is its OWN PR in a QUIET window, NEVER co-mingled with a content wave or refactor (§ R-SITE-INCREMENTAL-CACHE-HYGIENE — it wipes the whole incrementalBuild cache), gated by full `npm run build` + `build:play` + Vitest + Playwright + dual-scheme screenshot-DoD; a MAJOR bump follows the § R-SITE-BUILD-AUTOMATION watched-CF-deploy playbook (reversible). Current-but-not-reckless: latest STABLE, not pre-release. The iOS sibling is `forgekit.md` § R-FORGEKIT-PIN-CONSISTENCY (+ the CLAUDE.md toolchain floor).**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-133_KEEP_TOOLCHAIN_DEPENDENCIES_CURRENT.md`.
## The CORE PROD build SELF-HEALS the Astro incrementalBuild chunk-regroup poison — keep incremental ON (fast default), auto cold-`--force` retry ONLY on the detected dangling-`/_astro` signature; never a manual ASTRO_CACHE_GEN treadmill, never force-cold every build (R-SITE-PROD-BUILD-SELFHEAL-REGROUP; 2026-09-15)

**Astro `experimental.incrementalBuild` (astro.config.mjs) carries the upstream "CSS chunk-regrouping" bug (withastro/astro#17976, UNMERGED): a SHARED-chrome chunk (`ReadingAccess` → every page) is renamed when an UNRELATED page's graph changes, but the cache-RESTORED HTML keeps the STALE `/_astro/<hash>.css` ref → the FULL main build's link-check 404s → CORE PROD FREEZE (recurred 2026-08-29, 2026-09-15). Keep incremental build the DEFAULT (fast; NEVER force-cold every build): `build-core.mjs` runs the normal incremental `astro build`, then on a FULL build (`!FAST_BUILD`) scans dist for the dangling-`/_astro` signature (`scripts/check-dist-astro-refs.mjs`) and, if present, auto-runs ONE cold `astro build --force` (re-emits the chunk + re-seeds a correct cache CF persists → next incremental build clean). FAST previews skip it (harmless there). RETIRES the manual `ASTRO_CACHE_GEN`-bump treadmill for prod (token stays a backstop). Guarded by `scripts/check-dist-astro-refs.test.mjs` (5/5). NOT the authoritative gate (the postbuild link-check still runs); can only IMPROVE a failing build or no-op a passing one. PLAY = tracked follow-on. COMPOSES § R-SITE-INCREMENTAL-CACHE-HYGIENE · § R-SITE-FAST-BUILD-DEV-SUBSET · `workflow.md` § R-DEPLOY-STATUS-FROM-CF-BUILD-LOGS.** Founder-flagged 2026-09-15; shipped site #3858 (g3 recovery) + #3860 (self-heal); upstream-fix adoption tracked `Docs/queue/q-eedb7b.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/GUIDE_CLOUDFLARE_BUILD_MONITORING.md` § class-4 + `Docs/queue/q-eedb7b.md`.
## The Astro-7 site carries an AGENT-LOOP + SECURITY posture — forbid the vulnerable Astro patterns so an agent never introduces one; allowlists by SHAPE; dev-server loopback-only; use Astro's managed background dev server for agent loops; version floor 7.2.4 (R-SITE-ASTRO-AGENT-POSTURE; 2026-08-21)

**The site is Astro `7.2.x` + `@astrojs/cloudflare` (node ≥22), so an agent working it obeys the Astro posture (Report No. 68 §12), each item a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) FORBID-THE-PATTERN — an agent MUST NOT introduce any of the 2026 Astro-CVE patterns (NONE present today — verified: no `src/middleware`, `src/fetch.ts` advanced-routing, or `src/actions`): advanced-routing `actions()` BEFORE `middleware()`; pathname-auth middleware without an encoded-path test; an unbounded-body Astro action; a Vercel-ISR `x_astro_path` edge-auth (authorize at the ORIGIN). If ever introduced, gate it with the matching CI test (the per-CVE detail is in `REGISTRY_ASTRO_AGENT_POSTURE.txt`). (2) ALLOWLISTS BY SHAPE — allow `astro check|build|dev status|stop|logs` + `astro add <int>` as full shapes; DENY agent edits to `astro.config.*` `security`/`adapter`/`cache` + `.env*` unless the task names them. (3) DEV-SERVER IS ATTACK SURFACE — loopback-only, NEVER `--host` / public ingress. (4) AGENT-LOOP — prefer Astro's managed background dev server (`/_astro/status` + JSON logs + `astro dev status|stop|logs`) so the agent never spawns duplicate/zombie servers (`R-GEN-NO-BACKGROUND` still binds PAID GEN, not the dev server). (5) VERSION FLOOR ≥ 7.2.4 (the 7.2.2→7.2.4 bump is a TRACKED quiet-window dep-bump per § R-SITE-DEPENDENCY-CURRENCY). Guard: `scripts/check_astro_agent_posture.py` (SHAPE-B WARN) + `Docs/REGISTRY_ASTRO_AGENT_POSTURE.txt`. Composes ABOVE § R-SITE-DEPENDENCY-CURRENCY + § R-SITE-LOCALHOST-R2-CORS + `workflow.md` § R-AGENT-SECURITY-POSTURE.** Founder-direct 2026-08-21 (Report No. 68 §12); decision `ADR-175`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-175_BURDEN_OF_PROOF_AGENTIC_PRACTICES.md` § §12.
## Build/deploy skip-friendliness is a first-class, FORWARD-LOOKING code-quality axis — author every new site surface so it stays skip-eligible; a self-checking guard flags regressions (R-SITE-BUILD-SKIP-FRIENDLY; 2026-08-14, ADR-134)

**The site's build/deploy cost is a DEPENDENCY GRAPH — Astro's two-part reuse gate (`cacheKey` [data] ⊕ full transitive module-graph hash [code]) × Cloudflare watch-paths (affected-unit selection) × build-output caching — so every NEW route/collection/component MUST be authored SKIP-FRIENDLY BY DEFAULT: (1) a compound `cacheKey` on every `getStaticPaths` route that can take one (a `getStaticPaths` route with NO cacheKey — and every non-`getStaticPaths` page — re-renders every build); (2) per-entity content collections (per-app), NEVER one `glob` over thousands of entries where ADDs are frequent (a set-grow busts every consuming page — the chapter-ADD class); (3) LEAN layouts + NARROW/DIRECT imports (never a broad barrel or churny shared-chrome import — `BaseLayout`→Footer/Nav/`site.ts`, `apps.generated`, manifests — widening a page's module-graph hash so unrelated edits bust it: "a bomb wired to every page"); (4) churny per-entity data in a `file()` collection read per-entry, OUT of the module graph. Protect the whole-cache + affected-unit selection: dep/config bumps in their OWN quiet-window PR (§ R-SITE-INCREMENTAL-CACHE-HYGIENE — they wipe the entire incrementalBuild cache); watch-paths re-synced on topology change (§ R-SITE-WATCH-PATH-SYNC); content pushes batched under the CF 3000-file / 20-commit force-build bypass (`workflow.md` § R-BATCH-DISTRIBUTION-PUSH). HONEST-YIELD: MEASURE-FIRST before ANY conversion refactor (census the cacheable ceiling + module-graph churn coupling + build-output caching) — a marginal high-blast conversion is a DECLINE by measurement, NOT a deliverable (the PLAY + CORE-remainder conversions were correctly DECLINED; this rule does NOT re-litigate them). The standing guard `scripts/check_build_skip_friendliness.py` + `Docs/REGISTRY_BUILD_SKIP_FRIENDLINESS.txt` (SHAPE-B WARN, `--ci-mode` fail-on-increase) flags a new skip-ineligible route / single-glob many-entity collection / broad-chrome coupling before it ships — a flagged item is a CANDIDATE/signal, never an auto-defect.** Founder-direct 2026-08-14 ("create a new prioritized program to refactor all code for the website to maximize build/deploy skip opportunities in the future … codify and track"); decision `ADR-134`; program `PLAN_BUILD_DEPLOY_SKIP_MAXIMIZATION_PROGRAM_2026-08-14`; evidence `RESEARCH_BUILD_DEPLOY_SKIP_MAXIMIZATION_2026-08-14`. Composes (never replaces) § R-SITE-BUILD-SELECTIVITY · § R-SITE-BUILD-AUTOMATION · § R-SITE-INCREMENTAL-CACHE-HYGIENE · § R-SITE-WATCH-PATH-SYNC · § R-SITE-DEPENDENCY-CURRENCY. Flagship gated refactor = per-app chapter collections (`q-588091`, extends skip EDITs→ADDs). OVERVIEW #12.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The `FAST_BUILD` route-SUBSET lever (pure SSG, NEVER `output:'server'`) is the DAYTIME-DEFAULT for CF PREVIEW builds; prod publishes FULL via the nightly staging→main release train; `promote-workers` promotes `alias=="main"` versions ONLY (previews never reach prod); dev rides per-branch preview URLs, NEVER `.org` (R-SITE-FAST-BUILD-DEV-SUBSET; 2026-08-18, release-train + FAST-default 2026-08-18 ADR-154)

**Fast builds use the `FAST_BUILD` route-SUBSET lever (`src/lib/fast-build.ts`; `build:fast`/`build:play:fast`; `FAST_BUILD=1|<n>`+`FAST_BUILD_APPS=<csv>`) — slices the heavy `getStaticPaths` routes + `/play` app-dirs, PURE SSG so every gate keeps working. `output:'server'` as a "temporary" flip is a DEFECT (ignores `getStaticPaths`; breaks link-check/screenshot-DoD/PWA/offline-bundle). FAST is the DAYTIME DEFAULT for CF PREVIEW builds (non-prod triggers run `FAST_BUILD=1 …`) AND for LOCAL builds (`npm run build[:play]` auto-defaults FAST when not in CI/CF — a `.fast-build` dist marker bridges the signal to the separate npm postbuild link-check; `build:full`/`FULL_BUILD=1` forces full); PROD (`main`) stays FULL (triggers pin `FULL_BUILD=1`, belt-and-suspenders); a `FAST_BUILD` artifact OMITS pages (link-check no-ops) → NEVER deployable to prod. 🔴 A PROGRAM or WORK ITEM that REQUIRES a full build is FOUNDER-GATED — get approval BEFORE implementation (founder-direct 2026-08-18); fast/subset is the default posture (composes § R-CI-SAFE-TO-SKIP + § R-SITE-BUILD-SKIP-FRIENDLY). Prod publishes via the NIGHTLY RELEASE TRAIN (`nightly-promote.yml` FF-promotes `staging`→`main` → one full prod build → live) — daytime work lands on `staging`/branches (FAST previews). 🔴 `promote-workers.yml` promotes ONLY `annotations."workers/alias"=="main"` versions — a preview version (branch-slug alias) must NEVER be promoted to prod (the preview-isolation P0). Dev rides Cloudflare per-branch PREVIEW URLs (open via `spark-anvil-hub/scripts/open_preview_url.py`), NEVER repurposing `spark-and-anvil.org` (immutable QR host, § `distributed-narrative.md` R-PDF-BOOK-QR-LINKS); both `.com`+`.org` stay full-prod, `publish = merge = full build`.** Founder-direct 2026-08-18; ADR-154; shipped site #2378/#2379/#2380.

**🔴 AMENDMENT — CF PREVIEW BUILDS ARE DISABLED ENTIRELY (founder-direct 2026-08-26, cost).** The two NON-PROD CF Workers-Builds triggers ("Deploy non-production branches" on `spark-anvil-play` `dd569058…` + `spark-anvil-site` `ae5948fb…`) are DISABLED: their `path_includes` is pinned to a never-match sentinel (`__PREVIEW_BUILDS_DISABLED__`), so NO `staging`/feature-branch push builds a CF preview (the branch match itself is NOT PATCH-editable — the API 12002-rejects a branch-field PATCH — so the path-sentinel is the disable lever). This SUPERSEDES the "Dev rides Cloudflare per-branch PREVIEW URLs" sub-point above: there are now **no daytime CF builds at all** — daytime verification is LOCAL (build gates + the screenshot-DoD against a local `dist`, § R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD) + the nightly train's GitHub-Actions **preflight DoD gate** (Vitest + FULL build + scoped e2e, `nightly-promote.yml`). PROD is UNAFFECTED — the "Deploy default branch" (`main`) triggers keep full watch-paths; the train + `promote-workers` + the 6-gate prod DoD (`workflow.md` § R-DEPLOY-STATUS-FROM-CF-BUILD-LOGS) are unchanged. `open_preview_url.py` is now DORMANT (exit 3 — no preview versions exist). **ENFORCED so it can't silently re-enable:** `scripts/sync_cf_watch_paths.py` (`PREVIEW_BUILDS_DISABLED=True`) targets the DISABLED sentinel at the non-prod triggers while keeping canonical watch-paths on prod, so a routine `--apply` (§ R-SITE-WATCH-PATH-SYNC) never restores previews. **REVERSAL** (re-enable previews): set `PREVIEW_BUILDS_DISABLED=False` in `sync_cf_watch_paths.py` → `--apply`. Founder-direct 2026-08-26; ADR-154 amendment.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-154_RELEASE_TRAIN_FAST_PREVIEW_AND_PROMOTER_PREVIEW_EXCLUSION.md` + `Docs/RUNBOOK_NIGHTLY_RELEASE_TRAIN_2026-08-18.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_ASTRO_HYBRID_ALTERNATIVES_REVIEW_2026-08-18.md` §6–§8.
## When it's SAFE to SKIP CI to cut Ubicloud cost — the post-merge full sweep is nightly-only; skipping is deadlock-free while the repo has NO branch protection + availability is not the priority (R-CI-SAFE-TO-SKIP; 2026-07-22)

**Ubicloud CI (`spark-anvil-site`'s `play-tests{,-full}.yml`) is billed per runner-minute, and the biggest daily cost is REDUNDANT runs, not missing coverage. A CI run is SAFE TO SKIP when skipping it (a) cannot deadlock a PR AND (b) loses no test signal that another gate doesn't recover within an acceptable latency. Two standing facts make aggressive skipping safe RIGHT NOW: the repo has NO branch protection (free-private) → a skipped/absent check leaves no "required" status pending, so nothing hangs "waiting for status"; and the site is UNDER CONSTRUCTION → availability is not the priority, so ≤24h detection latency on a merge-only regression is acceptable. A run that IS a correctness gate for a change that can break a shipped clone's mechanic / bank / render is NOT safe to skip.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-22 (Ubicloud cost too high on a daily basis + "the live website is still under development/construction so availability is not the biggest priority"). Decision: `ADR-069`; audit + run-volume data: `AUDIT_CI_COST_SKIP_2026-07-22.md`. This is the SKIP companion to § R-SITE-BUILD-AUTOMATION (the run-CHEAPER lever set); both share the "attack the workload, not the provider" thesis.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A lane adding/editing a `/play` clone's FLEET-INVARIANT surface MUST run the fleet-invariant Vitest before merge — else it merges green-locally but reds the release-train PREFLIGHT, silently blocking EVERY lane's promote until diagnosed (R-CLONE-FLEET-INVARIANT-BEFORE-MERGE; 2026-08-30)

**A lane that ADDS or EDITS a `/play` clone's FLEET-INVARIANT surface — `clone.meta.ts` (`level` MUST be 1–5, `gradeMin` 0–12; `subject` MUST keyword-match a real cluster in `clusters.ts` `clusterOf`, never fall to "More") · `nav.ts` (`auditCloneNav`: home + `links` ≤ 5 VISIBLE tabs, overflow in `more` — ADR-076) · an SEL-app ROUTE (`SEL_APPS` in `tests/e2e/routes.ts` — EVERY route MUST render the always-visible crisis footer `aside.sel-crisis` with 988, `sel-safety.spec.ts` / R-WEB-CLONE-SEL-CRISIS-FOOTER-SCOPE) · any fleet-GLOBBED registry/collection — MUST run the FLEET-INVARIANT Vitest (+ the SEL e2e for an SEL route) locally BEFORE merge: `npx vitest run src/data/play/clone-registry.test.ts src/lib/play/_shared/cloneNav.fleet.test.ts src/lib/play/_shared/cloneNav.bandcap.test.ts src/lib/play/clusters.test.ts src/lib/home/cardCopy.test.ts` (fast, ~1s, no build) + `npx playwright test sel-safety` for an SEL clone. Because R-CI-SAFE-TO-SKIP + FAST-local-builds mean these FLEET tests (which glob EVERY clone) are NOT run at the lane's merge — only at the RELEASE-TRAIN promote PREFLIGHT — a new clone's out-of-range `level` or over-cap `nav` merges GREEN-locally but reds staging and BLOCKS the release train for the WHOLE fleet (nightly + forced) until someone diagnoses it. GENERALIZES: any fleet-globbed invariant test a lane skips accumulates red until a promote; the class fix is "run the fleet-invariant tests for a fleet-touching lane, not just the scoped ones." Proactive hub tripwire (run ANYTIME to confirm "staging is promote-ready"): `python3 scripts/check_clone_fleet_invariants.py --ci-mode` (reads origin/staging; checks level + nav-cap + subject-clusters + SEL-route crisis-footers + the app-count floor; SHAPE-B WARN, `--ci-mode` fails on any violation) + `Docs/REGISTRY_CLONE_FLEET_INVARIANTS.txt`. Fix a violation by clamping `level`→≤5 (or deliberately raising the test cap) and moving nav overflow to `more` — NEVER by force-promoting past the red gate (that ships broken tests + half-finished sibling work to prod). Composes R-CI-SAFE-TO-SKIP (the root — skipping is safe EXCEPT for fleet-invariant tests) + workflow.md § R-CHECK-CONCLUSION-NOT-STATUS (a full-sweep failure orthogonal to your diff → flag + owning lane) + § R-SITE-FORCE-PROMOTE-AND-VERIFY.** Codified per the 2026-08-30 incident (`q-c4e407`): a founder force-promote surfaced 3 staging-red failures — p100/p101 clones `level` 6–7 (vivaquest/inferforge/essayforge) + tourneyforge (8 links) + bottleneckforge (5 links) nav over-cap — all merged green-locally but blocked the promote; fixed (levels→5, nav→4+`more`, 210 clones now clean) + guarded.

## A STALLED release train (prod stale, `staging` piling up) is DIAGNOSED + RECOVERED by a fixed method — find the red pre-flight fleet-invariant, FIX it on `staging`, re-dispatch the promote, verify prod through the build-live lag; NEVER force-past (R-RELEASE-TRAIN-STALL-DIAGNOSE-RECOVER; 2026-09-06)

**When prod looks stale / a founder asks "what's the status of the prod deploys" / the promote hasn't fired in a while, DIAGNOSE + RECOVER the `nightly-promote.yml` `staging→main` release train with this exact method — because a SINGLE red pre-flight fleet-invariant silently blocks EVERY lane's promote (the § R-CLONE-FLEET-INVARIANT-BEFORE-MERGE class), and the train stays stalled until a human fixes that ONE red.
