Prod is UP-but-STALE during a stall (the CF probe stays fresh, `cf_fail=0`); it is NOT down. NEVER "force-promote past the red gate" — that ships broken tests + half-finished sibling work to prod (§ R-SITE-FORCE-PROMOTE-AND-VERIFY).** Codified per the 2026-09-06 incident: the train stalled **~33 h / 263 commits** on THREE stacked pre-flight reds (a `till`-substring Vitest false-positive, a missing `/play/lexitales` landing `<h1>`, a `stamppals`↔`sumpals` `smp` namespace collision); fixed all three + guarded (§ R-WEB-CLONE-LANDING-H1 · § R-DOMAIN-PURITY-REGEX-WORD-BOUNDARY · R-WEB-CLONE-PROGRESS-NS-UNIQUE) → the next scheduled promote did a clean **304-commit** promote, prod verified fresh. Later recurrences → `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § R-RELEASE-TRAIN-STALL-DIAGNOSE-RECOVER.

**The diagnosis + recovery recipe (in order):**
1. **Spot the stall** — `git log origin/main --grep='promote staging' -5` (last promote timestamp) + `git rev-list --count origin/main..origin/staging` (commits stuck). A gap of many hours + a large staging-ahead count = stalled. **⚠ Distinguish a TRUE STALL (the LATEST run(s) failing + staging piling up) from a SELF-HEALED transient flake (an ISOLATED failed run BRACKETED by a later SUCCESS + `staging..main` back to 0 → the train already recovered on its own; NO action needed — do NOT "recover" a train that self-healed). A "what's the prod status" query most often finds the latter: a single mid-day failed promote that the next scheduled run cleared. Read the run HISTORY (`gh run list --workflow=nightly-promote.yml`), not just the one failed run. ⚠ A THIRD healthy-not-stalled case: `watch_cf_build.py --verify` prints `DEPLOY VERIFY: FAIL` on a genuinely-live prod when CF returns a TRUNCATED build log (page-count + link-check parse gaps) — the tell is a FAIL driven ONLY by the two CORE log-derived checks while `outcome=success` + FULL + build-live-convergence + live-assets all pass; that is a HEALTHY prod, NOT a stall — never force-promote/cache-bust on it (fixed 2026-09-18 to derive-pass; `GUIDE_CLOUDFLARE_BUILD_MONITORING.md` § 5d).**
2. **Find the red** — `gh run list --workflow=nightly-promote.yml --limit 5` → the latest FAILED run → `gh run view <id> --json jobs`: a `staging pre-flight (Vitest + full build)` **and/or** `(scoped e2e)` = FAILURE with `promote` = **skipped** is the signature. READ THE ACTUAL FAILED LOG (`gh run view --job=<jid> --log-failed`, `workflow.md` § R-BUILD-FAILURE-READ-FULL-LOG-FIRST) — the pre-flight red is a specific fleet-invariant test, not a vague build issue.
3. **Classify + FIX on `staging`** — the red is almost always a fleet-invariant that merged green-locally at a lane (level/nav-cap/cluster/SEL-footer · a11y-landing-`<h1>` · domain-purity-regex · progress-namespace-uniqueness · a bank/mechanic Vitest). Fix the ROOT on `staging` via a normal PR (base=`staging`), verified locally with the specific test (`npx vitest run <the-file>` / the guard). If a sibling owns the offending clone, coordinate (`workflow.md` § R-PARALLEL-HUB-AGENTS) — but an unowned promote-blocker is fair to fix as part of the unblock.
4. **Re-promote NOW** (don't wait for the next schedule) — `gh workflow run nightly-promote.yml --ref staging` ("publish now" `workflow_dispatch`). ⚠ A manual dispatch can catch a TRANSIENT e2e timeout flake under pool pressure (a 30 s `getAttribute` timeout ≠ a content bug); if the dispatch reds on a flake, the next SCHEDULED run (or a re-dispatch) usually passes — re-read the log to confirm flake-vs-real before re-fixing. **The `Vitest + full build` pre-flight ALSO flakes transiently on a build-time `fetch failed` / `internalConnectMultiple` during `generating static routes` (`@astrojs/cloudflare` prerenderer `getStaticPaths` — an outbound fetch during SSG prerender timing out; the 2026-09-13 14:47Z isolated failure, self-healed by the 00:30Z run). Like the e2e-timeout flake it is NOT a fleet-invariant blocker + is unguardable at the lane — confirm transient by the NEXT run passing the identical gate + `staging..main`→0; only a REPEATED same-fetch failure warrants investigating the prerender-fetching page.** **BUT a REPEATED (≥2 consecutive) element-read timeout on the SAME never-scoped route is NOT a flake — it is an O(n)-per-element serial-loop test blowing the 30 s budget as the fleet grew (§ R-E2E-BATCH-ELEMENT-READS): fix the TEST (batch the reads via `evaluateAll`), NEVER bump the timeout.** **⚠ DISTINCT class (the OPPOSITE fix) — a chronic (≥2 consecutive, incl. a MANUAL dispatch) `Vitest + full build` pre-flight CANCELLED at the timeout mark on `build:play:full` is NOT a flake and NOT a test defect: the FULL build (CORE+PLAY, ~4243 routes) genuinely OUTGREW the job's `timeout-minutes` on the 2-core Ubicloud runner (the 2026-09-16 stall — 3/3 scheduled AND a manual dispatch all cancelled at ~45m20s). Here BUMPING `timeout-minutes` IS the correct durable fix (headroom only; Ubicloud = zero GitHub minutes → cost-free) — the tell that separates it from the e2e-serial-loop above is that it's the BUILD step, not a per-test budget, timing out. PR #3921 bumped preflight 45→60. Editing `.github/workflows/*` needs a `workflow`-scoped token (memory `hub-gh-token-scopes`; founder-grant via `gh auth refresh -s workflow` in a real terminal if missing).**
5. **Verify prod through the build-live lag** — the `promote` job SUCCESS + a new `release: promote staging→main [N commit(s)]` commit on `main` means the promote LANDED, but `promote-live` then runs the full CF prod build (~30–40 min, all routes) before the alias flips. **NEWLY-promoted routes 404 on prod until that build deploys** — that is EXPECTED build-live lag, not a failure. Confirm fresh with a browser-UA `curl -sL` → 200 on the specific just-promoted routes (+ the fix is visibly live), and `staging..main` back to a small number (backlog cleared). Only THEN is the deploy "fresh."

**The KNOWN pre-flight fleet-invariant set (the complete "will the promote pass?" checklist — run these against `staging` to confirm promote-readiness BEFORE a dispatch):** `check_clone_fleet_invariants.py --ci-mode` (level 1–5 · nav ≤5 · subject-cluster · SEL crisis-footer · app-count floor) · `check-play-namespace-unique.mjs` + `namespace-unique.test.ts` (progress-ns uniqueness) · `check-play-landing-h1.mjs` (every landing a visible `<h1>`) · `check-single-main-landmark.mjs` (a BaseLayout page renders NO own `<main>` — the 2026-09-23 `/partners` double-`<main>` stall — § R-SITE-SINGLE-MAIN-LANDMARK) · `check-play-domain-purity-tokens.mjs` (no `till`-class substring landmine) · `check-e2e-no-serial-element-loop.mjs` (no O(n) per-element serial `nth(i)` read-loop in a `tests/e2e/*.spec.ts` — § R-E2E-BATCH-ELEMENT-READS) · `check-turnlink-test-timeout.mjs` (every `*TurnLink.test.ts` self-play oracle declares `{ timeout } ≥ 8000ms` — a heavy Vitest oracle timing out at the 5s default reds the pre-flight Vitest, the 2026-09-12 xiangqi stall — § R-VITEST-HEAVY-ORACLE-TIMEOUT) · `check-heavy-oracle-test-timeout.mjs` (the GENERALIZED sibling — every registry-listed heavy self-play/termination/enumeration/engine-verified oracle BEYOND `*TurnLink` declares `{ timeout } ≥ 8000ms`; catches the broad contention-timeout pool that whack-a-mole per-file fixing can't converge on — P#144 2026-09-15) · `check-chapter-manifests-sync.mjs` (invariant 3/POPULATED — `src/data/multibeat-chapters.json` ≥1000 entries; catches the env-unset false-empty regen that reds `storySampler.test`, the 2026-09-09 stall) · `check-prebuild-fail-closed.mjs` (the chain itself is a pure `&&` chain — a top-level `|| true` silently disables every guard above; § R-PREBUILD-CHAIN-FAIL-CLOSED) · `extract-theme-accents.mjs --verify` (every generated clone theme ≥3:1 light+dark) · `check-sampler-bank-sync.mjs` (every homepage `SAMPLER_PUZZLES` id's `fen`/`bestMove`/`difficulty` matches `public/play/gambittales/puzzles.json` — catches the p145-bank-re-derivation drift that reds `sampler.test.ts`, the 2026-09-14 stall q-9e5e51; a prebuild guard so it reds the LANE not the promote — § R-SITE-PLAYABLE-SAMPLER) · the `runmany`/bank Vitest suite. A new fleet-invariant discovered in a future stall gets ADDED to this list + (ideally) a prebuild guard so it fails at the lane, not the promote (the § R-CLONE-FLEET-INVARIANT-BEFORE-MERGE "class fix"). Composes § R-CLONE-FLEET-INVARIANT-BEFORE-MERGE (the don't-skip half) + § R-SITE-FORCE-PROMOTE-AND-VERIFY (never force-past) + `workflow.md` § R-BUILD-FAILURE-READ-FULL-LOG-FIRST + § R-DEPLOY-STATUS-FROM-CF-BUILD-LOGS.

## The prebuild guard chain is FAIL-CLOSED only while it is a pure `&&` chain — a single top-level `|| true` / `;` / newline makes every guard BEFORE it fail-OPEN, so the train stays green while failures pile up; an advisory step is scoped `(cmd || true)`, and `check-prebuild-fail-closed.mjs` (first in both chains) enforces it (R-PREBUILD-CHAIN-FAIL-CLOSED; 2026-09-27)

**Shell `&&`/`||` are left-associative at EQUAL precedence, so `g1 && … && gN || true && M` parses as `(g1 && … && gN) || true && M` — ANY earlier guard failure is SWALLOWED. A green release train is therefore NOT proof the pre-flight ran: if a guard prints ✖ and later guards print NOTHING while the build proceeds, the chain is fail-open. Invariants, each a defect on the dark-route footing: (1) every build-path npm script (`pre*`/`build*`/`test*`) is a pure `&&` chain; an advisory step is SCOPED `&& (cmd --ci-mode || true) &&` around ONE command, never a bare `|| true`, never `;`/newline/`&`, never a group that chains `&&` or `;` inside; (2) enforced by `scripts/check-prebuild-fail-closed.mjs`, which runs FIRST in `prebuild` + `prebuild:play` (itself fail-closed) + the hub tripwire `scripts/check_site_prebuild_fail_closed.py` (reads site origin/staging); (3) re-arming a gate that was fail-open MUST first run every swallowed guard individually on fresh staging and root-fix each failure (a re-armed gate over hidden reds stalls the whole fleet's train) — never blanket-baseline a real defect; (4) a large pre-existing backlog behind a newly-armed gate becomes a GRANDFATHERED fail-on-NEW baseline that only ratchets DOWN (the `check-css-raw-px` / DN-baseline shape), never a silent skip. Incident 2026-09-22→27: 7 real failures hid behind a green train (53 un-themed index cards · 34 `/advanced` readers without prose snapshots · 4 orphaned themes · raw-px drift · 2 guard false-positives); fixed site #4180.** Program #206.

→ **Full detail (the diagnosis recipe, the 7 fixes, the reusable repair tools `extract-theme-accents.mjs` / `reanchor-multibeat-snapshots.py`):** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § R-PREBUILD-CHAIN-FAIL-CLOSED.

## An e2e spec that iterates a FLEET-GROWING element collection MUST batch its per-element reads in ONE round-trip (`locator.evaluateAll` / one `page.evaluate`) — a serial `nth(i)` read-loop is O(n) protocol calls that blows the 30 s per-test budget as the fleet grows and, on a never-scoped route, STALLS the release-train pre-flight for every lane; NEVER "fix" it by bumping the timeout (R-E2E-BATCH-ELEMENT-READS; 2026-09-07)

**Any `tests/e2e/*.spec.ts` assertion that iterates a FLEET-GROWING element collection (every visible `<button>` in `<main>`, every card/tile/row on a catalog page) MUST extract per-element data in ONE browser round-trip via `locator.evaluateAll(els => …)` (or one `page.evaluate`), NEVER a serial `for (i<n){ const el = loc.nth(i); await el.getAttribute/innerText/… }` loop. A serial loop is O(n) Playwright protocol round-trips — fast locally (passes at merge) but under CI pool latency it exceeds the 30 s per-test budget once the collection crosses a few-hundred elements, and on a NEVER-SCOPED marketing route it reds the scoped-e2e pre-flight on every PR → the whole fleet's promote stalls (§ R-RELEASE-TRAIN-STALL-DIAGNOSE-RECOVER, the 2026-09-07 incident). The batch is the ONLY correct fix: COUNT-INDEPENDENT (O(1) round-trips), assertion IDENTICAL (§ R-TEST-INTEGRITY — never weaken it); bumping the timeout is a DEFECT (re-regresses at the next growth threshold, hides the slow test). Guarded by the site prebuild `scripts/check-e2e-no-serial-element-loop.mjs` (SHAPE-A; flags a `for`/`while` body with a variable-index `.nth()` + a data-read accessor; excludes constant-index + click/fill loops) so a NEW serial read-loop fails at the LANE not the promote. Composes § R-WEB-CLONE-TEST + § R-RELEASE-TRAIN-STALL-DIAGNOSE-RECOVER + § R-CLONE-FLEET-INVARIANT-BEFORE-MERGE.** Founder-flag 2026-09-07.

## A heavy Vitest ORACLE test (a turn-link/board self-play Monte-Carlo oracle that runs games×plies with a per-ply codec round-trip) MUST declare an explicit `{ timeout }` — the default 5000ms is unsafe for a heavy engine and its cost is driven by per-ply ENGINE work, NOT loop bounds (so it's not statically predictable); a bounded+deterministic oracle's declared timeout is STABLE (distinct from the fleet-growing e2e loop, which is BATCHED not timeout-bumped) (R-VITEST-HEAVY-ORACLE-TIMEOUT; 2026-09-12)

**A heavy Vitest ORACLE test — the `src/lib/play/**/*TurnLink.test.ts` self-play oracle (`encode∘decode round-trips over N self-play games …`) that plays games×plies of random self-play with a full codec round-trip EVERY ply, and by generalization any Monte-Carlo/self-play unit oracle — MUST declare an explicit per-test `{ timeout: N }` (≥8000ms), because the Vitest default is 5000ms and the per-test cost is driven by the ENGINE'S per-ply work, NOT the loop bounds (xiangqi ~40 legal moves/ply → 7550ms; draughts does 120×400 plies in <1s) → it is NOT statically predictable, and a heavy engine silently exceeds the 5s budget and STALLS the release-train pre-flight (the pre-flight Vitest is fail-closed on the full fleet suite, so ONE timed-out test blocks EVERY lane's promote — 2026-09-12: the brand-new xiangqi turn-link oracle stalled the train 127 commits / ~24h). The fix is R-TEST-INTEGRITY-preserving (assertion unchanged): the work is BOUNDED + DETERMINISTIC (seeded prng, fixed games×plies), so a generous declared timeout is STABLE — it will NOT re-regress with fleet growth. This is the CRUCIAL distinction from § R-E2E-BATCH-ELEMENT-READS: that rule's e2e loop is O(n) in a FLEET-GROWING collection → BATCH it, NEVER bump the timeout (which re-regresses); THIS oracle's work is FIXED per test → DECLARE the timeout (stable). Do NOT cite the e2e rule to reject a declared timeout on a fixed-work oracle. Guarded by the site prebuild `scripts/check-turnlink-test-timeout.mjs` (SHAPE-A; every `*TurnLink.test.ts` self-play oracle `it()` must declare `{ timeout } ≥ 8000ms`) wired into `prebuild` + `prebuild:play`, so the NEXT heavy oracle (the #138 turn-link program keeps adding games) fails at the LANE, not the promote. Composes § R-WEB-CLONE-TEST + § R-TEST-INTEGRITY + § R-RELEASE-TRAIN-STALL-DIAGNOSE-RECOVER + § R-CLONE-FLEET-INVARIANT-BEFORE-MERGE; sibling of § R-E2E-BATCH-ELEMENT-READS (the Playwright half of "a test blows the CI budget").** Founder ask 2026-09-12 (prod-deploy status → diagnose+recover the stalled train → codify/guard/track/generalize); ✅ SHIPPED site #3773→staging.

**AMENDMENT — GENERALIZED beyond `*TurnLink` to ALL heavy oracles + a registry-driven guard (2026-09-15, P#144; site #3887).** The class is BROADER than turn-link: ANY heavy self-play / bounded-termination / move-enumeration / engine-verified oracle `it()` at the 5000ms default can flake under full-parallel contention. `--sequence.shuffle` reproduced bot·spanforge·isleforge·retryReview·partyGames·atlastales·agentforge·lexitales — all BOUNDED (isolated 2–4s) → `{ timeout: 15000 }`; the two ultra-heavy sync gates (gambittales puzzles ~60s · motifs ~24s) got 120000/60000 budgets. NEW guard `scripts/check-heavy-oracle-test-timeout.mjs` + `Docs/REGISTRY_HEAVY_ORACLE_TEST_TIMEOUT.txt` (HARD-FAIL if a listed oracle loses its timeout; WARN-on-NET-NEW, 29-entry ACK-FAST baseline), sibling of the auto-discovering `*TurnLink` guard, wired into `prebuild`+`prebuild:play`. 🔴 **Don't-re-derive:** the pre-flight runs `ubicloud-standard-2-arm` (~2 workers) — a CI-faithful 2-worker sweep found ONLY puzzles/motifs >2.5s, so the *active* CI risk is oracles whose **ISOLATED** body >5000ms (e.g. xiangqiTurnLink ~11s); the ~30 edge oracles flake ONLY under LOCAL many-worker OVER-subscription (a dev-machine stress artifact, NOT CI) → measure ISOLATED time first, don't panic-fix a local-only flake. (Vitest v4 preempts a timeout EXCEPT on a pure-sync no-yield body — why puzzles-60s passes at the default; a version quirk → the sync-gate budgets are future-proofing.) Table tests count too: `it.each([...])('name', fn, N)` is read by the guard since P#209 S4 (the TriPeaks oracle hid there at 30 s and red CI at 31.8 s → 120 s).

## Every `/play/<app>` landing MUST present a visible hero `<h1>` (WCAG 2.4.6) — a console-on-index clone whose console emits NO heading reds ONLY the release-train a11y PREFLIGHT, silently blocking the whole fleet's promote (R-WEB-CLONE-LANDING-H1; 2026-09-05)

**Every `/play/<app>` landing route MUST present a VISIBLE hero `<h1>` — either a STATIC `<h1>` in `src/pages/play/<app>/index.astro` OR one EMITTED at runtime by an imported `lib/play/<app>/*` console module. A landing with no visible `<h1>` violates WCAG 2.4.6 AND reds `tests/e2e/a11y.spec.ts` "a11y landing heading: /play/<app>" — but that a11y test is a FLEET-INVARIANT that runs ONLY in the full/nightly + release-train-preflight e2e, NOT the scoped per-PR gate, so a heading-less landing merges GREEN-locally and blocks EVERY lane's promote until diagnosed (an instance of § R-CLONE-FLEET-INVARIANT-BEFORE-MERGE).** The load-bearing trap is the **console-on-index** clone: most clones use a static-hero magazine landing (h1 in the astro), but a few render the game console directly on the index (`<div id="…-root">` + a `run…()` script) and rely on the console to inject the heading — if that console emits no `<h1>` (or an immersion refactor drops it), the landing ships headless. Codified per the 2026-09-04→05 incident: LexiTales (Program #100), a console-on-index clone whose `lexitalesConsole` emitted zero `<h1>`, shipped headless and — together with a Vitest false-positive (§ R-DOMAIN-PURITY-REGEX-WORD-BOUNDARY) — **blocked the staging→main promote ~33h / 263 commits** (nightly release train RED at pre-flight). Fixed by adding a static `<h1 class="lt-h">LexiTales</h1>` + dark-safe `.lt-home-head`; 31 other static-h1-less landings were confirmed to inject their h1 via a console module (no false-positive). **Guard (build-time, prebuild + prebuild:play → catches it at the lane, not the promote): `scripts/check-play-landing-h1.mjs`** — for each landing, PASS iff a static `<h1>` is present OR an imported `lib/play/<app>/*` module emits an h1 (`<h1`, `createElement('h1'`, `el('h1'`, `'h1'`); SHAPE-A build-fail, zero false-positives verified fleet-wide (274/274). Fix a violation by adding a static hero `<h1>` to the landing (matching sibling console clones), never by weakening the a11y test.

**Landing:** immersive ones need `pc-immersive-home`, else the hero overlaps (`check-play-landing-home-shell.mjs`).
## A BaseLayout page MUST NOT render its OWN `<main>` — BaseLayout owns the sole `<main id="main-content" role="main">` (the WCAG 2.1.1 skip-target), so a page that wraps its slot content in a second `<main>` renders TWO main landmarks + (usually) a duplicate `id="main-content"` — a WCAG 1.3.1 defect that reds the release-train a11y/smoke preflight only when the page is in the tested route set, silently blocking the whole fleet's promote (R-SITE-SINGLE-MAIN-LANDMARK; 2026-09-23)

**A `src/pages/**/*.astro` that imports `BaseLayout` MUST NOT contain its OWN `<main>` tag — `BaseLayout` unconditionally renders the sole `<main id="main-content" role="main" tabindex="-1">` with a `<slot/>`, so a page-level `<main>` produces TWO nested `<main>` landmarks + (usually) a duplicate `id="main-content"` = a WCAG 1.3.1 violation + invalid HTML. The shared `a11y.spec.ts` + `smoke.spec.ts` assert `expect(locator('main#main-content')).toBeVisible()` (strict-mode violation on 2 matches) AND `page.locator('main').count()===1` — but ONLY on the routes in the tested set (`allPlayRoutes()` + `marketingRoutes()`), so a double-`<main>` page NOT in that set ships GREEN-locally and, the moment it IS in the set, REDS the promote and blocks EVERY lane (§ R-CLONE-FLEET-INVARIANT-BEFORE-MERGE). The correct idiom is a `<div>`/`<section>` content wrapper (BaseLayout owns the `<main>` + skip-target — the `search.astro`/`about.astro` pattern); keep the wrapper's classes/style, DROP the `<main>` tag + its `id="main-content"`.** The 2026-09-23 stall: the P199 `/partners` page (a `marketingRoutes()` top-level page) shipped a second `<main id="main-content">` → 4 e2e fails (a11y + smoke × light + dark) → the staging→main promote stalled **~23h / 36 commits** (last promote 2026-09-22 16:04Z). Fixed `/partners` (site #4099) + 16 other latent double-`<main>` BaseLayout pages fleet-wide (site #4100: `for-parents-educators/{dashboard,family-safety,how-our-ai-works,managed,transparency,transparency-by-subject}` · `play/{commons,guild,me,topics/index,topics/[slug]}` · `p/[game]` · `pilot/interleaved/*`). **Guard (build-time, prebuild + prebuild:play → catches it at the lane, not the promote): `scripts/check-single-main-landmark.mjs`** — for each BaseLayout-importing page, PASS iff it contains no own `<main[\s>]` tag (comment-stripped scan so a `<main>` in prose/`{/* */}` isn't a false positive; 5025 pages clean). SHAPE-A build-fail. Sibling of § R-WEB-CLONE-LANDING-H1 (the same "release-train a11y fleet-invariant, guarded at the SOURCE" family).

## A run-100 "distinct domain" EXCLUSION regex MUST use unambiguous domain words — never a short English substring/word (`till` ⊂ "still"/"until"), which false-positives on innocent prose and reds the promote for the fleet (R-DOMAIN-PURITY-REGEX-WORD-BOUNDARY; 2026-09-05)

**A `/play` clone's run-100-times "distinct domain" Vitest guard asserts the clone's copy does NOT read like ANOTHER clone's statistic, via an EXCLUSION regex `expect(/…/i.test(blob)).toBe(false)`. Every token in that FORBIDDEN regex MUST be an UNAMBIGUOUS domain word — NEVER a short common-English word or a substring of one. A token like `till` (meant for the cash-register/checkout-queue domain) substring-matches ordinary prose — `till` ⊂ "still", "until", "leave it till last" — flipping the assertion to red on innocent copy.** Because these are fleet-Vitest tests that surface at the RELEASE-TRAIN promote pre-flight, one such false-positive reds the promote for the WHOLE fleet (§ R-CLONE-FLEET-INVARIANT-BEFORE-MERGE): the 2026-09-04→05 incident — riddlerealm's forbidden regex carried `till`, its own copy said "leave it till last"/"would still crack" → **1 red test / 31,336 passed blocked staging→main ~33h / 263 commits**. Fix = DROP the ambiguous token (the sibling domain stays guarded by its unambiguous tokens — `queue|checkout|rush-hour|utilisation`), never word-boundary it (`\btill\b` still matches standalone "till"=until) and never edit the innocent copy. **A token in a REQUIRED (`.toBe(true)`) list is fine** — bottleneckforge REQUIRES `till` (the cash-register till IS its checkout domain); the ban applies ONLY to EXCLUSION (`.toBe(false)`) lists. **Guard (prebuild + prebuild:play): `scripts/check-play-domain-purity-tokens.mjs`** — fleet-scans every `runmany.test.ts` forbidden regex for a banned substring-landmine token (denylist, seeded `{till}`; SHAPE-A build-fail; extend only with PROVEN substring-of-common-English tokens, never legit short domain words like `code`/`poll`/`rock`). Also fixed the latent `till` landmine in alcumusforge's forbidden list (same drop-token fix) before it could red a future promote.

## `build:play` is not a full verification — a core-only lib's syntax error slips past it (R-SITE-CORE-PARSE-GATE; 2026-07-11)

**Verifying a change with `npm run build:play` does NOT prove the CORE build is green — the play unit relocates the `/cast` + chapter routes out of `src/pages/`, so any module imported ONLY by those core routes (e.g. `src/lib/image-url.ts`, `audio-url.ts`, chapter/cast components) is never bundled by `build:play` and its syntax errors go uncaught until the Cloudflare CORE build fails.** Any change that touches a **core-only** lib/component MUST be parse-verified against the core build (a `tsc --noEmit`, a full `npm run build`, or at minimum an `esbuild` transform of the changed file), not just `build:play`. Codified per user-direct 2026-07-11 after a P0: `image-url.ts` broke the core Cloudflare build (`Unexpected "*"`) while every `build:play` verification (mine + the parallel clone agents') stayed green.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## A COMMITTED, FS-derived generated MANIFEST that build STRUCTURE imports (the per-app `chapters_<app>` collection set) MUST be committed-FRESH; a stale one silently drops a whole app's `/cast` routes while the FS-scanned `cast-slug-map` still emits its links → broken links only red the FULL prod build; guard it at PREBUILD so a FAST staging preview catches it (R-SITE-CHAPTER-MANIFEST-SYNC; 2026-08-22)

**`content.config.ts` defines one `chapters_<app>` content collection PER APP listed in the COMMITTED `src/data/chapter-apps.generated.json` (imported as `CHAPTER_APP_DIRS`; ADR-134/q-588091 skip-friendliness). The `/cast/[app]/[char]` routes build ONLY from those collections (`getAllChapters`). So if an app has chapter MDs on disk but is MISSING from the committed list, its `chapters_<app>` collection is never defined → its `/cast/<app>/*` routes are NEVER BUILT — while `build-cast-slug-map.mjs` (a direct FS scan) DOES know the app, so `hasChapter()` returns true and the cast/story pages EMIT `/cast/<app>/<char>` links → links to unbuilt routes → the postbuild `check-site-internal-links.py` FAILS the CORE build. TWO invariants, each a hard prebuild FAIL: (1) FRESHNESS — the committed `chapter-apps.generated.json` MUST equal a fresh FS scan of `src/content/chapters/` (it is the LOAD-BEARING committed import that fixes the collection SET; `build-chapter-apps-list.mjs` auto-regenerates the working-tree copy, but content.config.ts resolves the COMMITTED value + the CF build-output cache can restore it, so it must be committed-fresh — a new-app spawn MUST regen+commit it); (2) SUBSET — every app referenced by `cast-slug-map.json` MUST have a chapter directory (else its `/cast` links 404). Enforced by `scripts/check-chapter-manifests-sync.mjs`, wired into `prebuild` + `prebuild:play` BEFORE the generators refresh the files — it is route-INDEPENDENT (readdirSync + set-compare), so it runs identically in a FAST subset build and reds a FAST staging PR instead of the FULL prod build.**

**GENERALIZE — the release-train FAST-preview blind spot for route-graph desyncs.** The `FAST_BUILD` subset previews (§ R-SITE-FAST-BUILD-DEV-SUBSET) that daytime `staging` PRs run do NOT build the `/cast` + `/story` route trees, and the internal-link check is a POSTBUILD gate over the full `dist` — so a broken cast/story/chapter link (or any linked-but-unbuilt-route desync) is INVISIBLE to staging and only reds the FULL prod CORE build after a promote (the 2026-08-22 pawnpals prod-RED: 6 `/cast/pawnpals/<char>` links, chapter MDs present but pawnpals absent from the committed `chapter-apps.generated.json`). The standing lesson: an invariant that guarantees a LINK resolves to a BUILT route must be enforced by a PREBUILD, route-independent guard (like this one), NOT left to the postbuild link-check that only the FULL build exercises. Sibling of § R-SITE-CORE-PARSE-GATE (core-only breakage invisible to `build:play`) + § R-SITE-BUILD-STASH-GLOB-COUPLING (a stashed subtree silently zeroes a build-time glob) + § R-CAST-ROUTE-COVERAGE (guard a cast link with `hasChapter`) + § R-SITE-FAST-BUILD-DEV-SUBSET (why staging is subset-blind). Bypass (emergency only, never to ship a real broken `/cast` link): `SKIP_CHAPTER_MANIFEST_SYNC=1`.

**⚠ MEMORY — a local FULL core build is a ~16GB-RSS MACHINE-LOAD RISK; discharge the `/cast`-freeze gate with the ROUTE-INDEPENDENT PREBUILD GUARDS locally + let CI/CF run the full build off-machine (R-SITE-NO-LOCAL-FULL-BUILD-MEMORY; founder-flagged 2026-09-08).** `FULL_BUILD=1 npm run build` renders ~3,400 routes at once → RSS ~16GB (past the 6GB heap cap), can starve the founder's machine. So for a `/cast`/chapter (or any content-route) merge, do NOT reflexively run a local FULL build: prove FLEET-FREEZE safety LOCALLY + cheaply via `check-chapter-manifests-sync.mjs` (primary freeze cause) + `check-cast-portrait-coverage.py` (slug↔portrait) + a FAST subset build (`FAST_BUILD=1`, <2GB), and let the AUTHORITATIVE full-build link-check run OFF-machine on the CI nightly-train PREFLIGHT (Ubicloud) + the CF prod build. A local FULL build only when a novel link-graph truly requires it (expect+warn the ~16GB spike; never background it). GENERALIZES to any large-route-set local build. (The `.m4a`/`.pdf` broken-link "FAIL" without the CDN env = the § R-SITE-LOCAL-BUILD-R2-ENV false-fail, not a defect — grep the broken list for YOUR slugs; zero hits = clean.)

**GENERALIZE #2 — a set-compare guard proves a collection is DEFINED, not that a route RENDERS; and a CLIENT-JS-rendered internal link is INVISIBLE to the static link-check (R-SITE-CAST-SLUG-MAP-FRESHNESS; founder-flagged 2026-08-22).** TutorForge shipped 17 T1 + 17 T2 `/cast/tutorforge/*` chapters to prod that **404 on the live site** — while every gate stayed GREEN: (a) tutorforge IS in `chapter-apps.generated.json` so FRESHNESS(1) passes + `getAllChapters()` iterates it → the guard proves the *collection is defined*, NOT that the *route rendered into the live artifact*; (b) `cast-slug-map.json` (the FS-derived committed file the client cast rail + `hasChapter()` read) is STALE-MISSING tutorforge — and it has **NO freshness guard** (unlike chapter-apps.generated.json); (c) the `/play` landing builds `/cast/<app>/<char>` links **CLIENT-SIDE in JS** (`href="/cast/' + c.app + '/' + c.char`), so `check-site-internal-links.py` (static-HTML scan) is BLIND to them → a dark cast route emits NO broken-link build-fail. Three standing lessons: (1) **`cast-slug-map.json` is a load-bearing committed generated file → it needs its OWN prebuild FRESHNESS check** (committed == fresh `build-cast-slug-map.mjs` regen), exactly like chapter-apps.generated.json — a chaptered app absent from it is dark on the cast rail; (2) a link rendered by CLIENT JS is un-seeable by the static link-check → cast/story reachability must be guarded at the DATA layer (freshness) AND the only ground truth is a **LIVE ROUTE PROBE** (`curl /cast/<app>/<char>` = 200) — fold a sample per recently-shipped chaptered app into the § `workflow.md` R-PROD-MONITORING content-assertions; (3) a passing manifest-sync guard is necessary-but-NOT-sufficient — it never proves the prod artifact actually built the route (build-cache staleness can drop it). Guard + fix tracked [[q-1cbf91]].

**GENERALIZE #3 — `chapters-pending-art.json` + `multibeat-chapters.json` are ALSO committed generated manifests that must be committed-FRESH; a stale one renders a BROKEN HERO IMG instead of the graceful 📖 placeholder (R-CHAPTER-ART-COMPLETION; founder-flagged 2026-08-23).** `ChapterIllustration` renders the 📖 placeholder ONLY when the chapter is in the `pendingArt` collection (from the committed `chapters-pending-art.json`); otherwise it renders `chapter_<char>_{opener,beat_00}.webp`. So a chaptered app MISSING from a STALE committed `chapters-pending-art.json` renders an `<img>` at a hero webp that 404s on R2 (no art shipped) → a **broken-image box atop every one of its chapter readers** — `onerror`-guarded, so the link-check EXCUSES it (green build) and only the screenshot-DoD catches it (2026-08-23 TutorForge: committed manifest 467 keys / 0 tutorforge vs a fresh regen 531 / +17 → all 34 T1+T2 readers showed a broken opener box; fix = commit the fresh regen → placeholders, site #2661). Both `chapters-pending-art.json` AND `multibeat-chapters.json` need the SAME prebuild FRESHNESS check as `chapter-apps.generated.json` (committed == fresh builder regen) so a stale commit reds a staging PR, not prod. Real hero-art gen = the separate enrichment drain [[q-751616]]; the freshness guard = [[q-d9875d]]. GENERALIZES: EVERY committed generated manifest a content collection reads needs a prebuild freshness guard. **✅ PARTIAL (2026-09-09, site #3635): `check-chapter-manifests-sync.mjs` invariant `3/POPULATED` now floor-guards `multibeat-chapters.json` (≥1000 entries) — catching the env-unset FALSE-EMPTY regen mode (`2942→0`, the 2026-09-09 release-train stall; § R-RELEASE-TRAIN-STALL-DIAGNOSE-RECOVER). A full committed-==-fresh-regen freshness check for both manifests remains [[q-d9875d]].**

**GENERALIZE #4 — the R2-HERO ROSTER (`chapters-hero-on-r2.json`) is a committed generated source a builder TRUSTS that silently DRIFTS on every gen wave → it needs a RECONCILER + drift-tripwire, not just a freshness check (R-CHAPTER-ART-COMPLETION; 2026-08-27, resolves [[q-751616]]).** The pending-art builder trusts the roster as the purge-proof "hero exists on R2" signal (the webp is git-rm'd → in CI the roster is the ONLY signal), but it was a FROZEN pre-purge snapshot with NO auto-sync, so post-purge gen+upload waves added art to R2 while the roster stayed stale → **~521 already-done MULTIBEAT chapters across ~90 apps false-pending** (`beat_00`=200 on R2; multibeat readers render beat art independent of pending-art → a TRACKING, not rendering, defect). Resolved [[q-751616]] with **zero paid gen** (art was already on R2; a gen wave would have been a redundant clobber — R-WAVE-RUNNER-R2-IDEMPOTENCY / R-VERIFY-FOLLOWON-NOT-ALREADY-DONE). Discipline: **after ANY chapter gen+upload wave run `scripts/sync_chapters_hero_on_r2_roster.mjs`** (R2-ground-truth, ADDITIVE; `--check`=drift detector) then rebuild pending-art; deterministic tripwire `scripts/check-chapter-hero-roster-drift.mjs` (no-network, baseline 0, WARN in CORE prebuild) flags any multibeat chapter in pending-art. Fixed site #2903 (595→74; the 74 = genuine single-beat opener gaps → [[q-896745]]).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## A localhost dev server CANNOT load `crossorigin` R2 media (audio/VTT) — the CDN allows only the prod origin, so audio/VTT-dependent behavior is verified on the DEPLOYED site, not localhost (R-SITE-LOCALHOST-R2-CORS; 2026-07-30)

**Any media served from the R2 CDN (`cdn.spark-and-anvil.com`) with a `crossorigin` attribute — the chapter/drama `<audio src>` + its WebVTT `<track src>` — is CORS-BLOCKED on an `astro dev`/static-serve localhost origin (the bucket only sends `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` for the PROD origin). So a localhost `/cast` render shows CORS/403/404 console noise + the `<audio>` fails to load + any audio/VTT-dependent behavior is non-functional. This is a LOCAL-DEV ARTIFACT, NOT a product bug — the DEPLOYED site loads the same media fine. Verify audio/VTT behavior on the deployed site, not localhost.**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## 🔴 CRITICAL: a local `spark-anvil-site` build MUST export the THREE `PUBLIC_*_CDN_URL` vars (not just `~/.r2-env.sh` creds) — else R2-media-derived manifests EMPTY locally, which not only FALSE-FAILS the link-check but VACUOUSLY FALSE-PASSES any render-parity/staleness before-vs-after gate (R-SITE-LOCAL-BUILD-R2-ENV; 2026-07-18, escalated CRITICAL + render-parity-false-pass dimension 2026-08-14)

**🔴 Before ANY local `npm run build` / render-parity / staleness / screenshot-DoD gate on `spark-anvil-site`: `source ~/.r2-env.sh` AND export all three `PUBLIC_AUDIO_CDN_URL`+`PUBLIC_IMAGE_CDN_URL`+`PUBLIC_PDF_CDN_URL`=`https://cdn.spark-and-anvil.com` (the env-file sets R2 *credentials* ONLY, not these three).** The prebuild manifest builders are R2-aware only via `Boolean(process.env.PUBLIC_*_CDN_URL)`: SET → they enumerate from committed `.vtt`/sidecars (CF-faithful); UNSET → they scan for R2-only `.m4a`/media (absent locally) → emit a near-EMPTY manifest, with three escalating failure modes — (a) FALSE-FAIL (link-check reds), (b) FALSE-HIGH (pending-* backlog explodes), and the critical (c) 🔴 **FALSE-PASS: a render-parity/staleness before-vs-after gate for a data-collection-migration refactor passes VACUOUSLY because BOTH the control and treatment builds regenerate the SAME empty manifest — the migrated render path is never exercised and "0 diffs" proves nothing** (surfaced isolating the `drama` manifest, q-5566a3 Tier-2: 0/2,619 diff while the collection was empty in both). **A before-vs-after gate is valid only once you VERIFY the migrated collection is NON-EMPTY (entry count ≈ committed manifest) with the env set.** An instance of `workflow.md` § R-ADVERSARIAL-REVIEW-AND-QA ("empirical execution is the decisive filter" + "use REALISTIC fixtures").

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § R-SITE-LOCAL-BUILD-R2-ENV.
## The shared `play.css` tail-append is a merge-collision hotspot — parse-gate it at prebuild (R-PLAY-CSS-PARSE-GATE; 2026-07-12)

**`src/styles/play.css` is a SHARED, tail-appended surface — every `/play/<app>` clone build appends its `.pc-theme-<app>` block to the end under R-PARALLEL-WEB-CLONE-BUILD single-flight — and concurrent clone merges routinely collide on that tail. A botched tail-append conflict resolution that DROPS a closing `}` red-builds the Cloudflare `build:play` with `[postcss] play.css:<EOF>:1: Unclosed block` — a P0 that surfaces ~1.6s deep in vite (after the whole prebuild) with the error line reported at EOF (useless for localizing).** The gate `scripts/check-play-css-parse.mjs` (postcss parse + a brace-balance localizer) is wired into BOTH `prebuild` and `prebuild:play` so this class fails LOUDLY at prebuild with the real unclosed-block line, not deep in the build. Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-12 (*"fix and codify"*) after the V121 P0.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Kill the parallel-fleet merge-races structurally — union-merge the append-only shared surfaces + per-app CSS files (R-WEB-CLONE-MERGE-HYGIENE; 2026-07-12)

**The parallel web-clone fleet's push/merge conflicts come almost entirely from a handful of APPEND-ONLY shared files that every clone touches at the same spot (the tail / the closing bracket). Three structural changes remove the conflicts at the source instead of resolving them by hand every time.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-12 (*"there are a lot of push/merge conflicts with all the parallel hub agents. what can we do about it?"*). The reactive discipline (rebase-then-resolve, keep-both, renumber-on-conflict — R-PARALLEL-WEB-CLONE-BUILD / R-PARALLEL-HUB-AGENTS) stays as the fallback, but these make it rarely needed.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## All hub-side `spark-anvil-site` work + Explore/research site-reads happen in a throwaway `git worktree` off `origin/staging` (the daytime SSOT) — never the shared clone, and never `origin/main` for current-state (R-SITE-WORKTREE; 2026-07-13; staging-basis 2026-08-22)

**Any hub session touching `spark-anvil-site` — building a clone, authoring tests, verifying a build, a docs/rule edit that must compile against the site — MUST work in a fresh `git worktree` off `origin/staging`, NOT the shared `/Volumes/.../spark-anvil-site` clone and NOT `origin/main`.** Under the release train (ADR-154 Amd 6/7) `origin/staging` IS the GitHub default + daytime SSOT + default PR base, so a worktree/PR off it diffs cleanly, while off `origin/main` re-creates the stale-base cross-diff the train avoids (reconcile staging UP first if behind — never base on `main`). **This EXTENDS to Explore/research agents (founder-direct 2026-08-22 "make the explore agent use site staging, not main"): any agent reading CURRENT site state — is clone X built? what does the landing render? what will my PR diff against? — reads `origin/staging`, NEVER `origin/main`.** `origin/main` REMAINS the prod/ground-truth ref the completeness/build-breaker GUARDS read explicitly (R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH) + a prod live-verify (`curl`) checks — the split is **current-daytime-state = `staging`** vs **prod-truth = `main`**; never flip a guard's deliberate `origin/main` read. Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-13 (worktree approach — shared clone found 8 commits behind → aborted `git pull --ff-only`); staging-basis added per founder-direct 2026-08-22.

Guards: `exists(.git)` not `isdir` (`check_git_worktree_safe.py`). **`npm ci` in the worktree, never symlink `node_modules`** (a symlinked install builds RED, not your diff). **A local build rewrites ~400 tracked files: commit by explicit path; run `check_site_branch_generated_churn.py --site-root <wt>` before pushing.**

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-SITE-LOCAL-BUILD-R2-ENV).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Local site gates run on NODE 22 (`.nvmrc`) — node 26+ BREAKS Playwright + Vitest; and the tracked-`node_modules`-symlink defect can REGRESS (R-SITE-LOCAL-GATE-NODE22; 2026-08-21)

**A hub session that must run the site gates (Vitest / Playwright / screenshot-DoD / `build:play`) MUST use NODE 22 — the repo's `.nvmrc` + `engines: node >=22.0.0`. Node 26+ (bleeding-edge) BREAKS the site toolchain: `playwright install` HANGS on node 26 ([microsoft/playwright#40724](https://github.com/microsoft/playwright/issues/40724)), and Vitest 4 / Astro 7 error under it — the "exit-194 / gates-unrunnable" state. If only node 26 is on PATH, the gates are unrunnable and un-gated `/play`/shared-engine code MUST NOT be merged (honest-yield).** Codified 2026-08-21 (founder "fix the env. deep web research and think harder") after a session found ONLY node 26 (no version manager) → all site gates dead.

**The fix (Homebrew Mac, no version manager):** `brew install node@22` (keg-only) → prepend `export PATH="/opt/homebrew/opt/node@22/bin:$PATH"` for the site work (do NOT `brew link --force` over node@26). Verify `node -v` = v22.x before `npm ci`. A version manager (fnm/nvm/volta reading `.nvmrc`) is equivalent but heavier; the keg + PATH-prepend is the minimal durable fix.

**⚠ The `node_modules` symlink is committed-DELETED-and-gitignored, but the tracked-symlink DEFECT can REGRESS (it did — 2026-08-21, live on staging+main).** A worktree that symlinks `node_modules` (discouraged — `npm ci` in the worktree, R-SITE-WORKTREE); a whole-index `git add` can commit that **symlink** into git, and `.gitignore`'s `node_modules/` (trailing-slash = DIRECTORY rule) does NOT match the symlink FILE → it slips in and a fresh clone / Cloudflare build resolves it to a nonexistent path → `npm ci`/build breaks (the site #1076 class, REGRESSED). **Detection:** `git ls-tree origin/staging node_modules` returns a `120000` (symlink) blob — should return nothing. **Fix:** `git rm --cached node_modules` + change `.gitignore` `node_modules/` → **bare `node_modules`** (matches dir AND symlink); PR to **staging**. **Prevention:** pathspec `git add <files>`, NEVER `git add -A`/`.` in a worktree with a `node_modules` symlink (workflow.md § R-PARALLEL-HUB-AGENTS discipline 6). A CI/prebuild guard failing on a tracked `node_modules` symlink is tracked (`q-nmslink-guard`).

**⚠ Do NOT background `git worktree add` / `git status --porcelain` on the full site tree** — a large checkout auto-backgrounds past the ~2-min cap; racing it (a concurrent `rm -rf`/second `worktree add`) WEDGES the dir + can leave a half-checkout that looks like mass deletions (do NOT commit that state). Let the checkout finish (foreground or await the task notification); verify a full checkout with TARGETED file checks (`ls .github/workflows/*.yml`, `[ -f package.json ]`), not `git status --porcelain` (slow/hangs on the big tree).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Parallel clone-building — one agent per app, disjoint by construction (R-PARALLEL-WEB-CLONE-BUILD; 2026-07-10)

**Multiple hub agents MAY build multiple `/play/<app>` clones in parallel — a specialization of `R-PARALLEL-HUB-AGENTS` (workflow.md) enabled by ADR-033 per-app namespacing.** Because every clone lives in its own disjoint `src/{pages,lib,data}/play/<app>/` + `public/play/<app>/` + `Docs/web/<app>/` subtree, two agents building different apps touch **zero common files** in the normal path — which designs out the dominant parallel-agent failure mode (shared-hotspot merge conflicts).

→ **Sub-rules** defined here (full detail in reference): (R-SITE-BUILD-QUIET-PRERENDER).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Build-disk budget + R2 media hosting (R-SITE-BUILD-DISK-BUDGET + R-SITE-MEDIA-R2; 2026-06-30)

**The site build has a finite disk budget, and heavy media committed into `public/` is the thing that blows it.** Codified after a 2026-06-30 Cloudflare `ENOSPC: no space left on device` build failure (during Astro `staticBuild` → `generatePath`, writing prerendered HTML). Work-queue § "V28 P0 — Cloudflare Pages build FAIL: ENOSPC".

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## No large data is inlined PER-PAGE — dicts/manifests/search-index/kit-banks go in a shared `_astro` chunk or lazy-load; a per-page HTML-weight budget guard enforces it (R-SITE-PAGE-WEIGHT-BUDGET; 2026-08-20)

**No page may inline large data into its rendered HTML — a dictionary, manifest, search-index, or kit-bank that repeats across pages MUST live in a SHARED `_astro` chunk (Vite-imported once) or be lazy-loaded, NEVER inlined via `define:vars`/`set:html` into every page's HTML. A per-page inline-data bloat multiplies across the page set and can freeze a deploy unit (the 2026-08-20 ENOSPC outage → reference). Enforced at BUILD time by `scripts/check-page-size.mjs` (postbuild + postbuild:play; `WARN ≥ 800 KB`, `FAIL ≥ 3.5 MB` per page — the heaviest legit page `/story` is ~2.3 MB; grandfather a genuine large index in `Docs/REGISTRY_PAGE_SIZE.txt`, `SKIP_PAGE_SIZE_GATE=1` escapes). Composes ABOVE § R-SITE-BUILD-DISK-BUDGET (the dir-level disk budget) + § R-SITE-BUILD-SKIP-FRIENDLY (this is the per-page-HTML-weight axis).** Founder-direct 2026-08-20 (q-bb0775).

**Repeated bytes (P#209 S4):** the SAME ≥ 8 KB inline script/style/attribute on ≥ 20 pages fails `scripts/check-repeated-inline-blobs.mjs` (postbuild, both units) unless allow-listed as pre-paint — ship it as a bundled script or a lazily fetched prerendered `.json`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Web performance is judged by REAL-USER Core Web Vitals (Cloudflare Web Analytics) — the lab probe diagnoses; every fleet-wide byte / CLS / load-time-fetch class the audit finds gets a deterministic guard (R-SITE-RUM-CWV-BUDGET; 2026-09-28)

**Performance work starts from REAL-USER data (`scripts/audit_cf_web_analytics.py`, Cloudflare RUM; p75 LCP ≤2.5 s · INP ≤200 ms · CLS ≤0.1; baseline `Docs/REGISTRY_RUM_CWV_BUDGET.txt`, `--ci-mode` fails only on a regression into poor) — trust a p75 only on ≥20 real beacons (sampled; our headless runs excluded). Guarded invariants: (1) shared-chrome `<img>` ≤60 KB with width/height; (2) i18n ships ONE language per chunk, English visitors load NONE (no value-import of `~/lib/i18n/{t,dict}`; eager pages carry `data-sa-eager-en`); (3) UI that appears after first paint reserves its space pre-paint; (4) no optional model/heavy runtime loads before the learner's gesture; (5) a clone page loads only its own theme CSS. Honest-yield: lab deltas are verified in RUM after deploy.** Founder-direct 2026-09-28; `ADR-302`; program #209.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-302_RUM_CWV_BUDGET.md` + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## A page NEVER crashes because site storage is blocked — the first-in-`<head>` StorageShim swaps in in-memory Storage when access throws (R-SITE-BLOCKED-STORAGE-SAFE; 2026-09-29)

**With cookies/site data blocked (some managed school devices, sandboxed embeds), touching `localStorage` — even `typeof localStorage` — throws `SecurityError`. Every layout that owns `<head>` renders `<StorageShim />` as its FIRST script; when access throws it defines in-memory `localStorage`/`sessionStorage` for the page's lifetime and sets `html[data-storage-blocked]`. Pages keep working, nothing persists, nothing is sent (COPPA-neutral). Never "fix" this per reader. Guards: `StorageShim.test.ts` (placement + behaviour) + `tests/e2e/blocked-storage.spec.ts` (simulated + real sandboxed-frame).** `ADR-302` Amd 3; program #209.

## Offline / installable PWA is a SITE-WIDE capability, not a per-clone axis — hybrid precache/runtime, persist, never-precache-media, room-MP-stays-online (R-SITE-OFFLINE-PWA; 2026-07-18)

**`spark-and-anvil.com` ships an offline-capable, installable PWA as ONE site-wide shell (service worker + Web App Manifest via `@vite-pwa/astro`/Workbox), NOT as an ADR-048 per-clone expansion axis — and it obeys the caching + degradation + persistence guardrails below. Offline is an ACCESS/DELIVERY baseline (equity / the homework-connectivity gap), inherited by every `/play` clone for free; it teaches nothing new, so it is a portfolio-wide capability like the `R-COGNITIVE-ACCESSIBILITY` `ReadingAccess` backbone — never an expansion lever an agent adds per clone.** Codified per founder-approval 2026-07-18 (ADR-049) on the evidence in `Docs/RESEARCH_SITE_OFFLINE_PWA_2026-07-18.md`; phased rollout in `Docs/PLAN_SITE_OFFLINE_PWA_2026-07-18.md`. **We are the CLEAN case:** prerendered-everything (`output:"hybrid"`, no `prerender=false` → CDN-cached static files; § R-SITE-BUILD-QUIET-PRERENDER) = exactly what Workbox precache wants (NOT the Cloudflare-SSR precaching pitfall), and the `/play` clones are already ~offline by construction (static kit JSON + client-side deterministic engines + no-identifier `localStorage` state).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## The whole portfolio ships as a PORTABLE OFFLINE BUNDLE — a zippable, self-contained, LOCAL-FIRST/ONLINE-OPTIONAL distribution for no-internet AND limited-internet environments; served by a bundled LOCAL SERVER (never `file://`), tiered, marketed on `/offline` (R-SITE-OFFLINE-PORTABLE-BUNDLE; 2026-08-15)

**The portfolio is producible as a self-contained, ZIPPABLE Portable Offline Bundle (POB) — site + every `/play` clone + the chosen tier of R2 media — that runs with NO internet and, in LIMITED-internet settings, keeps networked FEATURES alive. It is DISTINCT from (and complementary to) the in-browser PWA (§ R-SITE-OFFLINE-PWA): the PWA caches what you visited on the live origin; the POB is complete-on-arrival, carry-it-on-a-USB, never needs the origin. Six load-bearing invariants, each a defect on the footing of a dark route: (1) BUNDLED LOCAL SERVER, NEVER `file://` — our SSG uses absolute `/` paths + client `fetch()` of kit JSON + a service worker, none of which `file://` can serve (SW needs a secure context; `file://` blocks local `fetch()`; absolute paths resolve to filesystem root) → ship a tiny cross-platform static-server launcher (or documented `python3 -m http.server`); a `file://` double-click bundle is OUT of scope. (2) LOCAL-FIRST / ONLINE-OPTIONAL — ALL static content + media is LOCAL (fully offline), but the inherently-networked FEATURES keep their ABSOLUTE production endpoints so they light up whenever ANY connectivity exists: room-code multiplayer (`/api/room/*` → `https://sparkanvil.org/api/room`, the V261 worker; pass-and-play always works locally, fully-offline shows "needs a connection") + feedback aggregate; PASSIVE TELEMETRY (Plausible) is OMITTED (a distributed USB artifact must not phone home — telemetry is not a feature). Line: content localized · live features keep absolute prod endpoints · telemetry dropped. (3) TIERED channels (Kolibri/RACHEL model) — LITE (~3 GB, site+clones+kits, no R2 media, the USB MVP) · STANDARD (~12 GB, +audio+beat-art+VTT) · COMPLETE (~34 GB, +all PDF books) · SLICE (per-cluster/app/band); ship LITE first. (4) BUILD-with-CDN-SET → post-build LOCALIZE — build with `PUBLIC_*_CDN_URL` SET (complete manifests; sidesteps § R-SITE-LOCAL-BUILD-R2-ENV); a hub-owned localizer scans dist for `cdn.spark-and-anvil.com/<path>` refs, downloads each (tier-filtered) to `dist/<path>`, rewrites TIER-INCLUDED prefixes to root-relative `/<path>` and LEAVES higher-tier media refs absolute (online-optional — they load from the CDN when connected); idempotent, resumable, R2-egress-free. **Plausible telemetry omission is enforced by the pipeline's REWIRE stage, which STRIPS the `plausible.io` `<script>` from the built HTML (belt; the event-bridge already no-ops without `window.plausible`) — NOT by a source `OFFLINE_BUILD` flag (a source guard on shared chrome `BaseLayout.astro` would risk racing concurrent site lanes; the pipeline strip is authoritative + collision-free). An optional `OFFLINE_BUILD` source guard MAY be added later in a quiet window.** (5) MARKET it — a `/offline` download hub (tier chooser + checksums + a "works-offline vs when-connected" table + the local-server how-to; zips hosted on R2, § R-R2-SYSTEM-OF-RECORD), reachable from `/for-parents-educators` + a homepage trust-band, as an access/equity moat pillar (composes with § R-SITE-AI-SAFETY-MOAT-MARKETING; honest-yield, COPPA Privacy link). (6) FOREGROUND-monitored, resumable execution drain (§ R-GEN-NO-BACKGROUND; R2 egress is free but time-bound); hub owns the pipeline + `/offline`, the ACCOUNT hosts the zips (flag in the PR). DoD = unzip→launch→localhost→OFFLINE (homepage+clone+story+search work, 0 external requests, 0 console errors)→RECONNECT (room-MP+feedback reach the live worker) + the dual-scheme screenshot-DoD on `/offline`.** Founder-direct 2026-08-15 (*"make the whole website including R2 media work offline/local … zipped up … no-internet AND limited-internet … keep networked room-code play … market this on our website … deep web research, codify and track everything"*); decision `ADR-144`; research `RESEARCH_SITE_OFFLINE_PORTABLE_BUNDLE_2026-08-15.md`; program `PLAN_SITE_OFFLINE_PORTABLE_BUNDLE_PROGRAM_2026-08-15.md`.

**Amendment invariants (full detail → the ADRs below; each a defect on the same dark-route footing):** **(Refresh cadence, ADR-144 W6)** a POB is a point-in-time SNAPSHOT — re-run per major content wave / quarterly floor, `--stamp YYYY-MM-DD`, surface the snapshot date on `/offline`; staleness is INFO not a defect (live features stay current). **(7) TABLETS (ADR-146)** — LAN thin-client PRIMARY (`serve.py` binds `0.0.0.0`, tablets browse `http://<lan-ip>:<port>/`, Kolibri/RACHEL model); HONEST secure-context caveat — over plain-HTTP LAN the SW/"install to home screen" will NOT activate (loopback-only carve-out) but everything else works: state it plainly, never hide it; a standalone on-device tablet app is Swift/Kotlin SOURCE → founder-gated app-spawn (hub authors spec+payload, NEVER source). **(8) ANTI-TAMPER (ADR-146)** — minisign(Ed25519)-signed manifest + per-file SHA-256 + a zero-dep in-bundle `verify.py`; CHANNEL SEPARATION (zips on R2, `.minisig`+SHA256SUMS+pubkey on HTTPS/repo/bundle); tamper-EVIDENT not tamper-proof (never say "tamper-proof"). **(9) DIRECT DOWNLOAD (ADR-149)** — no email/form/account/lead-capture wall (COPPA/no-PII; R2 zero-egress); ⏸ the public launch stays GATED "Coming soon" (`DOWNLOADS_ENABLED=false`) — a DEFERRAL, not request-gating. (History: `q-4b5e78` GO'd downloads LIVE 2026-08-17, but the founder REVERTED to "Coming soon" 2026-09-02 [site #3169; `AUDIT_POB_UNLIMITED_DOWNLOAD_RISK_2026-08-17`=SUPERSEDED] → re-enabling is a FOUNDER-GATED one-line flip to `true`, NOT "pending an assessment.") **(10) VERSIONING (ADR-149)** — CalVer payload + SemVer format, IMMUTABLE bytes (a signed zip binds to exact bytes); publish-order fail-closed (immutable bytes → per-(tier×variant) `latest.json` → mutable "latest" pointer LAST). **(11) TWO VARIANTS (ADR-149)** — UNRESTRICTED (full) + RESTRICTED (geopolitically-sensitive clones excluded; per-clone `geopoliticalSensitivity` extends § R-DV1-POLITICAL-SENSITIVITY); MARKET by content-fit ("Full library"/"Core edition"), NEVER "censored/safe/unsafe" (stigmatizing). **(KEY CUSTODY, ADR-146 amend)** — HUB MAY hold the signing keys (founder-approved 2026-08-16); the SECRET key is NEVER committed (0600, only the PUBLIC key published).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/PLAN_SITE_OFFLINE_PORTABLE_BUNDLE_PROGRAM_2026-08-15.md` (Waves 7–8) + `Docs/ADR-144_SITE_OFFLINE_PORTABLE_BUNDLE.md` + `Docs/ADR-146_POB_TABLET_DELIVERY_AND_ANTITAMPER.md` + `Docs/ADR-149_POB_BUNDLE_VERSIONING_AND_RESTRICTED_VARIANTS.md` + `Docs/DECISION_OFFLINE_BUNDLE_DIRECT_DOWNLOAD_VS_GATED_2026-08-16.md` + `Docs/DECISION_OFFLINE_DOWNLOADS_COMING_SOON_RISK_ASSESSMENT_2026-08-16.md` + `Docs/RESEARCH_POB_TABLET_ANTITAMPER_2026-08-16.md`. Pipeline: `scripts/build_offline_bundle.py` (5 stages + `--tier` + `--slice app|cluster|band` + `--measure` + `--pubkey`; DRY-RUN by default; `serve.py` LAN binding + PACKAGE anti-tamper set). OVERVIEW program #48.

## Diagnose a FAILED build from its ACTUAL error line — never from inference, and never from a TRUNCATED log (R-BUILD-FAILURE-READ-FULL-LOG-FIRST; 2026-07-19)

**When a build FAILS (Cloudflare Workers Builds, CI, or local), the FIRST step is to read the COMPLETE build log and find the actual error/failure line — BEFORE forming any theory about the cause. NEVER infer a build-failure cause from indirect signals (build duration, "killed mid-output", a resource-limit guess, "it OOM'd") or from a TRUNCATED log tail. The error message is authoritative; a theory built on a partial log wastes hours and misdirects the fix (and the money).** Codified per founder 2026-07-19 (*"why you didn't check the build logs before?"* + *"codify this"*).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A published chapter's HERO ART ships in the same wave — and an art-less chapter can NEVER break the build OR be silently forgotten (R-CHAPTER-ART-COMPLETION; 2026-07-19)

**Publishing chapter CONTENT (a `src/content/chapters/<app>/<char>.md`, i.e. an app-repo `Docs/dn-s/chapters/` chapter synced to the site, or a DN-S/spawn integration that registers chapter MDs) OBLIGATES shipping that chapter's HERO ILLUSTRATION in the SAME wave. A chapter with content but no hero is not "done" — it is a tracked debt that MUST be drained (generate + distribute the art), never a silent gap. The build is protected so an art-less chapter can NEVER again break it, and the gap is surfaced three ways so it can NEVER be silently forgotten.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-19 (*"codify the workflow so that the missing illustrations gen is guaranteed to be picked up and completed"* + *"and not forgotten silently"*), after the V385 younger-3 incident (see § R-BUILD-FAILURE-READ-FULL-LOG-FIRST): a DN-S `/story` integration registered 12 chapter MDs + 12 portraits but generated NO chapter illustrations, so `ChapterIllustration`'s non-multibeat fallback emitted 12 broken `chapter_<char>_opener.webp` links → the postbuild `check-site-internal-links.py` gate failed the WHOLE core build → **every core-page deploy was blocked** (the `/story` age-band selector + all core changes stuck) until the links were fixed.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Missing MULTI-BEAT chapters are a TRACKED backlog, never silently forgotten — a standing coverage queue + build-log signal (R-CHAPTER-MULTIBEAT-COMPLETENESS; 2026-07-20)

**R-MULTIBEAT-DEFAULT makes the 5-beat chapter the forward authoring standard, and the opportunistic-upgrade discipline converts legacy single-beat chapters on-touch — but for a long time NOTHING surfaced HOW MANY chapters were still single-beat, so the conversion backlog was silently forgotten (audit 2026-07-20: 626 of 1,161 T1 chapters — only 46% multi-beat). Every published T1 chapter that is NOT yet multi-beat MUST appear in a standing, committed coverage queue that regenerates every build + logs a build-line — so the backlog (and its drain) is always visible, never silently forgotten.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-20 (*"audit for missing … multi-beat chapters. codify so they are [not] silently forgotten"*). This is the multi-beat-coverage sibling of § R-CHAPTER-ART-COMPLETION (hero art) — same anti-silent-forget discipline, the coverage axis.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## A pending-art / multibeat gen-drain candidate MUST be verified against R2 CDN GROUND TRUTH before any paid gen — the committed pending manifest lies in BOTH directions (R-GEN-DRAIN-VERIFY-R2-GROUND-TRUTH; 2026-07-23)

**Before firing ANY paid Gemini gen to "drain" the pending-art or pending-multibeat queue, you MUST (1) regenerate the manifests WITH the CDN env vars set, AND (2) curl-verify each remaining candidate against R2 CDN GROUND TRUTH (browser-UA curl `chapter_<slug>_beat_02.webp` / `beat_00`) — because the COMMITTED pending queue is a STALE SECONDARY ARTIFACT that misrepresents the backlog in BOTH directions. R2 is the system-of-record for shipped art/audio, NEVER the committed manifest (§ R-CANONICAL-DOC-GROUND-TRUTH + § R-R2-SYSTEM-OF-RECORD). A "config-clean/configured" count is NOT an R2-verified count; verify the DRAIN against R2. The committed `REGISTRY_CHAPTERS_PENDING_MULTIBEAT.txt` is a permanent FALSE-HIGH (Cloudflare regenerates it R2-correctly at deploy; the committed snapshot froze pre-CDN) — a bare read would trigger a redundant $-gen wave clobbering shipped R2 takes (§ R-WAVE-RUNNER-R2-IDEMPOTENCY).**

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Slimming `spark-anvil-site` git history — large-push + purge-during-active-content discipline (R-SITE-HISTORY-PURGE; 2026-07-11)

**When rewriting `spark-anvil-site` history to reclaim git bloat (committed regenerable media — see ADR-034), two things are load-bearing and were learned the hard way in the V89 purge (which took the repo 9.13 → 2.84 GiB).** This rule is the standing distillation; ADR-034 + `RUNBOOK_SITE_GIT_HISTORY_PURGE_2026-07-10.md` are the full method, `PLAN_SITE_REPO_FURTHER_REDUCTION_2026-07-11.md` is the remaining-levers roadmap.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Blobless clones are the standing default for `spark-anvil-site` — no history rewrite required (R-SITE-BLOBLESS-CLONE; 2026-07-11)

**Every consumer that clones `spark-anvil-site` — local dev, a CI runner, a throwaway build worktree, a subagent — SHOULD clone it blobless: `git clone --filter=blob:none <url>`.** This is ADR-034 Track A promoted from "decided" to a standing default because it is the single **best bang-for-zero-risk** repo-size lever: it cuts fresh-clone transfer to ~the commit+tree objects (file *contents* are fetched lazily, on demand, only for the blobs you actually touch), it changes **no SHA**, it rewrites **no history**, and it is completely orthogonal to any purge — so it delivers most of the clone-time win **now**, and keeps delivering it as beat `.webp` re-accretes between purge windows.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The site repo's health is a periodic-ritual hygiene axis — LOCAL-reclaim the non-main-reachable bloat (no history rewrite), audit `public/` for orphan clone assets + media-that-belongs-on-R2, keep blobless (R-SITE-REPO-HYGIENE; 2026-07-29)

**`spark-anvil-site`'s git + working-tree health has THREE re-accreting sources — NONE requiring a history rewrite as the first move — and each has a standing action. A site whose fresh clones re-bloat, whose `public/` accretes dead clone assets, or that drifts off the blobless default is suffering this class, NOT (necessarily) a media re-bloat needing the R-SITE-HISTORY-PURGE `filter-repo`. Audit + attack these three FIRST; the history rewrite (R-SITE-HISTORY-PURGE) is the last resort, exactly as R-HUB-REPO-HYGIENE frames the hub's own bloat.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-29 (*"audit the website repo and clean it up"*), the site sibling of `portfolio.md` § R-HUB-REPO-HYGIENE, grounded in the 2026 large-repo-health canon (periodic-ritual cleanup + partial-clone default + CI size-guards; `Docs/PLAN_REPO_HEALTH_HYGIENE_2026-07-29.md` § Research §1). Deep-web-research-confirmed; audit `Docs/AUDIT_SITE_REPO_HEALTH_2026-07-29.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Distribute ≠ upload: audio must reach R2 in the same wave (R-R2-AUDIO-UPLOAD-COMPLETENESS; 2026-07-03)

**The site serves EVERY chapter-narration + audio-drama `.m4a` from Cloudflare R2 (`cdn.spark-and-anvil.com`), NOT from the repo. A content wave that distributes a chapter but forgets to push its `.m4a` to R2 ships a SILENT production 404 — the audio player renders (its `.vtt` is committed) but the audio fails to load, with ZERO build-time signal.** This is the load-bearing companion to R-SITE-MEDIA-R2: that rule says "`git rm` the `.m4a` out of `public/`"; THIS rule says "…but only AFTER it is verified on R2." The two together are the complete discipline; doing the `git rm` without the upload is the exact defect this rule exists to prevent.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Wave-runner idempotency is `.vtt`-present, NOT `.m4a`-present (R-WAVE-RUNNER-R2-IDEMPOTENCY; 2026-07-08)

**A chapter-audio wave runner's "already shipped?" skip-check MUST treat a chapter as shipped when EITHER the local site `.m4a` exists OR the committed site `.vtt` exists — NEVER a bare `[ -f "$site_m4a" ]`.** Post-`R-SITE-MEDIA-R2` the chapter-narration `.m4a` is pruned from `spark-anvil-site/public/` and lives ONLY on R2, so a local-`.m4a`-only test fails for **every** already-shipped chapter, and the runner **over-regenerates all of them** — burning paid Gemini TTS, overwriting the shipped R2 audio take, and desyncing the committed `.vtt` (line-cue timings drift against the new take). The committed `.vtt` is the durable proxy for "this chapter shipped."

**R-R2-NO-ROLLBACK:** never upload a git checkout over a newer R2 take (`upload_audio_to_r2.py` keeps it; ADR-309 Amd 7).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## R2 is the system-of-record for audio — it MUST be backed up off-R2 (R-R2-SYSTEM-OF-RECORD; 2026-07-07)

**Because R-SITE-MEDIA-R2 `git rm`'d the chapter-narration + audio-drama `.m4a` out of `spark-anvil-site/public/`, Cloudflare R2 (`spark-anvil-books`) is the SYSTEM-OF-RECORD for that audio — not a rebuildable cache. R2 has no automatic backup; a bucket deletion / corruption / accidental lifecycle purge would lose the exact shipped audio take with no one-command restore. Therefore every R2-only `.m4a` MUST have a byte-identical copy committed off-R2 (GitHub).** Codified after the V32 P0 backup audit (`Docs/AUDIT_ASSET_BACKUP_COVERAGE_2026-07-06.md`) proved 0 permanent-loss orphans but surfaced 744 `.m4a` (717 chapter-narration + 27 dramas) whose only copy was on R2 — regenerable from committed text via paid Gemini TTS (~$75–150, and a DIFFERENT voice take with drifted VTT), which is a lossy fallback, **not** a backup.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Cast portrait slug convention (R-CAST-PORTRAIT-SLUG; 2026-06-05)

**The portrait file at `spark-anvil-site/public/cast/<app>/<char>.webp` MUST match the chapter MD filename slug at `src/content/chapters/<app>/<char>.md`.** Both are the same `<char>` token. This is load-bearing because chapter pages at `/cast/<app>/<char>` render `<img src="/cast/<app>/<char>.webp">` with no fallback; Astro static-build doesn't verify `<img src>` targets, so a slug mismatch ships as a silently-broken portrait link with no build-time error.

**R-PORTRAIT-NO-CHECKER:** no painted checker in a portrait: `check_cast_portrait_interior_checker.py`.

**R-PORTRAIT-IDENTITY-REQUIRED (P#221):** a paid `gen_cast_portraits.py` run refuses a role-only character (it drew animals as kids); author the identity in `scripts/data/cast_portrait_identities.json` first (`--allow-role-only` only on purpose). Name a pose and prop, no symbol; "full body".

**AMENDMENT — ENSEMBLE chapters need a portrait too, and a MISSING one FREEZES prod (R-CAST-PORTRAIT-ENSEMBLE-COMPLETENESS; founder-flagged 2026-08-23).** An ENSEMBLE chapter (pair/cohort — `character:` is "A and B" / "A, B, and C") ALSO renders `<img src="/cast/<app>/<slug>.webp">` (keyed on the CHAPTER slug, not a member), so it needs its OWN pair/cohort portrait — gen it with `scripts/gen_cast_portraits.py --pairs <app>:<slug>` / `--cohorts <app>:<slug>` (reuses `_DEFAULT_STYLE` + the chapter frontmatter identity; MUST be animal/human-form-consistent with the members' solo portraits — the CHARACTER_FORM_OVERRIDES check). A chapter (solo OR ensemble) shipped WITHOUT its portrait is a hard broken `<img>` → `check-site-internal-links` CORE FAIL → **no deploy → prod frozen on a stale-cache fallback** ([[q-9376cd]]). TWO guard gaps this exposes (tracked [[q-d9875d]]): the portrait-coverage guard (a) SKIPS web-pioneered apps not yet in `apps.generated` `dnCast`, and (b) may not enumerate ensemble chapter slugs — so a missing ensemble portrait goes uncaught until it freezes prod; it MUST cover ensemble slugs + web-pioneered site-side apps + FAIL route-independently at PREBUILD (reds a staging PR, not prod). Composes with § R-CHAPTER-ART-COMPLETION + § R-SITE-CAST-SLUG-MAP-FRESHNESS.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Chapter front-matter duplicate-key gate (R-CHAPTER-YAML-DUP-KEY; 2026-06-26)

**Chapter MD YAML front-matter MUST NOT have any top-level key listed twice.** js-yaml strict mode (used by Astro's `gray-matter` content-collection loader) rejects duplicate keys with `duplicated mapping key` error → Cloudflare Workers Builds prebuild fails. Closes the V21+ P0 incident class surfaced 2026-06-26 evening (depthquest/trench.md + numbersense/pivot-pia.md both shipped with `gate-allow-text: []` listed twice).

**Sibling — colon-space scalar quoting (R-CHAPTER-YAML-SCALAR-QUOTE; 2026-08-14):** a chapter front-matter scalar VALUE containing an internal `: ` (colon-space) — routinely `role:`/`primitive:` (e.g. `primitive: ONE RULE…: Ada finds…`) — MUST be QUOTED, or js-yaml throws `mapping values are not allowed here` on any COLD build. **This failure is CACHE-MASKED in CI** by the incrementalBuild/content-layer cache (a green CI ≠ a green cold build for content-sync), so it only surfaces on a fresh worktree / cold `build:play` — diagnose from a cold build, and fix at the GEN source + a prebuild YAML-parse gate (q-3fa694). Reference: the 282-MD parser-guided fix, site #2181 (q-c5dca5).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Cast-member route-link coverage (R-CAST-ROUTE-COVERAGE; 2026-06-27)

**Any component or page that renders a `/cast/<app>/<char>` LINK from a cast member MUST guard it with `hasChapter(app, member.name)` AND derive the slug with `chapterSlugFor(app, member.name)` — never `slugChar()` directly, and never an unguarded `chapterSlugFor()`.** A member with no authored chapter has no route; linking to it ships a broken internal link → `check-site-internal-links.py` FAIL → red Cloudflare deploy.

**AMENDMENT — a BARE `/cast/<app>` app-index link is INVALID (no such route), and ONE broken link FREEZES a whole deploy unit (R-CAST-ROUTE-COVERAGE-NO-BARE-APP-LINK; founder-flagged 2026-08-23).** There is NO `/cast/<app>` app-index route — only `/cast/<app>/<char>` (+ `/advanced`) and the aggregate `/cast`. A HAND-AUTHORED prose link (`<a href="/cast/<app>">`) bypasses the `hasChapter` guard entirely, so it isn't caught by the guarded-component path above — it must instead point at a real per-char chapter (`/cast/<app>/<slug>`) or the aggregate `/cast`. Load-bearing because ONE broken internal link fails the postbuild `check-site-internal-links` → **no deploy → stale-cache fallback → that ENTIRE deploy unit is FROZEN** (2026-08-23 TutorForge PLAY-unit freeze: a single bare `/cast/tutorforge` in `coaches.astro` froze the PLAY unit → the ADR-177 `/study` upload input never went live; the CORE unit was simultaneously frozen by 4 missing ensemble portraits — § R-CAST-PORTRAIT-ENSEMBLE-COMPLETENESS; [[q-9376cd]]). And FAST staging previews do NOT build the `/cast`+`/play` route trees (§ R-SITE-FAST-BUILD-DEV-SUBSET), so a bare-app-link / missing-portrait passes staging SILENTLY and only reds — and freezes — the FULL prod build. So this class MUST be caught by a route-independent PREBUILD guard (a `src/`-wide grep for bare `/cast/<app>` links + the portrait-completeness guard), NOT left to the postbuild link-check that only the full prod build exercises. Tracked [[q-d9875d]]. Composes with § R-SITE-CAST-SLUG-MAP-FRESHNESS + § R-SITE-CHAPTER-MANIFEST-SYNC (same "prebuild route-independent guard, not postbuild-that-freezes-prod" discipline).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Multi-beat chapter snapshot convention (R-MULTIBEAT-SNAPSHOT; 2026-06-10)

**Multi-beat chapter pages read prose from a SNAPSHOT at `public/chapters/<app>/chapter_<char>.md` — NOT from `src/content/chapters/<app>/<char>.md`.** When the source-of-truth chapter MD is rewritten (e.g., Option C register cleanup, content corrections, register rewrites), **the snapshot must be regenerated alongside the per-beat sidecar + illustrations + audio**, because the sidecar's `prose-range: { from-line, to-line }` indexes against the snapshot's line numbers AND the per-beat audio narration speaks the snapshot's prose.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Path B illustration prompt parity (R-PATH-B-PROMPT-PARITY; 2026-06-11)

**Per-beat illustration prompts in `scripts/pilot_interleaved_ensemble_chapter.py` MUST include (a) chapter prose for the beat's `prose-range`, (b) a character-identity block from the chapter's YAML front-matter + opening passage, AND (c) a per-app `base_style` resolved via `STYLE_REGISTRY`.** **(d) R-SCENE-IDENTITY-REFERENCE (2026-10-03):** a cast scene carries its shipped portrait as reference (form canon if no beat pin); an ensemble opener names each member against the group portrait; `check_scene_identity_wiring.py`. **(e) R-BEAT-DISTINCT-MOMENTS:** five beats, five moments; no opener reference when a portrait exists; v13 gates copies + photoreal beats → REFERENCE.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Pre-distribute anatomy gate (R-ANATOMY-GATE; 2026-06-29)

**Every newly-generated cast artifact (chapter beat / cast portrait / book cover) MUST pass an anatomy-defect gate before distribution, the same way it must pass the text-leak gate.** Sister rule to R-PATH-B-TEXT-LEAK-GATE. Codified after a user-reported defect ("cast character has 3 hands") + the V25 portfolio anatomy sweep (`scripts/audit_image_anatomy.py --all-sweep`), which surfaced glitches the text-leak gate never looked at (e.g. `chanceforge/flipside` — two faces on one head).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every generated illustration is visually reviewed for UNINTENDED INAPPROPRIATE READINGS — anatomically-suggestive shapes + racial/cultural caricature (R-IMAGE-APPROPRIATENESS; 2026-07-18)

**Every generated character/scene illustration on a kid-facing surface (chapter beat / opener / spot · cast portrait · book cover · mascot · any `/story`, `/cast`, `/play`, or homepage art) MUST be VISUALLY REVIEWED by the in-session agent — the agent Reads the rendered image — for UNINTENDED INAPPROPRIATE READINGS before it ships, and any image that carries one is regenerated with a tightened prompt, never shipped. This is the appropriateness sibling of R-ANATOMY-GATE + R-PATH-B-TEXT-LEAK-GATE, and — like the screenshot-DoD — it is a HUMAN/agent VISUAL judgment, because an automated classifier cannot reliably catch "this shape/feature happens to look like X."** Codified per founder-flag 2026-07-18 (the bugscamp `/story` "Wiggle" card: the under-stone garden grub — a pale, smooth, C-curled tubular form with a rounded tip — read as **anatomically suggestive (a male private part)**; regenerated + shipped as bugscamp-app #53 / site #1029).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Every generated CHARACTER is depicted with ATTIRE-DIGNITY + ANATOMICAL WHOLENESS — no ragged/patched/impoverished clothing (poverty stereotype), no unintended peg-leg/stump/hook/missing-limb caricature, legs VISIBLE not waist-cropped; enforced BY CONSTRUCTION via a shared gen-prompt DIGNITY_CLAUSE + a fleet audit (R-CHARACTER-ATTIRE-DIGNITY; 2026-08-27)

**Every kid-facing generated CHARACTER (cast portrait · mascot · book cover · chapter beat · any `/play`/`/story`/homepage character art) MUST be depicted with DIGNITY + WHOLENESS: (1) clothing NEAT/CLEAN/WHOLE — NEVER ragged/torn/patched/threadbare/impoverished (a poverty stereotype that strips dignity, UNICEF); (2) anatomically WHOLE + able-bodied — both arms + BOTH natural legs, NO peg-leg/wooden-leg/stump/hook/missing-limb/prosthetic added as flavour (the unintended "disabled-villain/pirate" caricature), UNLESS a disability is an EXPLICIT, dignified, CANONICALLY-specified trait of THIS named character; (3) LEGS VISIBLE in frame (full figure or legs-in-view) — never a waist-crop/prop-occlusion that reads as "no legs". Gen models HALLUCINATE these from a character's NAME/THEME with no canonical basis (seed: pipquest "Peglegra the Bold" — role "the brave forward-run", rendered a peg-legged pirate in patched rags on both its `/cast` portrait AND its book cover). Enforced BY CONSTRUCTION: a shared `DIGNITY_CLAUSE` (`scripts/gen_book_covers.py`) is injected into EVERY character-gen prompt (cover · strategy-cover · cast-portrait); the fleet's already-generated art is remediated worst-first by the CADA audit (regen dignified + REDISTRIBUTE to all surfaces, R-RESOURCE-FIX-REDISTRIBUTE). A ragged/peg-leg/legless UNINTENDED depiction is a defect on the R-IMAGE-APPROPRIATENESS footing; attire/wholeness sibling of § R-ANATOMY-GATE + § R-IMAGE-APPROPRIATENESS (which a valid-anatomy peg-leg + non-text patched clothes slip past). Deliberate dignified disability representation is explicitly ALLOWED — this targets the unintended caricature only.** Founder-direct 2026-08-27; decision `ADR-195`; program `PLAN_CADA_CHARACTER_ATTIRE_DIGNITY_2026-08-27.md`; research `RESEARCH_CHARACTER_ATTIRE_DIGNITY_2026-08-27.md`. Tracked `Docs/REGISTRY_CHARACTER_ATTIRE_DIGNITY.txt` + `scripts/check_character_attire_dignity.py` (SHAPE-B WARN; `--ci-mode` FAILS on a DIGNITY_CLAUSE wiring regression).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-195_CHARACTER_ATTIRE_DIGNITY.md` + `Docs/PLAN_CADA_CHARACTER_ATTIRE_DIGNITY_2026-08-27.md`.
## Chapter hero source-of-truth (R-CHAPTER-HERO-SOURCE; 2026-06-11)

**For multi-beat chapters, beat 0 IS the chapter hero. The top-of-page `chapter_<char>_opener.webp` (rendered via `<ChapterIllustration variant="opener" />`) MUST NOT also render** — doing both creates visual redundancy (two opening-scene heroes within 200px) and wastes gen budget at portfolio scale.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
