## Content upload + manifest rebuild discipline (R-CONTENT-UPLOAD-MANIFEST-DISCIPLINE; 2026-06-19)

**Every content upload to spark-anvil-site MUST result in the corresponding freshness manifest being rebuilt before/during the next Cloudflare Workers Builds deploy.** The site's `package.json` `prebuild` chain handles 5 of 6 manifests automatically via filesystem-scan or git-mtime-scan builders. The 6th manifest (`pdfs-recency.json`) lives hub-side and requires explicit re-run after every PDF render wave.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Sidecar `tier` field required (R-SIDECAR-TIER-REQUIRED; 2026-06-19)

**Every multi-beat sidecar manifest MUST carry a `tier` field with integer value 1 or 2.** Applies to BOTH source-of-truth sidecars in `labsmith/Resources/AutoSegmentedChapters/<app>/<char>.beats.json` AND distributed copies in `spark-anvil-site/public/chapters/<app>/chapter_<char>.beats.json`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Tier-2 `/advanced` route needs a content-collection entry (R-TIER-2-CONTENT-ENTRY; 2026-06-30)

**A Tier-2 `/advanced` page ONLY builds if a `src/content/chapters/<app>/<char>-advanced.md` content-collection entry exists.** Shipping the `public/chapters/<app>/chapter_<char>-advanced.*` asset set (snapshot + sidecar + beats + audio + vtt) and getting the chapter into `multibeat-chapters.json` is **NOT sufficient** — the route `src/pages/cast/[app]/[char]/advanced.astro` builds its paths from `getCollection('chapters')` filtered to `*-advanced.md`, so with no content entry the route never generates and the page **404s** despite every asset being present.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Read-aloud MUST use the shared best-voice helper (never a bare default-voice utterance) + be AVAILABLE-BUT-OPTIONAL on every band's prompt/stem (R-WEB-CLONE-READ-ALOUD-VOICE; 2026-07-24)

**Every `/play` read-aloud / speak / "🔊 Hear it again" surface MUST speak through the shared `src/lib/play/_shared/speech.ts` helper (`import { speak } from '../_shared/speech'; speak(text, { rate, pitch, lang })`) — NEVER a hand-rolled bare `new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text)` + default voice. The default browser/OS voice is the robotic legacy voice on most platforms (macOS Fred-era · Android/Linux eSpeak · low-quality "compact"), which is why younger-band prompt audio "sounds terrible" (founder-flag V584); the helper picks the BEST available natural voice for the utterance's language (`pickBestVoice` — ranks network Google / "Natural"/"Neural"/"Enhanced"/"Premium"/known-good-named voices, de-prioritizes novelty/compact/eSpeak; resolves the async `voiceschanged` race) and speaks with a warm, calm register. A bare-default-voice utterance in a clone is a defect on the same footing as a dark surface.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-24 (*"why the prompt audio in the younger bands sound so terrible? can we make it better? codify all these"* + *"make prompt audio available but optional for the 9-14, 15-18 age bands too"*).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading). Cross-refs: `cognitive-accessibility.md` § R-COGNITIVE-ACCESSIBILITY (Reading-Access read-aloud backbone) · `distributed-narrative.md` § R-YOUNGER-BAND-CONTENT-REGISTER (audio-first is the younger delivery) + § R-BRIDGE-BAND-DESIGN (`mcRound readAloud`) · § R-SITE-FEEDBACK (no dark-pattern) · § R-WEB-CLONE-SCREENSHOT-DOD · queue V584/V585.
## Gemini API key single-flight discipline (R-GEMINI-KEY-SERIAL; 2026-06-30)

**The hub shares ONE Gemini key (`~/.config/labsmith/gemini_api_key`) that throttles hard: ONE key-consuming operation at a time.**

**Enforced:** every Gemini script calls `require_key_free()` (`scripts/lib/gemini_key_preflight.py`) first; it registers the caller, so a spender of any name blocks others; `check_gemini_preflight_coverage.py --ci-mode` fails one that doesn't. No gen process ≠ free key: a drain takes a lease (`--lease <lane> --minutes N`).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## Estimate Gemini gen cost from TOKENS (usageMetadata × per-modality rates), reconciled against Cloud Billing — NEVER a static `$/image` heuristic (R-GEMINI-COST-ESTIMATION; 2026-08-09)

**Gemini gen cost is TOKEN-priced across MULTIPLE modalities + a billed THINKING component — so a flat `$/image` (or `$/asset`) heuristic systematically UNDER-estimates it (measured ~5× low, founder 2026-08-09). Estimate cost from the `usageMetadata` the API returns on EVERY call × the CURRENT per-modality rates, summed per asset/wave, and RECONCILED against the Google Cloud Billing actual. The per-asset "ceilings" in `portfolio.md` § Asset-generation are hand-guessed $/image numbers → they are KNOWN-UNDERESTIMATED and MUST be re-derived from measured `usageMetadata` (a tracked build), not trusted.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-09 (*"actual costs are about 5× your estimates … deep web research + think harder about how to correctly estimate"*); full method + rates + the 5× root-cause + the dashboard/billing ground truth in `Docs/RESEARCH_GEMINI_COST_ESTIMATION_2026-08-09.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/RESEARCH_GEMINI_COST_ESTIMATION_2026-08-09.md`.
## Long single-flight gen pipelines are DRIVEN with foreground sleep-waits — never background-and-stop (R-GEN-FOREGROUND-DRIVE; 2026-07-14)

**When the founder has said "do not stop until fully done" (or otherwise authorized an autonomous multi-app run), a long single-flight gen pipeline — the coverage program, a cast-expansion wave, any pipeline whose steps serialize on the shared Gemini key (R-GEMINI-KEY-SERIAL) — MUST be driven by the agent with FOREGROUND `sleep`-poll waits between the key-serialized steps, NOT by launching a background gen and ENDING the turn to await a task-notification.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-14 (*"resume and do not stop. use sleep if needed"* → *"again: resume and do not stop. use sleep instead"* → *"codify this rule in repo"*), after a session repeatedly backgrounded each ~15-min pilot / ~35-min T2 gen and ended its turn, forcing the founder to type "resume" once per step — which defeats the auto-cycle and stalls a weeks-long program on human keystrokes.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## Prefer `-latest` model aliases in pipeline scripts (R-GEMINI-MODEL-ALIAS; 2026-07-09)

**Every Gemini-backed pipeline script MUST reference a rotation-proof `-latest` model alias (or the current preview family) — NEVER a pinned mid-generation version ID like `gemini-2.5-flash` that a family rotation can silently 404 out from under a running batch.** Codified after the V60 incident (2026-07-09): mid-V45 the **entire `gemini-2.5` `generateContent` family was retired** — `gemini-2.5-flash`, `gemini-2.5-pro`, `gemini-2.0-flash`, `gemini-2.5-flash-image-preview` all began returning **`404 NOT_FOUND`** — while ~1300 in-flight text-leak audit images errored and every pipeline script hardcoding a 2.5 ID broke at once. (Wrinkle: the retired IDs still appear in `models.list()` with lagging metadata, so a list check is NOT sufficient to confirm a model is live — you must probe `generateContent`.)

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## An orientation surface (homepage / age-band zone) may carry an EXPLANATORY hero illustration via the shared `<ExplainerHero>` — depict-what-you-do, age-appropriate, dark-safe-by-frame; reuse the component, never hand-roll (R-SITE-EXPLANATORY-HERO; 2026-07-22)

**When a page or an age-band zone's PURPOSE may be too abstract for a first-time visitor, it MAY carry an EXPLANATORY hero illustration — a picture that makes "what you do here" concrete — rendered via the shared `src/components/ExplainerHero.astro` component (NEVER a hand-rolled per-page `<img>`+frame). The illustration MUST be EXPLANATORY (depict the surface's actual activity), AGE-APPROPRIATE per the band, DARK-SAFE by the frame, and placed at a LOW-load ORIENTATION boundary (top of a page/zone) — never interleaved through a card grid or overlaid on an active learning loop.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-07-22 (extend the `/play/teach` explanatory hero to the homepage + per-age-band `/play` & `/story`), on the evidence in `Docs/RESEARCH_EXPLANATORY_HERO_ILLUSTRATIONS_PER_AGE_BAND_2026-07-22.md`: explanatory illustrations on low-load orientation surfaces AID comprehension (the beneficial case — NOT the "seductive detail" that hurts on high-load learning tasks; composes with § R-NARRATIVE-BETWEEN-NOT-DURING + § R-GUARD-THE-RATIO). Shipped: site PR #1234 (homepage + `/play` 4 bands + `/story` 4 bands).

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).
## The /play fleet supports DUAL-POINTER input + CLASS-CODE/guest (no-login) entry — the LOW-POWER school-device floor (single-threaded-WASM / no-cross-origin-isolation / WebGL2-floor / no-heavy-eager-runtime / thermal-budget) is RETIRED (R-WEB-CLONE-SCHOOL-DEVICE-PHYSICS; low-power P1/P2 retired 2026-09-25, ADR-293)

> **🔴 AMENDED 2026-09-25 (founder-direct "remove [the] low power school device[s] requirement"; decision `ADR-293`).** The LOW-POWER / low-end-hardware delivery floor is RETIRED: **P1** (single-threaded WASM · no `SharedArrayBuffer` · no `crossOriginIsolated`/COOP-COEP dependency · WebGL2 floor · no heavy eager runtime) and **P2** (passively-cooled thermal budget) are NO LONGER a codified requirement — a `/play` surface MAY now use **WebGPU-required rendering, multi-threaded WASM, `SharedArrayBuffer`/COOP-COEP, and heavier eager runtimes** (⚡ **Amd 1, 2026-09-25:** with **Safari 26 as the approved browser floor**, **WebGPU is an APPROVED BASELINE** — a surface MAY require WebGPU with **no mandated WebGL2 fallback floor**; ⚡ **Amd 2, 2026-09-25: multi-thread WASM / cross-origin isolation is APPROVED too** — scope COOP/COEP PER-ROUTE not site-wide (site-wide breaks R2-media/Plausible/embeds; prefer COEP `credentialless` + per-resource CORP), and fail-safe to the single-threaded build on the headerless POB-offline path [single-thread = the OFFLINE floor]; page-weight + the deterministic fail-safe floor still bind; ⚡ **Amd 3, 2026-09-27 (ADR-297): full multi-threaded WASM is the ONLINE DEFAULT + the low-end Chromebook/pre-Safari-26 iOS-Safari online tier is DROPPED** — single-thread = POB-offline floor + runtime fail-safe only (NOT the online default); modern Safari-26+ supported; the #87 stonesong Go Leg-2 low-end-latency flip-gate is RETIRED; execution DEFERRED to `q-p297-full-multithread`; ⚡ **Amd 5, 2026-09-29 (ADR-297 § Amd 2; supersedes Amd 4 "Chromebooks unsupported"): CHROMEBOOKS ARE SUPPORTED, no advanced feature removed** — capability-tiered defaults per § R-DEVICE-TIER-PROGRESSIVE-ENHANCEMENT; post-AUE devices best-effort); the P1-regression guards (hub `check_school_device_physics.py` + the site prebuild twin `check-school-device-physics.mjs`) are retired/slimmed. **P3 and P4 STILL STAND (unchanged, load-bearing):** **(P3) DUAL-POINTER input** — touch AND mouse AND pen via ONE Pointer Events handler set + `touch-action`, never a mouse-only path (composes § R-WEB-CLONE-DRAG-TARGET: single-pointer alt + keyboard + ≥44px); **(P4) CLASS-CODE / GUEST entry, NO logins (COPPA follows you to the web)** — no account/login/PII wall to enter, guest-by-default, multiplayer = ephemeral room-CODE (ADR-041), progress on-device (`localStorage`, no identifier — § R-WEB-CLONE-PROGRESS-NS-UNIQUE); a login/PII entry wall on a kid surface is a CRITICAL defect. The COPPA no-account/no-PII guarantee also stands independently on § R-SITE-FEEDBACK · § R-WEB-CLONE-MULTIPLAYER · § R-OLDER-BAND-ACCOUNT-APPS (the sole 13-18 pseudonymous carve-out); the no-third-party-tracking-SDK posture stands on § R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE (the retired site guard's 3P-CDN-`<script>` scan is RE-HOMED there, not dropped). **⚠ Retiring the LOW-POWER floor does NOT auto-relax its sibling perf rules — each keeps its own rationale + gate:** § R-SITE-PAGE-WEIGHT-BUDGET (per-page HTML weight) · § R-WEB-CLONE-CHESS-ENGINE-WASM / ADR-094 (Stockfish single-thread, chess-scoped) · § R-WEB-CLONE-SAMPLER-RENDERER-DUAL / ADR-212/251 (lazy heavy-3D, LCP-path) · § R-SITE-SDK-POSTURE + § R-DEPENDENCY-SUPPLY-CHAIN (adopting WebGPU/a multi-threaded/heavy runtime is still a founder+ADR decision). So "the web can look native" is now allowed by rule, but a specific heavy-runtime/WebGPU adoption is still gated by those siblings, and school-device support becomes a design JUDGMENT (test on real low-end hardware when it matters), not a hard fleet floor.** Founder-direct 2026-09-25 (scope-clarified from "remove the entire rule" → "low power school devices"). Historical: the original P1–P4 spec + the 2026-08-21 audit are preserved in `ADR-173` (status AMENDED) + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading) + `RESEARCH_WEB_CLONE_SCHOOL_DEVICE_PHYSICS_2026-08-21.md`.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-297_FULL_MULTITHREAD_DEFAULT_DROP_LOWEND_TIER.md` (Amd 3) + `Docs/ADR-294_TECHNICALLY_GATED_REJECTIONS_APPROVED.md` + `Docs/ADR-293_RETIRE_SCHOOL_DEVICE_LOW_POWER_FLOOR.md` (the low-power P1/P2 retirement) + `Docs/AUDIT_SCHOOL_DEVICE_FLOOR_RETIREMENT_REVISIT_2026-09-25.md` (the revisit of every prior rejection gated on the floor — full-engine / WebGPU / multi-thread WASM / Stockfish / Maia / heavy-3-D / Genie / TestDriver) + `ADR-173` (historical P1–P4) + `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## A modest device (a 4 GB school Chromebook) is SUPPORTED by PROGRESSIVE ENHANCEMENT — capability tiers pick a lighter DEFAULT, never a removal; detect capability, NEVER the user-agent; the learner can always override; RUM + an emulated-device e2e track it (R-DEVICE-TIER-PROGRESSIVE-ENHANCEMENT; 2026-09-29)

**Supporting a platform keeps every advanced feature available on it; only the DEFAULT adapts. (1) Capability, never the user-agent: the shared `_shared/deviceTier.ts` returns `full` | `lite` (≤ 4 GB `deviceMemory` or ≤ 2 cores; unknown memory = `full`). (2) A tier changes a default, never an availability. (3) The learner can override on-device (Reading Access → Heavy features). (4) Engine threads leave the page a core. Tracked by RUM `platform:` rows + `REGISTRY_DEVICE_SUPPORT_MATRIX.txt` + `tests/e2e/chromebook.spec.ts`; guard `check_chromebook_support.py`.** Founder-direct 2026-09-29; `ADR-297` § Amendment 2; program #209.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/REFERENCE_SPARK_ANVIL_WEBSITE.md` § (this heading).

## The Open Ludeme Commons — publish OUR OWN clean-room game DESCRIPTIONS under CC BY 4.0 (the proactive half of the clean-room posture); publish is COUNSEL-GATED (R-OPEN-LUDEME-COMMONS; 2026-09-05)

**Spark & Anvil publishes its OWN-authored, clean-room game DESCRIPTIONS (the parameterized `GameSpec`/ludeme rule-DATA behind AtlasTales / RelicForge / future heritage games — NOT engine code, NOT app UI, NOT cast art, NEVER any child data) under CC BY 4.0 (the recommended default; CC0 for the rawest reconstruction data only) as an "open ludeme commons" — the open alternative Ludii's BY-NC-ND structurally cannot offer. This is the PROACTIVE half of the licensing posture (#118/ADR-235 is the DEFENSIVE half); founder-approved 2026-09-05, but the FIRST PUBLIC PUBLISH is GATED on legal-counsel review (brief §10.7 — founder approval ≠ counsel approval; a public content commons is hard to reverse).** The commons is a LICENCE + ATTRIBUTION + PROVENANCE + INDEX layer over descriptions we already own (a curated manifest, `Docs/COMMONS_LUDEME_DESCRIPTIONS_2026-09-05.md`), NOT a fork or copy — the descriptions stay where they live. Load-bearing invariants (each a defect on the dark-route footing): (1) **DESCRIPTIONS ONLY** — never engine code / app source / cast art / kid-PII; (2) **CLEAN-ROOM PROVENANCE per description** (carries ADR-235 inv-3) — every published description cites its PRIMARY source (Bell/Murray/Culin/museum catalogue/Wikipedia CC-BY-SA/CC0 reconstruction data), authored NEVER from Ludii `.lud`/prose; a description without a provenance line is NOT publishable; (3) **COUNSEL-GATE the first publish** — an IP lawyer reviews the clean-room record + licence choice BEFORE the first public release; until then the manifest + licence scaffold are prepared but NOT published (logged pending in `REGISTRY_FOUNDER_CONFLICT_DEFERRALS.txt`); (4) **NO "Ludii"/"BGA"/publisher mark** in the commons name (clears R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED / R-NO-COMPETITOR-OWNED-TERMS); (5) **cultural-sensitivity gates bind** (cite sources · described-vs-observed rulesets · NAGPRA/UNDRIP register · R-SITE-NO-FOREIGN-FLAGS · community-consent provenance for heritage games); (6) **HONEST-YIELD** — a public-good/differentiation/open-education claim, NEVER a proven learning-gain ("the open alternative to Ludii's ND" is positioning, not efficacy); (7) **on-device/COPPA unchanged** — it publishes RULE DATA, never a child's data. The moderated public GALLERY is the teen Rules-as-Code Studio / StudioForge (#113) contribution path (human-reviewed, contributor keeps credit, CC BY 4.0); the Heritage Field-Kit (ADR-242 🟡 connector) is the community supply side. Decision `ADR-241`; execution `Docs/PLAN_BGA_LUDII_NETNEW_EXECUTION_2026-09-05.md`; manifest `Docs/COMMONS_LUDEME_DESCRIPTIONS_2026-09-05.md`. Cross-refs: `portfolio.md` § R-LUDII-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#118, ADR-235 defensive hygiene) · § R-BGA-LUDII-CONCEPT-PROGRAM (#120, the census) · `distributed-narrative.md` R-NAME-CHECK-LIVE-APPS-GENERATED.

→ **Full detail:** `Docs/ADR-241_OPEN_LUDEME_COMMONS.md`.

## The forward-port BUILD program (new deterministic-manipulative features on `/play` clones) is ordered by the SAME PEDAGOGICAL CLUSTER PRIORITY, executed as a priority-ordered ROUND-ROBIN at the CLONE grain — Logic first, then the foundational academics (Math·ELA·Science·Social-Studies), then the enrichment tail; thinnest-coverage-first within a cluster; §5b-verified genuinely-unbuilt at authoring-start; deep-web-researched foreground-sequential (R-WEB-CLONE-FORWARD-PORT-PEDAGOGICAL-PRIORITY; 2026-08-03)

**When the forward-port BUILD axis (net-new competitive + parity-gap deterministic-manipulative features — R-WEB-CLONE-FORWARD-PORT-PLAN / -FEATURE-REGISTRY) is being DRAINED as a "prioritize new features for pedagogically-important clusters" program, the WAVE ORDER is by pedagogical CLUSTER PRIORITY — (1) Logic & Puzzles [founder-elevated highest], then the foundational academics (2) Math · (3) English & Language Arts · (4) Science · (5) Social Studies, then the enrichment tail (CS · SEL · Visual Arts · Music) — executed as a PRIORITY-ORDERED ROUND-ROBIN at the CLONE grain (one clone from each priority cluster per round, Logic leading every round). Within a cluster, thinnest-coverage-first (fewest shipped forward-port features = richest gap; `Docs/REGISTRY_FORWARD_PORT_PEDAGOGICAL_WORKLIST.txt`). The per-clone TARGET is the genuinely-UNBUILT richest deterministic-manipulative P1 (a §5b judgment made against the FRESH origin tree at authoring-start — most rich 9-14/15-18 clones already ship their HEADLINE engine, so the wave must pick a DISTINCT surface. **⚠ A "gap" the rule NAMES is illustrative, NOT a live target — §5b-verify it against fresh origin, because it may have SHIPPED since (2026-08-09: nashforge's "equilibrium ≠ social-optimum" POE is already built — `welfare.ts`/`runDilemma.ts`, V787 W19 — so a worklist `feat=N` undercounts SURFACES vs filed backport handoffs, the V815 REGISTRY-thin≠SURFACE-thin caveat; the round §5b-confirmed Logic-top rich and moved to the calcquest "Smooth or Sharp?" differentiability-decision POE, site #2003, as the genuinely-unbuilt research-gated gap). ⚠ **The §5b-verify MUST read the EXISTING engine's PURPOSE/header, NOT just grep for the plan's PROPOSED filename — a plan's named P1 can already be SHIPPED under a DIFFERENT filename/route than the plan proposed, so a filename-only origin check gives a FALSE-unbuilt (2026-08-09: proofquest's forward-port plan proposed `bridge.ts`/`/bridge` for the BridgePuzzle/Euler-path gap; it was ALREADY built as `euler.ts` + `/proofquest/bridges`, V739 — a grep for `bridge.ts` would wrongly read it unbuilt + rebuild a duplicate; reading `euler.ts`'s header revealed it IS the BridgePuzzle port). Verify by the FEATURE/primitive present in the clone's existing surfaces, never the plan's proposed path.**** Each wave: deep-web-research FOREGROUND-SEQUENTIAL (R-WEBSEARCH-FANOUT-RATELIMIT, NEVER `Skill(deep-research)`) + think-harder → §5b re-verify unbuilt → pure exported engine + engine⇔bank Vitest (R-WEB-CLONE-BOARD-GAME-ENGINE / -PUZZLE-ENGINE-VERIFIED) → dual-scheme × desktop+mobile screenshot-DoD (MAIN-SERIAL, R-PARALLEL §10) → site PR + hub PARITY row + iOS `_WEB_BACKPORT` handoff + `check_forward_port_features.py --write-registry`.** Codified per founder-direct 2026-08-03 (*"prioritize new features pedagogically important subjects and clusters such as logic, math, ela, science, social studies … work in round robin too. deep web research and think harder. audit, verify, track and codify everything"*). This is the forward-port-BUILD SIBLING of § R-GEN-DRAIN-PEDAGOGICAL-PRIORITY (the paid-gen drain) — SAME cluster-priority round-robin, applied to the free in-session-Opus deterministic-engine BUILD axis instead of the paid segment→TTS→art gen.
